--!nonstrict
--[[
	khookclone.lua
	A clean-room reimplementation of khook's menu and feature set.

	khook itself only exists as an obfuscated decompile (khook.lua, 253k lines,
	16,669 unbound upvalues -- it cannot be executed). This file rebuilds the
	same product from that source's observable structure: the window geometry,
	theme palette, element specs, tab tree and the complete default-config
	schema were all read out of khook.lua and are reproduced here 1:1.

	Target game: Rivals (Nosniy Games).

	Layout of this file
		1  Environment + small utilities        (Signal, Trove, Tween batch)
		2  Theme
		3  Config store (defaults + path get/set/watch + save/load)
		4  UI library    -- Window / Tab / Page / Grid / Section / Group / rows
		5  UI elements   -- Toggle Slider RangeSlider Dropdown MultiDropdown
		                    Keybind Color TextBox Button Label List Divider Gear
		6  Overlays      -- notifications, keybind list, watermark
		7  Game adapter  -- entity/weapon resolution for Rivals, with fallbacks
		8  Features      -- combat, ESP, movement, world, weapon, misc
		9  Menu build    -- every tab, section and element
		10 Bootstrap
]]

local CLONE_VERSION = "1.0.0"

--============================================================================--
-- 1. ENVIRONMENT + UTILITIES
--============================================================================--

local cloneref = cloneref or function(o) return o end

local Players            = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local RunService         = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))

local TweenService       = cloneref(game:GetService("TweenService"))

local HttpService        = cloneref(game:GetService("HttpService"))
local ReplicatedStorage  = cloneref(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
local SoundService       = cloneref(game:GetService("SoundService"))
local Workspace          = cloneref(game:GetService("Workspace"))
local CoreGui            = cloneref(game:GetService("CoreGui"))

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Camera      = Workspace.CurrentCamera

-- executor surface, all optional
local getgenv     = getgenv     or function() return _G end

local writefile   = writefile
local readfile    = readfile

local ROOT_FOLDER = "khookclone"
local CONFIG_FOLDER = ROOT_FOLDER .. "/configs"

--- Shared environment guard: a second execution tears the first one down.
do
	local g = getgenv()
	local running = g.__khookclone or (shared and shared.__khookclone)
	if running and running.Unload then
		pcall(running.Unload)
	end
end

--- Forward declaration: the notification queue lives in section 6, but the
--- keybind element in section 5 already needs to post to it.
local Notify

local function round(n, step)
	if step and step > 0 then
		return math.floor(n / step + 0.5) * step
	end
	return math.floor(n + 0.5)
end

local function clamp(n, lo, hi)
	return n < lo and lo or (n > hi and hi or n)
end

local function lerp(a, b, t) return a + (b - a) * t end

--- Multiply a colour's brightness -- khook uses this for hover/press states.
local function shade(c, f)
	return Color3.new(
		clamp(c.R * f, 0, 1),
		clamp(c.G * f, 0, 1),
		clamp(c.B * f, 0, 1)
	)
end

local function deepCopy(t)
	if typeof(t) ~= "table" then return t end
	local out = {}
	for k, v in pairs(t) do
		out[k] = typeof(v) == "table" and deepCopy(v) or v
	end
	return out
end

--- Deep-merge `src` into `dst`, only where `dst` already has the key.
--- Used when loading a saved config so unknown/stale keys are dropped.
local function deepMergeKnown(dst, src)
	if typeof(dst) ~= "table" or typeof(src) ~= "table" then return end
	for k, v in pairs(src) do
		local cur = dst[k]
		if typeof(cur) == "table" and typeof(v) == "table" then
			deepMergeKnown(cur, v)
		elseif cur ~= nil and typeof(cur) == typeof(v) then
			dst[k] = v
		elseif cur == nil then
			dst[k] = v -- free-form maps (atomic lists) keep unknown keys
		end
	end
end

--- Overwrite `dst`'s contents with `src`'s, reusing every existing sub-table.
--- Table identity is preserved all the way down, which is what lets features
--- hold direct references into the config tree across a load or reset.
local function mergeInto(dst, src)
	for k in pairs(dst) do
		if src[k] == nil then dst[k] = nil end
	end
	for k, v in pairs(src) do
		if typeof(v) == "table" and typeof(dst[k]) == "table" then
			mergeInto(dst[k], v)
		else
			dst[k] = v
		end
	end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------- Signal --

local Signal = {}
Signal.__index = Signal

function Signal.new()
	return setmetatable({ _handlers = {} }, Signal)
end

function Signal:Connect(fn)
	local h = { fn = fn, connected = true }
	table.insert(self._handlers, h)
	return {
		Disconnect = function()
			h.connected = false
			for i, v in ipairs(self._handlers) do
				if v == h then table.remove(self._handlers, i) break end
			end
		end,
	}
end

function Signal:Fire(...)
	for _, h in ipairs(table.clone(self._handlers)) do
		if h.connected then
			local ok, err = pcall(h.fn, ...)
			if not ok then warn("[khookclone] signal handler: " .. tostring(err)) end
		end
	end
end

function Signal:Destroy()
	table.clear(self._handlers)
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------- Trove --
-- khook's cleanup primitive: everything a feature creates is added to a trove
-- and released in one call. Extend() makes a child trove owned by the parent.

local Trove = {}
Trove.__index = Trove

function Trove.new(name)
	return setmetatable({ _name = name, _items = {} }, Trove)
end

function Trove:Add(obj)
	table.insert(self._items, obj)
	return obj
end

function Trove:Connect(signal, fn)
	local c = signal:Connect(fn)
	table.insert(self._items, c)
	return c
end

function Trove:Extend()
	return self:Add(Trove.new(self._name))
end

function Trove:Remove(obj)
	for i, v in ipairs(self._items) do
		if v == obj then
			table.remove(self._items, i)
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function releaseItem(item)
	local t = typeof(item)
	if t == "function" then
		pcall(item)
	elseif t == "RBXScriptConnection" then
		pcall(function() item:Disconnect() end)
	elseif t == "Instance" then
		pcall(function() item:Destroy() end)
	elseif t == "table" then
		if item.Destroy then
			pcall(function() item:Destroy() end)
		elseif item.Disconnect then
			pcall(function() item:Disconnect() end)
		elseif item.Remove then
			pcall(function() item:Remove() end)
		end
	end
end

function Trove:Clean()
	local items = self._items
	self._items = {}
	for i = #items, 1, -1 do
		releaseItem(items[i])
	end
end

Trove.Destroy = Trove.Clean

------------------------------------------------------------------ Tween/Batch --

local TWEEN_FAST = TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local TWEEN_MAIN = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
--- khook's drag tween (khook.lua:29897 + DragSpeed 0.05 at 29834). Everything
--- that chases the pointer -- picker cursors, gradient markers, the stop handle
--- -- eases with this instead of snapping.
local TWEEN_DRAG = TweenInfo.new(0.05, Enum.EasingStyle.Exponential, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

local function tween(obj, props, info)
	local t = TweenService:Create(obj, info or TWEEN_MAIN, props)
	t:Play()
	return t
end
--============================================================================--
-- 2. THEME
--============================================================================--
-- Palette taken verbatim from khook.lua's frozen default config (Theme table).

local Theme = {}
Theme.Colors = {
	Accent                     = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 95),
	Outline                    = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38),
	Background                 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18),
	ElementBackground          = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 23),
	TabButtonSelected          = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 34),
	Unselected                 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 102, 120),
	TextColor                  = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238),
	ToggleCircleUnselected     = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 52),
	ToggleBackgroundUnselected = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 28),
}
Theme.TabHighlight = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 95)
Theme.Changed = Signal.new()
Theme.AccentChanged = Signal.new()

function Theme.get(key)
	return Theme.Colors[key] or Color3.new(1, 0, 1)
end

function Theme.set(key, color)
	Theme.Colors[key] = color
	if key == "Accent" then
		Theme.TabHighlight = color
		Theme.AccentChanged:Fire(color)
	end
	Theme.Changed:Fire(key, color)
end

--- A "batch" binds instance properties to theme keys so a palette change
--- repaints everything without the caller tracking instances.
local Batch = {}
Batch.__index = Batch

function Batch.new(trove)
	local self = setmetatable({ _trove = trove, _binds = {}, _stateful = {} }, Batch)
	trove:Connect(Theme.Changed, function(key, color)
		for _, b in ipairs(self._binds) do
			if b.key == key and b.inst.Parent ~= nil then
				pcall(function() b.inst[b.prop] = color end)
			end
		end
		for _, s in ipairs(self._stateful) do
			if s.key == key then pcall(s.fn, color) end
		end
	end)
	return self
end

function Batch:Bind(inst, prop, key)
	table.insert(self._binds, { inst = inst, prop = prop, key = key })
	pcall(function() inst[prop] = Theme.get(key) end)
	return inst
end

function Batch:BindStateful(key, fn)
	table.insert(self._stateful, { key = key, fn = fn })
	pcall(fn, Theme.get(key))
end

--- khook paints every panel with a subtle vertical gradient rather than a flat
--- fill; this reproduces it against whichever theme key backs the surface.
function Batch:BindGradient(gradient, key)
	local function apply(c)
		gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, shade(c, 1.06)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c),
		})
	end
	self:BindStateful(key, apply)
end

--============================================================================--
-- 3. CONFIG STORE
--============================================================================--
-- The schema below is the same tree khook freezes as its default profile.
-- Every UI element is bound to a path into this tree, and every feature
-- subscribes to the paths it cares about -- so "the UI" and "the features"
-- never talk to each other directly.

local function animBlock(shimmerColor, perimeter, ppBack, ppMain)
	return {
		Enabled = false,
		Kind = "Shimmer",
		Shimmer  = { Speed = 0.65, Color = shimmerColor or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40) },
		Perimeter = { Speed = 1, Color = perimeter or ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
		}) },
		PingPong = {
			Speed = 1,
			BackgroundColor = ppBack or Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20),
			MainColor = ppMain or Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255),
			Rotation = 0,
		},
	}
end

local function keybind(kind, showInList)
	return {
		State = false,
		Kind = kind or "Always",
		Bind = nil,
		ShowInList = showInList ~= false,
		Invisible = false,
	}
end

local function targetBlock(defaultType)
	return {
		Conditions = { Visible = true, Vulnerable = true, NotDeflecting = true, NotShielded = true },
		Type = defaultType,
		TargetParts = {
			Air    = { HitboxHead = true, HitboxBody = true },
			Ground = { HitboxHead = true, HitboxBody = true },
		},
		PartSelection = { Type = "Closest", HeadPercent = 50 },
		Surface = { ReductionPercentage = 0, Center = true },
	}
end

local function fovBlock()
	return {
		Enabled = true,
		Kind = "Circle",
		Circle = {
			Visible = false, Radius = 150, FollowTarget = true, FromMuzzle = false,
			Stroke = {
				Gradient = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
				Transparency = 0, Thickness = 1,
			},
			Fill = {
				Enabled = false,
				Gradient = ColorSequence.new({
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   Color3.fromRGB(255, 245, 238)),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 180)),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 105)),
				}),
				Transparency = 0.15,
			},
			Animated = { Enabled = false, Speed = 90 },
		},
		Custom = {
			AssetId = "", Visible = true, ScaleFactor = 1, Rotation = 0,
			Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0), Transparency = 1,
		},
	}
end

--- One ESP faction block (khook keeps an independent copy for Enemy and Team).
local function espFaction()
	return {
		Enabled = false,
		Keybind = keybind("Always", true),
		Name       = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), Transparency = 0, Animation = animBlock() },
		Box        = { Enabled = false, Style = "Full", Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), Animation = animBlock() },
		FilledBox  = {
			Enabled = false,
			Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1)),
			Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.8),
			Gradient = { Enabled = false, Animate = false, Color = ColorSequence.new({
				ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)),
				ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(190,39,255)),
			}) },
		},
		BoxImage   = { Enabled = false, Path = "", Transparency = 0 },
		HealthBar  = { Enabled = false, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.new(0,1,0)), ColorMode = "Reactive", Animate = false },
		HealthNumber = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1) },
		HeldWeapon = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1), Transparency = 0, Animation = animBlock() },
		AmmoBar    = { Enabled = false, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.new(0,1,0)), ColorMode = "Reactive", Animate = false },
		Distance   = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1), Animation = animBlock() },
		Rank       = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1), Animation = animBlock() },
		Winstreak  = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1), Animation = animBlock() },
		Deflecting = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1), Transparency = 0, Animation = animBlock() },
		Chams      = {
			Enabled = false,
			InnerColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0), InnerTransparency = 0,
			OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(100,0,0), OutlineTransparency = 0.6,
			Glow = false, GlowColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0),
		},
		Skeleton   = {
			Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1), Transparency = 0, Thickness = 1,
			Gradient = false,
			GradientColor = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.fromRGB(0,170,255)),
		},
		HeadMarker = {
			Enabled = false, Shape = "Cross", Filled = true, Outline = false,
			Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,80,80), Transparency = 0,
			OutlineColor = Color3.new(0,0,0), OutlineTransparency = 0,
		},
		Tracer     = {
			Enabled = false, Color = Color3.new(1,1,1), Transparency = 0, Thickness = 1,
			Gradient = false,
			GradientColor = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.fromRGB(0,170,255)),
			Origin = "Bottom", Target = "Feet",
			Outline = false, OutlineColor = Color3.new(0,0,0),
			OutlineTransparency = 0, OutlineThickness = 1,
		},
	}
end

local function buildDefaults()
	local d = {}

	------------------------------------------------------------------ combat --
	d.TargetLock = {
		Enabled = false, LockOnly = false, Mode = "Lock",
		Bind = Enum.KeyCode.Y, BindShowInList = true, BindInvisible = false,
	}

	d.Aimbot = {
		Enabled = false,
		Keybind = keybind("Always", true),
		SwitchDelay = 0, ReactionTime = 0,
		DisableOnReload = true, EnableOnlyOnScope = false,
		Mode = { Type = "Linear", Smoothing = 1, Speed = 0.1 },
		Target = targetBlock("Surface"),
		FovSettings = fovBlock(),
		GunSpecific = { Enabled = false },
	}

	d.SilentAim = {
		Enabled = false,
		Keybind = keybind("Always", true),
		ActivationChance = 100, ReactionTime = 0, SwitchDelay = 0,
		Target = targetBlock("Center"),
		FovSettings = fovBlock(),
		GunSpecific = { Enabled = false },
	}

	d.Ragebot = {
		Enabled = false,
		Keybind = keybind("Always", true),
		Stability = 0.15, ShootFrames = 1, PrioritizeHackers = false,
		Weapons = {
			Priority = { "Primary", "Secondary", "Melee" },
			Enabled  = { Primary = true, Secondary = true, Melee = true },
			OnEmpty  = "SwapOrReload",
		},
		Evasion = {
			Mode = "Random",
			Random = { AnchorFromCharacter = false, BaseRadius = 100, RadiusRandomFactor = 0.5 },
			ProjectileBreaker = {
				DepthForward = { Min = 0, Max = 4 }, DepthForwardFrequency = 5,
				DepthUp = { Min = 0, Max = 5.5 },    DepthUpFrequency = 5,
				RepositionInterval = 0.3,
				FallbackAnchorFromCharacter = false,
				FallbackBaseRadius = 100, FallbackRadiusRandomFactor = 0.5,
			},
			Translocate = { Offset = -5 },
		},
		HitscanOffsets = { Down = 1.75, Up = -0.25 },
		MeleeOffsets   = { Down = 4, Up = -4 },
	}

	d.Triggerbot = {
		Enabled = false,
		Keybind = keybind("Always", true),
		DisableOnReload = false, ActivateOnScope = false,
		NotDeflecting = true, NotShielded = true,
		ReactionTime = 0, ReleaseTime = 0,
		TargetParts = {
			Air    = { HitboxHead = true, HitboxBody = true },
			Ground = { HitboxHead = true, HitboxBody = true },
		},
		GunSpecific = { Enabled = false },
	}

	d.Flickbot = {
		Enabled = false,
		Keybind = keybind("Hold", true),
		Shoot = false, ShotDelay = 0, Cooldown = 250,
		FlickDuration = 110, Curvature = 12, Humanness = 30,
	}

	--------------------------------------------------------------------- esp --
	local enemy = espFaction()
	d.ESP = {
		Main = { Enabled = false, Keybind = keybind("Always", true), Mode = "Static" },
		Enemy = enemy,
		Team  = deepCopy(enemy),
		Settings = {
			UseDisplayName = false,
			Font = "Inconsolata", FlagFont = "Inconsolata",
			FontSize = 16, FlagFontSize = 12,
			TextCase = "Standard", FlagTextCase = "UPPERCASE",
			OverrideRectSize = { Enabled = false, Width = 1, Height = 1 },
			Spacing = { Left = 1, Top = 1, Right = 1, Bottom = 1 },

local Config = {}
Config.defaults = buildDefaults()
Config.data     = deepCopy(Config.defaults)
Config.changed  = Signal.new()   -- (pathString, value)
Config._watchers = {}            -- [pathString] = { fn, ... }
Config.CurrentName = "default"
Config.revision = 0              -- invalidates derived gun-specific views

local function pathKey(path)
	return table.concat(path, ".")
end

function Config.get(path)
	local node = Config.data
	for i = 1, #path do
		if typeof(node) ~= "table" then return nil end
		node = node[path[i]]
	end
	return node
end

--- Live handle to a branch of the tree.
---
--- Hot loops must not call Config.get: the path literal allocates a table on
--- every frame, and walking it re-indexes the whole chain. Features resolve a
--- node once at start-up and then read plain fields off it. This is only sound
--- because Config.load merges into Config.data in place rather than replacing
--- it, so a handle never goes stale.
function Config.node(path)
	local node = Config.data
	for i = 1, #path do
		local key = path[i]
		if typeof(node[key]) ~= "table" then
			node[key] = node[key] == nil and {} or node[key]
		end
		node = node[key]
		if typeof(node) ~= "table" then return nil end
	end
	return node
end

function Config.rawSet(path, value)
	local node = Config.data
	for i = 1, #path - 1 do
		local k = path[i]
		if typeof(node[k]) ~= "table" then node[k] = {} end
		node = node[k]
	end
	node[path[#path]] = value
end

function Config.set(path, value, silent)
	Config.rawSet(path, value)
	Config.revision += 1
	if silent then return end
	local key = pathKey(path)
	local list = Config._watchers[key]
	if list then
		for _, fn in ipairs(list) do
			local ok, err = pcall(fn, value, path)
			if not ok then warn("[khookclone] watcher " .. key .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
		end
	end
	Config.changed:Fire(key, value)
end

--- Subscribe to a path. Fires immediately with the current value so features
--- do not need a separate "apply initial state" pass.
function Config.watch(path, fn, skipInitial)
	local key = pathKey(path)
	local list = Config._watchers[key]
	if not list then list = {}; Config._watchers[key] = list end
	table.insert(list, fn)
	if not skipInitial then
		pcall(fn, Config.get(path), path)
	end
	return function()
		for i, v in ipairs(list) do
			if v == fn then table.remove(list, i) break end
		end
	end
end

--- Watch several paths with one callback (any of them changing re-runs it).
function Config.watchAll(paths, fn)
	local unsubs = {}
	for i, p in ipairs(paths) do
		table.insert(unsubs, Config.watch(p, fn, i > 1))
	end
	return function() for _, u in ipairs(unsubs) do u() end end
end

--- Re-fire every watcher; used after loading a config file.
function Config.refreshAll()
	Config.revision += 1
	for key, list in pairs(Config._watchers) do
		local path = string.split(key, ".")
		local value = Config.get(path)
		for _, fn in ipairs(list) do pcall(fn, value, path) end
	end
end

------------------------------------------------------------ serialisation --
-- Color3 / ColorSequence / NumberSequence / EnumItem do not survive JSON, so
-- they are boxed with a __t tag on the way out and rebuilt on the way in.

local function encodeValue(v)
	local t = typeof(v)
	if t == "Color3" then
		return { __t = "Color3", r = v.R, g = v.G, b = v.B }
	elseif t == "ColorSequence" then
		local kp = {}
		for _, k in ipairs(v.Keypoints) do
			table.insert(kp, { t = k.Time, r = k.Value.R, g = k.Value.G, b = k.Value.B })
		end
		return { __t = "ColorSequence", kp = kp }
	elseif t == "NumberSequence" then
		local kp = {}
		for _, k in ipairs(v.Keypoints) do
			table.insert(kp, { t = k.Time, v = k.Value, e = k.Envelope })
		end
		return { __t = "NumberSequence", kp = kp }
	elseif t == "EnumItem" then
		return { __t = "EnumItem", e = tostring(v.EnumType), n = v.Name }
	elseif t == "Vector2" then
		return { __t = "Vector2", x = v.X, y = v.Y }
	elseif t == "table" then
		local out = {}
		for k, val in pairs(v) do out[tostring(k)] = encodeValue(val) end
		return out
	end
	return v
end

local function decodeValue(v)
	if typeof(v) ~= "table" then return v end
	local tag = v.__t
	if tag == "Color3" then
		return Color3.new(v.r, v.g, v.b)
	elseif tag == "ColorSequence" then
		local kp = {}
		for _, k in ipairs(v.kp) do
			table.insert(kp, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(k.t, Color3.new(k.r, k.g, k.b)))
		end
		if #kp < 2 then
			return ColorSequence.new(kp[1] and kp[1].Value or Color3.new(1,1,1))
		end
		return ColorSequence.new(kp)
	elseif tag == "NumberSequence" then
		local kp = {}
		for _, k in ipairs(v.kp) do
			table.insert(kp, NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(k.t, k.v, k.e or 0))
		end
		if #kp < 2 then
			return NumberSequence.new(kp[1] and kp[1].Value or 0)
		end
		return NumberSequence.new(kp)
	elseif tag == "EnumItem" then
		local enumName = string.gsub(v.e, "^Enum%.", "")
		local ok, item = pcall(function() return (Enum :: any)[enumName][v.n] end)
		return ok and item or nil
	elseif tag == "Vector2" then
		return Vector2.new(v.x, v.y)
	end
	local out = {}
	for k, val in pairs(v) do
		local nk = tonumber(k) or k
		out[nk] = decodeValue(val)
	end
	return out
end

local function ensureFolders()
	if not (isfolder and makefolder) then return false end
	if not isfolder(ROOT_FOLDER) then makefolder(ROOT_FOLDER) end
	if not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) end
	return true
end

function Config.list()
	local names = {}
	if not (listfiles and ensureFolders()) then return names end
	for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(CONFIG_FOLDER)) do
		local name = string.match(f, "([^/\\]+)%.json$")
		if name then table.insert(names, name) end
	end
	table.sort(names)
	return names
end

function Config.save(name)
	if not (writefile and ensureFolders()) then return false, "no filesystem" end
	local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
		return HttpService:JSONEncode(encodeValue(Config.data))
	end)
	if not ok then return false, tostring(encoded) end
	local ok2, err = pcall(writefile, CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json", encoded)
	if not ok2 then return false, tostring(err) end
	Config.CurrentName = name
	return true
end

function Config.load(name)
	if not (readfile and isfile) then return false, "no filesystem" end
	local file = CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
	if not isfile(file) then return false, "not found" end
	local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
		return decodeValue(HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(file)))
	end)
	if not ok then return false, tostring(decoded) end
	-- Merge in place: Config.data keeps its identity so every live node handle
	-- held by a running feature stays valid across a config load.
	local fresh = deepCopy(Config.defaults)
	deepMergeKnown(fresh, decoded)
	mergeInto(Config.data, fresh)
	Config.CurrentName = name
	Config.refreshAll()
	return true
end

function Config.delete(name)
	if not (delfile and isfile) then return false end
	local file = CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
	if isfile(file) then delfile(file) return true end
	return false
end

function Config.reset()
	mergeInto(Config.data, deepCopy(Config.defaults))
	Config.refreshAll()
end

-- khook.lua:2611-2868.  Menu controls can edit a selected gun profile without
-- changing their public config path. Reads fall back to General when that
-- profile has no leaf; writes always land in GunSpecific[gun][feature].
Config._editProfiles = {}
Config.editProfileChanged = Signal.new()

function Config.setEditProfile(feature, profile)
	if Config._editProfiles[feature] == profile then return end
	Config._editProfiles[feature] = profile
	Config.editProfileChanged:Fire(feature, profile)
end

function Config.resolveEditPath(path, writing)
	local feature = path and path[1]
	local profile = feature and Config._editProfiles[feature] or nil
	local base = feature and Config.data[feature] or nil
	if profile == nil or type(base) ~= "table"
		or type(base.GunSpecific) ~= "table" or base.GunSpecific.Enabled ~= true
		or path[2] == "GunSpecific" then
		return path
	end
	local gun = Config.data.GunSpecific and Config.data.GunSpecific[profile]
	local active = type(gun) == "table" and gun.Active or nil
	if type(active) ~= "table" or active[feature] ~= true then return path end
	local redirected = { "GunSpecific", profile }
	for i = 1, #path do redirected[#redirected + 1] = path[i] end
	if writing or Config.get(redirected) ~= nil then return redirected end
	return path
end

function Config.uiGet(path)
	return Config.get(Config.resolveEditPath(path, false))
end

function Config.uiSet(path, value, silent)
	return Config.set(Config.resolveEditPath(path, true), value, silent)
end
--============================================================================--
-- 4. UI LIBRARY -- shell
--============================================================================--
-- Geometry mirrors khook: 919x643 design size, 700x400 minimum, a 100px icon
-- rail on the left, tab pages inset at (118,18), sub-tab pages at (17,169).

local UI = {}
UI.Trove   = Trove.new("menu")
UI.Fonts   = Fonts
UI.Icons   = Icons
UI.AccentChanged = Theme.AccentChanged
UI.Scale   = 1
UI.Visible = true

local DESIGN_SIZE = Vector2.new(919, 643)
local MIN_SIZE    = Vector2.new(700, 400)
local RAIL_WIDTH  = 100
local PAGE_TOP    = 169    -- y of a sub-tab page inside its tab
local SUBTAB_H    = 50

--------------------------------------------------------------- primitives --

local function make(class, props, children)
	local inst = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
		if k ~= "Parent" then inst[k] = v end
	end
	for _, c in ipairs(children or {}) do c.Parent = inst end
	if props and props.Parent then inst.Parent = props.Parent end
	return inst
end

local function corner(parent, radius)
	return make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 6), Parent = parent })
end

local function padding(parent, t, r, b, l)
	return make("UIPadding", {
		PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t or 0), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or 0),
		PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or 0), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l or 0),
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

local function vlist(parent, pad, align)
	return make("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, pad or 0),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		HorizontalAlignment = align or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

local function hlist(parent, pad, valign)
	return make("UIListLayout", {
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, pad or 0),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		VerticalAlignment = valign or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

local function nextLayoutOrder(parent)
	local n = 0
	for _, c in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("GuiObject") then n += 1 end
	end
	return n
end

--- khook's `addGlow(batch, inst, { Amount, DampingFactor })`: a bloom around
--- an element, built by stacking strokes of growing thickness whose
--- transparency falls off by the damping factor each ring. Used on the window
--- edge, the top accent hairline and the panel underline.
local function addGlow(batch, inst, amount, damping, key)
	local transparency = damping or 0.6
	for i = 1, (amount or 3) do
		local ring = make("UIStroke", {
			Thickness = i,
			Transparency = clamp(1 - (1 - transparency) / (i * 1.6), 0, 1),
			Parent = inst,
		})
		batch:Bind(ring, "Color", key or "Accent")
	end
end

--- khook's `box`: rounded, stroked, vertically-graded surface used for every
--- section body, dropdown popup and panel.
local function box(batch, parent, key)
	local f = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get(key or "ElementBackground"),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	local grad = make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = f })
	batch:BindGradient(grad, key or "ElementBackground")
	batch:Bind(f, "BackgroundColor3", key or "ElementBackground")
	corner(f, 6)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = f })
	batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")
	return f
end

local function elementsContainer(parent, pos, size)
	local f = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = pos or UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
		Size = size or UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	vlist(f, 12)
	make("UIPadding", { PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12), Parent = f })
	return f
end

--- Click + press feedback on any frame (khook binds these to every row).
local function connectClick(trove, guiObject, fn)
	trove:Add(guiObject.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			fn()
		end
	end))
end

local function connectHover(trove, guiObject, onEnter, onLeave)
	trove:Add(guiObject.MouseEnter:Connect(onEnter))
	trove:Add(guiObject.MouseLeave:Connect(onLeave))
end

------------------------------------------------------------------ tooltip --

local Tooltip = { _frame = nil }

function Tooltip.init(parent, batch)
	local f = make("Frame", {
		Visible = false,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ElementBackground"),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ZIndex = 900,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	batch:Bind(f, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementBackground")
	corner(f, 5)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = f })
	batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")
	padding(f, 6, 9, 6, 9)
	local label = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		TextSize = 13,
		FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
		TextWrapped = true,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ZIndex = 901,
		Parent = f,
	})
	batch:Bind(label, "TextColor3", "TextColor")
	make("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(260, math.huge), Parent = label })
	Tooltip._frame, Tooltip._label = f, label
end

function Tooltip.show(text)
	if not Tooltip._frame or not text or text == "" then return end
	Tooltip._label.Text = text
	Tooltip._frame.Visible = true
	Tooltip._active = true
end

function Tooltip.hide()
	Tooltip._active = false
	if Tooltip._frame then Tooltip._frame.Visible = false end
end

--- Attach hover-tooltip behaviour to a row.
local function bindTooltip(trove, guiObject, text)
	if not text or text == "" then return end
	connectHover(trove, guiObject,
		function() Tooltip.show(text) end,
		function() Tooltip.hide() end)
end

--============================================================================--
-- Menu root
--============================================================================--

--- `AbsolutePosition` and `GetMouseLocation` are both reported against the
--- inset-adjusted viewport, but our ScreenGui sets `IgnoreGuiInset`, so a raw
--- offset written back as `Position` lands one topbar too high. Anything that
--- places an overlay against another element's absolute box has to add this.
function UI.inset()
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return cloneref(game:GetService("GuiService")):GetGuiInset()
	end)
	return (ok and value) or Vector2.zero
end

function UI.Tween(_, obj, props, info)
	return tween(obj, props, info)
end

local Menu = {
	Fonts = Fonts,
	AccentChanged = Theme.AccentChanged,
	Tween = function(_, obj, props, info) return tween(obj, props, info) end,
}

function UI.build()
	local trove = UI.Trove
	local batch = Batch.new(trove)
	UI.Batch = batch

	local gui = make("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "\0khookclone\0",
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
		DisplayOrder = 2 ^ 30,
		IgnoreGuiInset = true,
	})
	pcall(protectgui, gui)
	gui.Parent = gethui()
	trove:Add(gui)
	UI.Gui = gui

	-- Root frame ------------------------------------------------------------
	-- MenuFrame is a CanvasGroup so the whole window can fade as one unit,
	-- exactly as khook builds it (khook.lua:140777).
	local root = make("CanvasGroup", {
		Name = "Menu",
		-- khook.lua:140786/140845 -- the design size is clamped to the viewport
		-- and the window is then centred by absolute offset, anchored at the
		-- origin so the drag clamp works in plain pixels.
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(DESIGN_SIZE.X, DESIGN_SIZE.Y),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Background"),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Active = true,
		GroupTransparency = 1,
		Visible = false,
		ClipsDescendants = false,
		Parent = gui,
	})
	batch:Bind(root, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")
	corner(root, 6)
	local rootStroke = make("UIStroke", { Transparency = 0.6, Parent = root })
	batch:Bind(rootStroke, "Color", "Outline")
	addGlow(batch, root, 5, 0.6, "Outline")
	UI.Root = root
	local uiScale = make("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = root })
	UI.UIScale = uiScale

	--- khook.lua:140427 `resolveDefaultSize` + `clampSizeToViewport`. A window
	--- larger than the viewport is shrunk to fit rather than hanging off the
	--- screen, and re-centred whenever the viewport changes.
	local function fitToViewport(recentre)
		local vp = Camera and Camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
		local w = math.min(DESIGN_SIZE.X, math.max(MIN_SIZE.X, vp.X - 16))
		local h = math.min(DESIGN_SIZE.Y, math.max(MIN_SIZE.Y, vp.Y - 16))
		if root.Size.X.Offset ~= w or root.Size.Y.Offset ~= h then
			root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
		end
		if recentre then
			root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
				math.max(0, math.floor((vp.X - w) / 2)),
				math.max(0, math.floor((vp.Y - h) / 2)))
		else
			root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
				clamp(root.Position.X.Offset, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - w)),
				clamp(root.Position.Y.Offset, 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - h)))
		end
	end
	fitToViewport(true)
	trove:Add(Camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
		fitToViewport(false)
	end))

	-- Bottom gradient strip (khook.lua:140855) ------------------------------
	local bottomStrip = make("Frame", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -8),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 8),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Parent = root,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = bottomStrip }), "Background")

	-- Top accent hairline: the window's signature. A 1px accent line whose
	-- gradient fades out at both ends, with a stroke and a glow on top
	-- (khook.lua:140864-140896).
	local topBand = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 5),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ZIndex = root.ZIndex + 1,
		Parent = root,
	})
	local hairline = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ZIndex = topBand.ZIndex,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		Parent = topBand,
	})
	batch:Bind(hairline, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	make("UIGradient", {
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.06, 0.35),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.94, 0.35),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
		}),
		Parent = hairline,
	})
	local hairStroke = make("UIStroke", { Transparency = 0.78, Parent = hairline })
	batch:Bind(hairStroke, "Color", "Outline")
	addGlow(batch, hairline, 2, 0.78, "Accent")

	Tooltip.init(gui, batch)

	-- Tooltip follows the cursor -------------------------------------------
	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local f = Tooltip._frame
		if f and f.Visible then
			local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			local inset = UI.inset()
			f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(m.X + 16 + inset.X, m.Y + 12 + inset.Y)
		end
	end))

	-- Drag ------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- khook installs a UIDragDetector on both the window and its modal top
	-- strip (khook.lua:74396-74457, 140933-140995).  The old clone only
	-- sampled UserInputService.InputChanged from the strip, which could miss a
	-- quick press/move transition or lose a touch when another finger moved.
	-- Keep the same two-detector arrangement and use the input path only as a
	-- compatibility fallback for clients without UIDragDetector.
	local function clampRootToViewport()
		local vp = Camera and Camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
		local size = root.AbsoluteSize
		root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
			clamp(root.Position.X.Offset, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - size.X)),
			clamp(root.Position.Y.Offset, 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - size.Y)))
	end

	local function attachDragDetector(parent)
		local ok, detector = pcall(Instance.new, "UIDragDetector")
		if not ok or detector == nil then return false end
		local configured = pcall(function()
			detector.DragStyle = Enum.UIDragDetectorDragStyle.TranslatePlane
			detector.ResponseStyle = Enum.UIDragDetectorResponseStyle.CustomOffset
			detector.Parent = parent
		end)
		if not configured then
			detector:Destroy()
			return false
		end
		trove:Add(detector)

		local origin
		trove:Add(detector.DragStart:Connect(function()
			origin = root.Position
		end))
		trove:Add(detector.DragContinue:Connect(function()
			if origin == nil then return end
			local delta = detector.DragUDim2
			root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
				origin.X.Offset + delta.X.Offset,
				origin.Y.Offset + delta.Y.Offset)
			clampRootToViewport()
		end))
		trove:Add(detector.DragEnd:Connect(function()
			origin = nil
			clampRootToViewport()
		end))
		return true
	end

	-- khook's drag strip: 20px tall (32 on touch), Modal, above everything.
	local dragBar = make("TextButton", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 32 or 20),
		BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 50, Modal = true,
		Parent = root,
	})

	attachDragDetector(root)
	attachDragDetector(dragBar)
	-- Keep the pointer implementation armed as a fallback even when the class
	-- exists. Some executor UI parents expose UIDragDetector but intermittently
	-- fail to route its drag signals; both paths calculate from the same origin,
	-- so running together cannot compound the offset.
	do
		local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil, nil
		local function finishDrag(input)
			if not dragging or dragInput == nil then return end
			if dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				if input ~= dragInput then return end
			elseif input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				return
			end
			dragging, dragInput = false, nil
			clampRootToViewport()
		end
		local function matchesPointer(input)
			if dragInput == nil then return false end
			if dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				return input == dragInput
			end
			return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
		end
		trove:Add(dragBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if dragging or (input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
				and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then return end
			dragging, dragInput = true, input
			dragStart, startPos = input.Position, root.Position
		end))
		trove:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if not dragging or not matchesPointer(input) then return end
			local delta = input.Position - dragStart
			root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
				startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
				startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
			clampRootToViewport()
		end))
		-- InputObject.Changed is not consistently delivered by executor-owned
		-- ScreenGuis; the global end stream is, and also preserves touch identity.
		trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(finishDrag))
	end

	-- Resize handle ---------------------------------------------------------
	-- Two -45 degree bars in the corner, as khook draws it (khook.lua:140619).
	local grip = make("TextButton", {
		Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), ZIndex = root.ZIndex + 6, Parent = root,
	})
	for _, spec in ipairs({
		{ pos = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4), size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2) },
		{ pos = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -7), size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 2) },
	}) do
		local bar = make("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
			Position = spec.pos,
			Size = spec.size,
			Rotation = -45,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = grip.ZIndex,
			Parent = grip,
		})
		batch:Bind(bar, "BackgroundColor3", "Outline")
	end
	local resizing, resizeStart, sizeStart = false, nil, nil
	trove:Add(grip.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			resizing = true
			resizeStart = input.Position
			sizeStart = root.AbsoluteSize
		end
	end))
	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if resizing and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local delta = input.Position - resizeStart
			root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
				math.max(MIN_SIZE.X, sizeStart.X + delta.X),
				math.max(MIN_SIZE.Y, sizeStart.Y + delta.Y))
		end
	end))
	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			resizing = false
		end
	end))

	-- Left rail (khook.lua:40955) -------------------------------------------
	-- Full-height 100px column: a graded backdrop, an 85px logo header with an
	-- accent underline, then a scrolling stack of 66x66 icon buttons with a
	-- sliding indicator behind the active one.
	local panel = make("Frame", {
		Name = "Panel",
		Size = UDim2.new(0, RAIL_WIDTH, 1, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Background"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = root,
	})
	batch:Bind(panel, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")

	local panelBackdrop = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		ZIndex = -1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Parent = panel,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = panelBackdrop }), "Background")

	-- logo header
	local header = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 85),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = panel,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = header }), "Background")

	local logo = make("ImageLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Image = Icons.Logo,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 37),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		Parent = header,
	})
	batch:Bind(logo, "ImageColor3", "Accent")

	local headerLine = make("Frame", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		Parent = header,
	})
	batch:Bind(headerLine, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	make("UIGradient", {
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0),
			NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
		}),
		Parent = headerLine,
	})
	addGlow(batch, headerLine, 2, 0.78, "Accent")

	-- button column
	local railScroll = make("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 102),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -101),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
		ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1,
		ScrollBarThickness = 0,
		ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		Parent = panel,
	})

	local railHolder = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = railScroll,
	})
	make("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
		HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Parent = railHolder,
	})

	-- the block that slides behind whichever tab is open
	local indicator = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(66, 66),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
		Visible = false,
		ZIndex = 0,
		Parent = railScroll,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = indicator }), "TabButtonSelected")
	corner(indicator, 6)

	-- Page holder: the rounded, stroked panel the tab pages live inside.
	local pageHolder = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(RAIL_WIDTH, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -RAIL_WIDTH, 1, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Background"),
		Parent = root,
	})
	batch:Bind(pageHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")
	corner(pageHolder, 12)
	local holderStroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = pageHolder })
	batch:Bind(holderStroke, "Color", "Outline")

	local window = {
		MenuFrame = root,
		PageHolder = pageHolder,
		Panel = { Frame = railHolder, Indicator = indicator, Scroll = railScroll },
		ActiveTab = nil,
		Tabs = {},
	}
	UI.Window = window

	--- Slide the indicator to a button, tracking the scroll offset.
	function window.Panel.MoveIndicator(_, button)
		local y = button.AbsolutePosition.Y - railHolder.AbsolutePosition.Y
		local target = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, y)
		if not indicator.Visible then
			indicator.Position = target
			indicator.Visible = true
			return
		end
		tween(indicator, { Position = target })
	end

	--- Rail button: 66x66, 26px icon on top, 10pt label underneath. Selected
	--- turns both the icon and the label accent (khook.lua:48474).
	function window.Panel.AddButton(_, trv, opts)
		local btn = make("TextButton", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(66, 66),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Text = "",
			AutoButtonColor = false,
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("TabButtonSelected"),
			Parent = railHolder,
		})
		batch:Bind(btn, "BackgroundColor3", "TabButtonSelected")

		local icon = make("ImageLabel", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
			Image = opts.Icon or FALLBACK_ICON,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 10),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
			Parent = btn,
		})
		local label = make("TextLabel", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -6),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 14),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Text = opts.Label,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
			TextSize = 10,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
			Parent = btn,
		})

		local api = { Clicked = Signal.new(), Button = btn, _selected = false }
		trv:Add(api.Clicked)
		trv:Add(btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() api.Clicked:Fire() end))

		function api.SetSelected(_, on)
			api._selected = on
			local c = on and Theme.get("Accent") or Theme.get("Unselected")
			tween(icon, { ImageColor3 = c })
			tween(label, { TextColor3 = c })
			if on then window.Panel.MoveIndicator(window.Panel, btn) end
		end

		batch:BindStateful("Accent", function(c)
			if api._selected then
				icon.ImageColor3 = c
				label.TextColor3 = c
			end
		end)
		batch:BindStateful("Unselected", function(c)
			if not api._selected then
				icon.ImageColor3 = c
				label.TextColor3 = c
			end
		end)

		trv:Add(function() btn:Destroy() end)
		return api
	end

	return window
end

--============================================================================--
-- Tab / Page / Grid
--============================================================================--

local Tab  = {}; Tab.__index  = Tab
local Page = {}; Page.__index = Page
local Grid = {}; Grid.__index = Grid

--------------------------------------------------------------------- Tab ---

--============================================================================--
-- Search palette
--============================================================================--
-- khook.lua:94477. Menu-wide search: every panel registers itself with a
-- breadcrumb, the bar scores every row label across the whole menu, and the
-- best three drop into a list under the bar. Hung off UI rather than declared
-- as locals -- the main chunk is at Lua's 200-local ceiling.

UI.Palette = { registrations = {}, scored = {}, selected = 1, rows = {} }

--- khook.lua:94622 `Register`. `host` is the grid's search host:
--- { TabLabel, PageLabel, Open }.
function UI.Palette.register(host, section)
	table.insert(UI.Palette.registrations, { host = host, section = section })
end

--- khook.lua:94633 -- the crumb is "Tab  >  Section", or with the page in the
--- middle when the section lives on a sub-tab.
function UI.Palette.collect()
	local out = {}
	for _, reg in ipairs(UI.Palette.registrations) do
		local host, section = reg.host, reg.section
		local crumb
		if host.PageLabel == nil then
			crumb = ("%s  \u{203A}  %s"):format(host.TabLabel, tostring(section.Title))
		else
			crumb = ("%s  \u{203A}  %s  \u{203A}  %s"):format(
				host.TabLabel, host.PageLabel, tostring(section.Title))
		end
		local ok = pcall(section.CollectSearchHits, section, crumb, host.Open, out)
		if not ok then end
	end
	return out
end

--- Lower is better, nil is no match. khook's scorer lives in a module the
--- decompile does not include; the ranking it feeds (contiguous beats spread,
--- earlier beats later) is reconstructed, but the +100 crumb penalty, the sort
--- and the three-result cap below are khook's.
function UI.Palette.score(text, query)
	if query == "" then return 0 end
	local hay, needle = string.lower(text), string.lower(query)
	local at = string.find(hay, needle, 1, true)
	if at ~= nil then return at - 1 end
	local from, last, gaps = 1, nil, 0
	for i = 1, #needle do
		local found = string.find(hay, string.sub(needle, i, i), from, true)
		if found == nil then return nil end
		if last ~= nil then gaps += found - last - 1 end
		last, from = found, found + 1
	end
	return 20 + gaps
end

function UI.Palette.build()
	if UI.Palette.frame then return UI.Palette.frame end
	local batch = UI.Batch

	local frame = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Visible = false,
		ZIndex = 700,
		Parent = UI.Gui,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = frame }), "Background")
	corner(frame, 8)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = frame })
	batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")
	addGlow(batch, frame, 4, 0.6, "Outline")

	local list = make("ScrollingFrame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 0,
		Selectable = false,
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		ZIndex = 701,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	make("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = list,
	})
	padding(list, 4, 4, 4, 4)

	local hint = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
		Text = "No matches.",
		TextSize = 12,
		Visible = false,
		FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		ZIndex = 702,
		Parent = list,
	})
	batch:Bind(hint, "TextColor3", "Unselected")

	-- khook.lua:94475 -- exactly three result rows
	for i = 1, 3 do
		local btn = make("TextButton", {
			Visible = false,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Text = "",
			LayoutOrder = i,
			AutoButtonColor = false,
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ElementBackground"),
			ZIndex = 702,
			Parent = list,
		})
		batch:Bind(btn, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementBackground")
		corner(btn, 4)
		padding(btn, 0, 8, 0, 8)
		local title = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 3),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
			TextSize = 13,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			FontFace = Fonts.Bold,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
			ZIndex = 703,
			Parent = btn,
		})
		batch:Bind(title, "TextColor3", "TextColor")
		local crumb = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13),
			TextSize = 11,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
			ZIndex = 703,
			Parent = btn,
		})
		batch:Bind(crumb, "TextColor3", "Unselected")

		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local entry = UI.Palette.scored[i]
			if entry then UI.Palette.navigate(entry.hit) end
		end)
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if UI.Palette.scored[i] then
				UI.Palette.selected = i
				UI.Palette.highlight()
			end
		end)
		UI.Palette.rows[i] = { Button = btn, Title = title, Crumb = crumb }
	end

	UI.Palette.frame, UI.Palette.hint = frame, hint
	return frame
end

function UI.Palette.highlight()
	for i, row in ipairs(UI.Palette.rows) do
		row.Button.BackgroundTransparency = (i == UI.Palette.selected) and 0 or 1
	end
end

--- khook.lua:94660 `Search`.
function UI.Palette.search(query)
	UI.Palette.build()
	UI.Palette.selected = 1
	local scored = {}
	for _, hit in ipairs(UI.Palette.collect()) do
		local score = UI.Palette.score(hit.Label, query)
		if score == nil and query ~= "" then
			local viaCrumb = UI.Palette.score(hit.Crumb, query)
			-- khook.lua:94668 -- a crumb-only match is pushed well down
			if viaCrumb ~= nil then score = viaCrumb + 100 end
		end
		if score ~= nil then table.insert(scored, { hit = hit, score = score }) end
	end
	-- khook.lua:238 `sharedHelper30`: score, then label alphabetically
	table.sort(scored, function(a, b)
		if a.score == b.score then return a.hit.Label < b.hit.Label end
		return a.score < b.score
	end)
	while #scored > 3 do table.remove(scored) end
	UI.Palette.scored = scored

	UI.Palette.hint.Visible = #scored == 0 and query ~= ""
	for i, row in ipairs(UI.Palette.rows) do
		local entry = scored[i]
		if entry ~= nil then
			row.Title.Text = entry.hit.Label
			row.Crumb.Text = entry.hit.Crumb
		end
		row.Button.Visible = entry ~= nil
	end
	UI.Palette.highlight()
	return #scored
end

--- khook.lua:94713 `ShowAt` -- the list hangs off the bar's bottom-right.
function UI.Palette.showAt(anchor)
	local frame = UI.Palette.build()
	local pos, size = anchor.AbsolutePosition, anchor.AbsoluteSize
	local inset = UI.inset()
	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(size.X), 0)
	frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
		math.floor(pos.X + size.X + inset.X),
		math.floor(pos.Y + size.Y + 4 + inset.Y))
	frame.Visible = true
end

function UI.Palette.hide()
	if UI.Palette.frame then UI.Palette.frame.Visible = false end
end

--- khook.lua:94457 `_navigateTo`: open the page the row lives on, then bring
--- its frame into view 80px down the column.
function UI.Palette.navigate(hit)
	UI.Palette.hide()
	pcall(hit.Open)
	task.defer(function()
		local frame = hit.GetFrame()
		if frame == nil or frame.Parent == nil then return end
		local scroll = frame.Parent
		while scroll ~= nil and not scroll:IsA("ScrollingFrame") do
			scroll = scroll.Parent
		end
		if scroll == nil then return end
		local delta = frame.AbsolutePosition.Y - scroll.AbsolutePosition.Y
		scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0,
			math.max(0, delta + scroll.CanvasPosition.Y - 80))
	end)
end

--- khook.lua:121229 `f8223`. Collapsed it is a 90x32 pill advertising
--- "Ctrl+F"; focused it widens to 260, turns accent and swaps the hint for a
--- text field. The badge on the right counts matches, and the x clears them.
UI.SEARCH_ICON  = "rbxassetid://72296609649861"
UI.SEARCH_CLEAR = "rbxassetid://116396312853810"

function UI.newSearchBar(tab, parent, batch)
	local COLLAPSED, EXPANDED, HEIGHT = 90, 260, 32
	local INFO = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

	local frame = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(COLLAPSED, HEIGHT),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = frame }), "Background")
	corner(frame, 8)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", {
		Color = Theme.TabHighlight, Transparency = 0, Parent = frame,
	})

	local icon = make("ImageLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Image = UI.SEARCH_ICON,
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		Parent = frame,
	})
	local hint = make("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "Ctrl+F",
		TextSize = 12,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		FontFace = Fonts.Bold,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	batch:Bind(hint, "TextColor3", "Unselected")

	local input = make("TextBox", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 1, -8),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "",
		PlaceholderText = "Search...",
		PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		TextSize = 14,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		FontFace = Fonts.Bold,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	batch:Bind(input, "TextColor3", "TextColor")

	local badge = make("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 18),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.85,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "0",
		TextSize = 11,
		FontFace = Fonts.Title,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	batch:Bind(badge, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	batch:Bind(badge, "TextColor3", "Accent")
	corner(badge, 4)

	local clear = make("ImageButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Image = UI.SEARCH_CLEAR,
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		Visible = false,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	-- khook.lua:121396 -- the whole pill is clickable, behind everything else
	local hit = make("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "",
		ZIndex = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Parent = frame,
	})

	local open = false
	local api = {}

	function api.Expand()
		if open then return end
		open = true
		hint.Visible = false
		input.Visible = true
		input:CaptureFocus()
		tween(frame, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(EXPANDED, HEIGHT) }, INFO)
		tween(stroke, { Color = Theme.get("Accent"), Transparency = 0 }, INFO)
		tween(icon, { ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Accent") }, INFO)
	end

	function api.Collapse()
		if not open then return end
		open = false
		pcall(function() input:ReleaseFocus() end)
		input.Text = ""
		input.Visible = false
		hint.Visible = true
		clear.Visible = false
		badge.Visible = false
		tween(frame, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(COLLAPSED, HEIGHT) }, INFO)
		tween(stroke, { Color = Theme.TabHighlight, Transparency = 0 }, INFO)
		tween(icon, { ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected") }, INFO)
		UI.Palette.hide()
	end

	hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(api.Expand)
	clear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		input.Text = ""
		input:CaptureFocus()
	end)
	-- khook.lua:28891 -- the count feeds the badge and decides whether the
	-- result list is shown at all.
	input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local text = input.Text
		local typed = text ~= ""
		clear.Visible = typed
		local hits = UI.Palette.search(text)
		badge.Text = tostring(hits)
		local show = typed and hits > 0
		badge.Visible = show
		if show then UI.Palette.showAt(frame) else UI.Palette.hide() end
	end)
	input.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		if input.Text == "" then api.Collapse() end
	end)

	api.Frame = frame
	return api
end

function UI.AddTab(window, opts)
	local trove = UI.Trove:Extend()
	local batch = Batch.new(trove)

	local button = window.Panel.AddButton(window.Panel, trove, {
		Label = opts.Label,
		Icon  = opts.Icon or FALLBACK_ICON,
	})

	local page = make("CanvasGroup", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		GroupTransparency = 0,
		Visible = false,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(118, 18),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -136, 1, -36),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = window.MenuFrame,
	})

	local self = setmetatable({
		_trove = trove,
		_batch = batch,
		_window = window,
		_button = button,
		_page = page,
		_title = nil,
		_desc = nil,
		_search = nil,
		Label = opts.Label,
		Description = opts.Description or "",
		Pages = {},
		ActivePage = nil,
		_grid = nil,
		_subtabs = nil,
		_openedOnce = false,
		_firstOpen = {},
		Opened = trove:Add(Signal.new()),
	}, Tab)

	button.Clicked:Connect(function() self:Open() end)
	table.insert(window.Tabs, self)
	if window.ActiveTab == nil then self:Open() end
	return self
end

--- khook.lua:104598 + 121440 `header.create`. The first sub-tab converts the
--- page into a full-height panel flush against the rail, then grows an 85px
--- band holding the title, the description, a search bar and the sub-tab
--- strip, with a separately graded backdrop beneath it.
function Tab:_ensureHeader()
	if self._subtabs then return self._subtabs end
	local batch = self._batch

	-- khook.lua:104600 -- the page geometry changes the moment a tab gains
	-- sub-tabs; it is only inset at (118,18) while it holds a bare grid.
	self._page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 0)
	self._page.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0)

	local holder = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = self._page,
	})

	local top = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 85),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = top }), "Background")

	local underline = make("Frame", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, -1),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = top,
	})
	batch:Bind(underline, "BackgroundColor3", "Outline")

	local textHost = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -17, 0, 85),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = top,
	})
	local stack = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = textHost,

	make("UIListLayout", {

		Padding = UDim.new(0, 2),

		Parent = stack,
	})
	local title = make("TextLabel", {
		TextSize = 18,
		FontFace = Fonts.Title,
		Text = self.Label,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		LayoutOrder = 1,
		Parent = stack,
	})
	batch:Bind(title, "TextColor3", "TextColor")
	local desc = make("TextLabel", {
		TextSize = 13,
		FontFace = Fonts.Bold,
		Text = self.Description,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		LayoutOrder = 2,
		Parent = stack,
	})
	batch:Bind(desc, "TextColor3", "Unselected")
	self._title, self._desc = title, desc
	self._search = UI.newSearchBar(self, textHost, batch)

	-- khook parents the sub-tab strip INSIDE the 85px band at y=85, so it
	-- overhangs into the body; that overhang is the row you actually see.
	local subtabs = make("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 85),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0),

		ScrollBarThickness = 0,
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X,

		Parent = top,
	})
	batch:Bind(subtabs, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	make("UIListLayout", {
		VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Parent = subtabs,
	})

	local backdrop = make("Frame", {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 85),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -85),

		Parent = holder,
	})
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = backdrop }), "Background")

	self._subtabs = subtabs
	return subtabs
end

--- Sub-tab. khook forbids mixing sub-tabs and a direct grid on one tab.
function Tab:AddTab(opts)
	assert(self._grid == nil, "Tab:AddTab() cannot be used on a tab that has a Grid")
	self:_ensureHeader()
	local page = UI.newPage(self, opts)
	table.insert(self.Pages, page)
	return page
end

function Tab:Grid(opts)
	assert(#self.Pages == 0, "Tab:Grid() cannot be used on a tab that has sub-tabs")
	if self._grid then return self._grid end
	-- khook.lua:104580 -- a grid-only tab registers under the tab's own label
	local tab = self
	self._grid = UI.newGrid(self._page, self._trove, self._batch, opts, {
		TabLabel = self.Label, PageLabel = nil,
		Open = function() tab:Open() end,
	})
	return self._grid
end

function Tab:Open()
	local window = self._window
	if window.ActiveTab == self then return end
	if window.ActiveTab then window.ActiveTab:_setOpen(false) end
	window.ActiveTab = self
	self:_setOpen(true)
end

--- Deferred construction. khook does not build a page until it is first
--- opened (`OnFirstOpen`, khook.lua:78880); with ~700 elements across the
--- menu that is the difference between a visible load hitch and none.
--- Registering after the page has already opened runs the builder at once.
function Tab:OnFirstOpen(fn)
	if self._openedOnce then
		task.spawn(fn)
	else
		table.insert(self._firstOpen, fn)
	end
	return self
end

function Tab:_realize()
	if self._openedOnce then return end
	self._openedOnce = true
	local pending = self._firstOpen
	self._firstOpen = {}
	for _, fn in ipairs(pending) do
		local ok, err = pcall(fn)
		if not ok then warn("[khookclone] tab '" .. self.Label .. "' build: " .. tostring(err)) end
	end
end

function Tab:_setOpen(on)
	self._button:SetSelected(on)
	if on then self:_realize() end
	if on then
		self._page.Visible = true
		self._page.GroupTransparency = 1
		tween(self._page, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
		self.Opened:Fire(true)
	else
		if self._search then self._search.Collapse() end
		local t = tween(self._page, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, TWEEN_FAST)
		t.Completed:Once(function()
			if self._window.ActiveTab ~= self then self._page.Visible = false end
		end)
		self.Opened:Fire(false)
	end
end

-------------------------------------------------------------------- Page ---

function UI.newPage(tab, opts)
	local trove = tab._trove:Extend()
	local batch = Batch.new(trove)

	local button = make("TextButton", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("TabButtonSelected"),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, SUBTAB_H),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Parent = tab._subtabs,
	})
	batch:Bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "TabButtonSelected")
	corner(button, 6)
	padding(button, 0, 16, 0, 13)
	hlist(button, 14)

	local icon = make("ImageLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24),
		Image = opts.Icon or FALLBACK_ICON,
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		LayoutOrder = 1,
		Parent = button,
	})

	local textHolder = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		LayoutOrder = 2,
		Parent = button,
	})
	vlist(textHolder, 4)
	local label = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = opts.Label,
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		TextSize = 16,
		FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
		Parent = textHolder,
	})
	padding(label, 4, 0, 4, 0)
	batch:Bind(label, "TextColor3", "TextColor")

	local page = make("CanvasGroup", {
		Visible = false,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		GroupTransparency = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(17, PAGE_TOP),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, -186),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = tab._page,
	})

	local self = setmetatable({
		_trove = trove,
		_batch = batch,
		_tab = tab,
		_button = button,
		_icon = icon,
		_page = page,
		Label = opts.Label,
		Description = opts.Description or "",
		_grid = nil,
		_selected = false,
		_openedOnce = false,
		_firstOpen = {},
		Opened = trove:Add(Signal.new()),
	}, Page)

	trove:Add(button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() self:Open() end))
	batch:BindStateful("Accent", function(c)
		if self._selected then icon.ImageColor3 = c end
	end)
	batch:BindStateful("Unselected", function(c)
		if not self._selected then icon.ImageColor3 = c end
	end)

	if tab.ActivePage == nil then self:Open() end
	return self
end

function Page:Grid(opts)
	if self._grid then return self._grid end
	local page = self
	self._grid = UI.newGrid(self._page, self._trove, self._batch, opts, {
		TabLabel = self._tab.Label, PageLabel = self.Label,
		Open = function() page:Open() end,
	})
	return self._grid
end

function Page:Open()
	local tab = self._tab
	if tab.ActivePage == self then
		tab:Open()
		return
	end
	if tab.ActivePage then tab.ActivePage:_setOpen(false) end
	tab.ActivePage = self
	self:_setOpen(true)
	tab:Open()
end

function Page:OnFirstOpen(fn)
	if self._openedOnce then
		task.spawn(fn)
	else
		table.insert(self._firstOpen, fn)
	end
	return self
end

function Page:_realize()
	if self._openedOnce then return end
	self._openedOnce = true
	local pending = self._firstOpen
	self._firstOpen = {}
	for _, fn in ipairs(pending) do
		local ok, err = pcall(fn)
		if not ok then warn("[khookclone] page '" .. self.Label .. "' build: " .. tostring(err)) end
	end
end

function Page:_setOpen(on)
	if on then self:_realize() end
	self._selected = on
	tween(self._button, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 })
	tween(self._icon, { ImageColor3 = on and Theme.get("Accent") or Theme.get("Unselected") })
	if on then
		self._page.Visible = true
		self._page.GroupTransparency = 1
		tween(self._page, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
		self.Opened:Fire(true)
	else
		local t = tween(self._page, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, TWEEN_FAST)
		t.Completed:Once(function()
			if self._tab.ActivePage ~= self then self._page.Visible = false end
		end)
		self.Opened:Fire(false)
	end
end

-------------------------------------------------------------------- Grid ---

--- khook.lua:125946 `t130.new`, columns from 1765 `makeInstance10`. The grid
--- is not one scrolling surface with plain frames inside it: the page itself
--- carries a horizontal UIListLayout with BOTH flex axes set to Fill, and each
--- column is its own ScrollingFrame that scrolls independently. Every gutter
--- and inter-section gap is 17, and each column is inset by 1 on all sides.
function UI.newGrid(parent, trove, batch, opts, host)
	local columns = (opts and opts.Columns) or 2
	local self = setmetatable({
		_trove = trove,
		_batch = batch,
		Columns = columns,
		_cols = {},
		_sections = {},
		-- khook.lua:125992 `_searchHost` -- { TabLabel, PageLabel, Open }
		_searchHost = host,
	}, Grid)

	make("UIListLayout", {
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
		HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
		VerticalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 17),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Parent = parent,
	})

	for i = 1, columns do
		local col = make("ScrollingFrame", {
			ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1,
			ScrollBarThickness = 0,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
			Selectable = false,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
			LayoutOrder = i,
			Parent = parent,
		})
		batch:Bind(col, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
		padding(col, 1, 1, 1, 1)
		make("UIListLayout", {
			Padding = UDim.new(0, 17),
			SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
			HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
			Parent = col,
		})
		self._cols[i] = col
	end

	-- khook.lua:125958 `v342` -- the full-width surface, hidden until a
	-- "full" section asks for it.
	local full = make("ScrollingFrame", {
		Visible = false,
		ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1,
		ScrollBarThickness = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Selectable = false,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	batch:Bind(full, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	padding(full, 1, 1, 1, 1)
	make("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 17),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		HorizontalFlex = Enum.UIFlexAlignment.Fill,
		Parent = full,
	})
	self._fullPage = full

	self._holder = parent
	return self
end

--- khook.lua:53752 `_setMode`. Full mode shows the single surface and takes
--- the columns out of the layout; anything else does the reverse.
function Grid:_setMode(full)
	if self._full == full then return end
	self._full = full
	self._fullPage.Visible = full
	for _, col in ipairs(self._cols) do col.Visible = not full end
end

--- khook.lua:53746 `f3842`, verbatim.
function Grid:_column(side)
	local full = (self.Columns == 1) or (side == "full")
	self:_setMode(full)
	if full then return self._fullPage end
	if side == "right" and self.Columns >= 2 then return self._cols[2] end
	if typeof(side) == "number" then
		return self._cols[clamp(side, 1, self.Columns)]
	end
	return self._cols[1]
end

function Grid:Realize() end
--============================================================================--
-- 5. UI LIBRARY -- sections, rows and elements
--============================================================================--

local Section = {}; Section.__index = Section
local Group   = setmetatable({}, { __index = Section }); Group.__index = Group

local ROW_HEIGHT = 24

--- khook's Row (khook.lua:86014-86240). A row is a full-width frame with the
--- title on the left and a **Right container** on the right: 55% of the row,
--- holding a right-aligned horizontal list with 11px gaps. Controls are
--- *attached* into that list rather than positioned by hand, which is what
--- lets a toggle, a keybind chip and a colour swatch share one row without
--- any of them knowing about the others.
--- khook.lua:119140. Register an entry on its panel and give it the separator
--- that trails it. `LayoutOrder` is the entry index doubled so the hairline can
--- sit at index*2 + 1, between this entry and the next.
--- Hung off Section rather than declared as a local: the main chunk is at
--- Lua's 200-local ceiling and one more declaration stops the file compiling.
function Section._addEntry(section, frame, opts, group)
	local entries = section._entries
	if entries == nil then return nil end
	local index = #entries + 1
	frame.LayoutOrder = index * 2

	local separator
	if not (opts and opts.NoSeparator) then
		separator = make("Frame", {
			Visible = false,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			LayoutOrder = index * 2 + 1,
			Parent = section._elements,
		})
		section._batch:Bind(separator, "BackgroundColor3", "Outline")
	end

	local entry = {
		frame = frame,
		separator = separator,
		group = group,
		row = nil,        -- filled in by newRow once the row table exists
		isDivider = opts and opts.IsDivider or false,
	}
	table.insert(entries, entry)
	return entry
end

--- khook.lua:119500 `f8123`, walked in reverse exactly as khook does: a
--- hairline is drawn only between two visible entries, and never immediately
--- ahead of a divider.
function Section:_refreshSeparators()
	local entries = self._entries
	if entries == nil then return end
	local sawVisible, afterDivider = false, false
	for i = #entries, 1, -1 do
		local entry = entries[i]
		local visible = entry.frame ~= nil and entry.frame.Visible
		if entry.separator ~= nil then
			entry.separator.Visible = visible and sawVisible and not afterDivider
		end
		if visible then
			sawVisible = true
			afterDivider = entry.isDivider == true
		end
	end
end

--- Coalesced to the end of the frame (khook.lua:119543): a group flipping
--- shows or hides several rows at once.
function Section:_markSeparatorsDirty()
	if self._separatorsDirty then return end
	self._separatorsDirty = true
	task.defer(function()
		self._separatorsDirty = false
		local ok = pcall(self._refreshSeparators, self)
		if not ok then self._entries = self._entries end
	end)
end

local function newRow(section, opts)
	local trove = section._trove:Extend()
	local height = opts.Height or ROW_HEIGHT

	-- khook.lua:86198 -- rows are transparent TextButtons.  Besides providing
	-- a consistent hit target, this is important for keyboard/gamepad focus.
	local frame = make("TextButton", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Text = "",
		Parent = section._elements,
	})

	local label
	if opts.Label then
		label = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
			Text = opts.Label,
			TextSize = 16,
			FontFace = opts.MediumTitle and Fonts.Medium or Fonts.Main,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Parent = frame,
		})
		section._batch:Bind(label, "TextColor3", "TextColor")
	end

	local right = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, opts.RightOffset or 0, 0, 0),
