		Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, height),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	make("UIListLayout", {
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
		HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
		VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 11),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Parent = right,
	})

	local row = {
		Frame = frame,
		Label = label,
		Right = right,
		Height = height,
		Trove = trove,
		Batch = section._batch,
		_attachCount = 0,
		_visible = true,
		_stacked = false,
	}

	-- khook.lua:86064-86163.  If a label no longer fits beside its controls,
	-- the controls move underneath it and take the full row width.  This is
	-- what keeps long labels intact in a two-column grid and when the window is
	-- clamped to a small viewport.
	local baseRightSize = right.Size
	local baseRightAnchor = right.AnchorPoint
	local baseRightPosition = right.Position
	local baseFrameSize = frame.Size
	local baseAutomaticSize = frame.AutomaticSize
	local updatingLayout = false
	local function rightNaturalWidth()
		local total, count = 0, 0
		for _, child in ipairs(right:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible then
				local w = child.AbsoluteSize.X
				if w <= 0 then
					w = child.Size.X.Offset
				end
				if w > 0 then
					total += w
					count += 1
				end
			end
		end
		if count > 1 then total += (count - 1) * 11 end
		return total
	end
	local function updateResponsiveLayout()
		if updatingLayout or label == nil or frame.Parent == nil then return end
		updatingLayout = true
		local width = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
		if width > 0 then
			local natural = rightNaturalWidth()
			local available = natural > 0 and (width - natural - 8) or (width * 0.45 - 8)
			local stacked = label.TextBounds.X > available
			if stacked ~= row._stacked then
				row._stacked = stacked
				if stacked then
					local labelHeight = math.max(label.TextBounds.Y, 16)
					label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.zero
					label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
					right.AnchorPoint = Vector2.zero
					right.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, labelHeight + 4)
					right.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
					frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
					frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, labelHeight + 4 + height)
				else
					label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
					label.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
					right.AnchorPoint = baseRightAnchor
					right.Position = baseRightPosition
					right.Size = baseRightSize
					frame.AutomaticSize = baseAutomaticSize
					frame.Size = baseFrameSize
				end
			end
		end
		updatingLayout = false
	end
	if label then
		trove:Add(frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateResponsiveLayout))
		trove:Add(label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(updateResponsiveLayout))
		task.defer(updateResponsiveLayout)
	end

	--- Put a control into the right-hand list. `leading` pins it leftmost,
	--- which is how khook keeps a keybind chip ahead of its toggle.
	function row:Attach(inst, leading)
		self._attachCount += 1
		inst.LayoutOrder = leading and -1 or self._attachCount
		inst.Parent = right
		task.defer(updateResponsiveLayout)
		return inst
	end

	function row:SetVisible(on)
		self._visible = on
		frame.Visible = on
		section:_markSeparatorsDirty()
	end

	function row:Destroy()
		trove:Destroy()
		frame:Destroy()
	end

	if section._rows then table.insert(section._rows, row) end
	row._entry = Section._addEntry(section, frame, opts, nil)
	if row._entry then row._entry.row = row end
	row._section = section
	section:_markSeparatorsDirty()
	bindTooltip(trove, frame, opts.Tooltip)
	return row
end

--- Common element scaffolding: config binding, change signals, group sourcing.
local function newElement(kind, section, row, opts)
	local el = {
		Kind = kind,
		Row = row,
		_trove = row.Trove,
		_menu = Menu,
		_section = section,
		Changed = row.Trove:Add(Signal.new()),
		ValueChanged = row.Trove:Add(Signal.new()),
		_onChanged = opts.OnChanged or function() end,
		_configPath = opts.Config,
	}

	function el:Connect(signal, fn)
		self._trove:Connect(signal, fn)
		return self
	end

	function el:SetVisible(on) self.Row:SetVisible(on) end
	function el:Destroy() self.Row:Destroy() end

	return el
end

--- Read the element's starting value: saved config first, then Default.
local function initialValue(opts, fallback)
	if opts.Config then
		local v = Config.uiGet(opts.Config)
		if v ~= nil then return v end
	end
	if opts.Default ~= nil then return opts.Default end
	return fallback
end

local function writeConfig(el, value)
	if el._configPath then
		Config.uiSet(el._configPath, value)
	end
end

--- Mirror external config changes (config load, another element) into this one.
local function bindConfig(el, applyFn)
	if not el._configPath then return end
	local function applyCurrent()
		local v = Config.uiGet(el._configPath)
		if v ~= nil and v ~= el.Value then
			applyFn(v)
		end
	end
	el._trove:Add(Config.watch(el._configPath, applyCurrent, true))
	el._trove:Connect(Config.changed, function(changed)
		local resolved = Config.resolveEditPath(el._configPath, false)
		if changed == pathKey(resolved) then applyCurrent() end
	end)
	el._trove:Connect(Config.editProfileChanged, function(feature)
		if el._configPath[1] == feature then applyCurrent() end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--
-- Section
--============================================================================--

function UI.newSection(grid, opts, parentOverride)
	local trove = grid._trove:Extend()
	local batch = grid._batch
	local parent = parentOverride or grid:_column(opts.Side)

	local root = make("Frame", {
		LayoutOrder = nextLayoutOrder(parent),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	vlist(root, 16)

	local title
	if opts.Title then
		title = make("TextLabel", {
			Text = opts.Title,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
			TextSize = 16,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
			Parent = root,
		})
		make("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, -1), Parent = title })
		batch:Bind(title, "TextColor3", "TextColor")
	end

	local body = box(batch, root, "ElementBackground")
	local elements = elementsContainer(body)

	local self = setmetatable({
		_trove = trove,
		_batch = batch,
		_grid = grid,
		_root = root,
		_body = body,
		_elements = elements,
		_rows = {},
		_entries = {},
		-- tabbed panes share one box, so only a standalone panel may hide it
		_ownsRoot = true,
		Title = opts.Title,
	}, Section)
	if grid._sections then table.insert(grid._sections, self) end
	if grid._searchHost then UI.Palette.register(grid._searchHost, self) end
	return self
end

function Grid:AddSection(opts)
	return UI.newSection(self, opts or {})
end

--- Tabbed sections: several titled panes sharing one box, switched by a header
--- row of underlined labels. Returns one section object per title.
function Grid:AddMultiSection(opts)
	local trove = self._trove:Extend()
	local batch = self._batch
	local parent = self:_column(opts.Side)

	local root = make("Frame", {
		LayoutOrder = nextLayoutOrder(parent),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	vlist(root, 16)

	local header = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = root,
	})
	hlist(header, 18)

	local body = box(batch, root, "ElementBackground")

	local sections, tabs = {}, {}
	local active = 1

	local function setActive(index)
		active = index
		for i, t in ipairs(tabs) do
			local on = (i == index)
			tween(t.Label, { TextColor3 = on and Theme.get("TextColor") or Theme.get("Unselected") })
			tween(t.Accent, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 })
			t.Pane.Visible = on
		end
		local opened = sections[index]
		if opened ~= nil and opened.Opened ~= nil then opened.Opened:Fire() end
	end

	for i, titleText in ipairs(opts.Titles) do
		local btn = make("TextButton", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Text = "",
			AutoButtonColor = false,
			LayoutOrder = i,
			Parent = header,
		})
		local lbl = make("TextLabel", {
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			Text = titleText,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
			TextSize = 16,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Parent = btn,
		})
		local accent = make("Frame", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -3),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Parent = btn,
		})
		batch:Bind(accent, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
		corner(accent, 3)

		local pane = elementsContainer(body, UDim2.fromOffset(13, 12), UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0))
		pane.Visible = false

		trove:Add(btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActive(i) end))
		tabs[i] = { Label = lbl, Accent = accent, Pane = pane }

		sections[i] = setmetatable({
			_trove = trove:Extend(),
			_batch = batch,
			_grid = self,
			_root = root,
			_body = body,
			_elements = pane,
			_rows = {},
			_entries = {},
			Title = titleText,
			-- khook.lua:103041 -- a pane announces when it becomes the active
			-- tab, which is how the ESP preview follows Team/Enemy.
			Opened = Signal.new(),
		}, Section)
		if self._sections then table.insert(self._sections, sections[i]) end
		if self._searchHost then UI.Palette.register(self._searchHost, sections[i]) end
	end

	setActive(1)
	return table.unpack(sections)
end

--- Nested variant used by Player Spoofer: the outer section supplies the
--- title/box and the returned panes share a compact tab strip inside it.
function Section:AddMultiSection(opts)
	local trove = self._trove:Extend()
	local host = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = self._elements,
	})
	local header = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = host,
	})
	hlist(header, 18)
	local paneHost = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 36),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = host,
	})
	local sections, tabs = {}, {}
	local function setActive(index)
		for i, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
			local on = i == index
			tab.Pane.Visible = on
			tween(tab.Label, { TextColor3 = on and Theme.get("TextColor") or Theme.get("Unselected") })
			tween(tab.Accent, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 })
		end
		self:_markSeparatorsDirty()
	end
	for i, titleText in ipairs(opts.Titles or {}) do
		local button = make("TextButton", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = i, Parent = header,
		})
		local label = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Text = titleText, TextSize = 15,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main, TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = button,
		})
		local accent = make("Frame", {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -2), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = button,
		})
		self._batch:Bind(accent, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
		local pane = make("Frame", {
			Visible = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = paneHost,
		})
		vlist(pane, 12)
		sections[i] = setmetatable({
			_trove = trove:Extend(), _batch = self._batch, _grid = self._grid,
			_root = self._root, _body = self._body, _elements = pane,
			_rows = self._rows, _entries = {}, Title = titleText,
		}, Section)
		tabs[i] = { Label = label, Accent = accent, Pane = pane }
		trove:Add(button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActive(i) end))
	end
	Section._addEntry(self, host, { NoSeparator = true }, {
		CollectSearchHits = function(_, crumb, open, out)
			for _, section in ipairs(sections) do section:CollectSearchHits(crumb, open, out) end
		end,
	})
	setActive(1)
	return table.unpack(sections)
end

--============================================================================--
-- Group -- a nested element list shown only while its source is satisfied
--============================================================================--

function Section:AddGroup(opts)
	local trove = self._trove:Extend()
	local host = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = self._elements,
	})
	vlist(host, 12)

	local group = setmetatable({
		_trove = trove,
		_batch = self._batch,
		_grid = self._grid,
		_root = self._root,
		_body = self._body,
		_elements = host,
		_host = host,
		-- a group's rows belong to the section that owns it, so a search hides
		-- and restores them with everything else on the panel
		_rows = self._rows,
		-- ...but its own entries, so the hairlines inside it are its own
		_entries = {},
	}, Group)
	Section._addEntry(self, host, opts, group)

	local source, option = opts.Source, opts.Option

	local function evaluate()
		if not source then host.Visible = true return end
		local v = source.Value
		local ok
		if option ~= nil then
			if typeof(v) == "table" then
				ok = v[option] == true or table.find(v, option) ~= nil
			else
				ok = (v == option)
			end
		else
			ok = (v == true) or (typeof(v) == "string" and v ~= "")
		end
		host.Visible = ok and true or false
		self:_markSeparatorsDirty()
	end

	if source then
		trove:Connect(source.ValueChanged, evaluate)
	end
	evaluate()

	group.Evaluate = evaluate
	return group
end

--- khook.lua:119700 `CollectSearchHits`. Every row with a label contributes
--- one hit carrying its breadcrumb, how to open the page it lives on, and a
--- way back to its frame; groups recurse into the same list.
function Section:CollectSearchHits(crumb, open, out)
	local entries = self._entries
	if entries == nil then return end
	for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
		local row = entry.row
		if row ~= nil then
			local label = row.Label and row.Label.Text or nil
			if label ~= nil and label ~= "" then
				table.insert(out, {
					Label = label, Crumb = crumb, Open = open,
					GetFrame = function() return row.Frame end,
				})
			end
		end
		if entry.group ~= nil then
			entry.group:CollectSearchHits(crumb, open, out)
		end
	end
end

--============================================================================--
-- Viewport (khook.lua:15563-16152)
--============================================================================--
-- A 3D preview pane: a ViewportFrame over a gradient card, with an orbit camera
-- you can drag, a wheel that dollies, and an idle auto-rotate that pauses while
-- you drag and only comes back if you had not switched it off yourself.

--- khook.lua:15761 `f1174` -- centre and radius from a Model's bounding box or
--- a BasePart's size; anything else gets khook's origin/4 fallback.
local function viewportBounds(object)
	if object:IsA("Model") then
		local cf, size = object:GetBoundingBox()
		return cf.Position, math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) * 0.5
	end
	if not object:IsA("BasePart") then return Vector3.zero, 4 end
	local size = object.Size
	return object.Position, math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) * 0.5
end

--- khook.lua:15776 `f1175` -- orbit the camera around the model centre at
--- `radius * 2.4 * distanceMultiplier`, never closer than 4.
local function fitViewportCamera(vp)
	if vp._model == nil then return end
	local centre = vp._modelCenter
	local distance = math.max(vp._modelRadius * 2.4 * vp._distanceMultiplier, 4)
	local elevation = clamp(vp._elevation, -math.pi / 2 + 0.1, math.pi / 2 - 0.1)
	local flat = math.cos(elevation)
	local offset = Vector3.new(
		math.cos(vp._rotation) * distance * flat,
		math.sin(elevation) * distance,
		math.sin(vp._rotation) * distance * flat)
	vp._camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(centre + offset, centre)
end

--- khook.lua:16032 `_new` + 15563 `f1163`
function Section:AddViewport(opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local height = opts.Height or 180
	local row = newRow(self, { Height = height + 8, NoSeparator = true })
	local trove = row.Trove:Extend()

	local camera = make("Camera", { FieldOfView = opts.FieldOfView or 50 })
	trove:Add(camera)
	local world = Instance.new("WorldModel")
	trove:Add(world)

	local autoRotate = opts.AutoRotate ~= false
	local vp = {
		Kind = "Viewport", Row = row, _trove = trove,
		_height = height,
		_rotation = 0,
		_elevation = math.rad(20),
		_autoRotate = autoRotate,
		_userWantsAutoRotate = autoRotate,
		_rotateSpeed = opts.RotateSpeed or 0.6,
		_distanceMultiplier = opts.DistanceMul or 1,
		_camera = camera, _worldModel = world, _model = nil,
		_modelCenter = Vector3.zero, _modelRadius = 4,
		_rt = nil, _onRealized = {},
	}

	--- khook.lua:16081 -- the model is CLONED in and the previous clone
	--- destroyed, so the caller keeps ownership of what it handed over.
	function vp:SetModel(model)
		local current = self._model
		if current ~= nil and current.Parent == self._worldModel then current:Destroy() end
		self._model = nil
		if model ~= nil then
			local clone = model:Clone()
			if clone ~= nil then
				clone.Parent = self._worldModel
				self._model = clone
				self._modelCenter, self._modelRadius = viewportBounds(clone)
				fitViewportCamera(self)
			end
		end
		return self
	end

	function vp:GetViewportFrame() return self._rt end

	--- khook.lua:16106 -- fires immediately when the frame already exists.
	function vp:OnRealized(fn)
		if self._rt == nil then
			table.insert(self._onRealized, fn)
			return self
		end
		fn(self._rt)
		return self
	end

	function vp:SetAutoRotate(on)
		self._autoRotate = on == true
		self._userWantsAutoRotate = on == true
		return self
	end
	function vp:SetRotateSpeed(speed) self._rotateSpeed = speed return self end
	function vp:SetDistance(mul)
		self._distanceMultiplier = math.max(0.1, mul)
		fitViewportCamera(self)
		return self
	end
	function vp:SetRotation(rotation)
		self._rotation = rotation
		fitViewportCamera(self)
		return self
	end
	function vp:SetVisible(on) self.Row:SetVisible(on) end
	function vp:Destroy() self.Row:Destroy() self._trove:Destroy() end

	------------------------------------------------------------- the card --
	local holder = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = row.Frame,
	})
	local card = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 0, Parent = holder,
	})
	-- khook.lua:15587 calls `Batch:BindGradient(gradient, <2-element table>)` --
	-- a key LIST whose contents the decompiler destroyed, leaving only a stray
	-- `f1164 = "GradientDark"` above it. That literal is NOT a khookclone theme
	-- key, and `Theme.get` answers an unknown key with magenta (line 279) on
	-- purpose, so the card rendered bright pink. Every other sunken surface here
	-- binds "ElementBackground" (line 1259), and khookclone's BindGradient
	-- derives the light stop itself with `shade(c, 1.06)`.
	row.Batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = card }), "ElementBackground")
	make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = card })

	-- khook.lua:15592 -- a fixed light rig, so the preview reads the same
	-- whatever the game's lighting happens to be doing.
	local frame = make("ViewportFrame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1).Unit,
		LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 150),
		CurrentCamera = camera,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = frame })
	row.Batch:Bind(make("UIStroke", { Parent = frame }), "Color", "Outline")
	world.Parent = frame
	vp._rt = frame

	local input = make("TextButton", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
		Parent = frame,
	})

	-- khook.lua:15630 -- one wheel notch is a fixed ratio, clamped to [0.2, 6].
	trove:Add(input.InputBegan:Connect(function(io)
		if io.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
			local direction = io.Position.Z > 0 and -1 or 1
			vp._distanceMultiplier = clamp(vp._distanceMultiplier * 1.15 ^ (-direction), 0.2, 6)
			fitViewportCamera(vp)
		end
	end))

	-- khook.lua:15649 -- drag to orbit.
	local dragging, last = false, nil
	trove:Add(input.InputBegan:Connect(function(io)
		if io.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or io.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging, last = true, nil
			vp._autoRotate = false
		end
	end))
	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(io)
		if not dragging then return end
		if io.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			and io.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
		local point = Vector2.new(io.Position.X, io.Position.Y)
		local previous = last
		last = point
		if previous == nil then return end
		local delta = point - previous
		vp._rotation -= delta.X * 0.01
		vp._elevation = clamp(vp._elevation - delta.Y * 0.01,
			-math.pi / 2 + 0.1, math.pi / 2 - 0.1)
		fitViewportCamera(vp)
	end))
	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(io)
		if io.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or io.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging, last = false, nil
			-- khook.lua:15666 -- only resume if the user had not turned it off.
			if vp._userWantsAutoRotate then vp._autoRotate = true end
		end
	end))

	-- khook.lua:15679 -- idle spin, and only while the frame is really on
	-- screen with something in it.
	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		if frame.Visible and frame.Parent ~= nil and vp._model ~= nil and vp._autoRotate then
			vp._rotation += dt * vp._rotateSpeed
			fitViewportCamera(vp)
		end
	end))

	-- khook.lua:15690 -- anything queued before the frame existed runs now.
	for _, fn in ipairs(vp._onRealized) do pcall(fn, frame) end
	table.clear(vp._onRealized)
	return vp
end

function Section:AddDivider(opts)
	local label = typeof(opts) == "table" and (opts.Label or opts.Text) or nil
	if label ~= nil then
		local host = make("Frame", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Parent = self._elements,
		})
		local textLabel = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
			Text = tostring(label),
			TextSize = 13,
			FontFace = Fonts.Bold,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Parent = host,
		})
		self._batch:Bind(textLabel, "TextColor3", "Unselected")
		local line = make("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Outline"),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = -1,
			Parent = host,
		})
		self._batch:Bind(line, "BackgroundColor3", "Outline")
		Section._addEntry(self, host, { NoSeparator = true, IsDivider = true }, nil)
		self:_markSeparatorsDirty()
		return host
	end
	local line = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Outline"),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = self._elements,
	})
	self._batch:Bind(line, "BackgroundColor3", "Outline")
	Section._addEntry(self, line, { NoSeparator = true, IsDivider = true }, nil)
	self:_markSeparatorsDirty()
	return line
end

--- khook uses labels as *carriers*: `AddLabel` hands back a row that a colour
--- swatch, a dropdown or a gear then attaches to, which is how "Fade Color" or
--- "Weapon Priority" end up with a control on the same line
--- (khook.lua:76896, 43955). So this builds a real row rather than a bare
--- TextLabel -- without the `.Row` the attach silently made its own row, and
--- `AddGear` errored outright.
function Section:AddLabel(opts)
	local text = typeof(opts) == "table" and (opts.Text or opts.Label) or tostring(opts)
	local o = typeof(opts) == "table" and opts or {}
	local row = newRow(self, {
		Label = text,
		Height = o.Height,
		NoSeparator = o.NoSeparator,
		RightOffset = o.RightOffset,
		MediumTitle = o.MediumTitle,
	})
	local lbl = row.Label
	return {
		Kind = "Label",
		Row = row,
		Instance = lbl,
		Set = function(_, t) if lbl then lbl.Text = t end end,
		SetVisible = function(_, v) row:SetVisible(v) end,
	}
end

--============================================================================--
-- Toggle
--============================================================================--
-- Pill 32x20, knob 16x16 at (3,2) off / (13,2) on -- khook's exact metrics.

function Section:AddToggle(opts)
	local row = newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("Toggle", self, row, opts)
	el.Value = initialValue(opts, false) == true

	local pill = row:Attach(make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 20),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
	}))
	local grad = make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = pill })
	row.Batch:BindGradient(grad, "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	row.Batch:Bind(pill, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(pill, 999)

	local fill = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Parent = pill,
	})
	corner(fill, 999)

	local knob = make("Frame", {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 2),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleCircleUnselected"),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = pill,
	})
	corner(knob, 999)

	local function render(instant)
		local t = el.Value and 0 or 1
		local knobColor = el.Value and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Theme.get("ToggleCircleUnselected")
		local knobPos = el.Value and UDim2.fromOffset(13, 2) or UDim2.fromOffset(3, 2)
		if instant then
			fill.BackgroundTransparency = t
			knob.BackgroundColor3 = knobColor
			knob.Position = knobPos
		else
			tween(fill, { BackgroundTransparency = t })
			tween(knob, { BackgroundColor3 = knobColor, Position = knobPos })
		end
	end

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		value = value == true
		if value == self.Value then return self end
		self.Value = value
		render(false)
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(value)
			self.Changed:Fire(value)
		end
		return self
	end

	connectClick(row.Trove, row.Frame, function() el:Set(not el.Value) end)
	connectHover(row.Trove, row.Frame,
		function() if el.Value then tween(fill, { BackgroundColor3 = shade(Theme.get("Accent"), 0.85) }, TWEEN_FAST) end end,
		function() if el.Value then tween(fill, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent") }, TWEEN_FAST) end end)

	row.Trove:Connect(Theme.AccentChanged, function(c) tween(fill, { BackgroundColor3 = c }) end)
	row.Batch:BindStateful("ToggleCircleUnselected", function(c)
		if not el.Value then knob.BackgroundColor3 = c end
	end)

	bindConfig(el, function(v)
		el.Value = v == true
		render(false)
		el.ValueChanged:Fire(el.Value)
	end)

	render(true)
	if el._configPath then Config.rawSet(el._configPath, el.Value) end
	return el
end

--============================================================================--
-- Slider
--============================================================================--

--- khook's slider (khook.lua:113277). Nothing like a conventional one: the
--- track is a **2px hairline** running the whole row minus the value box, the
--- fill is accent-coloured with its own stroke, the knob is a 14px circle
--- pinned to the fill's right edge, and the readout is an **editable TextBox**
--- whose width -- and therefore the track's -- follows the text.
local SLIDER_BOX_MIN, SLIDER_BOX_MAX = 44, 96
local SLIDER_TEXT_SIZE = 14

function Section:AddSlider(opts)
	local row = newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("Slider", self, row, opts)

	local min, max = opts.Min or 0, opts.Max or 100
	local step = opts.Step or 1
	local suffix = opts.Suffix or ""
	el.Min, el.Max, el.Step = min, max, step
	el.Value = clamp(tonumber(initialValue(opts, min)) or min, min, max)

	-- The slider owns the whole right-hand container.
	local host = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, row.Height),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = row.Frame,
	})
	row:Attach(host)
	host.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, row.Height)

	local boxWidth = SLIDER_BOX_MIN

	local track = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -boxWidth - 11, 0, 2),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		Parent = host,
	})
	row.Batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Parent = track }), "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	row.Batch:Bind(track, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	local trackStroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = track })
	row.Batch:Bind(trackStroke, "Color", "Outline")
	corner(track, 999)

	-- 44px tall invisible grab area so a 2px line is still easy to hit
	local hit = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Active = true,
		ZIndex = 0,
		Parent = track,
	})

	local fill = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		Parent = track,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(fill, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	local fillStroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = fill })
	row.Batch:Bind(fillStroke, "Color", "Accent")

	local knob = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		ZIndex = 2,
		Parent = fill,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(knob, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(knob, 999)

	local valueBox = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(boxWidth, 22),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		Parent = host,
	})
	row.Batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = valueBox }), "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	row.Batch:Bind(valueBox, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	make("UISizeConstraint", { MinSize = Vector2.new(24, 22), Parent = valueBox })
	corner(valueBox, 5)
	local boxStroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = valueBox })
	row.Batch:Bind(boxStroke, "Color", "Outline")

	local valueText = make("TextBox", {
		FontFace = Fonts.Medium,
		Text = "",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		TextSize = SLIDER_TEXT_SIZE,
		TextScaled = false,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
		Parent = valueBox,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(valueText, "TextColor3", "TextColor")
	padding(valueText, 0, 5, 0, 5)

	local function fmt(v)
		if step < 1 then
			local decimals = math.max(0, -math.floor(math.log10(step) + 1e-9))
			return string.format("%." .. decimals .. "f", v) .. suffix
		end
		return tostring(math.floor(v + 0.5)) .. suffix
	end

	--- Shrink the text once past five characters, then size the box to the
	--- text and give the remainder back to the track -- khook's f7816.
	local function updateValueBoxSize()
		local len = #valueText.Text
		local size = SLIDER_TEXT_SIZE
		if len > 5 then
			size = math.max(9, SLIDER_TEXT_SIZE - (len - 5))
		end
		if valueText.TextSize ~= size then
			tween(valueText, { TextSize = size }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end
		local want = clamp(len * size * 0.6 + 12, SLIDER_BOX_MIN, SLIDER_BOX_MAX)
		if math.abs(valueBox.Size.X.Offset - want) >= 1 then
			boxWidth = want
			tween(valueBox, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(want, 22) }, TWEEN_FAST)
			tween(track, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -want - 11, 0, 2) }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end
	end

	local function render(instant)
		local alpha = (max > min) and ((el.Value - min) / (max - min)) or 0
		valueText.Text = fmt(el.Value)
		if instant then
			fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 1)
		else
			tween(fill, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 1) }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end
		updateValueBoxSize()
	end

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		value = clamp(round(tonumber(value) or min, step), min, max)
		if value == self.Value then return self end
		self.Value = value
		render(false)
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(value)
			self.Changed:Fire(value)
		end
		return self
	end

	------------------------------------------------------------- dragging --
	local dragging = false
	local function setFromX(x)
		local a = clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
		el:Set(min + a * (max - min))
	end
	local function setDragging(on)
		dragging = on
		tween(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(on and 18 or 14, on and 18 or 14) }, TWEEN_FAST)
	end

	local function beginDrag(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
		setDragging(true)
		setFromX(input.Position.X)
	end
	row.Trove:Add(hit.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag))
	row.Trove:Add(knob.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag))

	row.Trove:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end))
	row.Trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			setDragging(false)
		end
	end))

	-- typing a value directly into the box
	row.Trove:Add(valueText.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local typed = tonumber(string.match(valueText.Text, "[%d%.%-]+"))
		if typed then el:Set(typed) else render(false) end
		updateValueBoxSize()
	end))

	bindConfig(el, function(v)
		el.Value = clamp(tonumber(v) or min, min, max)
		render(false)
		el.ValueChanged:Fire(el.Value)
	end)

	render(true)
	if el._configPath then Config.rawSet(el._configPath, el.Value) end
	return el
end

--- Two-handle slider writing to a {Min=,Max=} pair.
function Section:AddRangeSlider(opts)
	local row = newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("RangeSlider", self, row, opts)
	local min, max, step = opts.Min or 0, opts.Max or 100, opts.Step or 1

	local startVal = initialValue(opts, { Min = min, Max = max })
	el.Value = { Min = startVal.Min or min, Max = startVal.Max or max }

	-- Same anatomy as the single slider: a 2px track with a 44px grab area,
	-- but with two knobs and the fill spanning between them.
	local host = row:Attach(make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, row.Height),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}))

	local readout = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(78, 22),
		TextSize = 14, FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		Parent = host,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(readout, "TextColor3", "Unselected")

	local track = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -89, 0, 2),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		Parent = host,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(track, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	local rsStroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = track })
	row.Batch:Bind(rsStroke, "Color", "Outline")
	corner(track, 999)

	local holder = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Active = true,
		ZIndex = 0,
		Parent = track,
	})

	local fill = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = track,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(fill, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	corner(fill, 999)

	local knobs = {}
	for i = 1, 2 do
		knobs[i] = make("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3, Parent = track,
		})
		row.Batch:Bind(knobs[i], "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
		corner(knobs[i], 999)
	end

	local function alphaOf(v) return (max > min) and ((v - min) / (max - min)) or 0 end
	local function render()
		local a1, a2 = alphaOf(el.Value.Min), alphaOf(el.Value.Max)
		fill.Position = UDim2.fromScale(a1, 0)
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(a2 - a1, 1)
		knobs[1].Position = UDim2.fromScale(a1, 0.5)
		knobs[2].Position = UDim2.fromScale(a2, 0.5)
		local d = step < 1 and 2 or 0
		readout.Text = string.format("%." .. d .. "f - %." .. d .. "f", el.Value.Min, el.Value.Max)
	end

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		local lo = clamp(round(value.Min or min, step), min, max)
		local hi = clamp(round(value.Max or max, step), min, max)
		if lo > hi then lo, hi = hi, lo end
		self.Value = { Min = lo, Max = hi }
		render()
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, self.Value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, self.Value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(self.Value)
			self.Changed:Fire(self.Value)
		end
		return self
	end

	local drag = nil
	row.Trove:Add(holder.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
		local a = clamp((input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
		drag = (math.abs(a - alphaOf(el.Value.Min)) <= math.abs(a - alphaOf(el.Value.Max))) and "Min" or "Max"
		local v = min + a * (max - min)
		el:Set({ Min = drag == "Min" and v or el.Value.Min, Max = drag == "Max" and v or el.Value.Max })
	end))
	row.Trove:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if drag and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local a = clamp((input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
			local v = min + a * (max - min)
			el:Set({ Min = drag == "Min" and v or el.Value.Min, Max = drag == "Max" and v or el.Value.Max })
		end
	end))
	row.Trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then drag = nil end
	end))

	render()
	return el
end

--============================================================================--
-- Dropdown / MultiDropdown
--============================================================================--

-- One popup at a time -- except that the colour picker realizes real menu
-- elements inside its own panel (khook.lua:123465), so a dropdown opened in
-- there must not tear down the picker hosting it. The slot is therefore a small
-- stack: level 1 is a panel opened from a row, level 2 is anything opened from
-- inside that panel. Closing a level closes everything above it too.
-- (One table rather than four locals: the main chunk is at Lua's 200 ceiling.)
local Popups = { stack = {} }

Popups.top = 0        -- highest occupied level; `#` is unusable with holes

function Popups.close(fromLevel)
	local floor = fromLevel or 1
	for i = Popups.top, floor, -1 do
		local closer = Popups.stack[i]
		Popups.stack[i] = nil
		if closer then pcall(closer) end
	end
	if Popups.top >= floor then Popups.top = floor - 1 end
end

--- Registers `closer` for the level a control at `level` opens into.
function Popups.push(level, closer)
	local at = level + 1
	Popups.close(at)
	Popups.stack[at] = closer
	if at > Popups.top then Popups.top = at end
end

--- Is anything open at or above this control?
function Popups.openAt(level)
	return Popups.stack[level + 1] ~= nil
end

--- khook's dropdown "pill" (khook.lua:46880): 137x24, corner 5, a bordered
--- stroke offset inward by 1, a 16pt left-aligned value label clipped at 5px
--- in, and a 9x5 chevron 8px from the right. Pressing it darkens the fill to
--- 85%.
local function dropdownButton(row, opts)
	local inline = opts.Inline == true
	local btn = row:Attach(make("TextButton", {
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Text = "",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0.5, 0),
		Size = inline and UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22) or UDim2.fromOffset(opts.Width or 137, 24),
		AutomaticSize = inline and Enum.AutomaticSize.X or Enum.AutomaticSize.None,
		ClipsDescendants = inline,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
	}))
	row.Batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = btn }), "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	row.Batch:Bind(btn, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(btn, 5)
	if inline then
		make("UISizeConstraint", {
			MaxSize = Vector2.new(opts.MaxWidth or 86, 22),
			Parent = btn,
		})
	end
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", {
		BorderOffset = inline and UDim.new(0, 0) or UDim.new(0, -1),
		ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
		Parent = btn,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")

	local text = make("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, 0),
		Size = inline and UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) or UDim2.new(1, -25, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = inline and Enum.AutomaticSize.XY or Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		TextSize = 16,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		FontFace = inline and Fonts.Main or Fonts.Medium,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
		Parent = btn,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(text, "TextColor3", "TextColor")

	local arrow = make("ImageLabel", {
		Image = Icons.DropdownArrow,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = inline and Vector2.zero or Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = inline and UDim2.new() or UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 5),
		LayoutOrder = inline and 1 or 0,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		Parent = btn,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(arrow, "ImageColor3", "Unselected")
	if inline then
		text.LayoutOrder = -1
		padding(text, 0, 0, 2, 0)
		make("UIListLayout", {
			VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
			FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
			Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
			SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
			Parent = btn,
		})
		padding(btn, 2, 6, 0, 6)
		local function fitInline()
			if text.Parent == nil then return end
			btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
				math.min(text.AbsoluteSize.X + 22, (opts.MaxWidth or 86) + 22), 22)
		end
		row.Trove:Add(text:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			task.defer(fitInline)
		end))
		task.defer(fitInline)
	end

	local pressed
	connectHover(row.Trove, btn,
		function()
			pressed = btn.BackgroundColor3
			tween(btn, { BackgroundColor3 = shade(pressed, 0.85) }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end,
		function()
			if pressed then
				tween(btn, { BackgroundColor3 = pressed }, TWEEN_FAST)
				pressed = nil
			end
		end)

	return btn, text, arrow
end

--- Popup list anchored under a button, parented to the ScreenGui so it draws
--- above the scrolling grid.
--- khook's popup (khook.lua:46650). It is anchored by its **top-right** corner
--- to the trigger, is at least 80 wide and otherwise `trigger width + 2`,
--- drops to `trigger.Y + 26`, grows with its content and is capped at 60% of
--- the viewport height (50% on touch). The scroll bar is 4px at half alpha.
local function buildPopup(anchor, _height, batch)
	local viewport = Camera and Camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
	local maxHeight = math.floor(viewport.Y * (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 0.5 or 0.6))

	local frame = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ElementBackground"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		ZIndex = 500,
		Parent = UI.Gui,
	})
	batch:Bind(frame, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementBackground")
	batch:BindGradient(make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Parent = frame }), "ElementBackground")
	corner(frame, 6)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = frame })
	batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")
	addGlow(batch, frame, 3, 0.72, "Outline")

	local scroll = make("ScrollingFrame", {
		Active = true,
		ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.5,
		ScrollBarThickness = 4,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		Selectable = false,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		ZIndex = 501,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	batch:Bind(scroll, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	make("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(math.huge, maxHeight), Parent = scroll })
	make("UIPadding", { PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = scroll })
	make("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = scroll })

	--- khook's Place(): right edge tracks the trigger, clamped on screen.
	local function reposition()
		local pos, size = anchor.AbsolutePosition, anchor.AbsoluteSize
		local width = math.max(math.floor(size.X + 2 + 0.5), 80)
		frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 0)
		local vp = Camera and Camera.ViewportSize or viewport
		local inset = UI.inset()
		local x = math.clamp(math.floor(pos.X + 0.5) + width, width, vp.X)
		local y = math.clamp(math.floor(pos.Y + 26), 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - frame.AbsoluteSize.Y))
		frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + inset.X, y + inset.Y)
	end
	reposition()
	return frame, scroll, reposition
end

local function popupOption(scroll, batch, text, order)
	local opt = make("TextButton", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		TextSize = 16,
		FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		LayoutOrder = order,
		ZIndex = 502,
		Parent = scroll,
	})
	local pad = padding(opt, 5, 5, 5, 5)
	local tick = make("ImageLabel", {
		Image = Icons.DropdownTick,
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
		ImageTransparency = 1,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-22, 2),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ZIndex = 503,
		Parent = opt,
	})
	batch:Bind(tick, "ImageColor3", "TextColor")
	batch:Bind(opt, "TextColor3", "Unselected")
	-- The source draws option text on the TextButton itself.  Returning the
	-- button twice preserves the small internal call-site API (`row, label`).
	return opt, opt, tick, pad
end

function Section:AddDropdown(opts)
	local popupLvl = self._popupLevel or 0
	local row = opts.Row or newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("Dropdown", self, row, opts)
	el.Options = opts.Options and table.clone(opts.Options) or {}
	el.Value = initialValue(opts, el.Options[1])

	local btn, text, arrow = dropdownButton(row, opts)

	--- khook lets a dropdown show a different string than it stores -- the
	--- ragebot's "On Empty" keeps the value `SwapOrReload` but displays
	--- "Swap or Reload" (khook.lua:43977 `t85.Labels`).
	local function display(option)
		if option == nil then return opts.Placeholder or "None" end
		local labels = opts.Labels
		local shown = labels and labels[option]
		return tostring(shown or option)
	end

	local function render()
		text.Text = display(el.Value)
	end

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		if value == self.Value then return self end
		self.Value = value
		render()
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(value)
			self.Changed:Fire(value)
		end
		return self
	end

	function el:SetOptions(list)
		self.Options = table.clone(list)
		if self.Value ~= nil and not table.find(self.Options, self.Value) then
			self:Set(self.Options[1])
		end
		render()
		return self
	end

	local function open()
		Popups.close(popupLvl + 1)
		local searchH = opts.Search and 26 or 0
		local height = math.min(opts.Height or 160, 8 + searchH + #el.Options * 26)
		local frame, scroll, reposition = buildPopup(btn, math.max(34 + searchH, height), row.Batch)
		tween(arrow, { Rotation = 180 }, TWEEN_FAST)

		-- Search field: khook enables it on long option lists (weapon pickers).
		local filter = ""
		local entries = {}
		local function repaint()
			local needle = string.lower(filter)
			for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
				entry.button.Visible = needle == ""
					or string.find(string.lower(entry.text), needle, 1, true) ~= nil
			end
		end

		if opts.Search then
			scroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4 + searchH)
			scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8 - searchH)
			local searchBox = make("TextBox", {
				Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, searchH - 4),
				BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Text = "",
				PlaceholderText = "Search...",
				PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
				TextSize = 13,
				FontFace = Fonts.Main,
				TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				ClearTextOnFocus = false,
				ZIndex = 502,
				Parent = frame,
			})
			make("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = searchBox })
			corner(searchBox, 4)
			searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
				filter = searchBox.Text
				repaint()
			end)
		end

		for i, option in ipairs(el.Options) do
			local shown = display(option)
			local o, lbl, tick, pad = popupOption(scroll, row.Batch, shown, i)
			-- search matches what the user can actually read
			table.insert(entries, { button = o, text = shown })
			if option == el.Value then
				lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor")
				tick.ImageTransparency = 0
				pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 29)
			end
			o.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				el:Set(option)
				Popups.close()
			end)
		end

		local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(reposition)
		local outside
		outside = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
				and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
			task.defer(function()
				local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				local p, s = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
				local inFrame = m.X >= p.X and m.X <= p.X + s.X and m.Y >= p.Y and m.Y <= p.Y + s.Y
				local bp, bs = btn.AbsolutePosition, btn.AbsoluteSize
				local inBtn = m.X >= bp.X and m.X <= bp.X + bs.X and m.Y >= bp.Y and m.Y <= bp.Y + bs.Y
				if not inFrame and not inBtn then Popups.close() end
			end)
		end)

		Popups.push(popupLvl, function()
			conn:Disconnect()
			outside:Disconnect()
			frame:Destroy()
			tween(arrow, { Rotation = 0 }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end)
	end

	row.Trove:Add(btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if Popups.openAt(popupLvl) then Popups.close(popupLvl + 1) else open() end
	end))

	bindConfig(el, function(v)
		el.Value = v
		render()
		el.ValueChanged:Fire(v)
	end)

	render()
	if el._configPath then Config.rawSet(el._configPath, el.Value) end
	return el
end

--- Multi-select. Value is a set: { [option] = true }.
function Section:AddMultiDropdown(opts)
	local popupLvl = self._popupLevel or 0
	local row = opts.Row or newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("MultiDropdown", self, row, opts)
	el.Options = opts.Options and table.clone(opts.Options) or {}

	local start = initialValue(opts, {})
	el.Value = {}
	if typeof(start) == "table" then
		-- accept either a set or an array
		local isArray = #start > 0
		for k, v in pairs(start) do
			if isArray then el.Value[v] = true else el.Value[k] = v == true end
		end
	end

	local btn, text, arrow = dropdownButton(row, opts)

	--- Same display-label indirection as the single dropdown (khook.lua:17837).
	local function display(option)
		local labels = opts.Labels
		return tostring((labels and labels[option]) or option)
	end

	local function summary()
		local picked = {}
		for _, o in ipairs(el.Options) do
			if el.Value[o] then table.insert(picked, display(o)) end
		end
		if #picked == 0 then return opts.Placeholder or "None" end
		if #picked > 2 then return #picked .. " selected" end
		return table.concat(picked, ", ")
	end
	local function render() text.Text = summary() end

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		self.Value = value or {}
		render()
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, self.Value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, self.Value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(self.Value)
			self.Changed:Fire(self.Value)
		end
		return self
	end

	function el:Toggle(option)
		local v = table.clone(self.Value)
		v[option] = not v[option] or nil
		self:Set(v)
	end

	function el:SetOptions(list)
		self.Options = table.clone(list)
		render()
		return self
	end

	local function open()
		Popups.close(popupLvl + 1)
		local height = math.min(opts.Height or 180, 8 + #el.Options * 26)
		local frame, scroll, reposition = buildPopup(btn, math.max(34, height), row.Batch)
		tween(arrow, { Rotation = 180 }, TWEEN_FAST)

		local rows = {}
		local function paint()
			for option, entry in pairs(rows) do
				local on = el.Value[option] == true
				entry.lbl.TextColor3 = on and Theme.get("TextColor") or Theme.get("Unselected")
				entry.tick.ImageTransparency = on and 0 or 1
				entry.padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, on and 29 or 5)
			end
		end

		for i, option in ipairs(el.Options) do
			local o, lbl, tick, pad = popupOption(scroll, row.Batch, display(option), i)
			rows[option] = { lbl = lbl, tick = tick, padding = pad }
			o.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				el:Toggle(option)
				paint()
			end)
		end
		paint()

		local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(reposition)
		local outside = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
				and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
			task.defer(function()
				local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				local p, s = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
				local inFrame = m.X >= p.X and m.X <= p.X + s.X and m.Y >= p.Y and m.Y <= p.Y + s.Y
				local bp, bs = btn.AbsolutePosition, btn.AbsoluteSize
				local inBtn = m.X >= bp.X and m.X <= bp.X + bs.X and m.Y >= bp.Y and m.Y <= bp.Y + bs.Y
				if not inFrame and not inBtn then Popups.close() end
			end)
		end)

		Popups.push(popupLvl, function()
			conn:Disconnect()
			outside:Disconnect()
			frame:Destroy()
			tween(arrow, { Rotation = 0 }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end)
	end

	row.Trove:Add(btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if Popups.openAt(popupLvl) then Popups.close(popupLvl + 1) else open() end
	end))

	bindConfig(el, function(v) el.Value = v or {} render() end)
	render()
	if el._configPath then Config.rawSet(el._configPath, el.Value) end
	return el
end

--============================================================================--
-- TextBox / Button
--============================================================================--

function Section:AddTextBox(opts)
	local row = newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("TextBox", self, row, opts)
	el.Value = tostring(initialValue(opts, "") or "")

	local holder = row:Attach(make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(opts.Width or 150, 24),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}))
	row.Batch:Bind(holder, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(holder, 5)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = holder })
	row.Batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")

	local input = make("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0),
		Text = el.Value,
		PlaceholderText = opts.Placeholder or "",
		PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		TextSize = 14,
		FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(input, "TextColor3", "TextColor")

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		value = tostring(value or "")
		self.Value = value
		if input.Text ~= value then input.Text = value end
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(value)
			self.Changed:Fire(value)
		end
		return self
	end

	if opts.FocusLostOnly ~= false then
		row.Trove:Add(input.FocusLost:Connect(function() el:Set(input.Text) end))
	else
		row.Trove:Add(input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() el:Set(input.Text) end))
	end
	row.Trove:Add(input.Focused:Connect(function()
		tween(stroke, { Color = Theme.get("Accent") }, TWEEN_FAST)
	end))
	row.Trove:Add(input.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		tween(stroke, { Color = Theme.get("Outline") }, TWEEN_FAST)
	end))

	bindConfig(el, function(v) el.Value = tostring(v or "") input.Text = el.Value end)
	if el._configPath then Config.rawSet(el._configPath, el.Value) end
	return el
end

function Section:AddButton(opts)
	local row = newRow(self, { Tooltip = opts.Tooltip, Height = 24 })
	-- Buttons are bare roots in khook; a scale-sized child inside an
	-- AutomaticSize.Y row creates a circular size dependency and can stretch
	-- to the rest of the scrolling column.
	row.Frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
	local el = newElement("Button", self, row, opts)

	local btn = make("TextButton", {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 22),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Parent = row.Frame,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(btn, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(btn, 5)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = btn })
	row.Batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")
	local lbl = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Text = opts.Label,
		TextSize = 16,
		FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
		Parent = btn,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(lbl, "TextColor3", "TextColor")

	connectHover(row.Trove, btn,
		function()
			tween(btn, { BackgroundColor3 = shade(Theme.get("Accent"), 0.55) }, TWEEN_FAST)
			tween(stroke, { Color = Theme.get("Accent") }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end,
		function()
			tween(btn, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected") }, TWEEN_FAST)
			tween(stroke, { Color = Theme.get("Outline") }, TWEEN_FAST)
		end)

	local pendingConfirm = false
	local confirmToken = 0
	row.Trove:Add(btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if opts.Confirm and not pendingConfirm then
			pendingConfirm = true
			confirmToken += 1
			local token = confirmToken
			lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(231, 92, 80)
			task.spawn(function()
				for seconds = 3, 1, -1 do
					if token ~= confirmToken or row.Frame.Parent == nil then return end
					lbl.Text = string.format("Are you sure? (%s)", tostring(seconds))
					task.wait(1)
				end
				if token == confirmToken then
					pendingConfirm = false
					lbl.Text = opts.Label
					lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor")
				end
			end)
			return
		end
		pendingConfirm = false
		confirmToken += 1
		lbl.Text = opts.Label
		lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor")
		pcall(opts.Callback or opts.OnClick or function() end)
		el.Changed:Fire()
	end))

	function el:SetLabel(t) lbl.Text = t end
	return el
end

--============================================================================--
-- Gradient stops
--============================================================================--
-- Direct port of khook's stop-list module (khook.lua:92540-92646). A gradient
-- is a plain array of { Time = 0..1, Value = Color3 } kept sorted ascending by
-- Time. The two endpoints are pinned to 0 and 1; only the stops between them
-- can be moved or deleted.
--
-- The marker artwork and the vertical shading on every colour chip are khook's
-- (khook.lua:122924 and 123416-123420 -- the latter reads as a discarded
-- `table.create(1)` in the decompile, which is the decompiler losing a table
-- constructor whose two keypoints are still visible on the following lines).

local Stops = {}

Stops.MARKER_IMAGE = "rbxassetid://127264563810956"
Stops.CHIP_SHADE = ColorSequence.new({
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(211, 211, 211)),
})

function Stops.clone(list)
	local out = {}
	if type(list) == "table" then
		for _, s in ipairs(list) do
			if type(s) == "table" and typeof(s.Value) == "Color3" then
				table.insert(out, { Time = clamp(tonumber(s.Time) or 0, 0, 1), Value = s.Value })
			end
		end
	end
	return out
end

--- Sorts in place by Time. Given `needle`, returns the index it landed on --
--- that is how khook keeps the active stop selected while it is dragged past
--- its neighbours (khook.lua:92586).
function Stops.sort(list, needle)
	table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.Time < b.Time end)
	if needle ~= nil then
		for i, s in ipairs(list) do
			if s == needle then return i end
		end
	end
	return math.max(#list, 1)
end

--- Normalised list: sorted, endpoints pinned to 0 and 1. Fewer than two stops
--- is not a gradient, and khook returns an empty list there, which every
--- caller reads as "fall back to Solid" (khook.lua:92606).
function Stops.ensure(list)
	local out = Stops.clone(list)
	if #out < 2 then return {} end
	Stops.sort(out)
	out[1].Time = 0
	out[#out].Time = 1
	return out
end

function Stops.fromSequence(seq)
	local out = {}
	if typeof(seq) == "ColorSequence" then
		for _, kp in ipairs(seq.Keypoints) do
			table.insert(out, { Time = kp.Time, Value = kp.Value })
		end
	elseif typeof(seq) == "Color3" then
		out = { { Time = 0, Value = seq }, { Time = 1, Value = seq } }
	end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:92617. Roblox rejects a keypoint that steps backwards in time, so
--- each one is clamped up to its predecessor (equal times are legal and are
--- what produces a hard stop), and the 0/1 ends are padded when the list does
--- not already reach them.
function Stops.toSequence(list)
	local src = Stops.clone(list)
	if #src == 0 then return ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1)) end
	Stops.sort(src)
	local kps = {}
	local first, last = src[1], src[#src]
	if first.Time > 0 then
		table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, first.Value))
	end
	for _, s in ipairs(src) do
		local t = clamp(s.Time, 0, 1)
		if #kps > 0 then t = math.max(t, kps[#kps].Time) end
		table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(t, s.Value))
	end
	if kps[#kps].Time < 1 then
		table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, last.Value))
	end
	return ColorSequence.new(kps)
end

--============================================================================--
-- Colour animator
--============================================================================--
-- khook.lua:28510 (`SetEntry`) and 135877 (`f9367`, the ticker). An animated
-- colour is not state on the element: khook keeps ONE registry keyed by the
-- colour's config path joined with "/" (khook.lua:28511) -- that is what the
-- already-ported but until now unused `ColorAnimations.Entries` slot is for --
-- and a single per-frame walk drives every entry.
--
-- Modes are None / Rainbow / Breathing. Breathing drives the *transparency*
-- slot rather than the colour, which is why khook silently downgrades it to
-- None when the element has no transparency path (khook.lua:28514-28518).

local ColorAnimator = { entries = {}, original = {} }

--- khook.lua:72120 -- hue advances at 0.2/sec per unit speed, so a full sweep
--- takes five seconds at speed 1.
function ColorAnimator.rainbow(t)
	return Color3.fromHSV(t * 0.2 % 1, 1, 1)
end

--- khook.lua:72161 -- a half-hertz sine remapped onto [min, max].
function ColorAnimator.breathing(minV, maxV, t)
	return minV + (maxV - minV) * ((math.sin(t * 0.5 * math.pi * 2) + 1) * 0.5)
end

function ColorAnimator.revert(key, entry)
	local saved = ColorAnimator.original[key]
	if saved == nil then return end
	if saved.color ~= nil then Config.set(entry.colorPath, saved.color) end
	if saved.transparency ~= nil and entry.transparencyPath ~= nil then
		Config.set(entry.transparencyPath, saved.transparency)
	end
	ColorAnimator.original[key] = nil
end

--- khook.lua:28510 `f2161`. Re-setting the same mode only refreshes the tuning
--- so the phase is not reset mid-animation; changing mode reverts first.
function ColorAnimator.set(colorPath, transparencyPath, settings)
	local key = table.concat(colorPath, "/")
	local mode = settings.Mode or "None"
	if mode == "Breathing" and transparencyPath == nil then mode = "None" end

	local entry = ColorAnimator.entries[key]
	if mode == "None" then
		if entry ~= nil then
			ColorAnimator.entries[key] = nil
			ColorAnimator.revert(key, entry)
		end
		return
	end
	if entry ~= nil and entry.mode == mode then
		entry.speed = settings.Speed or entry.speed
		entry.transparencyPath = transparencyPath
		entry.breathingMin = settings.BreathingMin or entry.breathingMin
		entry.breathingMax = settings.BreathingMax or entry.breathingMax
		return
	end
	if entry ~= nil then ColorAnimator.revert(key, entry) end
	ColorAnimator.original[key] = {
		color = Config.get(colorPath),
		transparency = transparencyPath and Config.get(transparencyPath) or nil,
	}
	ColorAnimator.entries[key] = {
		colorPath = colorPath,
		transparencyPath = transparencyPath,
		mode = mode,
		speed = settings.Speed or 1,
		t = 0,
		breathingMin = settings.BreathingMin or 0,
		breathingMax = settings.BreathingMax or 1,
	}
end

--- khook.lua:135877 `f9367`.
function ColorAnimator.step(dt)
	for _, e in pairs(ColorAnimator.entries) do
		e.t = e.t + dt * e.speed
		if e.mode == "Rainbow" then
			-- Same rule as the picker's own write (khook.lua:148978): a slot
			-- that holds a ColorSequence keeps holding one. FilledBox.Color is
			-- both animatable and a sequence, so writing a bare Color3 here
			-- would corrupt its type sixty times a second.
			local cur = Config.get(e.colorPath)
			local rgb = ColorAnimator.rainbow(e.t)
			Config.set(e.colorPath,
				typeof(cur) == "ColorSequence" and ColorSequence.new(rgb) or rgb)
		elseif e.mode == "Breathing" and e.transparencyPath ~= nil then
			local cur = Config.get(e.transparencyPath)
			local a = ColorAnimator.breathing(e.breathingMin, e.breathingMax, e.t)
			Config.set(e.transparencyPath,
				typeof(cur) == "NumberSequence" and NumberSequence.new(a) or a)
		end
	end
end

--- Replays every saved entry after a config load.
function ColorAnimator.restore()
	local store = Config.get({ "ColorAnimations", "Entries" })
	if type(store) ~= "table" then return end
	for key, saved in pairs(store) do
		if type(saved) == "table" and type(saved.ColorPath) == "table" then
			ColorAnimator.set(saved.ColorPath, saved.TransparencyPath, saved)
		end
	end
end

--============================================================================--
-- Color picker
--============================================================================--
-- Supports Color3 and ColorSequence targets (khook binds both), plus an
-- optional alpha/transparency channel.

local function h2c(h, s, v) return Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v) end

function Section:AddColor(opts)
	local popupLvl = self._popupLevel or 0
	local row = opts.Row or newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("Color", self, row, opts)
	local function readPath(path) return Config.uiGet(path) end
	local function writePath(path, value) return Config.uiSet(path, value) end

	-- khook has three flavours of colour binding, all reaching this one
	-- element (khook.lua:77074 bindFilledGradient, 77133 bindSolidOrGradient,
	-- 76842 bindReactiveEndpoint):
	--   * plain          -- the config slot is a Color3
	--   * sequence       -- the slot is a ColorSequence, always a gradient
	--   * solid-or-gradient ("editable") -- a Color3 slot, a ColorSequence slot
	--     and a boolean deciding which is live; the panel grows a Solid/Gradient
	--     dropdown (khook.lua:122980)
	--   * endpoint       -- the slot is a ColorSequence but this element edits
	--     only one end of it, which is how the Reactive Full/Low pair works
	local seqEnd = opts.SequenceEnd                        -- "high" | "low"
	local gradEnabledPath = opts.GradientEnabledConfig
	local gradColorPath = opts.GradientColorConfig
	local dualMode = (gradEnabledPath ~= nil and gradColorPath ~= nil)
	local canGradient = (opts.Gradient == true) and seqEnd == nil
	local pureSequence = canGradient and not dualMode

	--- Live mode. Only a dual-mode picker can change it at runtime.
	local mode = (pureSequence and "Gradient")
		or (dualMode and (readPath(gradEnabledPath) and "Gradient" or "Solid"))
		or "Solid"

	local function endpointOf(seq)
		if typeof(seq) ~= "ColorSequence" then return Color3.new(1, 1, 1) end
		local kp = seq.Keypoints
		return (seqEnd == "low") and kp[1].Value or kp[#kp].Value
	end

	--- Reads whatever the element is actually pointed at right now.
	local function readValue()
		if seqEnd then return endpointOf(readPath(opts.Config or {})) end
		if mode == "Gradient" then
			local p = dualMode and gradColorPath or opts.Config
			local vv = readPath(p or {})
			return (typeof(vv) == "ColorSequence") and vv or ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
		end
		local vv = opts.Config and readPath(opts.Config) or nil
		-- khook.lua:148953 -- a solid control over a ColorSequence slot reads
		-- the first stop, which is the whole value when the stops are equal.
		if typeof(vv) == "ColorSequence" then return vv.Keypoints[1].Value end
		return (typeof(vv) == "Color3") and vv or (opts.Default or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
	end

	--- True while the bound slot is one of khook's ColorSequence slots and this
	--- element is editing it as a single colour.
	local function solidOverSequence()
		return opts.Config ~= nil and mode ~= "Gradient" and seqEnd == nil
			and typeof(readPath(opts.Config)) == "ColorSequence"
	end

	--- Writes it back to the right slot, rebuilding the sequence when this
	--- element only owns one endpoint of it.
	local function storeValue(value)
		if seqEnd then
			local seq = readPath(opts.Config or {})
			local kp = (typeof(seq) == "ColorSequence") and seq.Keypoints or nil
			local lowC = kp and kp[1].Value or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			local highC = kp and kp[#kp].Value or Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
			if seqEnd == "low" then lowC = value else highC = value end
			if opts.Config then writePath(opts.Config, ColorSequence.new(lowC, highC)) end
			return
		end
		if mode == "Gradient" then
			if dualMode then
				if gradColorPath then writePath(gradColorPath, value) end
			elseif opts.Config then
				writePath(opts.Config, value)
			end
		elseif opts.Config then
			-- khook.lua:148978 -- collapse to a one-colour sequence rather than
			-- changing the slot's type out from under the other controls bound
			-- to it (the bars' Reactive and Gradient pickers share this path).
			if typeof(readPath(opts.Config)) == "ColorSequence" then
				writePath(opts.Config, ColorSequence.new(value))
			else
				writePath(opts.Config, value)
			end
		end
	end

	local isSequence = (mode == "Gradient")
	local startValue = readValue()
	el.Value = startValue

	local baseColor = (typeof(startValue) == "ColorSequence")
		and startValue.Keypoints[1].Value or startValue
	local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(baseColor)

	--- Some transparency slots hold a NumberSequence (FilledBox.Transparency),
	--- so the alpha channel reads and writes the first keypoint there.
	local function readAlpha()
		if not opts.AlphaConfig then return 0 end
		local a = readPath(opts.AlphaConfig)
		if typeof(a) == "NumberSequence" then return a.Keypoints[1].Value end
		return tonumber(a) or 0
	end
	local function storeAlpha(a)
		if not opts.AlphaConfig then return end
		local cur = readPath(opts.AlphaConfig)
		if typeof(cur) == "NumberSequence" then
			writePath(opts.AlphaConfig, NumberSequence.new(a))
		else
			writePath(opts.AlphaConfig, a)
		end
	end
	local alpha = opts.Alpha and readAlpha() or 0

	-- Colour-animation state. khook persists it in the shared registry keyed by
	-- the colour's config path (khook.lua:28511), not on the element.
	-- khook.lua:148572 `f10182` -- a picker becomes animatable exactly when it
	-- is bound to a colour *and* a transparency slot (that is the
	-- bindColorAndTransparency pairing), and the same condition is what makes
	-- Breathing available, since Breathing has nothing to drive without one.
	local animatable = (opts.Animatable ~= false)
		and opts.Config ~= nil and opts.AlphaConfig ~= nil
	local allowBreathing = animatable
	local animKey = opts.Config and table.concat(opts.Config, "/") or nil
	local animSaved = animKey and (Config.get({ "ColorAnimations", "Entries" }) or {})[animKey] or nil
	local animMode = (animSaved and animSaved.Mode) or "None"
	local animSpeed = (animSaved and animSaved.Speed) or 1
	local animMin = (animSaved and animSaved.BreathingMin) or 0
	local animMax = (animSaved and animSaved.BreathingMax) or 1

	--- Writes the registry entry and (re)arms the ticker. khook keeps Breathing
	--- out of the list when there is no transparency slot to drive.
	local function pushAnimation()
		if not animatable or animKey == nil then return end
		local store = Config.get({ "ColorAnimations", "Entries" }) or {}
		if animMode == "None" then
			store[animKey] = nil
		else
			store[animKey] = {
				ColorPath = opts.Config,
				TransparencyPath = opts.AlphaConfig,
				Mode = animMode, Speed = animSpeed,
				BreathingMin = animMin, BreathingMax = animMax,
			}
		end
		Config.set({ "ColorAnimations", "Entries" }, store)
		ColorAnimator.set(opts.Config, opts.AlphaConfig, {
			Mode = animMode, Speed = animSpeed,
			BreathingMin = animMin, BreathingMax = animMax,
		})
	end

	-- Gradient state. khook keeps this on the picker itself rather than on the
	-- popup panel so a stop selection survives closing and reopening
	-- (khook.lua:80607 -- Stops / ActiveStopIndex / _dragMode).
	-- A dual-mode picker keeps a stop list even while it is showing a solid
	-- colour, so flipping to Gradient does not lose the gradient the user built.
	local stops = canGradient and Stops.fromSequence(
		dualMode and (readPath(gradColorPath) or ColorSequence.new(baseColor)) or startValue) or nil
	local activeStop = 1
	local dragMode = nil
	local gradientRepaint = nil   -- set while the popup is open
	local syncModeControl = nil   -- set while a dual-mode popup is open
	local syncing = false         -- guards the HSV re-derive during commit

	-- khook.lua:80742 `f5724` -- the swatch is an 18x18 chip with a 5px radius
	-- and no stroke. khookclone had it at 38x20 with an outline, which is why
	-- every colour row read as a big button instead of a swatch.
	local swatch = row:Attach(make("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
		BackgroundColor3 = baseColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}))
	corner(swatch, 5)
	local swGrad
	if canGradient then
		swGrad = make("UIGradient", {
			Color = (typeof(startValue) == "ColorSequence") and startValue or ColorSequence.new(baseColor),
			Enabled = isSequence,
			Parent = swatch,
		})
	end

	local function currentColor() return h2c(h, s, v) end

	local function render()
		if isSequence then
			swGrad.Color = el.Value
			swGrad.Enabled = true
			swatch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		else
			if swGrad then swGrad.Enabled = false end
			swatch.BackgroundColor3 = el.Value
		end
	end

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		self.Value = value
		-- A commit already holds the authoritative stop list and HSV; only an
		-- outside write (config load, another element) re-derives them.
		if not syncing then
			if typeof(value) == "ColorSequence" then
				stops = Stops.fromSequence(value)
				activeStop = clamp(activeStop, 1, math.max(#stops, 1))
				local a = stops[activeStop]
				h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(a and a.Value or value.Keypoints[1].Value)
			else
				h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(value)
			end
		end
		render()
		if not silent then
			storeValue(value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(value)
			self.Changed:Fire(value)
		end
		if isSequence and not syncing and gradientRepaint then gradientRepaint(true) end
		return self
	end

	--- khook.lua:80707 `SetMode`. Switching writes the boolean and republishes
	--- the value from whichever slot just became live.
	local function setMode(newMode, silent)
		if not dualMode or newMode == mode then return end
		mode = newMode
		isSequence = (mode == "Gradient")
		if gradEnabledPath then writePath(gradEnabledPath, isSequence) end
		local value = isSequence and Stops.toSequence(stops) or currentColor()
		syncing = true
		el:Set(value, silent)
		syncing = false
		if syncModeControl then syncModeControl(mode) end
		if gradientRepaint then gradientRepaint(true) end
	end

	--- The picker's HSV is always the *active stop's* colour, so committing
	--- writes it back into that stop before rebuilding the sequence
	--- (khook.lua:80822 -- `Stops[ActiveStopIndex].Value = rgb`).
	local function commit()
		if isSequence then
			local a = stops[activeStop]
			if a then a.Value = currentColor() end
			syncing = true
			el:Set(Stops.toSequence(stops))
			syncing = false
		else
			el:Set(currentColor())
		end
	end

	--- khook's picker popup (khook.lua:80552): a flat 300-wide panel with a
	--- 13px radius and a glow, dropped at the trigger's x+1 / y+83.
	local function openPicker()
		Popups.close(popupLvl + 1)
		local W = 300
		local H = opts.Alpha and 208 or 186
		local conns = {}
		-- forward-declared: the animation controls are built above the drag
		-- machinery but connect into it
		local dragTarget, updateDrag, syncAnimation
		local frame = make("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ElementBackground"),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H),
			ZIndex = 600,
			Parent = UI.Gui,
		})
		row.Batch:Bind(frame, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementBackground")
		corner(frame, 13)
		local st = make("UIStroke", { Parent = frame })
		row.Batch:Bind(st, "Color", "Outline")
		addGlow(row.Batch, frame, 3, 0.72, "Outline")

		local function reposition()
			local p = swatch.AbsolutePosition
			local viewport = Camera and Camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
			local inset = UI.inset()
			local x = math.min(p.X + 1, viewport.X - W - 8)
			-- read the live height: the gradient row makes the panel taller
			local y = math.min(p.Y + 83, math.max(8, viewport.Y - frame.Size.Y.Offset - 8))
			frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + inset.X, y + inset.Y)
		end
		reposition()

		-- khook's picker body (khook.lua:69300-69420): a 234px-tall SV square,
