		-- then a horizontal strip holding an 8px hue bar and an 8px alpha bar
		-- spaced 23 apart. Cursors are 12x12 (corner 7) and 16x8 (pill), both
		-- with a 2px stroke.
		local BODY_H = 234

		--- khook does not hand-roll controls inside the picker: it builds a bare
		--- Row and realizes a real Dropdown / Slider / RangeSlider into the
		--- panel (khook.lua:123465-123543). This adapter gives the popup the
		--- same menu widgets, so the panel is made of the same parts as the
		--- rest of the UI rather than look-alikes.
		local function panelSection(container)
			local sec = setmetatable({
				_trove = row.Trove:Extend(),
				_batch = row.Batch,
				_grid = self._grid,
				_root = frame,
				_body = frame,
				_elements = container,
				-- controls realized inside the panel open at level 2, so their
				-- popups stack on top of the picker instead of closing it
				_popupLevel = popupLvl + 1,
			}, Section)
			table.insert(conns, { Disconnect = function() sec._trove:Destroy() end })
			return sec
		end

		-- khook.lua:123422-123543 -- an animatable picker grows an Animation
		-- row (inline dropdown), a Speed slider (0..5 step 0.1) and, for
		-- Breathing only, a two-handle Alpha Range in transparency percent.
		-- khook stores alpha, so the range reads inverted: UI Min is
		-- floor((1 - alphaMax) * 100 + 0.5), UI Max comes from alphaMin
		-- (khook.lua:123514-123521, 148603-148609).
		local animHost, animSpeedEl, animModeEl

			animHost = make("Frame", {
				Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, ANIM_Y),
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 0),
				AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				ZIndex = 601,
				Parent = frame,
			})
			local asec = panelSection(animHost)

			-- khook.lua:122989 -- {None, Rainbow}, Breathing inserted only when
			-- there is a transparency slot for it to drive.
			local modes = { "None", "Rainbow" }
			if allowBreathing then table.insert(modes, "Breathing") end

			animModeEl = asec:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Animation",
				Options = modes,
				Default = animMode,
				MaxWidth = 100,
				OnChanged = function(v)
					animMode = v
					pushAnimation()
				end,
			})
			animSpeedEl = asec:AddSlider({
				Label = "Speed", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.1,
				Default = animSpeed,
				OnChanged = function(v) animSpeed = v pushAnimation() end,
			})
			if allowBreathing then
				local breathGroup = asec:AddGroup({ Source = animModeEl, Option = "Breathing" })
				breathGroup:AddRangeSlider({
					Label = "Alpha Range", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1,
					Default = {
						Min = math.floor((1 - animMax) * 100 + 0.5),
						Max = math.floor((1 - animMin) * 100 + 0.5),
					},
					OnChanged = function(v)
						-- back out of transparency percent into alpha
						animMax = 1 - (v.Min or 0) / 100
						animMin = 1 - (v.Max or 100) / 100
						pushAnimation()
					end,
				})
			end
			ANIM_H = 9 + ROW_HEIGHT * 2 + (allowBreathing and ROW_HEIGHT or 0)
		end

		--- khook.lua:123884 `SyncAnimation` -- mirror state back into the
		--- controls. The Breathing-only range hides itself through its group.
		function syncAnimation()
			if not animatable then return end
			if animModeEl then animModeEl:Set(animMode, true) end
			if animSpeedEl then animSpeedEl:Set(animSpeed, true) end
		end

		-- hex field sits under the body, pushed down by the gradient row
		local hexBox = make("TextBox", {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, animatable
				and (ANIM_Y + ANIM_H + 9)
				or (canGradient and (GRAD_Y + GRAD_H + 9) or (TOP + BODY_H + 8))),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22),
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Text = "#FFFFFF",
			TextSize = 13,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
			ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			ZIndex = 601,
			Parent = frame,
		})
		corner(hexBox, 4)
		row.Batch:Bind(hexBox, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
		row.Batch:Bind(hexBox, "TextColor3", "TextColor")
		frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, hexBox.Position.Y.Offset + 22 + 10)

		--- Port of khook's picker repaint (sharedHelper108). The hue tint lands
		--- instantly, but all three cursors ease to their new spot -- only an
		--- `instant` repaint (opening the panel, a config load) snaps them. The
		--- hex field is left alone while it has focus so typing is not clobbered.
		local function paint(instant)
			local c = currentColor()
			sv.BackgroundColor3 = h2c(h, 1, 1)
			local svPos = UDim2.fromScale(s, 1 - v)
			local huePos = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, h)
			local alphaPos = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, alpha)
			if instant then
				svCursor.Position = svPos
				hueCursor.Position = huePos
				if alphaCursor then alphaCursor.Position = alphaPos end
			else
				tween(svCursor, { Position = svPos }, TWEEN_DRAG)
				tween(hueCursor, { Position = huePos }, TWEEN_DRAG)
				if alphaCursor then tween(alphaCursor, { Position = alphaPos }, TWEEN_DRAG) end
			end
			if alphaFill then alphaFill.BackgroundColor3 = c end
			if not hexBox:IsFocused() then
				hexBox.Text = string.format("#%02X%02X%02X",
					math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
			end
		end
		paint(true)

		------------------------------------------------------ gradient stops ----
		-- The state operations are ports of khook's `t590` (khook.lua:80607);
		-- the repaint is `f8381`/`f8377` (khook.lua:123690-123771).
		local markers, markerConns = {}, {}
		local selectStop, moveActiveStop, rebuildMarkers, refreshMarkers

		--- Nearest stop to a viewport X, khook.lua:123548.
		local function stopAtX(x)
			local w = math.max(gradBar.AbsoluteSize.X - 1, 1)
			local t = clamp((x - gradBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / w, 0, 1)
			local best, bestD = 1, math.huge
			for i, stop in ipairs(stops) do
				local d = math.abs(stop.Time - t)
				if d < bestD then best, bestD = i, d end
			end
			return best
		end

		function refreshMarkers(instant)
			for i, m in ipairs(markers) do
				local stop = stops[i]
				if stop then
					local active = (i == activeStop)
					local pos = UDim2.new(stop.Time, -1, 0, -1)
					local size = active and UDim2.fromOffset(10, 29) or UDim2.fromOffset(8, 24)
					local trans = active and 0 or 0.1
					local aSize = active and UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 19) or UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 16)
					if instant then
						m.Marker.Position = pos
						m.Marker.Size = size
						m.Marker.ImageTransparency = trans
						m.Accent.Size = aSize
						m.Accent.BackgroundColor3 = stop.Value
					else
						tween(m.Marker, { Position = pos, Size = size, ImageTransparency = trans }, TWEEN_DRAG)
						tween(m.Accent, { Size = aSize, BackgroundColor3 = stop.Value }, TWEEN_DRAG)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		--- khook.lua:123591. Markers are torn down and rebuilt whenever the
		--- count changes; a count-preserving change only moves them.
		function rebuildMarkers()
			for _, c in ipairs(markerConns) do c:Disconnect() end
			table.clear(markerConns)
			for _, m in ipairs(markers) do m.Marker:Destroy() end
			table.clear(markers)
			for i, stop in ipairs(stops) do
				local active = (i == activeStop)
				local mk = make("ImageButton", {
					Active = true,
					AutoButtonColor = false,
					ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Outline"),
					ImageTransparency = active and 0 or 0.1,
					AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
					Image = Stops.MARKER_IMAGE,
					BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					Position = UDim2.new(stop.Time, -1, 0, -1),
					Size = active and UDim2.fromOffset(10, 29) or UDim2.fromOffset(8, 24),
					BorderSizePixel = 0,
					ZIndex = 603,
					Parent = markerLayer,
				})
				local inner = make("ImageLabel", {
					Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2),
					Image = Stops.MARKER_IMAGE,
					BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
					BorderSizePixel = 0,
					ZIndex = 604,
					Parent = mk,
				})
				local accent = make("Frame", {
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
					Size = active and UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 19) or UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 16),
					BackgroundColor3 = stop.Value,
					BorderSizePixel = 0,
					ZIndex = 605,
					Parent = inner,
				})
				make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = Stops.CHIP_SHADE, Parent = accent })
				markers[i] = { Marker = mk, Accent = accent }

				local idx = i
				table.insert(markerConns, mk.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					selectStop(idx)
				end))
				-- press-to-select, then the drag continues as a stop move
				table.insert(markerConns, mk.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
					if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
						or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						selectStop(idx)
						dragTarget = "stop"
						dragMode = "stop"
						gradientRepaint(true)
					end
				end))
			end
		end

		--- khook.lua:123743. Repaints the bar fill, the active handle and every
		--- marker; `instant` is khook's `_applyState(true)`.
		gradientRepaint = function(instant)
			if gradBar == nil then return end
			-- khook.lua:123875 SyncMode -- the whole row hides in Solid mode.
			gradRow.Visible = isSequence
			if not isSequence then return end
			gradFill.Color = Stops.toSequence(stops)
			local a = stops[activeStop]
			local col, pos = currentColor(), UDim2.new(0.5, -1, 0, -1)
			if a then
				col = a.Value
				pos = UDim2.new(a.Time, -1, 0, -1)
			end
			handleChip.BackgroundColor3 = col
			local size = (dragMode == "stop") and UDim2.fromOffset(12, 31) or UDim2.fromOffset(10, 29)
			if #markers == #stops then refreshMarkers(instant) else rebuildMarkers() end
			if instant then
				handle.Position = pos
				handle.Size = size
			else
				tween(handle, { Position = pos, Size = size }, TWEEN_DRAG)
			end
		end

		--- khook.lua:80624. Any stop can be selected, including the endpoints.
		function selectStop(i)
			if #stops == 0 then return end
			activeStop = clamp(math.floor(i), 1, #stops)
			local a = stops[activeStop]
			if a then h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(a.Value) end
			paint(true)
			gradientRepaint(true)
		end

		--- khook.lua:80641. The endpoints are pinned at 0 and 1 and refuse to
		--- move; everything between re-sorts live, so the active index follows
		--- the stop as it is dragged past its neighbours.
		function moveActiveStop(t)
			local a = stops[activeStop]
			if a == nil or activeStop == 1 or activeStop == #stops then return end
			a.Time = clamp(t, 0, 1)
			activeStop = Stops.sort(stops, a)
			commit()
			gradientRepaint(false)
		end

		--- khook.lua:80656. The new stop lands halfway to the active one's
		--- right neighbour, or to its left one when the active stop is last.
		local function addStop()
			-- Adding a stop to a solid picker promotes it to Gradient first
			-- (khook.lua:80657).
			if mode ~= "Gradient" then setMode("Gradient") end
			local a = stops[activeStop]
			local t = 0.5
			if a then
				if activeStop < #stops then
					t = (a.Time + stops[activeStop + 1].Time) * 0.5
				elseif activeStop > 1 then
					t = (stops[activeStop - 1].Time + a.Time) * 0.5
				end
			end
			local ns = { Time = t, Value = currentColor() }
			table.insert(stops, ns)
			activeStop = Stops.sort(stops, ns)
			commit()
			gradientRepaint(false)
		end

		--- khook.lua:80675. Never drops below two stops, never deletes an
		--- endpoint, and hands selection to whichever stop sat closest in time
		--- to the one that went away.
		local function removeStop()
			if #stops <= 2 then return end
			if activeStop == 1 or activeStop == #stops then return end
			local gone = stops[activeStop].Time
			table.remove(stops, activeStop)
			local best, bestD = 1, math.huge
			for i, stop in ipairs(stops) do
				local d = math.abs(stop.Time - gone)
				if d < bestD then best, bestD = i, d end
			end
			activeStop = best
			dragMode = nil
			local a = stops[activeStop]
			if a then h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(a.Value) end
			paint(false)
			commit()
			gradientRepaint(false)
		end

		function updateDrag(pos)
			-- A stop drag owns its own commit and repaint (khook.lua:123582).
			if dragTarget == "stop" then
				moveActiveStop(clamp(
					(pos.X - gradBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(gradBar.AbsoluteSize.X - 1, 1), 0, 1))
				return
			end
			if dragTarget == "sv" then
				s = clamp((pos.X - sv.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
				v = 1 - clamp((pos.Y - sv.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.Y), 0, 1)
			elseif dragTarget == "hue" then
				h = clamp((pos.Y - hue.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, hue.AbsoluteSize.Y), 0, 1)
			elseif dragTarget == "alpha" and alphaTrack then
				alpha = clamp((pos.Y - alphaTrack.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, alphaTrack.AbsoluteSize.Y), 0, 1)
				storeAlpha(alpha)
			end
			paint(false)
			commit()
			-- An HSV edit lands on the active stop, so the bar has to follow.
			if canGradient then gradientRepaint(true) end
		end

		local function hookDrag(inst, name)
			table.insert(conns, inst.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
					or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					dragTarget = name
					updateDrag(input.Position)
				end
			end))
		end
		hookDrag(sv, "sv")
		hookDrag(hue, "hue")
		if alphaTrack then hookDrag(alphaTrack, "alpha") end

		if canGradient then
			-- Pressing the bar selects the nearest stop and then drags it
			-- (khook.lua:123839-123858); the handle drags the active one
			-- straight away, without re-selecting.
			table.insert(conns, gradBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
					or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					selectStop(stopAtX(input.Position.X))
					dragTarget = "stop"
					dragMode = "stop"
					gradientRepaint(true)
				end
			end))
			table.insert(conns, handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
					or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					dragTarget = "stop"
					dragMode = "stop"
					gradientRepaint(true)
				end
			end))
			table.insert(conns, addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(addStop))
			table.insert(conns, delBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(removeStop))
			rebuildMarkers()
			gradientRepaint(true)
		end

		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if dragTarget and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				updateDrag(input.Position)
			end
		end))
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				local wasStop = (dragTarget == "stop")
				dragTarget = nil
				if wasStop then
					dragMode = nil
					gradientRepaint(true)
				end
			end
		end))
		table.insert(conns, hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
			local hex = string.gsub(hexBox.Text, "#", "")
			if #hex == 6 then
				local ok, c = pcall(Color3.fromHex, hex)
				if ok then h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(c) paint(true) commit() end
			end
		end))
		table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(reposition))
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
			task.defer(function()
				if dragTarget then return end
				local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				local p, sz = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
				local inFrame = m.X >= p.X and m.X <= p.X + sz.X and m.Y >= p.Y and m.Y <= p.Y + sz.Y
				local sp, ss = swatch.AbsolutePosition, swatch.AbsoluteSize
				local inSwatch = m.X >= sp.X and m.X <= sp.X + ss.X and m.Y >= sp.Y and m.Y <= sp.Y + ss.Y
				if not inFrame and not inSwatch then Popups.close() end
			end)
		end))

		Popups.push(popupLvl, function()
			Popups.close(popupLvl + 2)
			for _, c in ipairs(conns) do c:Disconnect() end
			for _, c in ipairs(markerConns) do c:Disconnect() end
			gradientRepaint = nil
			syncModeControl = nil
			dragMode = nil
			frame:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	row.Trove:Add(swatch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if Popups.openAt(popupLvl) then Popups.close(popupLvl + 1) else openPicker() end
	end))

	-- A plain picker owns its config slot outright, so the generic watcher is
	-- fine. The endpoint and solid-or-gradient bindings read across two or
	-- three slots, so they re-derive through readValue() instead -- pushing the
	-- raw slot value straight in would write a ColorSequence where a Color3
	-- belongs (or the reverse) the first time a config is loaded.
	if seqEnd or dualMode or solidOverSequence() then
		local function resync()
			if dualMode and gradEnabledPath then
				mode = readPath(gradEnabledPath) and "Gradient" or "Solid"
				isSequence = (mode == "Gradient")
				if syncModeControl then syncModeControl(mode) end
			end
			el:Set(readValue(), true)
			if gradientRepaint then gradientRepaint(true) end
		end
		local watched = {}
		local function watchPath(path)
			if path == nil then return end
			watched[#watched + 1] = path
			el._trove:Add(Config.watch(path, resync, true))
		end
		watchPath(opts.Config)
		watchPath(gradColorPath)
		watchPath(gradEnabledPath)
		el._trove:Connect(Config.changed, function(changed)
			for _, path in ipairs(watched) do
				if changed == pathKey(Config.resolveEditPath(path, false)) then
					resync()
					break
				end
			end
		end)
		el._trove:Connect(Config.editProfileChanged, function(feature)
			if opts.Config and opts.Config[1] == feature then resync() end
		end)
	else
		bindConfig(el, function(nv) el:Set(nv, true) end)
		if el._configPath then Config.rawSet(el._configPath, el.Value) end
	end
	if opts.AlphaConfig then
		local function resyncAlpha() alpha = readAlpha() end
		el._trove:Add(Config.watch(opts.AlphaConfig, resyncAlpha, true))
		el._trove:Connect(Config.changed, function(changed)
			if changed == pathKey(Config.resolveEditPath(opts.AlphaConfig, false)) then
				resyncAlpha()
			end
		end)
		el._trove:Connect(Config.editProfileChanged, function(feature)
			if opts.AlphaConfig[1] == feature then resyncAlpha() end
		end)
	end
	render()
	return el
end

--============================================================================--
-- Keybind
--============================================================================--
-- Attaches to an existing row (khook passes `Row = toggle.Row`) or makes its
-- own. Modes: Always / Toggle / Hold / Tap / Off.

local KeybindRegistry = {
	entries = {},            -- flag -> entry
	changed = Signal.new(),
}
KeybindRegistry.MODES = { "Always", "Toggle", "Hold", "Tap", "Off" }

local function keyName(key)
	if key == nil then return "None" end
	if typeof(key) == "EnumItem" then
		local n = key.Name
		n = string.gsub(n, "^Mouse", "M")
		n = string.gsub(n, "Button", "B")
		return n
	end
	return tostring(key)
end

function Section:AddKeybind(opts)
	local popupLvl = self._popupLevel or 0
	local row = opts.Row or newRow(self, opts)
	local el = newElement("Keybind", self, row, opts)
	local cfg = opts.ConfigMap or {}

	local default = opts.Default or {}
	local storedKey = cfg.Key and Config.get(cfg.Key) or nil
	el.Key  = storedKey or (default.Key ~= "None" and default.Key) or nil
	el.Mode = (cfg.Mode and Config.get(cfg.Mode)) or default.Mode or "Always"
	el.State = false
	el.Enabled = true
	el.Value = el.Key
	el.ListLabel = opts.ListLabel or (row.Label and row.Label.Text) or "Bind"
	el.InKeybindList = opts.InKeybindList ~= false
	el.ShowInList = cfg.ShowInList and Config.get(cfg.ShowInList) ~= false or true
	el.Invisible = cfg.Invisible and Config.get(cfg.Invisible) == true or false

	-- chip sits left of whatever control already owns the right edge
	-- khook does NOT put a text chip on the row (khook.lua:130753). The row
	-- carries a 16x16 gear; clicking it opens a panel holding the key capture,
	-- "Show In List", "Invisible" (touch only) and the Mode dropdown -- which
	-- is exactly the four fields the Keybind config block stores.
	local chip = row:Attach(make("ImageButton", {
		Image = "rbxassetid://6031280882",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
	}), true)
	row.Batch:Bind(chip, "ImageColor3", "TextColor")

	local capturing = false
	local panelRefs = {}

	local function render()
		chip.ImageTransparency = el.Enabled and 0 or 0.55
		local keyLabel = panelRefs.keyLabel
		if keyLabel then
			keyLabel.Text = capturing and "..." or keyName(el.Key)
			keyLabel.TextColor3 = el.Key ~= nil
				and Theme.get("TextColor") or Theme.get("Unselected")
		end
	end

	local function register()
		if not el.InKeybindList then return end
		KeybindRegistry.entries[el] = el
		KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire()
	end

	function el:SetKey(key, silent)
		self.Key = key
		self.Value = key
		if cfg.Key then Config.set(cfg.Key, key, silent) end
		render()
		register()
		if not silent then self.ValueChanged:Fire(key) end
	end

	function el:SetMode(mode, silent)
		self.Mode = mode
		if cfg.Mode then Config.set(cfg.Mode, mode, silent) end
		register()
	end

	--- The owning toggle drives this: a keybind on a disabled feature is inert.
	function el:SetFeatureEnabled(on)
		self.Enabled = on and true or false
		render()
		register()
	end

	function el:IsActive()
		if not self.Enabled then return false end
		if self.Mode == "Off" then return false end
		if self.Key == nil then return self.Mode == "Always" end
		if self.Mode == "Always" then return true end
		if self.Mode == "Hold" then return self.State end
		return self.State
	end

	--- Capture the next key press. Backspace clears the bind.
	local function captureKey()
		if capturing then return end
		capturing = true
		render()
		local conn
		conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
			if gpe then return end
			local key
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				key = input.KeyCode
			elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then
				key = input.UserInputType
			else
				return
			end
			conn:Disconnect()
			capturing = false
			el:SetKey(key == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace and nil or key)
		end)
		row.Trove:Add(conn)
	end

	--- The gear's panel: Key / Show In List / Invisible / Mode.
	local function openPanel()
		Popups.close(popupLvl + 1)
		local frame, scroll, reposition = buildPopup(chip, 0, row.Batch)
		frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 0)
		make("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6),
			PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8), Parent = scroll })

		local function panelRow(height)
			local f = make("Frame", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				ZIndex = 502,
				Parent = scroll,
			})
			return f
		end
		local function panelLabel(parent, text)
			local l = make("TextLabel", {
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
				Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
				AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Text = text,
				TextSize = 14,
				FontFace = Fonts.Main,
				TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
				ZIndex = 503,
				Parent = parent,
			})
			return l
		end
		--- Compact right-aligned pill used by the panel's three sub-controls.
		local function panelPill(parent, width, height)
			local b = make("TextButton", {
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
				Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.5),
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height),
				BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Text = "",
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				ZIndex = 503,
				Parent = parent,
			})
			corner(b, 5)
			make("UIStroke", { Color = Theme.get("Outline"), Parent = b })
			return b
		end

		-- Key
		local keyRow = panelRow(24)
		panelLabel(keyRow, "Key")
		local keyBtn = panelPill(keyRow, 66, 20)
		local keyLabel = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
			Text = capturing and "..." or keyName(el.Key),
			TextSize = 13,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			ZIndex = 504,
			Parent = keyBtn,
		})
		panelRefs.keyLabel = keyLabel
		keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(captureKey)

		-- Mode
		local modeRow = panelRow(24)
		panelLabel(modeRow, "Mode")
		local modeBtn = panelPill(modeRow, 74, 20)
		local modeLabel = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
			Text = el.Mode,
			TextSize = 13,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
			ZIndex = 504,
			Parent = modeBtn,
		})
		modeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local modes = opts.Modes or KeybindRegistry.MODES
			local i = table.find(modes, el.Mode) or 1
			el:SetMode(modes[(i % #modes) + 1])
			modeLabel.Text = el.Mode
		end)

		--- Small checkbox for the two boolean panel options.
		local function panelToggle(text, height, get, set)
			local r = panelRow(height)
			panelLabel(r, text)
			local box = panelPill(r, 18, 18)
			local tick = make("ImageLabel", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
				Image = "rbxassetid://6031094667",
				ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
				Visible = get(),
				ZIndex = 504,
				Parent = box,
			})
			box.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				set(not get())
				tick.Visible = get()
			end)
		end

		panelToggle("Show In List", 24,
			function() return el.ShowInList end,
			function(v)
				el.ShowInList = v
				if cfg.ShowInList then Config.set(cfg.ShowInList, v) end
				KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire()
			end)

		-- khook only offers Invisible on touch devices
		if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			panelToggle("Invisible", 20,
				function() return el.Invisible end,
				function(v)
					el.Invisible = v
					if cfg.Invisible then Config.set(cfg.Invisible, v) end
					KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire()
				end)
		end

		local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(reposition)
		local outside = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
				and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
			task.defer(function()
				if capturing then return end
				local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				local p, s = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
				local inFrame = m.X >= p.X and m.X <= p.X + s.X and m.Y >= p.Y and m.Y <= p.Y + s.Y
				local cp, cs = chip.AbsolutePosition, chip.AbsoluteSize
				local inChip = m.X >= cp.X and m.X <= cp.X + cs.X and m.Y >= cp.Y and m.Y <= cp.Y + cs.Y
				if not inFrame and not inChip then Popups.close() end
			end)
		end)

		Popups.push(popupLvl, function()
			conn:Disconnect()
			outside:Disconnect()
			panelRefs.keyLabel = nil
			frame:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	row.Trove:Add(chip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if Popups.openAt(popupLvl) then Popups.close(popupLvl + 1) else openPanel() end
	end))

	bindTooltip(row.Trove, chip, "Keybind settings")

	render()
	register()
	row.Trove:Add(function()
		KeybindRegistry.entries[el] = nil
		KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire()
	end)
	return el
end

--- Keyboard/mouse dispatch for every registered bind.
local function startKeybindDispatch(trove)
	local function matches(el, input)
		if el.Key == nil then return false end
		if typeof(el.Key) == "EnumItem" and el.Key.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then
			return input.KeyCode == el.Key
		end
		return input.UserInputType == el.Key
	end

	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
		if gpe then return end
		for el in pairs(KeybindRegistry.entries) do
			if el.Enabled and matches(el, input) then
				if el.Mode == "Toggle" then
					el.State = not el.State
				elseif el.Mode == "Hold" then
					el.State = true
				elseif el.Mode == "Tap" then
					el.State = true
					task.delay(0.05, function() el.State = false end)
				end
				el.ValueChanged:Fire(el.Key)
				KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire()
			end
		end
	end))

	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		for el in pairs(KeybindRegistry.entries) do
			if matches(el, input) and el.Mode == "Hold" then
				el.State = false
				KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire()
			end
		end
	end))
end

--============================================================================--
-- List (read-only rows, used by the mod/hacker detectors and config browser)
--============================================================================--

--- khook.lua:18801 / 43972 `AddOrderedList`. Holds an ordered array and lets
--- the user re-rank it -- the ragebot's weapon priority is the only user.
--- khook's own widget reorders by dragging; the decompiled body is not
--- recoverable, so this exposes the same ordering through up/down controls.
function Section:AddOrderedList(opts)
	local items = opts.Items or {}
	local row = newRow(self, { Height = opts.Height or (#items * 26 + 8) })
	row.Frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
	local el = newElement("OrderedList", self, row, opts)

	local start = initialValue(opts, opts.Default or table.clone(items))
	el.Value = (typeof(start) == "table" and #start > 0) and table.clone(start) or table.clone(items)

	-- drop anything unknown, append anything missing: a saved config must not
	-- be able to hide a weapon class or resurrect a removed one
	do
		local seen, cleaned = {}, {}
		for _, v in ipairs(el.Value) do
			if table.find(items, v) and not seen[v] then seen[v] = true table.insert(cleaned, v) end
		end
		for _, v in ipairs(items) do
			if not seen[v] then table.insert(cleaned, v) end
		end
		el.Value = cleaned
	end

	local holder = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = row.Frame,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(holder, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(holder, 5)
	local hs = make("UIStroke", { Parent = holder })
	row.Batch:Bind(hs, "Color", "Outline")
	local list = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	vlist(list, 2)

	local render
	local function move(index, delta)
		local target = index + delta
		if target < 1 or target > #el.Value then return end
		el.Value[index], el.Value[target] = el.Value[target], el.Value[index]
		el:Set(el.Value)
		render()
	end

	function render()
		for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("GuiObject") then c:Destroy() end
		end
		for i, item in ipairs(el.Value) do
			local entry = make("Frame", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				LayoutOrder = i,
				Parent = list,
			})
			make("TextLabel", {
				FontFace = Fonts.Main,
				Text = i .. ".  " .. tostring(item),
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				TextSize = 14,
				TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
				Parent = entry,
			})
			local function arrow(text, offset, delta, enabled)
				local b = make("TextButton", {
					AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
					Position = UDim2.new(1, offset, 0.5, 0),
					Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
					BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					BorderSizePixel = 0,
					AutoButtonColor = false,
					FontFace = Fonts.Main,
					Text = text,
					TextSize = 14,
					TextColor3 = enabled and Theme.get("TextColor") or Theme.get("Unselected"),
					Parent = entry,
				})
				if enabled then
					row.Trove:Add(b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() move(i, delta) end))
				end
			end
			arrow("\u{25B2}", -22, -1, i > 1)
			arrow("\u{25BC}", -2, 1, i < #el.Value)
		end
	end

	function el:Set(value, silent)
		self.Value = value or {}
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, self.Value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, self.Value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(self.Value)
			self.Changed:Fire(self.Value)
		end
		return self
	end

	bindConfig(el, function(v)
		if typeof(v) == "table" then el.Value = table.clone(v) render() end
	end)
	render()
	if el._configPath then Config.rawSet(el._configPath, el.Value) end
	return el
end

function Section:AddList(opts)
	local row = newRow(self, { Height = opts.Height or 120 })
	row.Frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
	local el = newElement("List", self, row, opts)

	local holder = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = row.Frame,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(holder, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(holder, 5)
	local st = make("UIStroke", { Parent = holder })
	row.Batch:Bind(st, "Color", "Outline")

	local scroll = make("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8),
		ScrollBarThickness = 2,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(scroll, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	vlist(scroll, 2)

	el.Selected = nil

	function el:SetItems(items)
		for _, c in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("GuiObject") then c:Destroy() end
		end
		for i, item in ipairs(items) do
			local o, lbl = popupOption(scroll, row.Batch, tostring(item), i)
			o.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				el.Selected = item
				el.Value = item
				el.ValueChanged:Fire(item)
				pcall(el._onChanged, item)
				for _, c in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do
					if c:IsA("TextButton") then
						c.TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected")
					end
				end
				lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.get("Accent")
			end)
		end
	end

	el:SetItems(opts.Items or {})
	return el
end

-- (AddOrderedList used to alias AddList here. It is a real ranked list now --
-- see above -- and the alias silently overwrote it.)

--============================================================================--
-- Skin changer grid
--============================================================================--
-- The cosmetics pages are a card grid, not a list: each entry is a tile with
-- a thumbnail and a name, filtered live by the section's Search box
-- (khook.lua:78735 AddSkinChanger + SetFilter).

local CARD_W, CARD_H = 104, 116

function Section:AddSkinChanger(opts)
	local row = newRow(self, { Height = opts.Height or 300 })
	row.Frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
	local el = newElement("SkinChanger", self, row, opts)
	el.Value = "Unselected"
	el.Items = {}

	local holder = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ToggleBackgroundUnselected"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = row.Frame,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(holder, "BackgroundColor3", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected")
	corner(holder, 6)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Parent = holder })
	row.Batch:Bind(stroke, "Color", "Outline")

	local scroll = make("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16),
		ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(scroll, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	make("UIGridLayout", {
		CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(CARD_W, CARD_H),
		CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Parent = scroll,
	})

	local empty = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Text = "No items",
		TextSize = 14,
		FontFace = Fonts.Main,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
		TextWrapped = true,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	row.Batch:Bind(empty, "TextColor3", "Unselected")

	local cards = {}
	local filter = ""

	local function paintSelection()
		for _, card in ipairs(cards) do
			local on = card.name == el.Value
			tween(card.stroke, {
				Color = on and Theme.get("Accent") or Theme.get("Outline"),
				Thickness = on and 2 or 1,
			}, TWEEN_FAST)
			tween(card.label, {
				TextColor3 = on and Theme.get("TextColor") or Theme.get("Unselected"),
			}, TWEEN_FAST)
		end
	end

	local function applyFilter()
		local needle = string.lower(filter)
		local shown = 0
		for _, card in ipairs(cards) do
			local visible = card.special or needle == ""
				or string.find(string.lower(card.name), needle, 1, true) ~= nil
			card.frame.Visible = visible
			if visible then shown += 1 end
		end
		empty.Visible = shown == 0
		empty.Text = #cards == 0 and "No items" or "No match"
	end

	function el:Set(name, silent)
		self.Value = name or "Unselected"
		paintSelection()
		if not silent then
			writeConfig(self, self.Value)
			pcall(self._onChanged, self.Value)
			self.ValueChanged:Fire(self.Value)
			self.Changed:Fire(self.Value)
		end
		return self
	end

	function el:SetFilter(text)
		filter = text or ""
		applyFilter()
		return self
	end

	--- items: array of strings, or of { name/Name =, image/Image =, special = }.
	--- khook's three action cards (Unselected, None, Random) are marked special
	--- so they remain visible while a search filter is active.
	function el:SetItems(items)
		for _, card in ipairs(cards) do card.frame:Destroy() end
		table.clear(cards)

		for i, item in ipairs(items or {}) do
			local name = typeof(item) == "table" and (item.name or item.Name) or tostring(item)
			local image = typeof(item) == "table" and (item.image or item.Image) or nil
			local special = typeof(item) == "table" and item.special == true

			local frame = make("TextButton", {
				BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ElementBackground"),
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Text = "",
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				LayoutOrder = i,
				Parent = scroll,
			})
			row.Batch:Bind(frame, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementBackground")
			corner(frame, 6)
			local cardStroke = make("UIStroke", { Color = Theme.get("Outline"), Parent = frame })

			make("ImageLabel", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
				Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8),
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CARD_W - 24, CARD_W - 24),
				Image = image or "rbxassetid://108815781932261",
				ImageColor3 = image and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Theme.get("Unselected"),
				ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
				Parent = frame,
			})

			local label = make("TextLabel", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
				Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -6),
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 26),
				Text = name,
				TextSize = 12,
				FontFace = Fonts.Main,
				TextColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
				TextWrapped = true,
				TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
				Parent = frame,
			})

			connectHover(row.Trove, frame,
				function()
					if el.Value ~= name then
						tween(cardStroke, { Color = shade(Theme.get("Accent"), 0.7) }, TWEEN_FAST)
					end
				end,
				function()
					if el.Value ~= name then
						tween(cardStroke, { Color = Theme.get("Outline") }, TWEEN_FAST)
					end
				end)

			row.Trove:Add(frame.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				el:Set(el.Value == name and "Unselected" or name)
			end))

			table.insert(cards, {
				frame = frame, stroke = cardStroke, label = label,
				name = name, special = special,
			})
		end

		el.Items = items or {}
		applyFilter()
		paintSelection()
		return el
	end

	el:SetItems(opts.Items or {})
	return el
end

--- "Gear": a small settings cog on a row that opens a sub-panel of extra
--- options. khook uses it to hide advanced per-element settings.
function Section:AddGear(opts)
	local row = opts.Row
	local btn = row:Attach(make("ImageButton", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Image = "rbxassetid://6031280882",
		ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Unselected"),
	}), true)
	row.Batch:Bind(btn, "ImageColor3", "Unselected")

	local sub = self:AddGroup({})
	sub._host.Visible = false
	row.Trove:Add(btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		sub._host.Visible = not sub._host.Visible
		tween(btn, { ImageColor3 = sub._host.Visible
			and Theme.get("Accent") or Theme.get("Unselected") }, TWEEN_FAST)
	end))
	return sub
end

--- Convenience used all over the menu build: a toggle plus its keybind chip.
function Section:AddFeature(opts)
	local toggle = self:AddToggle({
		Label = opts.Label,
		Tooltip = opts.Tooltip,
		Config = opts.Config,
		Default = opts.Default,
		OnChanged = opts.OnChanged,
	})
	local bind
	if opts.Keybind then
		bind = self:AddKeybind({
			Row = toggle.Row,
			ListLabel = opts.ListLabel or opts.Label,
			InKeybindList = true,
			Default = { Key = "None", Mode = opts.KeybindMode or "Always" },
			ConfigMap = opts.Keybind,
		})
		bind:SetFeatureEnabled(toggle.Value == true)
		toggle:Connect(toggle.ValueChanged, function(v) bind:SetFeatureEnabled(v == true) end)
	end
	return toggle, bind
end

-- 6. OVERLAYS -- notifications, keybind list, watermark

local Overlay = {}
Overlay.Trove = Trove.new("overlay")

local function overlayGui()
	if Overlay.Gui and Overlay.Gui.Parent then return Overlay.Gui end
	local gui = make("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "\0khookclone_overlay\0",
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		DisplayOrder = 2 ^ 30 - 1,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
	})
	pcall(protectgui, gui)
	gui.Parent = gethui()
	Overlay.Trove:Add(gui)
	Overlay.Gui = gui
	return gui
end

local CORNER_ANCHORS = {
	TopLeft     = { anchor = Vector2.new(0, 0), pos = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),   dir = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,    hx = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left },
	TopRight    = { anchor = Vector2.new(1, 0), pos = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 16),   dir = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,    hx = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right },
	BottomLeft  = { anchor = Vector2.new(0, 1), pos = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -16),   dir = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom, hx = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left },
	BottomRight = { anchor = Vector2.new(1, 1), pos = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16),  dir = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom, hx = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right },
}

------------------------------------------------------------- notifications --

local notifyHolder
local function ensureNotifyHolder()
	if notifyHolder and notifyHolder.Parent then return notifyHolder end
	local side = Config.get({ "Notifications", "Side" }) or "TopLeft"
	local a = CORNER_ANCHORS[side] or CORNER_ANCHORS.TopLeft
	notifyHolder = make("Frame", {
		Name = "Notifications",
		AnchorPoint = a.anchor,
		Position = a.pos,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Parent = overlayGui(),
	})
	make("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		VerticalAlignment = a.dir,
		HorizontalAlignment = a.hx,
		Parent = notifyHolder,
	})
	return notifyHolder
end

Config.watch({ "Notifications", "Side" }, function()
	if notifyHolder then notifyHolder:Destroy() notifyHolder = nil end
end, true)

local notifyOrder = 0

--- Assigned to the forward-declared `Notify` so earlier sections can call it.
function Notify(text, duration, accent)
	if Config.get({ "Notifications", "Enabled" }) == false then return end
	local ok = pcall(function()
		local holder = ensureNotifyHolder()
		notifyOrder += 1

		local size = Config.get({ "Notifications", "Size" }) or 15
		local card = make("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("ElementBackground"),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			LayoutOrder = notifyOrder,
			Parent = holder,
		})
		corner(card, 6)
		local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Color = Theme.get("Outline"), Transparency = 1, Parent = card })
		local bar = make("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = accent or Theme.get("Accent"),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -12),
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 6),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Parent = card,
		})
		corner(bar, 2)
		local label = make("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 8),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			Text = tostring(text),
			TextSize = size,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get("TextColor"),
			TextTransparency = 1,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextWrapped = true,
			Parent = card,
		})
		make("UIPadding", { PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8), Parent = card })

		tween(card, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.05 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
		tween(stroke, { Transparency = 0 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
		tween(bar, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
		tween(label, { TextTransparency = 0 }, TWEEN_MAIN)

		task.delay(duration or 3, function()
			tween(card, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
			tween(stroke, { Transparency = 1 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
			tween(bar, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
			local t = tween(label, { TextTransparency = 1 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
			t.Completed:Once(function() card:Destroy() end)
		end)
	end)
	if not ok then warn("[khookclone] notify failed: " .. tostring(text)) end
end

------------------------------------------------------------- keybind list --

local KeybindList = { Enabled = true }

function KeybindList.init(trove)
	local frame = make("Frame", {
		Name = "Keybinds",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -16),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Background"),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = overlayGui(),
	})
	corner(frame, 6)
	make("UIStroke", { Color = Theme.get("Outline"), Parent = frame })
	padding(frame, 8, 10, 8, 10)
	vlist(frame, 4)

	local header = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
		Text = "Keybinds",
		TextSize = 14,
		FontFace = Fonts.Bold,
		TextColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		LayoutOrder = 0,
		Parent = frame,
	})
	trove:Connect(Theme.AccentChanged, function(c) header.TextColor3 = c end)

	local rows = {}

	local function refresh()
		local wanted = {}
		for el in pairs(KeybindRegistry.entries) do
			if el.InKeybindList and el.ShowInList and not el.Invisible and el.Key ~= nil then
				table.insert(wanted, el)
			end
		end
		table.sort(wanted, function(a, b) return a.ListLabel < b.ListLabel end)

		for _, r in pairs(rows) do r:Destroy() end
		table.clear(rows)

		for i, el in ipairs(wanted) do
			local row = make("Frame", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
				LayoutOrder = i,
				Parent = frame,
			})
			make("TextLabel", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0),
				Text = el.ListLabel,
				TextSize = 13,
				FontFace = Fonts.Main,
				TextColor3 = el:IsActive() and Theme.get("TextColor") or Theme.get("Unselected"),
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
				Parent = row,
			})
			make("TextLabel", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
				Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
				Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 16),
				Text = ("[%s] %s"):format(keyName(el.Key), string.sub(el.Mode, 1, 1)),
				TextSize = 12,
				FontFace = Fonts.Main,
				TextColor3 = el:IsActive() and Theme.get("Accent") or Theme.get("Unselected"),
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
				Parent = row,
			})
			table.insert(rows, row)
		end
		frame.Visible = KeybindList.Enabled and #wanted > 0
	end

	trove:Connect(KeybindRegistry.changed, function() task.defer(refresh) end)
	-- keep the active/inactive colouring live for Hold binds
	trove:Add(task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(0.15) do
			if frame.Parent == nil then break end
			if frame.Visible then refresh() end
		end
	end))
	KeybindList.refresh = refresh
	KeybindList.Frame = frame
	refresh()
end

--------------------------------------------------------------- watermark ---

local Watermark = {}

--- khook.lua:75138 `t127.new`, part builders at 1765-1800 (makeInstance7/8/9).
--- The watermark is a draggable pill at (50,50) -- not a corner anchor -- laid
--- out as eight list items:
---   [logo] khook.lua | [person] Username | [gauge] 60 FPS
--- with the icons tinted Accent, the text TextColor and the "|" Unselected.
function Watermark.init(trove)
	local PERSON_ICON = "rbxassetid://83187029320661"
	local FPS_ICON    = "rbxassetid://113103422688495"

	local frame = make("Frame", {
		Name = "Watermark",
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.04,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 50),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		BackgroundColor3 = Theme.get("Background"),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Active = true,
		Parent = overlayGui(),
	})
	corner(frame, 6)
	local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1, Color = Theme.get("Outline"), Parent = frame })
	trove:Connect(Theme.Changed, function(key, colour)
		if key == "Background" then frame.BackgroundColor3 = colour end
		if key == "Outline" then stroke.Color = colour end
	end)
	make("UIPadding", {
		PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 7),
		PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 11), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 13),
		Parent = frame,
	})
	make("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 10),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
		Parent = frame,
	})

	--- khook.lua:1800 `makeInstance9`
	local function icon(order, image)
		local img = make("ImageLabel", {
			LayoutOrder = order,
			Image = image,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ImageColor3 = Theme.get("Accent"),
			Parent = frame,
		})
		trove:Connect(Theme.AccentChanged, function(c) img.ImageColor3 = c end)
		return img
	end

	--- khook.lua:1783 `makeInstance8` / 1765 `makeInstance7`
	local function text(order, str, key)
		local lbl = make("TextLabel", {
			LayoutOrder = order,
			FontFace = Fonts.Main,
			Text = str,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
			TextSize = 16,
			TextColor3 = Theme.get(key),
			Parent = frame,
		})
		make("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 1), Parent = lbl })
		trove:Connect(Theme.Changed, function(k, colour)
			if k == key then lbl.TextColor3 = colour end
		end)
		return lbl
	end

	icon(1, Icons.Logo)
	-- khook.lua:248485-248488 -- runtime title passed into Menu.new and then
	-- reused verbatim by the watermark chrome.
	text(2, "Vexx V2 | Rivals | Public Beta Build", "TextColor")
	text(3, "|", "Unselected")
	icon(4, PERSON_ICON)
	text(5, LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name or "?", "TextColor")
	text(6, "|", "Unselected")
	icon(7, FPS_ICON)
	local fpsLabel = text(8, "60 FPS", "TextColor")
	fpsLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	fpsLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 0)

	-- khook.lua:75191 -- the watermark is draggable and remembers where it was
	local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
	trove:Add(frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			dragStart = input.Position
			startPos = frame.Position
		end
	end))
	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local delta = input.Position - dragStart
			frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
				startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
		end
	end))
	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = false
		end
	end))

	local frames, acc = 0, 0
	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		frames += 1
		acc += dt
		if acc >= 0.5 then
			-- khook.lua:75200 -- "%s FPS"
			fpsLabel.Text = ("%d FPS"):format(math.floor(frames / acc + 0.5))
			frames, acc = 0, 0
		end
	end))

	Config.watch({ "Watermark", "Enabled" }, function(v) frame.Visible = v ~= false end)
	-- khook drags rather than docking, but khookclone's menu exposes a Side
	-- control, so honour it as a jump-to-corner.
	Config.watch({ "Watermark", "Side" }, function(side)
		local a = CORNER_ANCHORS[side or "TopRight"]
		if a == nil then return end
		frame.AnchorPoint = a.anchor
		frame.Position = a.pos
	end, true)
	Watermark.Frame = frame
end
--============================================================================--
-- 6b. RESOLVER -- finding the game's own tables
--============================================================================--
-- khook does not hardcode Rivals' internals. It sweeps the garbage collector
-- for tables/functions matching a signature, caches the hit, then patches it
-- with debug.setupvalue. (khook.lua:389, 20875, 103486 -- the error tags
-- "prototype_lookup" / "function_lookup" / "upvalue_scan" name each stage.)
-- This reproduces that mechanism generically.

local Resolver = {}
Resolver.cache = {}
Resolver.ready = Signal.new()
Resolver.Trove = Trove.new("resolver")

local getgc = getgc
local getrawmetatable = getrawmetatable or debug.getmetatable
local dbg = debug

local function hasKeys(t, keys)
	for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
		if rawget(t, k) == nil then return false end
	end
	return true
end

--- Registered signatures: name -> { kind = "table"|"function", match = fn }
Resolver.signatures = {}

function Resolver.register(name, kind, match)
	Resolver.signatures[name] = { kind = kind, match = match }
end

--- One sweep. Returns how many new handles were resolved.
function Resolver.sweep()
	if not getgc then return 0 end
	local wanted = {}
	local count = 0
	for name, sig in pairs(Resolver.signatures) do
		if Resolver.cache[name] == nil then
			wanted[name] = sig
			count += 1
		end
	end
	if count == 0 then return 0 end

	local found = 0
	local ok = pcall(function()
		for _, obj in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
			local t = type(obj)
			for name, sig in pairs(wanted) do
				if sig.kind == t then
					local hit
					local matched = pcall(function() hit = sig.match(obj) end)
					if matched and hit then
						Resolver.cache[name] = (hit ~= true) and hit or obj
						wanted[name] = nil
						found += 1
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	if not ok then return found end
	if found > 0 then Resolver.ready:Fire(found) end
	return found
end

function Resolver.get(name)
	return Resolver.cache[name]
end

--- Wait (bounded) for a handle; features call this once at startup rather
--- than polling, so a missing handle costs nothing per frame.
function Resolver.await(name, timeout)
	local deadline = os.clock() + (timeout or 8)
	while Resolver.cache[name] == nil and os.clock() < deadline do
		Resolver.sweep()
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
	return Resolver.cache[name]
end

--- Locate an upvalue of `fn` by predicate; returns index, value.
function Resolver.findUpvalue(fn, predicate)
	if not dbg.getupvalues then return nil end
	local ok, ups = pcall(dbg.getupvalues, fn)
	if not ok or not ups then return nil end
	for i, v in pairs(ups) do
		local hit
		if pcall(function() hit = predicate(v, i) end) and hit then
			return i, v
		end
	end
	return nil
end

--- Replace an upvalue and hand back a restore closure.
function Resolver.patchUpvalue(fn, index, value)
	if not dbg.setupvalue then return nil end
	local ok, old = pcall(dbg.getupvalues, fn)
	local previous = ok and old and old[index] or nil
	local applied = pcall(dbg.setupvalue, fn, index, value)
	if not applied then return nil end
	return function()
		pcall(dbg.setupvalue, fn, index, previous)
	end
end

--- Pull a method out of a class prototype the way khook's hooks do:
--- getrawmetatable(instance).__index[name].
function Resolver.prototypeMethod(object, methodName)
	if not getrawmetatable then return nil end
	local mt = getrawmetatable(object)
	if not mt then return nil end
	local index = rawget(mt, "__index")
	if type(index) ~= "table" then return nil end
	return rawget(index, methodName), index
end

------------------------------------------------------------- signatures ----
-- Each predicate is deliberately narrow so a sweep cannot bind the wrong
-- table. If Rivals renames something the handle simply stays nil and the
-- dependent feature reports itself unavailable instead of misfiring.

Resolver.register("WeaponInfo", "table", function(t)
	-- the weapon stat catalogue: a map of weapon name -> stat block
	if hasKeys(t, { "Damage" }) and (rawget(t, "FireRate") or rawget(t, "Cooldown")) then
		return true
	end
	return false
end)

--- The live local fighter. Matching on `GetCameraData` alone finds the class
--- table instead -- verified in game: the instance holds `Items`, `Data` and
--- `Player`, and reaches the methods through its metatable, so require those
--- fields and an inherited `EquipItem`.
Resolver.register("ClientFighter", "table", function(t)
	if rawget(t, "Items") == nil or rawget(t, "Player") == nil then return false end
	if rawget(t, "IsLocalPlayer") ~= true then return false end
	local mt = getmetatable(t)
	local index = type(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index") or nil
	return type(index) == "table" and rawget(index, "EquipItem") ~= nil
end)

-- Rivals' movement controller. khook resolves the prototype method rather
-- than writing Humanoid.WalkSpeed: both normal movement and Slide call
-- `_GetWalkSpeed`, whose BASE_WALKSPEED upvalue is the single authoritative
-- speed source (khook.lua:107315-107396).
Resolver.register("MechanicsController", "table", function(t)
	local mt = getmetatable(t)
	local index = type(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index") or nil
	return type(index) == "table" and type(rawget(index, "_GetWalkSpeed")) == "function"
end)

-- Rivals' camera controller. Third person is allowed only while its private
-- POV state is FirstPerson (khook.lua:105925-105999).
Resolver.register("CameraController", "table", function(t)
	local state = rawget(t, "CameraState")
	return type(state) == "table" and rawget(t, "POVStateChanged") ~= nil
		and rawget(t, "Rotation") ~= nil
end)

--- khook.lua:88650/88656 -- the fighters collection, keyed by player, with a
--- separate enemies list. This is how khook reaches *other* players' items;
--- Rivals does not surface weapons as Tool instances on the character.
Resolver.register("Fighters", "table", function(t)
	return rawget(t, "byPlayer") ~= nil and rawget(t, "enemies") ~= nil
end)

Resolver.register("ItemCatalog", "table", function(t)
	return rawget(t, "Skins") ~= nil
		and rawget(t, "Wraps") ~= nil
		and rawget(t, "Charms") ~= nil
end)

Resolver.register("ViewProfileFetch", "function", function(f)
	local i = Resolver.findUpvalue(f, function(v)
		return typeof(v) == "Instance" and v.ClassName == "ReplicatedStorage"
	end)
	return i ~= nil
end)

--============================================================================--
-- 6c. ENTITY REGISTRY -- one snapshot per frame, shared by every consumer
--============================================================================--
-- Previously each feature called Game.entities(), so a frame with aimbot +
-- ESP + triggerbot + sound viz rebuilt the same list four times and allocated
-- a fresh table per player per call. Now a single Heartbeat pass refreshes
-- reusable per-player records in place; consumers read Registry.list.


--============================================================================--
-- 8. FEATURES
--============================================================================--

--============================================================================--
-- Rivals item wire protocol
--============================================================================--
-- khook does not click the mouse to shoot. It builds the game's own item
-- payload and fires it at the replication remote (khook.lua:70733-70772):
--
--   ShootAt(from, to, opts)   -> ShootEncoded(enc(from), enc(to), a, b)
--   ShootEncoded(p1,p2,p3,p4) -> UseItemRemote:FireServer(objectId,
--                                  enum("StartShooting"), payload, nil)
--   payload = isRaycast and { ["\1"] = body, ["\2"] = true } or { ["\1"] = body }
--   body    = { ["\0"] = p1, ["\1"] = p2, ["\2"] = p3, ["\3"] = p4 }
--
-- Melee is the same remote with an animation enum attached
-- (khook.lua:84796 `AttackEncoded`), and Reload is
-- enum("StartReloading") with { ["\1"] = enum("Reload"), ["\2"] = enum("Reload") }
-- (khook.lua:70766).

local Rivals = {}

--- Cached module handles. All three are the game's own, so nothing about the
--- wire format has to be guessed at.
---
--- A failed require is NOT cached: `LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts` disappears while
--- the character is being rebuilt, and remembering that miss would disable the
--- whole fire path for the rest of the session.
function Rivals.module(getter, key)
	local hit = Rivals[key]
	if hit ~= nil then return hit end
	local ok, value = pcall(getter)
	if ok and value ~= nil then
		Rivals[key] = value
		return value
	end
	return nil
end

function Rivals.utility()
	return Rivals.module(function()
		return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Utility)
	end, "_utility")
end

function Rivals.enums()
	return Rivals.module(function()
		return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.EnumLibrary)
	end, "_enums")
end

--============================================================================--
-- PlayerData hook (khook.lua:125322 `DataHook`)
--============================================================================--
-- Every cosmetic feature khook has runs through this one object, and it is the
-- piece khookclone was missing outright.
--
-- Rivals keeps the local player's saved data in
-- `PlayerScripts.Controllers.PlayerDataController.CurrentData.Data`. khook
-- swaps that table for a proxy (khook.lua:125301 `f8479`): reads consult a
-- per-field spoof function first and fall through to the real table, writes go
-- straight to the real table, and `_Revert` puts the original back. Nothing is
-- ever mutated in place, so unloading leaves the player's data untouched.
--
-- Verified against the running game: `CosmeticLibrary:OwnsCosmetic` and
-- `PlayerDataUtility:GetWeaponData` both resolve through it, so spoofing
-- `CosmeticInventory` and `WeaponInventory` is all the unlocker and the skin
-- changer need.
Rivals.DataHook = { spoofs = {}, restore = nil, conn = nil, loaded = false }

function Rivals.DataHook.controller()
	return Rivals.module(function()
		return require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.PlayerDataController)
	end, "_playerData")
end

--- khook.lua:125301. The `__index` body is destroyed in the decompile; this is
--- the only behaviour consistent with `GetOriginal` and with the spoofs being
--- stored as functions over the real value (khook.lua:19584 `f1460`).
--- `__iter` is khookclone's: khook sets only the two metamethods, but the proxy
--- table is empty, so generalized iteration over `Data` would otherwise yield
--- nothing at all instead of the player's real fields.
function Rivals.DataHook._load(current)
	local hook = Rivals.DataHook
	local inner = rawget(current, "Data")
	if inner == nil then return false end
	if hook.restore ~= nil then
		if hook.restore.current == current then return true end
		hook._revert()
	end
	local proxy = setmetatable({}, {
		__index = function(_, key)
			local spoof = hook.spoofs[key]
			if spoof ~= nil then return spoof(inner[key]) end
			return inner[key]
		end,
		__newindex = function(_, key, value) inner[key] = value end,
		__len = function() return #inner end,
		__iter = function() return next, inner end,
	})
	rawset(current, "Data", proxy)
	hook.restore = { current = current, inner = inner }
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:125416 `_Revert`
function Rivals.DataHook._revert()
	local hook = Rivals.DataHook
	local restore = hook.restore
	if restore == nil then return end
	hook.restore = nil
	rawset(restore.current, "Data", restore.inner)
end

--- khook.lua:125343 `_Initialize` -- the hook re-attaches on PlayerDataAdded,
--- because the controller replaces CurrentData wholesale when data arrives.
function Rivals.DataHook.initialize()
	local hook = Rivals.DataHook
	if hook.loaded then return true end
	local controller = hook.controller()
	if controller == nil then return false end
	local added = rawget(controller, "PlayerDataAdded")
	if added ~= nil then
		local ok, conn = pcall(function()
			return added:Connect(function()
				local current = rawget(controller, "CurrentData")
				if current ~= nil then hook._load(current) end
			end)
		end)
		if ok then hook.conn = conn end
	end
	hook.loaded = true
	local current = rawget(controller, "CurrentData")
	if current ~= nil then return hook._load(current) end
	return false
end

--- khook.lua:125425 `Set` / 125430 `Unset` / 125434 `GetOriginal`
function Rivals.DataHook.set(field, fn)
	Rivals.DataHook.spoofs[field] = fn
	Rivals.DataHook.initialize()
end

function Rivals.DataHook.unset(field)
	Rivals.DataHook.spoofs[field] = nil
end

function Rivals.DataHook.getOriginal(field)
	local restore = Rivals.DataHook.restore
	if restore == nil then
		local controller = Rivals.DataHook.controller()
		local current = controller ~= nil and rawget(controller, "CurrentData") or nil
		local data = current ~= nil and rawget(current, "Data") or nil
		return data ~= nil and data[field] or nil
	end
	return restore.inner[field]
end

--- khook.lua:125442 `TriggerDataChangedSignal`. The game's own change signal
--- lives on the replicated-class instance and is fired as `(value, field)`
--- (ReplicatedClass:Replicate, read out of the running game).
function Rivals.DataHook.trigger(field)
	local hook = Rivals.DataHook
	local controller = hook.controller()
	if controller == nil then return end
	local current = (hook.restore ~= nil and hook.restore.current)
		or rawget(controller, "CurrentData")
	if current == nil then return end
	pcall(function()
		local signal = current:GetDataChangedSignal(field)
		if signal ~= nil then signal:Fire(current.Data[field], field) end
	end)
end

--- khook.lua:125457 `Destroy`
function Rivals.DataHook.destroy()
	local hook = Rivals.DataHook
	local fields = {}
	for field in pairs(hook.spoofs) do fields[#fields + 1] = field end
	table.clear(hook.spoofs)
	hook._revert()
	if hook.conn ~= nil then
		pcall(function() hook.conn:Disconnect() end)
		hook.conn = nil
	end
	hook.loaded = false
	for _, field in ipairs(fields) do hook.trigger(field) end
end

--- The game's enum table. Keys on a replicated serial are encoded through it
--- (`ToEnum("Name") == "f"`, `ToEnum("Data") == "\u{00b8}"`).
function Rivals.enumLibrary()
	return Rivals.module(function()
		return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.EnumLibrary)
	end, "_enumLibrary")
end

function Rivals.fighterController()
	return Rivals.module(function()
		return require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.FighterController)
	end, "_fighterController")
end

--- The game encodes a CFrame itself (`Utility:EncodeCFrame`). Verified against
--- the running game: it emits all six fields -- bytes 0..2 for position and
--- 3..5 for orientation. khook's decompiled copies drop the byte-4 assignment
--- and leave an unassigned local behind, so that omission is a decompiler
--- artifact, not the real format.
function Rivals.encodeCFrame(cf)
	local util = Rivals.utility()
	if util ~= nil then
		local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return util:EncodeCFrame(cf) end)
		if ok and type(encoded) == "table" then return encoded end
	end
	local rx, ry, rz = cf:ToOrientation()
	return {
		[utf8.char(0)] = cf.X, [utf8.char(1)] = cf.Y, [utf8.char(2)] = cf.Z,
		[utf8.char(3)] = rx,   [utf8.char(4)] = ry,   [utf8.char(5)] = rz,
	}
end

--- khook.lua:80177 -- action names travel as the game's enum values. The
--- library exposes `ToEnum`; the `_to_enum` table behind it gives the same
--- byte ("StartShooting" -> 26, "StartReloading" -> 27).
function Rivals.enum(name)
	local lib = Rivals.enums()
	if lib == nil then return name end
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return lib:ToEnum(name) end)
	if ok and value ~= nil then return value end
	local to = rawget(lib, "_to_enum")
	if type(to) == "table" and to[name] ~= nil then return to[name] end
	return name
end

--- khook.lua:14818 -- confirmed in the live tree at
--- Remotes.Replication.Fighter.UseItem.
function Rivals.useItemRemote()
	local cached = Rivals._useItem
	if cached ~= nil then return cached or nil end
	local found = false
	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local replication = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Replication")
	local fighter = replication and replication:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
	local useItem = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("UseItem")
	if useItem and useItem:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		found = useItem
	elseif replication then
		for _, d in ipairs(replication:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") and d.Name == "UseItem" then found = d break end
		end
	end
	Rivals._useItem = found
	return found or nil
end

local function itemField(object, key)
	if object == nil then return nil end
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return object[key] end)
	if ok then return value end
	return nil
end
Rivals.field = itemField

--- The live local fighter, straight off the controller -- no GC sweeping.
function Rivals.localFighter()
	local controller = Rivals.fighterController()
	if controller == nil then return nil end
	return itemField(controller, "LocalFighter")
end

--- The same controller khook's camera director and flickbot call. Keeping the
--- write on SetRotation lets Rivals apply recoil, sway and POV offsets after
--- the requested combat angle instead of having our CFrame overwritten.
function Rivals.cameraController()
	if Rivals._cameraController ~= nil then
		return Rivals._cameraController or nil
	end
	local scripts = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
	local controllers = scripts and scripts:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
	local module = controllers and controllers:FindFirstChild("CameraController")
	local found = false
	if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
		if ok and type(value) == "table" then found = value end
	end
	Rivals._cameraController = found
	return found or nil
end

--- khook.lua:88650 `_fighters.byPlayer` -- the controller keeps exactly that
--- map, so remote fighters need no GC sweeping either. It retains dead entries
--- across respawns, so a fighter with a live item list wins over one without.
function Rivals.fighterFor(player)
	player = player or LocalPlayer
	if player == LocalPlayer then
		local live = Rivals.localFighter()
		if live ~= nil then return live end
	end
	local controller = Rivals.fighterController()
	local map = controller and rawget(controller, "_player_to_fighter")
	if type(map) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local best
	for key, fighter in pairs(map) do
		if key == player or itemField(fighter, "Player") == player then
			if best == nil or next(itemField(best, "Items") or {}) == nil then
				best = fighter
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end

--- Read one of the game's replicated records. Most live builds expose the
--- friendly key directly, while others keep the EnumLibrary-encoded key that
--- khook's `getInnerField` resolves (for example Entity / IsInvincible).
function Rivals.replicatedField(object, key)
	if object == nil then return nil end
	local value = itemField(object, key)
	if value ~= nil then return value end

	local enums = Rivals.enumLibrary()
	if enums ~= nil then
		local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return enums:ToEnum(key) end)
		if ok and encoded ~= nil then
			value = itemField(object, encoded)
			if value ~= nil then return value end
		end
	end

	local ok, got = pcall(function() return object:Get(key) end)
	if ok then return got end
	return nil
end

--- khook.lua:3422-3428 / 88683. Ragebot does not infer spawn protection
--- from character attributes: it asks the fighter's replicated Entity. A
--- fighter whose Entity has not arrived yet is deliberately invincible, so
--- the ragebot stays in its evade position instead of firing during spawn.
function Rivals.isInvincible(fighter)
	if fighter == nil then return nil end

	local ok, direct = pcall(function() return fighter:IsInvincible() end)
	if ok and direct ~= nil then return direct == true end

	local entity = Rivals.replicatedField(fighter, "Entity")
	if entity == nil then return true end
	local value = Rivals.replicatedField(entity, "IsInvincible")
	if type(value) == "function" then
		local called, result = pcall(value, entity)
		if called then value = result end
	end
	return value == true
end

--- khook.lua:88686 -- the fighter answers whether it is alive.
function Rivals.isAlive(fighter)
	if fighter == nil then return false end
	local ok, alive = pcall(function() return fighter:IsAlive() end)
	if ok and alive ~= nil then return alive == true end
	return nil
end

--- Everything the local fighter is holding, keyed 1..4.
function Rivals.items()
	local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
	if fighter == nil then return nil end
	local direct = itemField(fighter, "Items")
	if type(direct) == "table" then return direct end
	return nil
end

--- The equipped item. The controller publishes it directly.
function Rivals.equippedItem()
	local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
	if fighter == nil then return nil end
	local equipped = itemField(fighter, "EquippedItem")
	if equipped ~= nil then return equipped end
	local items = Rivals.items()
	if items ~= nil then
		for _, entry in pairs(items) do
			if itemField(entry, "IsEquipped") == true then return entry end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

--- khook.lua:70272 `GetOrigin`. The FOV indicator is anchored to the game's
--- rendered muzzle, not to the camera. ClientViewModel already handles a
--- hidden viewmodel, multiple muzzle attachments and the third-person body
--- model; falling back to the camera is reserved for callers which need a
--- world-space shot origin even while no viewmodel exists.
function Rivals.muzzlePosition()
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item == nil then return nil end
	local viewModel = itemField(item, "ViewModel")
	if viewModel == nil then return nil end
	local getMuzzle = itemField(viewModel, "GetMuzzlePosition")
	if type(getMuzzle) ~= "function" then return nil end
	local ok, position = pcall(getMuzzle, viewModel)
	return ok and typeof(position) == "Vector3" and position or nil
end

--- Items answer `Get(key)` for their replicated data; Data/Info are the same
--- values reached directly.
function Rivals.get(item, key)
	if item == nil then return nil end
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return item:Get(key) end)
	if ok and value ~= nil then return value end
	local data = itemField(item, "Data")
	local hit = itemField(data, key)
	if hit ~= nil then return hit end
	return itemField(itemField(item, "Info"), key)
end

function Rivals.equippedValue(container, key)
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item == nil then return nil end
	return itemField(itemField(item, container), key)
end

--- khook.lua:70709 -- "Gun", "Melee" or "Throwable".
function Rivals.itemType(item)
	item = item or Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item == nil then return nil end
	return itemField(itemField(item, "Info"), "Type")
end

--- The game hands the weapon class over as Info.Class ("Primary", "Secondary",
--- "Melee", "Utility"); khook derived the same thing from the slot index.
function Rivals.itemClass(item)
	if item == nil then return nil end
	local class = itemField(itemField(item, "Info"), "Class")
	if class ~= nil then return class end
	local index = Rivals.itemIndex(item)
	return index and ({ "Primary", "Secondary", "Melee" })[index] or nil
end

function Rivals.itemIndex(item)
	if item == nil then return nil end
	return itemField(itemField(item, "Data"), "ItemIndex")
end

function Rivals.isGun()
	return Rivals.itemType() == "Gun"
end

function Rivals.isMelee()
	return Rivals.itemType() == "Melee"
end

function Rivals.equippedName()
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item == nil then return nil end
	return itemField(item, "Name") or Rivals.get(item, "Name")
end

--- khook.lua:3007 -- the equipped weapon's name for any fighter.
function Rivals.equippedGunName(player)
	local fighter = Rivals.fighterFor(player)
	if fighter == nil then return nil end
	local equipped = itemField(fighter, "EquippedItem")
	if equipped ~= nil then return itemField(equipped, "Name") end
	local items = itemField(fighter, "Items")
	if type(items) == "table" then
		for _, item in pairs(items) do
			if itemField(item, "IsEquipped") == true then return itemField(item, "Name") end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

--- khook.lua:70710 -- what the payload is addressed to.
function Rivals.equippedObjectId(item)
	return Rivals.get(item or Rivals.equippedItem(), "ObjectID")
end

--- khook.lua:70711 -- kept for the item Info, though the shot payload does not
--- carry a raycast flag (see shootAt).
function Rivals.isRaycast()
	return Rivals.equippedValue("Info", "IsRaycast") == true
end

--- The game's own fire path, used when the encoded call cannot be built.
function Rivals.shootViaItem()
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item == nil then return false end
	return (pcall(function() item:StartShooting() end))
end

--- Triggerbot uses the fighter/item input path in khook, rather than virtual
--- mouse input. This preserves automatic weapons and the game's own input
--- bookkeeping; executor mouse functions remain a compatibility fallback.
function Rivals.startShooting()
	local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
	if fighter ~= nil then
		local ok = pcall(function() fighter:Input("StartShooting") end)
		if ok then return true end
	end
	return false
end

function Rivals.reloadViaItem()
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item == nil then return false end
	return (pcall(function() item:StartReloading() end))
end

--- khook.lua:185001 `aY` -- the pair that fills body slots 2 and 3: the part
--- itself, and the impact point as an *object-space* CFrame relative to that
--- part. khook's non-objectSpace branch encodes a world point; a working
--- reference implementation encodes
--- `part.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(point))`, and object space is what the
--- server accepts, so the world point is converted here.
function Rivals.hitDescriptor(originCF, part, objectSpace)
	if part == nil then return nil, nil end
	-- khook.lua:185001 / 70733: an explicit object-space point is encoded
	-- directly; otherwise the closest surface point to the shot origin is used.
	local point = objectSpace or part:GetClosestPointOnSurface(originCF.Position)
	return part, Rivals.encodeCFrame(CFrame.new(point))
end

--- khook.lua:70733 `ShootAt` into 70739 `ShootEncoded`.
---
---   UseItem:FireServer(objectId, enum("StartShooting"), { [1] = body }, nil)
---   body = { [0] = origin CFrame, [1] = end CFrame, [2] = part,
---            [3] = object-space impact CFrame }
---
--- Keys are the raw bytes 0..3. khook adds a trailing `[2] = true` on the outer
--- table for raycast weapons (khook.lua:70744); a working reference sends the
--- body alone for a raycast gun, so the flag is left off.
function Rivals.shootAt(fromCF, toCF, part, objectSpace)
	local remote = Rivals.useItemRemote()
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	local objectId = Rivals.equippedObjectId(item)
	if remote == nil or objectId == nil or part == nil then
		return Rivals.shootViaItem()
	end
	local hitPart, hitCF = Rivals.hitDescriptor(fromCF, part, objectSpace)
	if hitPart == nil then return Rivals.shootViaItem() end

	local body = {
