		[utf8.char(0)] = Rivals.encodeCFrame(fromCF),
		[utf8.char(1)] = Rivals.encodeCFrame(toCF),
		[utf8.char(2)] = hitPart,
		[utf8.char(3)] = hitCF,
	}
	local isRaycast = Rivals.field(Rivals.field(item, "Info"), "IsRaycast") == true
	local outer = { [utf8.char(1)] = body }
	if isRaycast then outer[utf8.char(2)] = true end
	local ok = pcall(function()
		remote:FireServer(objectId, Rivals.enum("StartShooting"), outer, nil)
	end)
	if not ok then return Rivals.shootViaItem() end
	return true
end

--- Aims from `origin` at `part` and fires, building both CFrames the way the
--- reference does: position plus the orientation of a look-at.
function Rivals.shootAtPart(origin, part)
	if part == nil then return false end
	local look = CFrame.lookAt(origin, part.Position)
	local aim = CFrame.new(origin) * CFrame.Angles(look:ToOrientation())
	local landing = CFrame.new(part.Position) * CFrame.Angles(look:ToOrientation())
	return Rivals.shootAt(aim, landing, part)
end

--- khook.lua:70766 `Reload`.
function Rivals.reload()
	local remote = Rivals.useItemRemote()
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	local objectId = Rivals.equippedObjectId(item)
	if remote == nil or objectId == nil then return Rivals.reloadViaItem() end
	local reloadEnum = Rivals.enum("Reload")
	local ok = pcall(function()
		remote:FireServer(objectId, Rivals.enum("StartReloading"),
			{ [utf8.char(1)] = reloadEnum, [utf8.char(2)] = reloadEnum }, nil)
	end)
	if not ok then return Rivals.reloadViaItem() end
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:12253 `SetForced` / 12265 `ClearForced`. Forced character state
--- is NOT a local flag: the encoded state name and its value go to the server
--- on Remotes.Replication.Fighter.UpdateState, and khook only fires on change.
--- Confirmed live: the remote exists and `fighter.Data.IsCrouching` carries the
--- genuine value that ClearForced restores.
function Rivals.updateStateRemote()
	local cached = Rivals._updateState
	if cached ~= nil then return cached or nil end
	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local rep = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Replication")
	local fighter = rep and rep:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
	local found = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("UpdateState")
	Rivals._updateState = (found ~= nil and found:IsA("RemoteEvent")) and found or false
	return Rivals._updateState or nil
end

function Rivals.setForcedState(name, value)
	local remote = Rivals.updateStateRemote()
	if remote == nil then return false end
	return (pcall(function()
		remote:FireServer(Rivals.enum(name), value)
	end))
end

--- The replicated value for a state, which is what khook writes back when it
--- clears a forced one rather than guessing `false`.
function Rivals.stateValue(name)
	local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
	return Rivals.field(Rivals.field(fighter, "Data"), name)
end

--- khook.lua:84796 `AttackEncoded` / 84808 `HeavyAttackEncoded`. Melee rides
--- the same remote as a shot, but the outer table carries an animation enum
--- and the heavy variant is sent as `StartAiming` rather than `StartShooting`.
function Rivals.attackEncoded(item, from, to, part, hitCF, heavy)
	local remote = Rivals.useItemRemote()
	local objectId = Rivals.equippedObjectId(item)
	if remote == nil or objectId == nil then return false end
	local body = {
		[utf8.char(0)] = from, [utf8.char(1)] = to,
		[utf8.char(2)] = part, [utf8.char(3)] = hitCF,
	}
	local outer = {
		[utf8.char(1)] = body,
		[utf8.char(2)] = Rivals.enum(heavy and "HeavyAttackAnimation1" or "AttackAnimation1"),
	}
	return (pcall(function()
		remote:FireServer(objectId,
			Rivals.enum(heavy and "StartAiming" or "StartShooting"), outer, nil)
	end))
end

--- khook.lua:664 `sharedHelper69`. The melee strategy never encodes a CFrame
--- at run time: it keeps four pre-encoded position templates and swaps a fresh
--- orientation into bytes 3..5 (khook.lua:39533-39566).
function Rivals.reorient(template, rx, ry, rz)
	return {
		[utf8.char(0)] = template[1],
		[utf8.char(1)] = template[2],
		[utf8.char(2)] = template[3],
		[utf8.char(3)] = rx, [utf8.char(4)] = ry, [utf8.char(5)] = rz,
	}
end

Rivals.Glue = { bindings = {}, glued = {}, far = nil, ourPart = nil }

--- khook.lua:144108 -- one fixed point per session, x/z negative, y = 100000.
function Rivals.Glue.point()
	if Rivals.Glue.far == nil then
		Rivals.Glue.far = CFrame.new(
			math.random(-100000, -10000), 100000, math.random(-100000, 10000))
	end
	return Rivals.Glue.far
end

--- khook.lua:144159 `_SetupGlue` -- break the hitbox off its weld and anchor
--- it, remembering what it was attached to. Reference counted.
function Rivals.Glue.setup(part)
	local entry = Rivals.Glue.glued[part]
	if entry ~= nil then
		entry.refCount += 1
		return
	end
	local weld = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("WeldConstraint")
	local original = nil
	if weld ~= nil then
		original = weld.Part1
		if original ~= nil then pcall(function() weld.Part1 = nil end) end
		pcall(function() part.Anchored = true end)
	end
	Rivals.Glue.glued[part] = { refCount = 1, weld = weld, original = original }
end

--- khook.lua:144179 `_ReleaseGlue` -- note khook unanchors the ORIGINAL part,
--- not the hitbox it anchored.
function Rivals.Glue.release(part)
	local entry = Rivals.Glue.glued[part]
	if entry == nil then return end
	entry.refCount -= 1
	if entry.refCount > 0 then return end
	if entry.weld ~= nil and entry.original ~= nil then
		pcall(function() entry.weld.Part1 = entry.original end)
		pcall(function() entry.original.Anchored = false end)
	end
	Rivals.Glue.glued[part] = nil
end

--- khook.lua:144132 `Acquire`. Returns the fixed point the caller offsets from.
function Rivals.Glue.acquire(ourPart, targetPart, keepRotation)
	if ourPart == nil or targetPart == nil then return nil end
	-- khook.lua:144114 -- written on thread identity 8; the property is hidden
	pcall(function()
		if sethiddenproperty then
			sethiddenproperty(ourPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", targetPart)
		end
	end)
	local bound = Rivals.Glue.bindings[ourPart]
	if bound ~= targetPart then
		if bound ~= nil then Rivals.Glue.release(bound) end
		Rivals.Glue.setup(targetPart)
		Rivals.Glue.bindings[ourPart] = targetPart
	end
	Rivals.Glue.ourPart = ourPart
	local far = Rivals.Glue.point()
	local cf = CFrame.new(far.Position)
	if keepRotation then cf = cf * targetPart.CFrame.Rotation end
	pcall(function() targetPart.CFrame = cf end)
	return far
end

--- khook.lua:144150 `Free`
function Rivals.Glue.free(ourPart)
	ourPart = ourPart or Rivals.Glue.ourPart
	if ourPart == nil then return end
	local bound = Rivals.Glue.bindings[ourPart]
	if bound == nil then return end
	Rivals.Glue.bindings[ourPart] = nil
	Rivals.Glue.release(bound)
end

--- khook.lua:25520 `f1901`. Verified live: the game's own
--- `Utility:EncodeCameraRotation(Vector2(pitchRad, yawRad))` produces exactly
--- the two bytes khook builds as `encodeSingle(pitch) .. encodeSingle(yaw)`,
--- and `DecodeCameraRotation` round-trips them. khookclone previously guessed
--- an 8-byte `string.pack("<f")` pair, which was wrong.
function Rivals.encodeViewAngles(angles)
	if angles.kind == "Unnormalized" then
		return utf8.char(math.clamp(angles.pitch, 0, 255))
			.. utf8.char(math.clamp(angles.yaw, 0, 255))
	end
	local util = Rivals.utility()
	if util == nil then return nil end
	local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
		return util:EncodeCameraRotation(
			Vector2.new(math.rad(angles.pitch), math.rad(angles.yaw)))
	end)
	return ok and encoded or nil
end

--- khook.lua:25557 `ViewAngleDriver`. Slots are keyed by a NUMBER; every khook
--- call site uses 20, and only the ragebot uses the driver at all -- the aim
--- features steer the real camera instead. Nothing here writes Camera.CFrame:
--- this is purely what the SERVER is told we are looking at.
Rivals.ViewAngles = { slots = {}, winning = nil, dirty = false, suppressed = false }

--- The joints class khook hooks (khook.lua:25443).
function Rivals.jointsModule()
	return Rivals.module(function()
		local modules = LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules
		return require(modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter
			.ClientFighterCharacter.Joints)
	end, "_joints")
end

--- khook.lua:25529 `f1902` -- the angle struct as the Vector2 the joints want.
--- khook round-trips each component through encodeSingle/decodeSingle to get
--- the quantised value; the game's own pair encoder does the same thing.
function Rivals.decodeViewAngles(angles)
	local util = Rivals.utility()
	if util == nil then return nil end
	if angles.kind == "Unnormalized" then
		local ok, value = pcall(function()
			return util:FromXYToCameraRotation(angles.pitch, nil)
		end)
		return ok and value or nil
	end
	local encoded = Rivals.encodeViewAngles(angles)
	if encoded == nil then return nil end
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return util:DecodeCameraRotation(encoded) end)
	return ok and value or nil
end

function Rivals.ViewAngles.remote()
	local cached = Rivals._camRotate
	if cached ~= nil then return cached or nil end
	local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local rep = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Replication")
	local fighter = rep and rep:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
	local found = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("UpdateCameraRotation")
	Rivals._camRotate = found or false
	return found or nil
end

--- khook's `_Resolve` survives only as `_winning = nil` -- its body is
--- destroyed -- so the tie-break is reconstructed as "highest slot wins".
--- With every khook caller on slot 20 there is never more than one.
function Rivals.ViewAngles.resolve()
	local best, bestKey = nil, nil
	for key, value in pairs(Rivals.ViewAngles.slots) do
		if value ~= nil and (bestKey == nil or key > bestKey) then
			best, bestKey = value, key
		end
	end
	Rivals.ViewAngles.winning = best
end

Rivals.ViewAngles.hooks = {}

--- khook.lua:25438 `_LoadJointsHook`. The local character's pose is driven from
--- `CameraRotationRaw`; without this the body would still face where the real
--- camera points while the server is told otherwise.
function Rivals.ViewAngles.loadJointsHook()
	local driver = Rivals.ViewAngles
	if driver.hooks.joints ~= nil then return true end
	local module = Rivals.jointsModule()
	if module == nil then return false, "Joints module not found" end
	local original = rawget(module, "Update")
	if type(original) ~= "function" then return false, "Joints.Update not found" end

	local function shim(self, a, b)
		local character = Rivals.field(self, "ClientFighterCharacter")
		local fighter = character ~= nil and Rivals.field(character, "ClientFighter") or nil
		if fighter == nil then return original(self, a, b) end
		if Rivals.field(fighter, "IsLocalPlayer") == true then
			local winning = driver.winning
			if winning ~= nil then
				local decoded = Rivals.decodeViewAngles(winning)
				if decoded ~= nil then
					pcall(function() b.CameraRotationRaw = decoded end)
				end
			end
		end
		return original(self, a, b)
	end

	local ok = pcall(function() module.Update = shim end)
	if not ok then return false, "Joints.Update is not writable" end
	driver.hooks.joints = { module = module, original = original }
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:25476 `_LoadReplicationHook`. The game's `_CameraReplicationLoop`
--- holds Utility as an upvalue and dedupes on the encoded value: if the
--- encoding has not changed it stops replicating. Swapping that upvalue for a
--- shim that keeps handing back `_last_encoded_camera_rotation` makes the loop
--- believe nothing changed, so it never puts the REAL rotation on the wire and
--- our own Flush is the only sender.
function Rivals.ViewAngles.loadReplicationHook()
	local driver = Rivals.ViewAngles
	if driver.hooks.replication ~= nil then return true end
	if debug.getupvalues == nil or debug.setupvalue == nil then
		return false, "executor has no debug upvalue access"
	end
	local controller = Rivals.fighterController()
	if controller == nil then return false, "FighterController unavailable" end
	local mt = getmetatable(controller)
	local proto = type(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index") or nil
	if type(proto) ~= "table" then return false, "FighterController prototype not found" end
	local loop = rawget(proto, "_CameraReplicationLoop")
	if type(loop) ~= "function" then
		return false, "_CameraReplicationLoop method not found"
	end

	-- khook.lua:25504 -- find it by signature, never by index
	local utility, index
	local ok, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, loop)
	if not ok or type(ups) ~= "table" then return false, "upvalues unreadable" end
	for i, value in pairs(ups) do
		if type(value) == "table" then
			local vmt = getmetatable(value)
			local vidx = type(vmt) == "table" and rawget(vmt, "__index") or nil
			if type(vidx) == "table" and rawget(vidx, "EncodeCameraRotation") ~= nil then
				utility, index = value, i
				break
			end
		end
	end
	if index == nil then return false, "Utility upvalue index not found" end

	local shim = {}
	function shim.EncodeCameraRotation(_, rotation)
		-- khook.lua:25534 -- with no slot set the real value passes straight through
		if next(driver.slots) == nil and not driver.suppressed then
			return utility:EncodeCameraRotation(rotation)
		end
		controller._replication_stopped = false
		-- khook returns the STALE `_last_encoded_camera_rotation` so the game's
		-- loop dedupes on it and stops sending, leaving khook's own Flush as the
		-- only sender. THIS build of Rivals has no such dedupe: the constants of
		-- `_CameraReplicationLoop` are {wait, LocalFighter, IsSpectating, Get,
		-- GetPublicState, Rotation, EncodeCameraRotation, task, defer, Vector2,
		-- zero} -- no `_last_encoded_camera_rotation` anywhere -- so returning it
		-- would change nothing. Handing the loop OUR encoding instead reaches the
		-- same end state khook gets: the only camera rotation that leaves the
		-- client is the spoofed one.
		local winning = driver.winning
		if winning ~= nil then
			local encoded = Rivals.encodeViewAngles(winning)
			if encoded ~= nil then return encoded end
		end
		local last = controller._last_encoded_camera_rotation
		if not last then last = utility:EncodeCameraRotation(rotation) end
		return last
	end

	local applied = pcall(debug.setupvalue, loop, index, shim)
	if not applied then return false, "setupvalue rejected" end
	driver.hooks.replication = { loop = loop, index = index, utility = utility }
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:25612 `_RevertHooks`
function Rivals.ViewAngles.revertHooks()
	local driver = Rivals.ViewAngles
	local joints = driver.hooks.joints
	if joints ~= nil then
		driver.hooks.joints = nil
		pcall(function() joints.module.Update = joints.original end)
	end
	local replication = driver.hooks.replication
	if replication ~= nil then
		driver.hooks.replication = nil
		pcall(debug.setupvalue, replication.loop, replication.index, replication.utility)
	end
end

--- khook.lua:25566 `SendViewAngles`. Note the hooks load HERE, lazily, and only
--- once an angle is actually winning -- loading khookclone patches nothing.
function Rivals.ViewAngles.send(slot, angles)
	local driver = Rivals.ViewAngles
	if driver.slots[slot] == angles then return end
	driver.slots[slot] = angles
	driver.dirty = true
	driver.resolve()
	if driver.winning == nil then return end
	local ok, err = driver.loadJointsHook()
	if ok then
		ok, err = driver.loadReplicationHook()
	end
	-- reported on the driver, not into Features: `Features` is a top-level local
	-- declared further down the file, so it is nil from in here
	driver.hookStatus = ok and "ok (joints + replication hooked)"
		or ("unavailable: " .. tostring(err))
end

function Rivals.ViewAngles.clearAll()
	table.clear(Rivals.ViewAngles.slots)
	Rivals.ViewAngles.winning = nil
	Rivals.ViewAngles.dirty = false
end

--- khook.lua:25600 `Flush`
function Rivals.ViewAngles.flush()
	local driver = Rivals.ViewAngles
	if not driver.dirty then return end
	if driver.suppressed then return end
	local winning = driver.winning
	if winning == nil then
		driver.dirty = false
		return
	end
	driver.dirty = false
	local remote = driver.remote()
	local payload = remote ~= nil and Rivals.encodeViewAngles(winning) or nil
	if payload ~= nil then
		pcall(function() remote:FireServer(payload, nil) end)
	end
end

function Rivals.equip(item)
	if item == nil then return false end
	if Rivals.field(item, "IsEquipped") == true then return true end
	local fighter = Rivals.field(item, "ClientFighter") or Rivals.localFighter()
	local index = Rivals.itemIndex(item)
	if fighter == nil or index == nil then
		-- the item can equip itself (read off the live object)
		return (pcall(function() item:Equip() end))
	end
	if pcall(function() fighter:EquipItem(index) end) then return true end
	return (pcall(function() item:Equip() end))
end

local Registry = {}
Registry.list = {}            -- dense array of live records, rebuilt in place
Registry.byPlayer = {}
Registry.byModel = {}
Registry._dummyList = {}        -- player -> record (records are reused)
Registry.count = 0
Registry.localRecord = nil
Registry.Trove = Trove.new("registry")
Registry.frame = 0
Registry._me = { id = nil, env = nil }   -- our TeamID / EnvironmentID, refreshed per frame

--- Character-level handles are cached on CharacterAdded rather than looked up
--- with FindFirstChild every frame.
local function bindCharacter(record, character)
	local previous = record.Character
	if previous ~= nil and Registry.byModel[previous] == record then
		Registry.byModel[previous] = nil
	end
	record.Character = character
	record.Root = nil
	record.Head = nil
	record.Humanoid = nil
	record.Motor = nil
	if record._characterTrove == nil then
		record._characterTrove = Trove.new("registry.character")
		Registry.Trove:Add(record._characterTrove)
	else
		record._characterTrove:Clean()
	end
	if not character then return end
	Registry.byModel[character] = record
	record.Root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		or character.PrimaryPart
	record.Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	-- Rivals gives every fighter dedicated hit volumes -- HitboxHead,
	-- HitboxBody and the *Small variants sit alongside the R15 limbs. These
	-- are what the server actually tests, so they are what we aim at; the
	-- visual Head/HumanoidRootPart are only fallbacks for the lobby.
	record.HitboxHead = character:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead")
	record.HitboxBody = character:FindFirstChild("HitboxBody")
	record.HitboxHeadSmall = character:FindFirstChild("HitboxHeadSmall")
	record.HitboxBodySmall = character:FindFirstChild("HitboxBodySmall")
	record.Head = record.HitboxHead or character:FindFirstChild("Head") or record.Root

	local function rebuildVisualParts()
		record.Parts = {}
		record.DynamicParts = {}
		record.SkeletonLinks = {}
		for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then
				record.Parts[part.Name] = part
				-- khook's skeleton takes the first Motor6D from each eligible limb
				-- and draws its Part0 -> Part1 connection, rather than assuming a
				-- fixed R6/R15 name graph.
				local motor = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("Motor6D")
				if motor and motor.Part0 and motor.Part1 then
					table.insert(record.SkeletonLinks, { motor.Part0, motor.Part1 })
				end
			end
		end
		-- Keep every descendant BasePart cached; dynamicBox performs khook's
		-- opaque-array test from the live Transparency value, so a part that
		-- becomes visible later enters the rectangle without a full rescan.
		for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then
				table.insert(record.DynamicParts, part)
			end
		end
	end
	rebuildVisualParts()
	record._characterTrove:Add(character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
			task.defer(rebuildVisualParts)
		end
	end))
	record._characterTrove:Add(character.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(descendant)
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
			task.defer(rebuildVisualParts)
		end
	end))
end

local function ensureRecord(player)
	local record = Registry.byPlayer[player]
	if record then return record end
	record = {
		Player = player,
		Character = nil, Root = nil, Head = nil, Humanoid = nil,
		Parts = {},
		Health = 0, MaxHealth = 100,
		Alive = false, Grounded = true,
		Team = nil, IsEnemy = true,
		Weapon = nil, Deflecting = false, Shielded = false,
		Rank = nil, Winstreak = nil,
		Distance = 0,
		-- reusable scratch so the ESP box math allocates nothing per frame
		_box = { X = 0, Y = 0, W = 0, H = 0, CenterX = 0, CenterY = 0, valid = false },
		_boxFrame = -1,
		_dynamicBox = { X = 0, Y = 0, W = 0, H = 0, CenterX = 0, CenterY = 0, valid = false },
		_dynamicBoxFrame = -1,
		_visible = false,
		_visFrame = -1,
	}
	Registry.byPlayer[player] = record

	local conn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
		bindCharacter(record, char)
		-- limbs stream in; rebind once descendants settle
		task.delay(0.5, function()
			if record.Character == char then bindCharacter(record, char) end
		end)
	end)
	Registry.Trove:Add(conn)
	if player.Character then bindCharacter(record, player.Character) end
	return record
end

local function releaseRecord(player)
	local record = Registry.byPlayer[player]
	if record ~= nil and record.Character ~= nil
		and Registry.byModel[record.Character] == record then
		Registry.byModel[record.Character] = nil
	end
	Registry.byPlayer[player] = nil
end

--- Attribute reads are cheap but not free; only the ones features actually
--- consume are polled, and only for live characters.
local function refreshRecord(record, myTeam, camPos)
	local character = record.Character
	if not character or not character.Parent then
		record.Alive = false
		return false
	end
	local root = record.Root
	if not root or not root.Parent then
		bindCharacter(record, character)
		root = record.Root
		if not root then record.Alive = false return false end
	end

	local humanoid = record.Humanoid
	if humanoid then
		record.Health = humanoid.Health
		record.MaxHealth = humanoid.MaxHealth > 0 and humanoid.MaxHealth or 100
		-- khook decides "airborne" from the humanoid *state*, not the floor
		-- material (khook.lua:116903). During a dash or a knockback the floor
		-- material can still read as ground while the fighter is in the air,
		-- and the Air/Ground hitbox profiles would pick the wrong set.
		record.Grounded = humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
	else
		record.Health = character:GetAttribute("Health") or 100
		record.MaxHealth = character:GetAttribute("MaxHealth") or 100
		record.Grounded = true
	end
	if record.Health <= 0 then
		record.Alive = false
		return false
	end

	record.Alive = true

	-- Rivals leaves the Teams service empty and replicates the side as a
	-- TeamID attribute on the Player, with EnvironmentID separating the lobby
	-- from each running duel. Anyone in another environment is not a target
	-- at all, which is what stops the lobby crowd from being aimed at.
	-- Shooting-range dummies are models with no Player, so the attributes are
	-- read off whichever object actually carries them.
	local owner = record.Player or character
	record.Team = owner:GetAttribute("TeamID")
	record.Environment = owner:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID")
	-- Both sides must be in the same live environment. While we are in the
	-- lobby our own EnvironmentID is nil, and then nobody is a combat target
	-- -- otherwise the aimbot would lock onto players in other arenas
	-- thousands of studs away.
	record.SameEnvironment = myTeam.env ~= nil and record.Environment == myTeam.env
	-- TeamID and EnvironmentID are encoded single characters, not readable
	-- names (verified: TeamID is the one byte 0x01 for everyone on a side,
	-- EnvironmentID is two bytes). They compare fine as strings; the only
	-- special case is a genuinely empty value, which means "no team" and must
	-- not be read as a side everyone shares.
	local myTeamId = myTeam.id
	if myTeamId == "" then myTeamId = nil end
	local theirTeam = record.Team
	if theirTeam == "" then theirTeam = nil end
	if record.IsDummy then
		-- khook.lua:28009 -- range dummies are their own entity class and are
		-- always valid targets inside the environment they belong to.
		record.IsEnemy = record.SameEnvironment
	else
		record.IsEnemy = record.SameEnvironment
			and (theirTeam == nil or myTeamId == nil or theirTeam ~= myTeamId)
	end

	record.Deflecting = character:GetAttribute("Deflecting") == true
		or character:GetAttribute("IsDeflecting") == true
	record.Shielded = character:GetAttribute("Shielded") == true
	-- khook.lua:88683 `IsInvincible()` -- the authoritative value lives on the
	-- fighter's replicated Entity. Attributes and a Roblox ForceField remain a
	-- fallback for dummies or the brief interval before a fighter is available.
	local fallbackInvincible = character:GetAttribute("Invincible") == true
		or character:GetAttribute("IsInvincible") == true
		or character:GetAttribute("Godmode") == true
		or character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") ~= nil
	local fighterInvincible = record.Player
		and Rivals.isInvincible(Rivals.fighterFor(record.Player)) or nil
	record.Invincible = fighterInvincible == nil and fallbackInvincible
		or fighterInvincible == true
	record.Position = root.Position
	record.Distance = (root.Position - camPos).Magnitude
	record._boxFrame = -1
	record._visFrame = -1
	return true
end

--- Slower-moving data (weapon name, rank, winstreak) refreshes on its own
--- cadence so the hot path stays property reads only.
local slowAccumulator = 0
local function refreshSlow(record)
	local character = record.Character
	if not character then return end
	-- khook.lua:3007 -- weapons live on the fighter, not on a Tool instance.
	record.Weapon = (record.Player and Rivals.equippedGunName(record.Player))
		or character:GetAttribute("EquippedItem")

	-- Rivals has no leaderstats: level, winstreak and ELO are Player
	-- attributes (Level / StatisticDuelsWinStreak / DisplayELO).
	local player = record.Player
	if player == nil then return end
	record.Rank = player:GetAttribute("Level")
	record.Winstreak = player:GetAttribute("StatisticDuelsWinStreak")
	record.ELO = player:GetAttribute("DisplayELO")
	record.Status = player:GetAttribute("PlayerStatus")
	record.Influencer = player:GetAttribute("IsInfluencer") == true
	record.RobloxEmployee = player:GetAttribute("IsRobloxEmployee") == true

	record.Ammo = character:GetAttribute("Ammo")
	record.MaxAmmo = character:GetAttribute("MaxAmmo")
end

--- khook.lua:28002 -- the entity registry classifies workspace models by name:
--- "Dummy" and "Target" are their own lists, separate from players. The
--- shooting range is built out of them, so without this the combat features
--- have nothing to work on there.
local function ensureDummy(model)
	local record = Registry.byModel[model]
	if record then return record end
	record = {
		Player = nil, IsDummy = true,
		Character = nil, Root = nil, Head = nil, Humanoid = nil,
		Parts = {},
		Health = 0, MaxHealth = 100,
		Alive = false, Grounded = true,
		Team = nil, IsEnemy = true,
		Weapon = nil, Deflecting = false, Shielded = false,
		Rank = nil, Winstreak = nil,
		Distance = 0,
		_box = { X = 0, Y = 0, W = 0, H = 0, CenterX = 0, CenterY = 0, valid = false },
		_boxFrame = -1,
		_visible = false,
		_visFrame = -1,
	}
	Registry.byModel[model] = record
	bindCharacter(record, model)
	return record
end

--- Collects every live range dummy. The container is created by the range, so
--- its absence simply means we are not in one.
function Registry.dummies()
	local found = Registry._dummyList
	table.clear(found)
	local container = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ShootingRangeEntities")
	if container == nil then return found end
	for _, model in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
		if model:IsA("Model") and model.Name == "Dummy" then
			table.insert(found, model)
		end
	end
	return found
end

function Registry.start(trove)
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= LocalPlayer then ensureRecord(player) end
	end
	trove:Add(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
		if player ~= LocalPlayer then ensureRecord(player) end
	end))
	trove:Add(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(releaseRecord))

	if LocalPlayer then
		Registry.localRecord = ensureRecord(LocalPlayer)
		Registry.byPlayer[LocalPlayer] = Registry.localRecord
	end

	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		Registry.frame += 1
		local myTeam = Registry._me
		myTeam.id = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("TeamID")
		myTeam.env = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID")
		local camPos = Camera.CFrame.Position
		local list = Registry.list
		local n = 0

		for player, record in pairs(Registry.byPlayer) do
			if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Parent then
				if refreshRecord(record, myTeam, camPos) then
					n += 1
					list[n] = record
				end
			end
		end

		-- khook.lua:28002 -- range dummies join the same list, so every feature
		-- that walks records picks them up without knowing about them.
		for _, model in ipairs(Registry.dummies()) do
			local record = ensureDummy(model)
			if refreshRecord(record, myTeam, camPos) then
				n = n + 1
				list[n] = record
			end
		end

		-- trim without reallocating
		for i = Registry.count, n + 1, -1 do list[i] = nil end
		Registry.count = n

		if Registry.localRecord then
			refreshRecord(Registry.localRecord, myTeam, camPos)
		end

		slowAccumulator += dt
		if slowAccumulator >= 0.25 then
			slowAccumulator = 0
			for i = 1, n do refreshSlow(list[i]) end
		end
	end))

	trove:Add(function()
		table.clear(Registry.byPlayer)
		table.clear(Registry.byModel)
		table.clear(Registry.list)
		Registry.count = 0
	end)
end

------------------------------------------------------- cached derivations --
-- Line-of-sight and screen-space boxes are computed at most once per record
-- per frame, no matter how many features ask for them.

local visParams = RaycastParams.new()
visParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local visFilter = { nil, nil }

function Registry.isVisible(record)
	if record._visFrame == Registry.frame then return record._visible end
	record._visFrame = Registry.frame
	local head = record.Head
	if not head then record._visible = false return false end
	local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
	local target = head.Position
	local localChar = Registry.localRecord and Registry.localRecord.Character
	visFilter[1] = localChar
	visFilter[2] = record.Character
	visParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = visFilter
	local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, target - origin, visParams)
	record._visible = hit == nil
	return record._visible
end

-- eight corner offsets, allocated once
local BOX_OFFSETS = {
	Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1), Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
	Vector3.new(-1,  1, -1), Vector3.new(-1,  1, 1),
	Vector3.new( 1, -1, -1), Vector3.new( 1, -1, 1),
	Vector3.new( 1,  1, -1), Vector3.new( 1,  1, 1),
}

function Registry.box(record)
	local box = record._box
	if record._boxFrame == Registry.frame then return box.valid and box or nil end
	record._boxFrame = Registry.frame
	box.valid = false

	local root = record.Root
	if not root then return nil end
	local head = record.Head or root
	-- Derive the extent from root + head instead of GetBoundingBox(), which
	-- walks every descendant. Same rectangle, a fraction of the cost.
	local centre = root.Position
	local height = math.max(4, (head.Position.Y - centre.Y) * 2 + 2)
	local halfW, halfH, halfD = 2, height / 2, 1.5

	local minX, minY = math.huge, math.huge
	local maxX, maxY = -math.huge, -math.huge
	local cf = CFrame.new(centre)

	-- EVERY corner has to be in front of the camera. Accepting a box when only
	-- *some* corner was in front let the ones behind contribute their projected
	-- garbage: measured live, that produced a 204,891px box in an 801px
	-- viewport, and the head marker's radius (0.12 * width) then painted the
	-- whole screen.
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local o = BOX_OFFSETS[i]
		local world = cf * CFrame.new(o.X * halfW, o.Y * halfH, o.Z * halfD)
		local p = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(world.Position)
		if p.Z <= 0 then return nil end
		if p.X < minX then minX = p.X end
		if p.X > maxX then maxX = p.X end
		if p.Y < minY then minY = p.Y end
		if p.Y > maxY then maxY = p.Y end
	end

	-- a target straddling the near plane can still project absurdly; nothing
	-- legitimate is wider than the viewport
	local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
	if (maxX - minX) > vp.X * 2 or (maxY - minY) > vp.Y * 2 then return nil end

	-- Off-screen targets are culled here rather than downstream: khook's
	-- `esp.core` never keeps a pane tree laid out for something the camera
	-- cannot see, and the panes are real GUI objects, so leaving them at
	-- (-6525, -3433) costs a layout pass every frame for nothing.
	if maxX < 0 or minX > vp.X or maxY < 0 or minY > vp.Y then return nil end

	box.X, box.Y = minX, minY
	box.W, box.H = maxX - minX, maxY - minY
	box.CenterX, box.CenterY = (minX + maxX) / 2, (minY + maxY) / 2
	box.valid = true
	return box
end

function Registry.dynamicBox(record)
	local box = record._dynamicBox
	if box == nil then
		box = { X = 0, Y = 0, W = 0, H = 0, CenterX = 0, CenterY = 0, valid = false }
		record._dynamicBox = box
	end
	if record._dynamicBoxFrame == Registry.frame then return box.valid and box or nil end
	record._dynamicBoxFrame = Registry.frame
	box.valid = false
	local parts = record.DynamicParts
	if type(parts) ~= "table" or #parts == 0 then return Registry.box(record) end
	local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
	local any = false
	for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
		if part.Parent ~= nil and part.Transparency < 1 then
			local half = part.Size * 0.5
			for i = 1, 8 do
				local o = BOX_OFFSETS[i]
				local world = part.CFrame * Vector3.new(o.X * half.X, o.Y * half.Y, o.Z * half.Z)
				local p = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(world)
				if p.Z > 0 then
					any = true
					minX, minY = math.min(minX, p.X), math.min(minY, p.Y)
					maxX, maxY = math.max(maxX, p.X), math.max(maxY, p.Y)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if not any then return nil end
	local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
	if maxX < 0 or minX > vp.X or maxY < 0 or minY > vp.Y
		or maxX - minX > vp.X * 2 or maxY - minY > vp.Y * 2 then return nil end
	box.X, box.Y, box.W, box.H = minX, minY, maxX - minX, maxY - minY
	box.CenterX, box.CenterY = (minX + maxX) * 0.5, (minY + maxY) * 0.5
	box.valid = true
	return box
end
--============================================================================--
-- 7. GAME ADAPTER
--============================================================================--
-- Thin surface over the registry, plus the local-player helpers. Every feature
-- reads Registry.list; nothing rebuilds a player list of its own.

local Game = {}
Game.Trove = Trove.new("game")

local FEATURE_TROVE = Trove.new("features")

local function localCharacter()
	local r = Registry.localRecord
	local c = r and r.Character
	if c and c.Parent then return c end
	local fallback = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
	return (fallback and fallback.Parent) and fallback or nil
end

local function localRoot()
	local r = Registry.localRecord
	if r and r.Root and r.Root.Parent then return r.Root end
	local c = localCharacter()
	return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function localHumanoid()
	local r = Registry.localRecord
	if r and r.Humanoid and r.Humanoid.Parent then return r.Humanoid end
	local c = localCharacter()
	return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function readAttr(inst, name)
	if not inst then return nil end
	local ok, v = pcall(inst.GetAttribute, inst, name)
	return ok and v or nil
end

Game.entities = function() return Registry.list, Registry.count end
Game.isVisible = Registry.isVisible
Game.box = Registry.box

function Game.toScreen(worldPos)
	local p = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPos)
	return Vector2.new(p.X, p.Y), p.Z > 0, p.Z
end

--============================================================================--
-- Root desync
--============================================================================--
-- khook.lua:138949-139008. The ragebot never actually teleports the character:
-- it desynchronises the root. Just before replication the root is snapped to a
-- chosen CFrame and the real one is remembered; at the start of the next frame
-- the real CFrame is put back. Roblox samples the position for replication in
-- that window, so the SERVER sees the chosen spot while the client never
-- leaves. Writing the root directly instead makes the game rebuild the
-- character and drop the equipped weapon, which is what a real teleport looks
-- like to it.
--
-- khook binds the restore at RenderPriority.First (khook.lua:138983) and sets
-- the fake CFrame on the replication tick; the pair is reproduced here.

Rivals.Desync = {}
Rivals.Desync._cframe = nil
Rivals.Desync._oldCFrame = nil
Rivals.Desync._part = nil

--- khook.lua:138990 `SetServerCFrame` -- store only; nothing moves yet.
function Rivals.Desync.set(cframe)
	Rivals.Desync._cframe = cframe
end

function Rivals.Desync.clear()
	Rivals.Desync._cframe = nil
end

--- khook.lua:138994 -- where the server currently believes we are.
function Rivals.Desync.serverCFrame(root)
	return Rivals.Desync._cframe or (root and root.CFrame)
end

--- khook.lua:139002 -- where we really are.
function Rivals.Desync.clientCFrame(root)
	return Rivals.Desync._oldCFrame or (root and root.CFrame)
end

--- Runs on the replication tick: swap in the fake CFrame.
function Rivals.Desync.push(root)
	local cframe = Rivals.Desync._cframe
	if cframe == nil or root == nil then return end
	Rivals.Desync._oldCFrame = root.CFrame
	Rivals.Desync._part = root
	root.CFrame = cframe
end

--- khook.lua:138940 -- runs at the top of the next frame: put the real one back.
function Rivals.Desync.restore()
	local old = Rivals.Desync._oldCFrame
	if old == nil then return end
	local part = Rivals.Desync._part
	Rivals.Desync._oldCFrame = nil
	if part ~= nil and part.Parent ~= nil then
		part.CFrame = old
	end
end

local Features = {}
Features.binds = {}
--- khook.lua:88649 `_playerTags:GetPlayersWith("Hacker")` -- filled by the
--- hacker detector, read by the ragebot's PrioritizeHackers.
Features.hackerTags = {}
Features.status = {}   -- name -> "ok" | "unavailable: <reason>"

local function markUnavailable(name, reason)
	Features.status[name] = "unavailable: " .. reason
end

------------------------------------------------------- shared target logic --

local Targeting = {}
Targeting._effective = {}

--- khook.lua:2611-2868. Gun-specific settings are not stored below each
--- Target block. They are feature overlays under
--- GunSpecific[weapon][feature], enabled by
--- GunSpecific[weapon].Active[feature] and the feature's master switch.
function Targeting._overlay(dst, src)
	if type(dst) ~= "table" or type(src) ~= "table" then return end
	for key, value in pairs(src) do
		if key ~= "Active" then
			if type(value) == "table" and type(dst[key]) == "table" then
				Targeting._overlay(dst[key], value)
			else
				dst[key] = deepCopy(value)
			end
		end
	end
end

function Targeting.effectiveConfig(name)
	local base = Config.data[name]
	if type(base) ~= "table" then return base end
	local master = base.GunSpecific
	if type(master) ~= "table" or master.Enabled ~= true then return base end

	local itemName = Targeting.heldItemName and Targeting.heldItemName() or nil
	local className = Targeting.baseWeapon and Targeting.baseWeapon(itemName) or nil
	local profiles = Config.data.GunSpecific
	local chosen, chosenKey = nil, nil
	if type(profiles) == "table" then
		for _, key in ipairs({ itemName, className }) do
			local profile = key and profiles[key] or nil
			local active = type(profile) == "table" and profile.Active or nil
			if type(active) == "table" and active[name] == true
				and type(profile[name]) == "table" then
				chosen, chosenKey = profile[name], key
				break
			end
		end
	end
	if chosen == nil then return base end

	local cache = Targeting._effective[name]
	if cache == nil then
		cache = { data = {} }
		Targeting._effective[name] = cache
	end
	if cache.revision ~= Config.revision or cache.profile ~= chosenKey
		or cache.base ~= base or cache.overlay ~= chosen then
		cache.data = deepCopy(base)
		Targeting._overlay(cache.data, chosen)
		cache.revision, cache.profile = Config.revision, chosenKey
		cache.base, cache.overlay = base, chosen
	end
	return cache.data
end

--- khook.lua:3288-3380. Tests whether a shot origin lies inside a defender's
--- horizontal and vertical protection arcs.
function Targeting._insideArc(root, center, point, cameraPitch, horizontal, down, up)
	if root == nil then return false end
	local delta = root.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(point - center)
	local azimuth = math.atan2(delta.X, -delta.Z)
	local pitch = (cameraPitch + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi
	local elevation = math.atan2(delta.Y,
		math.sqrt(delta.X * delta.X + delta.Z * delta.Z))
	local difference = pitch - elevation
	return math.abs(azimuth) <= horizontal
		and difference >= -down and difference <= up
end

function Targeting.shooterOrigin()
	-- The helper body is lost in the decompile. Its surviving FOV counterpart
	-- and Rivals' live ClientGun both obtain their origin from ClientViewModel.
	-- Protection checks still need a usable origin between equips, hence the
	-- same camera fallback used by the game when no rendered muzzle is present.
	return Rivals.muzzlePosition() or (Camera and Camera.CFrame.Position or nil)
end

function Targeting.isProtected(record, origin, checkDeflect, checkShield)
	if not (checkDeflect or checkShield) or record == nil or not record.Alive
		or origin == nil then return false end
	local root = record.Root
	if root == nil then return false end

	local center = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.75, 0)
	local fighter = record.Player and Rivals.fighterFor(record.Player) or nil
	local rotation = nil
	if fighter ~= nil then
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return fighter:GetCameraRotation() end)
		if ok then rotation = value end
		if rotation == nil then
			local data = Rivals.field(fighter, "Data")
			rotation = Rivals.field(data, "CameraRotation")
		end
	end
	local cameraPitch = 0
	if rotation ~= nil then
		pcall(function() cameraPitch = rotation.X end)
	end

	local equipped = fighter and Rivals.field(fighter, "EquippedItem") or nil
	local itemName = equipped and Rivals.field(equipped, "Name") or nil
	local isMelee = equipped ~= nil and Rivals.itemType(equipped) == "Melee"
	local deflecting = record.Deflecting == true
	local attacking = false
	if isMelee then
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return equipped:IsDeflecting() end)
		if ok and value ~= nil then deflecting = value == true end
		ok, value = pcall(function() return equipped:IsAttacking() end)
		if ok and value ~= nil then attacking = value == true end
	end

	if checkDeflect and isMelee and deflecting
		and Targeting._insideArc(root, center, origin, cameraPitch,
			0.776, 0.776, 0.776) then
		return true
	end
	if checkShield and itemName == "Riot Shield" and not attacking
		and Targeting._insideArc(root, center, origin, cameraPitch,
			1.167, 1.17, 1.16) then
		return true
	end

	if checkShield and itemName ~= "Riot Shield" and fighter ~= nil then
		local hasShield = false
		local items = Rivals.field(fighter, "Items")
		if type(items) == "table" then
			for _, item in pairs(items) do
				if Rivals.field(item, "Name") == "Riot Shield" then
					hasShield = true
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if hasShield then
			local behind = center + (center - origin)
			if Targeting._insideArc(root, center, behind, cameraPitch,
				0.774, 0.8, 0.8) then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
	return false
end

--- khook.lua:100137-100177. Rivals fighters carry five hit volumes, and which
--- ones are live depends on the weapon: a sniper's shot is tested against the
--- *Small pair, so khook keeps a per-weapon profile that EXCLUDES
--- HitboxHead/HitboxBody/PhysicalHitboxHead and defaults to
--- HitboxHeadSmall/HitboxBodySmall. Verified against the running game -- all
--- five parts exist, and the small pair really is smaller (head 1.75^3 vs 2^3,
--- body 2.75x4x1.75 vs 3.5x4x2.5).
---
--- Without this an aimbot holding a sniper aims at a volume the server does not
--- test, which is the single biggest behavioural gap against khook.
local HitboxProfiles = {}

HitboxProfiles.DEFAULT = {
	ExcludedParts = {},
	DefaultParts = { HitboxHead = true, HitboxBody = true },
	AvailableOptions = { "HitboxHead", "HitboxBody" },
}

HitboxProfiles.Sniper = {
	ExcludedParts = { HitboxHead = true, HitboxBody = true, PhysicalHitboxHead = true },
	DefaultParts = { HitboxHeadSmall = true, HitboxBodySmall = true },
	AvailableOptions = { "HitboxHeadSmall", "HitboxBodySmall" },
}

--- khook keys the profile table by weapon category. Every sniper skin in the
--- ItemLibrary keeps "Sniper" in its name ("Kraken Sniper", "Glorious Sniper",
--- ...), and the ViewModels entries carry no base-weapon field, so the name is
--- what there is to match on.
function HitboxProfiles.get(weaponName)
	if type(weaponName) == "string" and string.find(weaponName, "Sniper", 1, true) then
		return HitboxProfiles.Sniper
	end
	return HitboxProfiles.DEFAULT
end

--- khook.lua:100154 `ResolveEffectiveParts`. A configured selection survives
--- only if at least one enabled part is not excluded by the profile; otherwise
--- the profile's own defaults take over. That is why picking "Head" and then
--- swapping to a sniper silently retargets rather than aiming at nothing.
function HitboxProfiles.resolve(configured, profile)
	if type(configured) == "table" then
		for name, on in pairs(configured) do
			if on and profile.ExcludedParts[name] == nil then return configured end
		end
	end
	return profile.DefaultParts
end

--- khook.lua:70708 -- the equipped weapon's name comes off the item object.
--- Rivals never puts the weapon in a Tool, so there is nothing to read there.
local function heldItemName()
	return Rivals.equippedName()
end
Targeting.heldItemName = heldItemName

--- Base weapon behind a possibly-skinned item name. `ItemOrder` holds the base
--- names; every skin in `ViewModels` keeps its base name as a suffix ("Kraken
--- Sniper", "Glorious Handgun"), and there is no back-reference field to use
--- instead, so the longest matching suffix wins.
-- cache + name list hang off HitboxProfiles: the main chunk is at Lua's
-- 200-local ceiling, so new state has to share an existing table
HitboxProfiles.baseCache = {}
HitboxProfiles.baseNames = nil
local function baseWeapon(itemName)
	if type(itemName) ~= "string" then return nil end
	local hit = HitboxProfiles.baseCache[itemName]
	if hit ~= nil then return hit ~= false and hit or nil end

	if HitboxProfiles.baseNames == nil then
		HitboxProfiles.baseNames = {}
		local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
		local module = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary")
		if module then
			local ok, lib = pcall(require, module)
			if ok and typeof(lib) == "table" and typeof(lib.ItemOrder) == "table" then
				for i = 1, 128 do
					local n = lib.ItemOrder[i]
					if type(n) == "string" then table.insert(HitboxProfiles.baseNames, n) end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local best = nil
	for _, n in ipairs(HitboxProfiles.baseNames) do
		if itemName == n or string.sub(itemName, -#n - 1) == " " .. n then
			if best == nil or #n > #best then best = n end
		end
	end
	HitboxProfiles.baseCache[itemName] = best or false
	return best
end
Targeting.baseWeapon = baseWeapon

--- The target-part set in force right now: a weapon's active overlay if it has
--- one, otherwise the shared block (khook.lua:125674).
local function effectiveParts(profile, groupKey, weapon)
	local overlays = profile.PerWeapon
	if weapon and type(overlays) == "table" then
		local entry = overlays[weapon]
		if type(entry) == "table" and entry.Active and type(entry[groupKey]) == "table" then
			return entry[groupKey]
		end
	end
	return profile.TargetParts[groupKey]
end

--- Which hitbox a profile allows for this record, split by airborne state and
--- filtered through the held weapon's profile.
local function allowedPart(profile, record, wantHead)
	local held = heldItemName()
	local configured = effectiveParts(profile, record.Grounded and "Ground" or "Air", baseWeapon(held))
	local hp = HitboxProfiles.get(held)
	local parts = HitboxProfiles.resolve(configured, hp)
	if wantHead then
		if parts.HitboxHeadSmall then return record.HitboxHeadSmall or record.HitboxHead or record.Head end
		if not parts.HitboxHead then return nil end
		return record.HitboxHead or record.Head
	end
	if parts.HitboxBodySmall then return record.HitboxBodySmall or record.HitboxBody or record.Root end
	if not parts.HitboxBody then return nil end
	return record.HitboxBody or record.Root
end

local function passesConditions(profile, record, origin)
	local c = profile.Conditions
	if c.Vulnerable and record.Invincible then return false end
	if origin ~= nil and Targeting.isProtected(record, origin,
		c.NotDeflecting, c.NotShielded) then return false end
	if c.Visible and not Registry.isVisible(record) then return false end
	return true
end

--- Nearest-to-crosshair target inside the FOV. Allocation-free: the caller
--- gets the record and part back directly, not a table.
function Targeting.pick(profile, fovSettings, radiusOverride, ignoreLock)
	-- khook.lua:114286 `f7878` -- target lock sits in FRONT of the selector,
	-- not beside it. A live lock is returned outright, and with Lock Only set
	-- the selector yields nothing at all rather than falling back to whoever is
	-- nearest. khookclone kept the lock as a standalone feature, so the aimbot
	-- ignored it completely and kept chasing the closest enemy.
	if not ignoreLock then
		local lockCfg = Config.get({ "TargetLock" })
		if lockCfg ~= nil and lockCfg.Enabled then
			local getter = Features.getLockedTarget
			local locked = getter and getter() or nil
			if locked ~= nil and (not locked.IsEnemy
				or (locked.Player ~= nil and Registry.byPlayer[locked.Player] ~= locked)) then
				if Features.setLockedTarget then Features.setLockedTarget(nil) end
				locked = nil
			end
			if locked ~= nil then
				if locked.Alive and passesConditions(profile, locked,
					Targeting.shooterOrigin()) then
					local part = allowedPart(profile, locked, true)
						or allowedPart(profile, locked, false)
					if part ~= nil then return locked, part, 0 end
				end
				-- SelectAware returns SelectPlayer's result directly. An invalid
				-- locked player therefore blocks fallback selection without losing
				-- the lock itself.
				return nil
			end
			if lockCfg.LockOnly then return nil end
		end
	end

	local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
	local cx, cy = vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5
	local radius = radiusOverride
	if not radius then
		radius = (fovSettings and fovSettings.Enabled == false)
			and math.huge or (fovSettings and fovSettings.Circle.Radius or 150)
	end
	local bestRecord, bestPart, bestDist = nil, nil, radius
	local origin = Targeting.shooterOrigin()

	local list, n = Registry.list, Registry.count
	for i = 1, n do
		local record = list[i]
		if record.IsEnemy and record.Alive and passesConditions(profile, record, origin) then
			-- khook.lua:114233/114238 -- `_SelectClosestFromState` weighs every
			-- allowed part, `_SelectRandomFromState` commits to one up front
			-- with the Head (%) bias (khook.lua:17880).
			local sel = profile.PartSelection
			local wantHeadOnly = nil
			if sel ~= nil and sel.Type == "Random" then
				wantHeadOnly = (math.random(100) <= (sel.HeadPercent or 50))
			end
			for pass = 1, 2 do
				local part = (wantHeadOnly == nil or wantHeadOnly == (pass == 1))
					and allowedPart(profile, record, pass == 1) or nil
				if part then
					local p = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
					if p.Z > 0 then
						local dx, dy = p.X - cx, p.Y - cy
						local d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
						if d < bestDist then
							bestRecord, bestPart, bestDist = record, part, d
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if lockCfg ~= nil and lockCfg.Enabled and lockCfg.Mode == "Unlock"
		and (Features.getLockedTarget == nil or Features.getLockedTarget() == nil)
		and bestRecord ~= nil and Features.setLockedTarget ~= nil then
		Features.setLockedTarget(bestRecord)
	end
	return bestRecord, bestPart, bestDist
end

--- Aim point inside the chosen part, honouring PartSelection / Surface.
function Targeting.aimPoint(profile, record, part)
	if profile.Type == "Surface" then
		local surface = profile.Surface
		if surface.Center ~= false then return part.Position end
		local reduce = 1 - (surface.ReductionPercentage or 0) / 100
		local size = part.Size
		return (part.CFrame * CFrame.new(
			(math.random() - 0.5) * size.X * reduce,
			(math.random() - 0.5) * size.Y * reduce,
			(math.random() - 0.5) * size.Z * reduce)).Position
	end
	-- Part choice belongs to the selector (khook splits SelectBest from the
	-- Closest/Random part pick), so re-rolling it here would fight it.
	return part.Position
end

--============================================================================--
-- View angle driver
--============================================================================--
-- khook never assigns Camera.CFrame (khook.lua:25557). Every aim feature puts
-- its desired angle into a *slot*; _Resolve picks one winner by priority, and
-- Flush applies it once per frame. Without this, the aimbot, target lock,
-- ragebot and flickbot all write the camera in the same frame and fight each
-- other -- which is exactly the jitter that makes a clone feel wrong.
--
-- khook additionally forwards the winning angle to Rivals'
-- Remotes.Replication.Fighter.UpdateCameraRotation so the server sees it. We
-- only do that when the remote is present and the caller opts in, because the
-- payload shape is the one thing the decompile does not spell out.

local ViewAngles = {}
ViewAngles.slots = {}          -- slot -> { cf = CFrame, priority = n }
ViewAngles.winning = nil
ViewAngles.dirty = false
ViewAngles.suppressed = false

--- Higher priority wins; ties go to whichever slot was set most recently.
--- These are khookclone's own: khook's ragebot is the only thing that touches
--- a view-angle driver, and it uses `Rivals.ViewAngles` (slot 20) to spoof the
--- SERVER's idea of the angle. What is arbitrated here is the LOCAL camera,
--- which the aim features have to share between them.
local SLOT_PRIORITY = {
	Flickbot = 40,
	TargetLock = 20,
	Aimbot = 10,
}

function ViewAngles.Send(slot, cf)
	local entry = ViewAngles.slots[slot]
	if cf == nil then
		if entry then ViewAngles.slots[slot] = nil ViewAngles.dirty = true end
		return
	end
	if entry and entry.cf == cf then return end
	ViewAngles.slots[slot] = { cf = cf, priority = SLOT_PRIORITY[slot] or 0 }
	ViewAngles.dirty = true
end

function ViewAngles.Clear(slot)
	ViewAngles.Send(slot, nil)
end

function ViewAngles.ClearAll()
	table.clear(ViewAngles.slots)
	ViewAngles.winning = nil
	ViewAngles.dirty = false
end

local function resolveAngles()
	local best, bestPriority = nil, -math.huge
	for _, entry in pairs(ViewAngles.slots) do
		if entry.priority > bestPriority then
			best, bestPriority = entry, entry.priority
		end
	end
	ViewAngles.winning = best
end

function ViewAngles.Flush()
	if not ViewAngles.dirty or ViewAngles.suppressed then return end
	ViewAngles.dirty = false
	resolveAngles()
	local winning = ViewAngles.winning
	if winning == nil then return end
	local controller = Rivals.cameraController()
	if controller ~= nil then
		local pitch, yaw = winning.cf:ToOrientation()
		local ok = pcall(function()
			controller:SetRotation(Vector2.new(pitch, yaw))
		end)
		if ok then return end
	end
	Camera.CFrame = winning.cf
end

function ViewAngles.init(trove)
	-- Applied at RenderStepped priority so it lands after the game's own
	-- camera update rather than being overwritten by it.
	trove:Add(RunService:BindToRenderStep("\0kc_viewangles\0",
		Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
			ViewAngles.Flush()
		end))
	trove:Add(function()
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("\0kc_viewangles\0") end)
		ViewAngles.ClearAll()
	end)
	Features.status.viewangles = Rivals.ViewAngles.remote() ~= nil
		and "ok (local camera + UpdateCameraRotation bound)"
		or "ok (local camera only -- UpdateCameraRotation not in this place)"
end

------------------------------------------------------------- FOV rendering --

local FovCircle = {}

function FovCircle.init(trove)
	local baseAimNode = Config.node({ "Aimbot" })
	local baseSilentNode = Config.node({ "SilentAim" })

	-- khook.lua:117224-117347. Each combat feature owns a distinct ring. The
	-- stock ring is a Frame with independent fill/stroke gradients; both can be
	-- visible simultaneously and maintain separate animation phases.
	local function newRenderer()
		local circle = make("Frame", {
			Visible = false, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			ZIndex = 49, Parent = UI.Gui,
		})
		corner(circle, 999)
		make("UIAspectRatioConstraint", { AspectRatio = 1, Parent = circle })
		local fillGradient = make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Parent = circle })
		local stroke = make("UIStroke", {
			ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = circle,
		})
		local strokeGradient = make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Parent = stroke })
		local custom = make("ImageLabel", {
			Visible = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32),
			ZIndex = 50, Parent = UI.Gui,
		})
		trove:Add(circle)
		trove:Add(custom)

		local renderer = {}
		function renderer.hide()
			circle.Visible = false
			custom.Visible = false
		end
		function renderer.update(settings, followPosition, dt)
			local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
			local node = settings.Circle
			local centre = (node.FollowTarget and followPosition)
				or Vector2.new(vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5)
			if node.FromMuzzle then
				local origin = Rivals.muzzlePosition()
				if origin ~= nil then
					local projected = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(origin)
					if projected.Z > 0 then centre = Vector2.new(projected.X, projected.Y) end
				end
			end

			if settings.Kind == "Custom" then
				circle.Visible = false
				local image = settings.Custom
				if image.Visible and image.AssetId ~= "" then
					local asset = tostring(image.AssetId)
					if not string.find(asset, "^rbxasset") then asset = "rbxassetid://" .. asset end
					custom.Image = asset
					custom.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(centre.X, centre.Y)
					local size = math.max(1, 32 * (image.ScaleFactor or 1))
					custom.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
					custom.Rotation = image.Rotation or 0
					custom.ImageColor3 = image.Color
					custom.ImageTransparency = image.Transparency
					custom.Visible = true
				else
					custom.Visible = false
				end
				return
			end

			custom.Visible = false
			if not node.Visible then circle.Visible = false return end
			if node.Animated.Enabled then
				strokeGradient.Rotation = (strokeGradient.Rotation + node.Animated.Speed * dt) % 360
				fillGradient.Rotation = (fillGradient.Rotation - node.Animated.Speed * dt) % 360
			end
			local radius = node.Radius or 150
			circle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(centre.X, centre.Y)
			circle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(radius * 2, radius * 2)
			stroke.Thickness = node.Stroke.Thickness
			stroke.Transparency = node.Stroke.Transparency
			strokeGradient.Color = node.Stroke.Gradient
			circle.BackgroundTransparency = node.Fill.Enabled and node.Fill.Transparency or 1
			fillGradient.Enabled = node.Fill.Enabled
			fillGradient.Color = node.Fill.Gradient
			circle.Visible = true
		end
		return renderer
	end

	local aimRenderer, silentRenderer = newRenderer(), newRenderer()
	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		for _, source in ipairs({
			{ name = "Aimbot", base = baseAimNode, renderer = aimRenderer,
				follow = Features.lastAimScreen },
			{ name = "SilentAim", base = baseSilentNode, renderer = silentRenderer,
				follow = Features.lastSilentScreen },
		}) do
			local cfg = Targeting.effectiveConfig(source.name) or source.base
			local bind = Features.binds[source.name]
			local active = cfg.Enabled and cfg.FovSettings.Enabled
				and Features.hasEquippedGun() and (bind == nil or bind:IsActive())
			if active then source.renderer.update(cfg.FovSettings, source.follow, dt)
			else source.renderer.hide() end
		end
	end))
	Features.status.fov = "ok (GUI gradient ring + live muzzle origin)"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Motion -- human-movement models recovered from khook
--============================================================================--
-- khook's flick trajectory is a Sigma-Lognormal model (Plamondon's kinematic
-- theory of rapid human movement): a primary lognormal stroke plus corrective
-- strokes, with Fitts's-law timing, curvature, Ornstein-Uhlenbeck drift,
-- physiological tremor and signal-dependent noise. Every piece below is a
-- direct port -- generator at khook.lua:40351-40499, profile at 72303-72325,
-- defaults at 72392-72423, playback at 30290-30309.
--
-- The aim-smoothing bodies (linear/ai/spring/sigmaMouse) are the one part the
-- decompiler destroyed -- khook.lua:116741-116748 are all empty. What survives
-- is the dispatch (116750), which fixes each mode's space and state:
--     linear(screen, Speed)              -- screen space, stateless
--     ai(screen, target, mode, st.ml)    -- screen space, stateful
--     spring(world, target, mode, st.spring)   -- WORLD space, stateful
--     sigmaMouse(screen, target, mode, st.sigma)
-- plus Speed being a 0..1 alpha (proved by the legacy migration at 27567-27586,
-- which folds Speed/Smoothing*Strength into a single clamped Speed).
-- Those four are reconstructions honouring that contract, not ports.

local Motion = {}

------------------------------------------------------------------ statistics --

local TWO_PI = 2 * math.pi
Motion.SQRT2 = math.sqrt(2)
Motion.SQRT2PI = math.sqrt(2 * math.pi)
-- max of x^2*(1-x)^3 is at x = 2/5, giving 0.03456 -- normalises the bump to 1
Motion.BUMP_NORM = 0.03456

--- Abramowitz & Stegun 7.1.26 error function (khook.lua:1328).
function Motion.erf(x)
	local sign = x < 0 and -1 or 1
	local a = math.abs(x)
	local t = 1 / (1 + 0.3275911 * a)
	return sign * (1 - (0.254829592 * t
		- 0.284496736 * t * t
		+ 1.421413741 * t ^ 3
		- 1.453152027 * t ^ 4
		+ 1.061405429 * t ^ 5) * math.exp(-a * a))
end

--- Standard normal CDF (khook.lua:72329).
function Motion.normalCdf(x)
	return 0.5 * (1 + Motion.erf(x / Motion.SQRT2))
end

--- Lognormal CDF -- a stroke's displacement profile (khook.lua:72333).
function Motion.lognCdf(t, t0, mu, sigma)
	if t <= t0 then return 0 end
	return Motion.normalCdf((math.log(t - t0) - mu) / sigma)
end

--- Lognormal PDF -- a stroke's velocity profile (khook.lua:72340).
function Motion.lognPdf(t, t0, mu, sigma)
	if t <= t0 then return 0 end
	local d = t - t0
	local z = (math.log(d) - mu) / sigma
	return 1 / (sigma * Motion.SQRT2PI * d) * math.exp(-0.5 * z * z)
end

--- Curvature envelope over stroke progress, peaking at 1 (khook.lua:72349).
function Motion.bump(x)
	if x <= 0 or x >= 1 then return 0 end
	return x * x * (1 - x) * (1 - x) * (1 - x) / Motion.BUMP_NORM
end

--- Direction-dependent curvature gain (khook.lua:1337). Human arcs are wider
--- on diagonal sweeps than on pure horizontal or vertical ones.
function Motion.directionGain(angle)
	return 0.5 + 0.8 * math.abs(math.sin(angle)) - 0.15 * math.abs(math.cos(angle))
end

--- Box-Muller normal sample (khook.lua:72358).
function Motion.gauss(rng, mu, sigma)
	local u1 = rng:NextNumber()
	local u2 = rng:NextNumber()
	if u1 < 1e-15 then u1 = 1e-15 end
	return mu + sigma * (math.sqrt(-2 * math.log(u1)) * math.cos(TWO_PI * u2))
end

--- Marsaglia-Tsang gamma sample (khook.lua:72367); inter-sample timing jitter.
function Motion.gamma(rng, shape, scale)
	local boost = 1
	if shape < 1 then
		boost = rng:NextNumber() ^ (1 / shape)
		shape = shape + 1
	end
	local a = shape - 1 / 3
	local c = math.sqrt(9 * a)
	while true do
		local z = Motion.gauss(rng, 0, 1)
		local v = (1 + z / c) ^ 3
		if v > 0 then
			local u = rng:NextNumber()
			if u < 1e-15 then u = 1e-15 end
			if u < 1 - 0.0331 * z * z * z * z
				or math.log(u) < 0.5 * z * z + a * (1 - v + math.log(v)) then
				return a * v * scale * boost
			end
		end
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------------- spring --

-- khook builds these as new(value, 15, 1) (khook.lua:70303-70317): frequency
-- 15, damping ratio 1, i.e. critically damped -- converges without overshoot.
Motion.SPRING_FREQ = 15
Motion.SPRING_DAMPING = 1

function Motion.newSpring(value, freq)
	return { p = value, v = value * 0, f = freq or Motion.SPRING_FREQ }
end

--- Exact critically-damped step. Type-generic: works on numbers, Vector2 and
--- Vector3 alike, which is what khook needs (screen points and world dirs).
function Motion.stepSpring(state, goal, dt, scale)
	local w = state.f * (scale or 1)
	if w <= 0 or dt <= 0 then return state.p end
	local e = math.exp(-w * dt)
	local delta = state.p - goal
	local vwd = state.v + delta * w
	state.p = goal + (delta + vwd * dt) * e
	state.v = (state.v - vwd * (w * dt)) * e
	return state.p
end

---------------------------------------------------- sigma-lognormal profiles --

--- khook's baseline profile (khook.lua:72392-72423).
function Motion.newSigmaProfile()
	return {
		fittsA = 50, fittsB = 100,
		targetWidth = 20,
		undershootMin = 0.92, undershootMax = 0.97,
		peakTimeRatio = 0.35,
		primarySigmaMin = 0.18, primarySigmaMax = 0.28,
		overshootProb = 0.15, overshootMin = 1.02, overshootMax = 1.08,
		correctionSigmaMin = 0.12, correctionSigmaMax = 0.2,
		secondCorrectionProb = 0.25,
		curvatureScale = 0.025,
		ouTheta = 3.5, ouSigma = 1.2,
		tremorFreqMin = 8, tremorFreqMax = 12,
		tremorAmpMin = 0.15, tremorAmpMax = 0.55,
		sdnK = 0.04,
		sampleDtMean = 7.8, gammaShape = 3.5,
		rng = Random.new(),
	}
end

--- Flickbot's override of that profile (khook.lua:72303). Tighter, faster and
--- far less noisy than the baseline; humanness and curvature come from the UI.
--- Note khook clamps curvature to 0..50 even though the slider runs to 100.
function Motion.applyFlickProfile(p, durationMs, curvature, humanness)
	local d = math.max(durationMs, 20)
	p.fittsA = d / 4
	p.fittsB = d / 4
	p.targetWidth = 80
	p.peakTimeRatio = 0.32
	p.primarySigmaMin = 0.2
	p.primarySigmaMax = 0.26
	p.undershootMin = 0.97
	p.undershootMax = 1
	p.overshootProb = 0.08
	p.overshootMin = 1.01
	p.overshootMax = 1.04
	p.secondCorrectionProb = 0
	p.correctionSigmaMin = 0.1
	p.correctionSigmaMax = 0.14
	p.curvatureScale = clamp(curvature, 0, 50) / 1000
	local h = clamp(humanness, 0, 100) / 30
	p.ouSigma = 0.5 * h
	p.tremorAmpMin = 0.05 * h
	p.tremorAmpMax = 0.18 * h
	p.sdnK = 0.02
	return p
end

--- Bake a screen-space trajectory from (x0,y0) to (x1,y1).
--- Returns an array of { x, y, t } with t in milliseconds (khook.lua:40351).
function Motion.generate(p, x0, y0, x1, y1)
	local rng = p.rng
	local function uni(a, b) return a + rng:NextNumber() * (b - a) end
	local function nrm(mu, sigma) return Motion.gauss(rng, mu, sigma) end

	local dx, dy = x1 - x0, y1 - y0
	local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
	if dist < 1 then return {} end
	local angle = math.atan2(dy, dx)
	local ux, uy = dx / dist, dy / dist

	-- Fitts's law: movement time grows with the log of the difficulty index.
	local id = math.log(dist / p.targetWidth + 1)
	local totalMs = math.max((p.fittsA + p.fittsB * (id / math.log(2))) * math.exp(nrm(0, 0.08)), 80)

	-- Primary stroke: usually falls slightly short, occasionally overshoots.
	local ratio = uni(0, 1) < p.overshootProb
		and uni(p.overshootMin, p.overshootMax)
		or uni(p.undershootMin, p.undershootMax)
	local primaryD = dist * ratio
	local primarySigma = uni(p.primarySigmaMin, p.primarySigmaMax)
	local primaryMu = math.log(totalMs * uni(p.peakTimeRatio - 0.03, p.peakTimeRatio + 0.03))
		+ primarySigma * primarySigma

	-- Corrective strokes closing whatever the primary stroke left over.
	local strokes = {}
	local residual = dist - primaryD
	if math.abs(residual) > 0.5 then
		local sgn = residual > 0 and 1 or -1
		local d2 = math.abs(residual) * uni(0.88, 1.02)
		local s2 = uni(p.correctionSigmaMin, p.correctionSigmaMax)
		table.insert(strokes, {
			D = d2,
			t0 = totalMs * uni(0.55, 0.68),
			mu = math.log(totalMs * uni(0.12, 0.18)) + s2 * s2,
			sigma = s2,
			dirX = ux * sgn, dirY = uy * sgn,
		})
		local residual2 = residual - d2 * sgn
		if math.abs(residual2) > 0.3 and uni(0, 1) < p.secondCorrectionProb then
			local sgn2 = residual2 > 0 and 1 or -1
			local s3 = uni(0.1, 0.16)
			table.insert(strokes, {
				D = math.abs(residual2) * uni(0.85, 1.05),
				t0 = totalMs * uni(0.78, 0.88),
				mu = math.log(totalMs * uni(0.08, 0.12)) + s3 * s3,
				sigma = s3,
				dirX = ux * sgn2, dirY = uy * sgn2,
			})
		end
	end

	local curveAmp = dist * p.curvatureScale * Motion.directionGain(angle) * nrm(0, 1)
	local tremorFreq = uni(p.tremorFreqMin, p.tremorFreqMax)
	local tremorAmp = uni(p.tremorAmpMin, p.tremorAmpMax)
	local phaseX, phaseY = uni(0, TWO_PI), uni(0, TWO_PI)
	local endMs = totalMs * 1.15

	-- Sample instants: gamma-distributed gaps, like a real polling device.
	local times = {}
	local acc = 0
	while acc < endMs do
		acc = acc + clamp(Motion.gamma(rng, p.gammaShape, p.sampleDtMean / p.gammaShape), 2, 25)
		if acc <= endMs + 15 then table.insert(times, acc) end
	end

	local out = {}
	local ouX, ouY = 0, 0
	for i = 1, #times do
		local t = times[i]
		local gapMs = i > 1 and (t - times[i - 1]) or p.sampleDtMean
		local gap = gapMs / 1000

		-- Displacement: primary stroke + perpendicular arc + corrections.
		local prog = Motion.lognCdf(t, 0, primaryMu, primarySigma)
		local arc = curveAmp * Motion.bump(prog)
		local x = x0 + ux * primaryD * prog + -uy * arc
		local y = y0 + uy * primaryD * prog + ux * arc

		-- Instantaneous speed, for signal-dependent noise and tremor damping.
		local speed = primaryD * Motion.lognPdf(t, 0, primaryMu, primarySigma)
		for j = 1, #strokes do
			local s = strokes[j]
			local c = Motion.lognCdf(t, s.t0, s.mu, s.sigma)
			x = x + s.dirX * s.D * c
			y = y + s.dirY * s.D * c
			speed = speed + s.D * Motion.lognPdf(t, s.t0, s.mu, s.sigma)
		end

		-- Ornstein-Uhlenbeck drift: slow, correlated wander around the path.
		ouX = ouX + (-p.ouTheta * ouX * gap + p.ouSigma * math.sqrt(gap) * nrm(0, 1))
		ouY = ouY + (-p.ouTheta * ouY * gap + p.ouSigma * math.sqrt(gap) * nrm(0, 1))

		-- Physiological tremor, suppressed while the hand is moving fast.
		local ts = t / 1000
		local damp = 1 / (1 + speed * 0.3)
		table.insert(out, {
			x = x + ouX + tremorAmp * damp * math.sin(TWO_PI * tremorFreq * ts + phaseX)
				+ p.sdnK * speed * nrm(0, 1),
			y = y + ouY + tremorAmp * damp * math.sin(TWO_PI * tremorFreq * ts + phaseY)
				+ p.sdnK * speed * nrm(0, 1),
			t = t,
		})
	end
	return out
end

--============================================================================--
-- Aimbot
--============================================================================--

function Features.Aimbot(trove)
	local baseCfg = Config.node({ "Aimbot" })
	local lastSwitch, reactionUntil = 0, 0
	local current = nil
	-- Per-mode smoothing state. khook keeps one bag per mode (st.ml, st.spring,
	-- st.sigma at khook.lua:116750) and it must not survive a target change.
	local springDir, aiLast, sigmaState = nil, nil, nil

	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		local cfg = Targeting.effectiveConfig("Aimbot") or baseCfg
		local mode = cfg.Mode
		Features.lastAimScreen = nil
		local function stand_down()
			current = nil
			springDir, aiLast, sigmaState = nil, nil, nil
			ViewAngles.Clear("Aimbot")
		end
		if UI.Visible or not cfg.Enabled then stand_down() return end
		local bind = Features.binds.Aimbot
		if bind and not bind:IsActive() then stand_down() return end
		-- khook: the aimbot only runs with a gun equipped (khook.lua:134430)
		if not Features.hasEquippedGun() then stand_down() return end
		if cfg.EnableOnlyOnScope and not Features.isScoped() then
			ViewAngles.Clear("Aimbot")
			return
		end
		if cfg.DisableOnReload and Features.isReloading() then
			ViewAngles.Clear("Aimbot")
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		local record, part = current, nil

		local keep = false
		if record and record.Alive and passesConditions(cfg.Target, record,
			Targeting.shooterOrigin()) and (now - lastSwitch) < cfg.SwitchDelay then
			part = allowedPart(cfg.Target, record, true) or allowedPart(cfg.Target, record, false)
			keep = part ~= nil
		end
		if not keep then
			record, part = Targeting.pick(cfg.Target, cfg.FovSettings)
			if record ~= current then
				lastSwitch = now
				reactionUntil = now + cfg.ReactionTime
				current = record
				-- A screen velocity measured against the old target is noise.
				-- The spring keeps its state on purpose: carrying momentum
				-- across a switch is what makes the sweep look continuous.
				aiLast = nil
			end
		end
		if not record or not part then stand_down() return end
		if now < reactionUntil then return end

		local aimPos = Targeting.aimPoint(cfg.Target, record, part)
		local screen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPos)
		Features.lastAimScreen = Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y)

		local camCf = Camera.CFrame
		local origin = camCf.Position
		local speed = clamp(mode.Speed, 0, 1)
		local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
		local cx, cy = vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5

		-- Screen-space modes steer the crosshair point, then read the world
		-- direction back off it; that is the space khook's dispatch hands them.
		local function sendScreen(px, py)
			local ray = Camera:ViewportPointToRay(px, py)
			ViewAngles.Send("Aimbot", CFrame.lookAt(origin, origin + ray.Direction))
		end

		if mode.Type == "Spring" then
			-- World space (khook.lua:116755). Critically damped, so it settles
			-- onto the target without ringing past it.
			local goal = (aimPos - origin).Unit
			if not springDir then springDir = Motion.newSpring(camCf.LookVector) end
			local dir = Motion.stepSpring(springDir, goal, dt, speed)
			if dir.Magnitude > 1e-6 then
				ViewAngles.Send("Aimbot", CFrame.lookAt(origin, origin + dir.Unit))
			end

		elseif mode.Type == "AI" then
			-- Screen space with persistent state (khook's `st.ml`). Leads the
			-- target by its own screen velocity and eases off as the crosshair
			-- closes, so the last few pixels are not a snap.
			local here = Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y)
			local lead = Vector2.zero
			if aiLast and dt > 0 then
				lead = (here - aiLast) * clamp(dt * 60, 0, 1)
			end
			aiLast = here
			local goal = here + lead
			local gap = (goal - Vector2.new(cx, cy)).Magnitude
			local alpha = clamp(speed * (0.35 + math.min(gap / vp.Y, 1)), 0, 1)
			sendScreen(cx + (goal.X - cx) * alpha, cy + (goal.Y - cy) * alpha)

		elseif mode.Type == "SigmaMouse" then
			-- Screen space plus the tremor and drift of the flick model, so a
			-- held aim never sits perfectly still.
			if not sigmaState then
				sigmaState = { p = Motion.newSigmaProfile(), ou = Vector2.zero, t = 0 }
			end
			local st = sigmaState
			st.t = st.t + dt
			local gap = dt > 0 and dt or 1 / 60
			local theta, sigma = st.p.ouTheta, st.p.ouSigma
			st.ou = st.ou + Vector2.new(
				-theta * st.ou.X * gap + sigma * math.sqrt(gap) * Motion.gauss(st.p.rng, 0, 1),
				-theta * st.ou.Y * gap + sigma * math.sqrt(gap) * Motion.gauss(st.p.rng, 0, 1))
			local tremor = st.p.tremorAmpMax * math.sin(TWO_PI * st.p.tremorFreqMin * st.t)
			sendScreen(
				cx + (screen.X - cx) * speed + st.ou.X + tremor,
				cy + (screen.Y - cy) * speed + st.ou.Y)

		else
			-- Linear: stateless screen-space lerp, alpha = Speed
			-- (khook.lua:116752 -- note `Smoothing` is a dead legacy field that
			-- khook's own migration folds into Speed and then deletes).
			sendScreen(cx + (screen.X - cx) * speed, cy + (screen.Y - cy) * speed)
		end
	end))
	Features.status.aimbot = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Silent Aim
--============================================================================--

function Features.SilentAim(trove)
	if not (hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod) then
		markUnavailable("silentaim", "executor has no hookmetamethod")
		return
	end
	local baseCfg = Config.node({ "SilentAim" })
	local cachedPos, cachedRecord, cachedPart, cachedFrame = nil, nil, nil, -1
	local current, switchUntil, reactionUntil = nil, 0, 0

	--- Resolved at most once per frame no matter how many raycasts the game
	--- fires; a full-auto weapon can call this dozens of times a tick.
	local function activeTarget()
		local cfg = Targeting.effectiveConfig("SilentAim") or baseCfg
		if UI.Visible or not cfg.Enabled then
			current = nil
			Features.lastSilentScreen = nil
			return nil
		end
		local bind = Features.binds.SilentAim
		if bind and not bind:IsActive() then return nil end
		if not Features.hasEquippedGun() then return nil end
		if cachedFrame == Registry.frame then return cachedPos, cachedRecord, cachedPart end
		cachedFrame = Registry.frame
		cachedPos, cachedRecord, cachedPart = nil, nil, nil
		local now = os.clock()
		local record, part = current, nil
		local origin = Targeting.shooterOrigin()
		if record ~= nil and record.Alive and now < switchUntil
			and passesConditions(cfg.Target, record, origin) then
			part = allowedPart(cfg.Target, record, true)
				or allowedPart(cfg.Target, record, false)
		end
		if part == nil then
			record, part = Targeting.pick(cfg.Target, cfg.FovSettings)
			if record ~= current then
				current = record
				switchUntil = now + (cfg.SwitchDelay or 0)
				reactionUntil = now + (cfg.ReactionTime or 0)
			end
		end
		if record == nil or part == nil then
			current = nil
			Features.lastSilentScreen = nil
			return nil
		end
		local indicatorPoint = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(
			Targeting.aimPoint(cfg.Target, record, part))
		if indicatorPoint.Z > 0 then
			Features.lastSilentScreen = Vector2.new(indicatorPoint.X, indicatorPoint.Y)
		end
		if now < reactionUntil then return nil end
		if math.random(100) > cfg.ActivationChance then return nil end
		if record and part then
			cachedPos = Targeting.aimPoint(cfg.Target, record, part)
			cachedRecord = record
			cachedPart = part
		end
		return cachedPos, cachedRecord, cachedPart
	end

	local function isWeaponRay(origin)
		if not Features.hasEquippedGun() or typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
		local cameraDistance = (origin - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
		local root = localRoot()
		return cameraDistance <= 16
			or (root ~= nil and (origin - root.Position).Magnitude <= 16)
	end

	-- Gun:_LocalTracers unconditionally indexes the four compact fields from
	-- ClientFighter:GetCameraData (Gun.lua:601-604). Some live sessions keep
	-- returning nil from the VM closure after an executor re-hook/reload; that
	-- is the exact source of the reported "Gun:613 ... index nil with ''".
	-- Preserve every normal result and only reconstruct the contract when the
	-- game returned no table at all.
	local cameraHookTarget, cameraHookOriginal = nil, nil
	local function attachCameraHook()
		if cameraHookTarget ~= nil or type(hookfunction) ~= "function" then return end
		local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
		local method = fighter and Resolver.prototypeMethod(fighter, "GetCameraData") or nil
		if type(method) ~= "function" then return end
		cameraHookTarget = method
		cameraHookOriginal = hookfunction(method, function(self, ...)
			local data = cameraHookOriginal(self, ...)
			if type(data) ~= "table" then
				local cameraCF = Camera.CFrame
				data = {
					[utf8.char(0)] = cameraCF,
					[utf8.char(1)] = cameraCF,
				}
			end
			if self == Rivals.localFighter() then
