				local aim, _, part = activeTarget()
				if aim ~= nil and part ~= nil then
					local originCF = data[utf8.char(0)]
					local origin = typeof(originCF) == "CFrame" and originCF.Position
						or Rivals.muzzlePosition() or Camera.CFrame.Position
					data[utf8.char(0)] = typeof(originCF) == "CFrame" and originCF or CFrame.new(origin)
					data[utf8.char(1)] = CFrame.lookAt(origin, aim)
					data[utf8.char(2)] = part
					data[utf8.char(3)] = part.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(aim))
				end
			end
			return data
		end)
	end
	attachCameraHook()
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(attachCameraHook))
	trove:Add(function()
		if cameraHookTarget ~= nil and restorefunction then pcall(restorefunction, cameraHookTarget) end
	end)

	local oldNamecall
	oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
		if checkcaller and checkcaller() then return oldNamecall(self, ...) end
		local method = getnamecallmethod()
		if method == "Raycast" and self == Workspace then
			local origin, direction, params = ...
			local aim = isWeaponRay(origin) and activeTarget() or nil
			if aim then
				if typeof(origin) == "Vector3" and typeof(direction) == "Vector3" then
					return oldNamecall(self, origin,
						(aim - origin).Unit * direction.Magnitude, params)
				end
			end
		elseif method == "FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList" or method == "FindPartOnRay" then
			local ray = ...
			local aim = typeof(ray) == "Ray" and isWeaponRay(ray.Origin)
				and activeTarget() or nil
			if aim then
				if typeof(ray) == "Ray" then
					return oldNamecall(self,
						Ray.new(ray.Origin, (aim - ray.Origin).Unit * ray.Direction.Magnitude),
						select(2, ...))
				end
			end
		end
		return oldNamecall(self, ...)
	end)
	trove:Add(function()
		if restorefunction then pcall(restorefunction, oldNamecall) end
	end)

	local oldIndex
	oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", function(self, key)
		if (key == "Hit" or key == "Target")
			and not (checkcaller and checkcaller())
			and typeof(self) == "Instance" and self:IsA("Mouse") then
			local aim, record, part = activeTarget()
			if aim then
				if key == "Hit" then return CFrame.new(aim) end
				if part then return part end
				if record then return record.Root end
			end
		end
		return oldIndex(self, key)
	end)
	trove:Add(function()
		if restorefunction then pcall(restorefunction, oldIndex) end
	end)
	Features.status.silentaim = cameraHookTarget ~= nil
		and "ok (StartShooting CameraData input bound)"
		or "ok (legacy ray/mouse binding; waiting for fighter)"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Triggerbot
--============================================================================--

--- Ported from khook's triggerbot (khook.lua:116774-116916). The shape of the
--- state machine matters as much as the raycast: reaction time is *elapsed
--- time on target*, not a fixed delay, and it resets the moment the crosshair
--- leaves. Release time continues issuing StartShooting after target loss.
function Features.Triggerbot(trove)
	local baseCfg = Config.node({ "Triggerbot" })
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	local filter = { nil, nil }

	local reactionElapsed = nil   -- nil while off-target
	local releaseRemaining = 0

	local function startShooting()
		-- khook.lua:116798 `f7984`: the fighter receives StartShooting on
		-- every firing frame. There is deliberately no paired StopShooting.
		Rivals.startShooting()
	end
	local function resetFiringState()
		reactionElapsed = nil
		releaseRemaining = 0
	end
	trove:Add(resetFiringState)

	--- khook's _CheckCrosshair: what is under the crosshair, and is it a
	--- legal target for this profile right now?
	local function checkCrosshair(cfg)
		local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
		local ray = Camera:ViewportPointToRay(vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5)
		local held = heldItemName()
		local hitboxProfile = HitboxProfiles.get(held)
		table.clear(filter)
		local character = localCharacter()
		if character ~= nil then table.insert(filter, character) end
		table.insert(filter, Camera)

		-- khook asks WorldInclude for params excluding the weapon profile's
		-- ExcludedParts. Recast through any such volume so a sniper can reach the
		-- Small hitbox behind the normal head/body volumes.
		local hit = nil
		for _ = 1, 8 do
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
			hit = Workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, params)
			if hit == nil or hitboxProfile.ExcludedParts[hit.Instance.Name] ~= true then break end
			table.insert(filter, hit.Instance)
		end
		if not hit then return false end

		local model = hit.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		if not model then return false end
		local record = Registry.byModel[model]
		if record == nil then
			local player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
			record = player and Registry.byPlayer[player] or nil
		end
		if record == nil or not record.IsEnemy or not record.Alive then return false end
		local invincible = record.Invincible
		if record.Player ~= nil then
			local live = Rivals.isInvincible(Rivals.fighterFor(record.Player))
			if live ~= nil then invincible = live end
		end
		if invincible then return false end

		-- The part set is keyed by the hit part's *name* -- which is exactly
		-- why the config stores "HitboxHead" / "HitboxBody" verbatim. It goes
		-- through the weapon's hitbox profile first, so a sniper accepts the
		-- *Small volumes it actually hits (khook.lua:100154).
		local parts = HitboxProfiles.resolve(
			effectiveParts(cfg, record.Grounded and "Ground" or "Air", baseWeapon(held)),
			hitboxProfile)
		if parts[hit.Instance.Name] ~= true then return false end

		if (cfg.NotDeflecting or cfg.NotShielded)
			and Targeting.isProtected(record, Targeting.shooterOrigin(),
				cfg.NotDeflecting, cfg.NotShielded) then return false end
		return true
	end

	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		local cfg = Targeting.effectiveConfig("Triggerbot") or baseCfg
		-- ShouldRun: Enabled and the bind held
		local bind = Features.binds.Triggerbot
		if not cfg.Enabled or (bind and not bind:IsActive()) then
			resetFiringState()
			return
		end
		if cfg.DisableOnReload and Features.isReloading() then return end
		if cfg.ActivateOnScope and not Features.isScoped() then return end
		if not Features.hasEquippedGun() then
			resetFiringState()
			return
		end

		local onTarget = checkCrosshair(cfg)
		if onTarget then
			reactionElapsed = (reactionElapsed or 0) + dt
		else
			reactionElapsed = nil
		end

		local reacted = reactionElapsed ~= nil and cfg.ReactionTime <= reactionElapsed
		if onTarget and reacted then
			releaseRemaining = cfg.ReleaseTime
			startShooting()
			return
		end

		releaseRemaining = math.max(0, releaseRemaining - dt)
		if releaseRemaining > 0 then
			startShooting()
		end
	end))
	Features.status.triggerbot = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Flickbot
--============================================================================--

-- A 1:1 port of khook's flickbot (khook.lua:29989-30116, 30290-30309). It is a
-- four-state machine, not a coroutine: on the press edge it bakes a whole
-- sigma-lognormal trajectory in screen space, converts every sample to a world
-- direction up front, then replays it against the clock. Baking up front is
-- what makes the motion reproducible; the drift term below is what keeps a
-- baked path honest when the target moves after the bake.
function Features.Flickbot(trove)
	local cfg = Config.node({ "Flickbot" })
	local baseAimCfg = Config.node({ "Aimbot" })
	local profile = Motion.newSigmaProfile()

	local state = "Idle"          -- Idle | Flicking | PostShot | Cooldown
	local held = false
	local timer = 0
	local selected = nil
	local trajectory, trajIndex, elapsedMs, totalMs, bakedEnd = nil, 1, 0, 0, Vector3.zAxis

	local function finishToCooldown()
		selected, trajectory = nil, nil
		ViewAngles.Clear("Flickbot")
		local cd = cfg.Cooldown
		if cd > 0 then
			state, timer = "Cooldown", cd / 1000
		else
			state = "Idle"
		end
	end

	local function startFlick()
		local aimCfg = Targeting.effectiveConfig("Aimbot") or baseAimCfg
		local record, part = Targeting.pick(aimCfg.Target, aimCfg.FovSettings)
		if not record or not part then return end

		local point = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
		if point.Z <= 0 then return end

		local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
		Motion.applyFlickProfile(profile, cfg.FlickDuration, cfg.Curvature, cfg.Humanness)
		local samples = Motion.generate(profile, vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5, point.X, point.Y)
		if #samples == 0 then return end

		local baked = table.create(#samples)
		for i = 1, #samples do
			local s = samples[i]
			baked[i] = { direction = Camera:ViewportPointToRay(s.x, s.y).Direction, t = s.t }
		end

		trajectory, trajIndex, elapsedMs = baked, 1, 0
		totalMs = baked[#baked].t
		bakedEnd = baked[#baked].direction
		selected = part
		state = "Flicking"
	end

	--- Advance the clock and read the path. Returns direction, finished.
	local function sampleTrajectory(dt)
		elapsedMs = elapsedMs + dt * 1000
		local i = trajIndex
		while i < #trajectory and trajectory[i + 1].t <= elapsedMs do
			i = i + 1
		end
		trajIndex = i
		local a, b = trajectory[i], trajectory[i + 1]
		if b == nil then
			return trajectory[#trajectory].direction, true
		end
		local span = b.t - a.t
		local alpha = span > 0 and clamp((elapsedMs - a.t) / span, 0, 1) or 1
		return a.direction:Lerp(b.direction, alpha), false
	end

	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		local bind = Features.binds.Flickbot
		local active = not UI.Visible and cfg.Enabled and bind ~= nil and bind:IsActive()

		-- Press edge only, and only from Idle (khook.lua:29994).
		if not active then
			held = false
		elseif not held then
			held = true
			if state == "Idle" then startFlick() end
		end

		if state == "Flicking" then
			if selected == nil or trajectory == nil or selected.Parent == nil then
				finishToCooldown()
				return
			end
			local dir, done = sampleTrajectory(dt)
			if totalMs > 0 then
				-- The path was baked against where the target was at press
				-- time. Blend the bake's own endpoint error away over the
				-- flick so a moving target still ends up under the crosshair.
				local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
				local live = (selected.Position - origin).Unit
				local a = clamp(elapsedMs / totalMs, 0, 1)
				dir = (dir + (live - bakedEnd) * a).Unit
			end
			local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
			ViewAngles.Send("Flickbot", CFrame.lookAt(origin, origin + dir))

			if done then
				if cfg.Shoot then
					state, timer = "PostShot", cfg.ShotDelay / 1000
				else
					finishToCooldown()
				end
			end

		elseif state == "PostShot" then
			timer = timer - dt
			if timer <= 0 then
				if mouse1click then pcall(mouse1click) end
				finishToCooldown()
			end

		elseif state == "Cooldown" then
			timer = timer - dt
			if timer <= 0 then state = "Idle" end
		end
	end))
	Features.status.flickbot = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Ragebot
--============================================================================--

function Features.Ragebot(trove)
	-- A port of khook's ragebot controller (khook.lua:61224-61410, the readable
	-- duplicate of 13013-13056) together with every piece it drives:
	--   SpatialLimitGate  136640   ShootLock        63289
	--   hitscan strategy   14265   melee strategy   39590
	--   evasion computes  126734 / 87944 / 136708
	--   defensive posture 144857   Above/Below     144752
	--
	-- The control flow is khook's, not a paraphrase of it:
	--   Update -> Translocate short-circuits -> target + action -> _Plan ->
	--   _ApplyPlan -> forced crouch -> plan.weaponAction()
	-- and every position it picks goes through the root desync, never through
	-- the character.
	local cfg = Config.node({ "Ragebot" })
	local Collection = cloneref(game:GetService("CollectionService"))
	local rng = Random.new()

	-- khook.lua:61185 -- part of the effective Ragebot enable state.
	local originalFallenHeight = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
	local engineEnabled = false
	local function setEngineEnabled(on)
		on = on == true
		if engineEnabled == on then return end
		engineEnabled = on
		local fallen = on and (0 / 0) or originalFallenHeight
		local ok = pcall(function() Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = fallen end)
		if not ok and sethiddenproperty then
			pcall(sethiddenproperty, Workspace, "FallenPartsDestroyHeight", fallen)
		end
		if setfflag then
			pcall(setfflag, "DFIntS2PhysicsSenderRate", on and "120" or "15")
			pcall(setfflag, "DFIntAssemblyHistoryBufferSize", on and "2147483648" or "15")
			pcall(setfflag, "DFIntAssemblyHistorySkipSize", on and "0" or "8")
		end
	end
	trove:Add(function() setEngineEnabled(false) end)

	--=====================================================================--
	-- ShootLock (khook.lua:63289)
	--=====================================================================--
	-- NOT a rate limiter. While the gate is open it returns true every single
	-- tick and pushes the lock forward; once the gate closes it keeps returning
	-- true for the remainder of that window. `ShootFrames` buys the grace
	-- period after the gate shuts, it does not space the shots out.
	local shootLocks = { hitscan = nil, melee = nil }

	local function shouldFire(strategy, gate, window)
		local now = os.clock()
		local lockedUntil = shootLocks[strategy]
		local wasLocked = lockedUntil ~= nil and now < lockedUntil
		if gate then shootLocks[strategy] = now + window end
		return wasLocked or gate
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Per-target state, read off the target's fighter
	--=====================================================================--
	-- khook.lua:88682 asks the target's own objects, not the character's
	-- attributes: `itemObserver:EquippedItemAsMelee():IsDeflecting()` and
	-- `itemObserver:GetEquippedAmmoState()`. Both are reachable through the
	-- fighter map, so they are read there; the attribute stays as a fallback
	-- for records with no fighter (range dummies).
	local function equippedOf(record)
		local fighter = record.Player and Rivals.fighterFor(record.Player)
		if fighter == nil then return nil end
		return Rivals.field(fighter, "EquippedItem")
	end

	--- khook.lua:88687 -- only a MELEE item can be deflecting.
	local function isDeflecting(record)
		local item = equippedOf(record)
		if item == nil then return record.Deflecting == true end
		if Rivals.itemType(item) ~= "Melee" then return false end
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return item:IsDeflecting() end)
		if ok and value ~= nil then return value == true end
		return record.Deflecting == true
	end

	--- khook.lua:136672 -- `GetEquippedAmmoState() ~= false`. An unknown
	--- reading counts as armed; only an explicit empty state does not.
	local function isArmed(record)
		local item = equippedOf(record)
		if item == nil then return record.Ammo == nil or record.Ammo > 0 end
		local ammo = Rivals.get(item, "Ammo")
		return ammo == nil or ammo > 0
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- SpatialLimitGate (khook.lua:136640)
	--=====================================================================--
	-- Watches whether the TARGET has thrown itself past Roblox's 2^22
	-- coordinate limit -- their own ragebot doing exactly what ours does. While
	-- they are out there and still armed the gate stays shut; it reopens once
	-- they have been gone as long as they historically stay, less Stability.
	local SPATIAL_LIMIT = 4194304
	local measurements = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

	local function beyondLimit(p)
		return SPATIAL_LIMIT <= math.abs(p.X)
			or SPATIAL_LIMIT <= math.abs(p.Y)
			or SPATIAL_LIMIT <= math.abs(p.Z)
	end

	local function gateTick(record)
		local now = os.clock()
		local m = measurements[record]
		if m == nil then
			m = { expectedDuration = 1 }
			measurements[record] = m
		end
		local entry = m.limitEntryTime
		local armed = isArmed(record)
		if not beyondLimit(record.Position) then
			if entry ~= nil then
				if armed then m.expectedDuration = now - entry end
				m.limitEntryTime = nil
			end
			return false
		end
		if entry == nil then
			m.limitEntryTime = now
			entry = now
		end
		if armed and m.expectedDuration - cfg.Stability <= now - entry then
			return false
		end
		return true
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Riot Shield posture (khook.lua:144752)
	--=====================================================================--
	-- "Above"/"Below" has nothing to do with relative height -- khookclone read
	-- it that way and was wrong. It asks which side the target's Riot Shield
	-- does NOT cover, from their aim pitch, and the strategies attack that side.
	local SHIELD = "Riot Shield"

	--- khook.lua:137560 `GetCameraRotation` = `fighterState._data.CameraRotation`.
	--- The live game exposes it as `fighter.Data.CameraRotation`, in radians over
	--- 0..2pi -- which is why khook's "Above" test spans 315..360 as well as
	--- 0..91. The look vector is only a fallback for a record with no fighter.
	local function aimPitch(record)
		local data = record.Player and Rivals.field(
			Rivals.fighterFor(record.Player), "Data") or nil
		local rot = Rivals.field(data, "CameraRotation")
		if rot ~= nil then
			local ok, x = pcall(function() return rot.X end)
			if ok and type(x) == "number" then return math.deg(x) % 360 end
		end
		local root = record.Root
		if root == nil then return 0 end
		return math.deg(math.asin(clamp(root.CFrame.LookVector.Y, -1, 1))) % 360
	end

	local function hasShield(items)
		if type(items) ~= "table" then return false end
		for _, it in pairs(items) do
			if Rivals.field(it, "Name") == SHIELD then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function shieldSide(record)
		local fighter = record.Player and Rivals.fighterFor(record.Player)
		if fighter == nil then return "None" end
		-- khook reads the target's replicated camera rotation; the closest
		-- thing visible without decoding that stream is where their character
		-- is actually looking.
		local pitch = aimPitch(record)
		local equipped = Rivals.field(fighter, "EquippedItem")
		if equipped ~= nil and Rivals.field(equipped, "Name") == SHIELD then
			if pitch > 22 and pitch < 91 then return "Below" end
			return "Above"
		end
		if hasShield(Rivals.field(fighter, "Items")) then
			if (pitch > 315 and pitch < 360) or (pitch > 0 and pitch < 91) then
				return "Above"
			end
			return "Below"
		end
		return "None"
	end

	--- Our own shield state, which is what selects the defensive posture.
	local function ownShieldMode()
		local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
		if item ~= nil and Rivals.field(item, "Name") == SHIELD then return "Equipped" end
		if hasShield(Rivals.items()) then return "Unequipped" end
		return "None"
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Evasion (khook.lua:126734 / 87944 / 136708)
	--=====================================================================--
	local FAR_AXIS = 1073741824

	--- A random point on the shell between two radii.
	local function shellPoint(anchor, minR, maxR)
		return CFrame.new(anchor + rng:NextUnitVector() * rng:NextNumber(minR, maxR))
	end

	--- khook.lua:126734. A shell offset with ONE randomly chosen axis slammed
	--- to 2^30 -- far past the spatial limit, so nothing can reach us between
	--- shots while the other two axes still scatter.
	local function scatter(clientCF, base, factor, fromCharacter)
		local pos = clientCF.Position
		local anchor = fromCharacter and pos or Vector3.new(0, pos.Y, 0)
		local cf = shellPoint(anchor, base, base + base * factor)
		local p = cf.Position
		local x, y, z = p.X, p.Y, p.Z
		local axis = rng:NextInteger(1, 3)
		if axis == 1 then x = FAR_AXIS
		elseif axis == 2 then y = FAR_AXIS
		else z = FAR_AXIS end
		return cf - p + Vector3.new(x, y, z)
	end

	--- khook.lua:136708. With no target it just scatters; with one it drops us
	--- under the out-of-bounds volume whose KillDelay is 0, which is the one
	--- that does not kill.
	local function translocate(clientCF, hasTargets)
		if not hasTargets then
			return shellPoint(clientCF.Position, 10000, 1000000000)
		end
		local part
		for _, p in ipairs(Collection:GetTagged("OutOfBoundsPart")) do
			if p:GetAttribute("KillDelay") == 0 then part = p break end
		end
		if part == nil then
			return shellPoint(clientCF.Position, 10000, 1000000000)
		end
		return part.CFrame
			* CFrame.new(0, -part.Size.Y / 2 + cfg.Evasion.Translocate.Offset, 0)
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Projectile breaker (khook.lua:88093)
	--=====================================================================--
	-- khookclone only ever ran this mode's fallback. The real thing pools
	-- surfaces out of the map and hides just behind one of them, and only
	-- bothers while an enemy is actually holding a projectile weapon.
	local breaker = { pool = {}, processed = {}, envId = nil, cooldown = -1, surface = nil }

	--- khook.lua:1780 `sharedHelper131` -- the depth settings are not constants:
	--- each oscillates between Min and Max at its own frequency in Hz.
	local function oscillate(range, freq)
		return range.Min + (range.Max - range.Min)
			* ((math.sin(os.clock() * (2 * math.pi * freq)) + 1) * 0.5)
	end

	--- khook.lua:1785 `sharedHelper132` -- sink 0.01 + `up` behind the surface
	--- along its normal and slide `forward` across its face.
	local function behindSurface(surface, up, forward)
		return CFrame.fromMatrix(
			surface.surfacePosition - surface.up * 0.01 - surface.up * up
				+ surface.forward * forward,
			surface.right, surface.up, -surface.forward)
	end

	--- khook.lua:1795 `sharedHelper133`
	local function isBlockedPart(inst)
		if inst.Name == "Barriers" then return true end
		local ok, tagged = pcall(function()
			return inst:HasTag("OutOfBoundsPart") or inst:HasTag("KillBrick")
		end)
		return (ok and tagged) or false
	end

	--- khook.lua:88112 `_HasProjectileThreat` -- reads as "a gun that is not
	--- raycast AND is named Slingshot", so the Slingshot is the only weapon
	--- that puts the breaker to work.
	local function hasProjectileThreat()
		local list, n = Registry.list, Registry.count
		for i = 1, n do
			local record = list[i]
			if record.IsEnemy and record.Alive then
				local item = equippedOf(record)
				if item ~= nil and Rivals.itemType(item) == "Gun"
					and Rivals.field(Rivals.field(item, "Info"), "IsRaycast") ~= true
					and Rivals.field(item, "Name") == "Slingshot" then
					return true
				end
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	--- khook's `f6204` turns a part into `{surfacePosition, up, right, forward}`
	--- and its body is destroyed in the decompile (it reads as `(false)()`), so
	--- this is the ONE piece of the breaker that is reconstructed: the part's
	--- top face, carrying the part's own axes.
	local function surfaceOf(part)
		if not part:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end
		local cf = part.CFrame
		return {
			surfacePosition = cf.Position + cf.UpVector * (part.Size.Y * 0.5),
			up = cf.UpVector, right = cf.RightVector, forward = cf.LookVector,
		}
	end

	--- khook.lua:88155 -- a candidate whose hiding spot is already inside
	--- geometry is thrown away.
	local overlap = OverlapParams.new()
	overlap.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	overlap.FilterDescendantsInstances = {}
	overlap.BruteForceAllSlow = true
	local function occupied(position)
		for _, hit in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(
			CFrame.new(position), Vector3.new(5, 5, 5), overlap)) do
			if isBlockedPart(hit) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	--- khook.lua:88133 `_ScanBatch` -- walks the environment's raycast
	--- whitelist, stopping after 64 parts examined or 30 surfaces pooled.
	local function scanBatch(envId)
		local pb = cfg.Evasion.ProjectileBreaker
		local midUp = (pb.DepthUp.Min + pb.DepthUp.Max) * 0.5
		local midForward = (pb.DepthForward.Min + pb.DepthForward.Max) * 0.5
		local seen = 0
		local function consider(inst)
			if not inst:IsA("BasePart") or breaker.processed[inst] then return end
			breaker.processed[inst] = true
			seen += 1
			local surface = surfaceOf(inst)
			if surface == nil then return end
			if occupied(behindSurface(surface, midUp, midForward).Position) then return end
			table.insert(breaker.pool, surface)
		end
		for _, root in ipairs(Collection:GetTagged("RaycastWhitelist" .. tostring(envId))) do
			if not isBlockedPart(root) then
				consider(root)
				if seen >= 64 or #breaker.pool >= 30 then return end
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
					if not isBlockedPart(d) then
						consider(d)
						if seen >= 64 or #breaker.pool >= 30 then return end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	--- khook.lua:88181 `_BreakLine` -- needs a pool of 30 before it commits,
	--- then arms the reposition cooldown and picks one at random.
	local function breakLine()
		local envId = breaker.envId
		if envId == nil then return nil end
		if #breaker.pool < 30 then scanBatch(envId) end
		if #breaker.pool < 30 then return nil end
		breaker.cooldown = os.clock() + cfg.Evasion.ProjectileBreaker.RepositionInterval
		return breaker.pool[rng:NextInteger(1, #breaker.pool)]
	end

	--- khook.lua:88093 `Compute`
	local function projectileBreaker(clientCF)
		local pb = cfg.Evasion.ProjectileBreaker
		local up = oscillate(pb.DepthUp, pb.DepthUpFrequency)
		local forward = oscillate(pb.DepthForward, pb.DepthForwardFrequency)
		if os.clock() < breaker.cooldown and breaker.surface ~= nil then
			return behindSurface(breaker.surface, up, forward)
		end
		if not hasProjectileThreat() then
			breaker.surface = nil
			return scatter(clientCF, pb.FallbackBaseRadius,
				pb.FallbackRadiusRandomFactor, pb.FallbackAnchorFromCharacter)
		end
		local surface = breakLine()
		if surface == nil then
			return scatter(clientCF, pb.FallbackBaseRadius,
				pb.FallbackRadiusRandomFactor, pb.FallbackAnchorFromCharacter)
		end
		breaker.surface = surface
		return behindSurface(surface, up, forward)
	end

	--- khook.lua:61338 `_EvadePlan`. Note it takes only our own CFrame -- the
	--- target plays no part in it, which is why khookclone anchoring the
	--- scatter on the target was wrong.
	local function evadePlan(clientCF, mode)
		if mode == "Off" then return {} end
		if mode ~= "ProjectileBreaker" then
			local r = cfg.Evasion.Random
			return {
				cframe = scatter(clientCF, r.BaseRadius,
					r.RadiusRandomFactor, r.AnchorFromCharacter),
			}
		end
		return { cframe = projectileBreaker(clientCF), shouldSkipDefense = true }
	end

	--- khook.lua:14291 -- hitscan parks symmetrically on X/Z.
	local function hitscanFarAway()
		return CFrame.new(
			math.random(-1000000, 1000000),
			math.random(5000, 10000),
			math.random(-1000000, 1000000))
	end

	--- khook.lua:652 -- melee parks on negative X/Z only.
	local function meleeFarAway()
		return CFrame.new(
			math.random(-10000000, -100000),
			math.random(5000, 10000),
			math.random(-10000000, -100000))
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Hitscan strategy (khook.lua:14265)
	--=====================================================================--
	local HITSCAN_ABOVE = Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
	local HITSCAN_BELOW = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)
	local ATTACK_DELAY = 0.06666666666666667
	local attackStart = nil

	local function hitscanPlan(dt, record, item, gate)
		-- khook always uses aliveState.hitboxHead; Ragebot does not inherit the
		-- Aimbot target-part profile.
		local head = record.HitboxHead or record.Head
		if head == nil then return hitscanFarAway(), nil end
		local headPos = head.Position
		local notBelow = shieldSide(record) ~= "Below"
		local offset = notBelow and HITSCAN_ABOVE or HITSCAN_BELOW
		local place = notBelow and CFrame.new(headPos + offset)
			or CFrame.lookAt(headPos + offset, headPos)

		if not shouldFire("hitscan", gate, dt * cfg.ShootFrames) then
			attackStart = nil
			return hitscanFarAway(), nil
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		attackStart = attackStart or now
		-- khook.lua:14303 -- hold the pose 1/15s so it replicates before firing
		if now - attackStart < ATTACK_DELAY then return place, nil end

		return place, function()
			-- khook.lua:14312 passes the SAME look-at CFrame as both the origin
			-- and the endpoint of the shot.
			local aim = CFrame.lookAt(place.Position, headPos)
			Rivals.shootAt(aim, aim, head)
		end
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Melee strategy (khook.lua:39590)
	--=====================================================================--
	local MELEE_ABOVE = Vector3.new(0, -0.7, 0.05)
	local MELEE_BELOW = Vector3.new(0, -3.85, 0.05)
	-- khook.lua:39533-39566. Positions only; the orientation is refilled per
	-- attack with the target's own yaw/roll and a +/-90 degree pitch.
	local ENC_FROM = { -9e+37, 0, 0 }
	local ENC_TO_ABOVE = { 0, -90000000, 0 }
	local ENC_TO_BELOW = { 0, 90000000, 0 }
	local ENC_HIT = { [utf8.char(0)] = 0, [utf8.char(1)] = 1, [utf8.char(2)] = 0,
		[utf8.char(3)] = 0, [utf8.char(4)] = 0, [utf8.char(5)] = 0 }
	local BACKSTAB_WINDOW, BACKSTAB_COOLDOWN = 0.625, 1.25
	local hitboxWindowUntil, attackCooldown = -1, -1
	local gluedOurPart = nil

	local function meleePlan(dt, record, item, gate)
		local head = record.HitboxHead or record.Head
		local root = record.Root
		if head == nil or root == nil then return meleeFarAway(), nil, nil end

		local notBelow = shieldSide(record) ~= "Below"
		local offset = notBelow and MELEE_ABOVE or MELEE_BELOW
		-- khook.lua:39604 -- the glue moves the TARGET's hitbox onto a fixed far
		-- point and repoints our physics replication root at it; everything is
		-- then positioned relative to that point.
		local ourRoot = localRoot()
		local anchor = Rivals.Glue.acquire(ourRoot, head)
		if anchor == nil then return meleeFarAway(), nil, nil end
		gluedOurPart = ourRoot
		local place = notBelow and (anchor + offset)
			or CFrame.lookAt(anchor.Position + offset, head.Position)

		local rx, ry, rz = root.CFrame:ToOrientation()
		local pitch = notBelow and (-math.pi / 2) or (math.pi / 2)
		local from = Rivals.reorient(ENC_FROM, pitch, ry, rz)
		local to = Rivals.reorient(notBelow and ENC_TO_ABOVE or ENC_TO_BELOW, pitch, ry, rz)
		local angles = { kind = "Normalized", pitch = math.deg(rx), yaw = math.deg(ry) }

		local now = os.clock()
		-- khook.lua:39641 -- inside the backstab window every swing is heavy
		if now < hitboxWindowUntil then
			return place, angles, function()
				Rivals.attackEncoded(item, from, to, head, ENC_HIT, true)
			end
		end
		if not shouldFire("melee", gate, dt * cfg.ShootFrames) then return meleeFarAway(), nil, nil end
		if now < attackCooldown then return meleeFarAway(), nil, nil end

		if Rivals.field(item, "Name") ~= "Knife" then
			return place, nil, function()
				Rivals.attackEncoded(item, from, to, head, ENC_HIT, false)
			end
		end
		-- khook.lua:39657 `_RecordBackstab`
		hitboxWindowUntil = now + BACKSTAB_WINDOW
		attackCooldown = now + BACKSTAB_COOLDOWN
		return place, angles, function()
			Rivals.attackEncoded(item, from, to, head, ENC_HIT, true)
		end
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Defensive posture (khook.lua:144857)
	--=====================================================================--
	local lastDefensiveAngles = nil

	local function defensiveCFrame(cf, mode, record)
		local target = record.Root and record.Root.Position
		if target == nil then return cf end
		local here = cf.Position
		-- shield out: face them so it blocks. shield stowed: face away.
		if mode == "Equipped" then return CFrame.new(here, target) end
		if mode == "Unequipped" then return CFrame.new(here, here + (here - target)) end
		local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
		if item == nil or Rivals.field(item, "Name") ~= "Knife" then return cf end
		-- holding a knife: spin so a backstab cannot be scored on us
		return CFrame.new(here) * CFrame.fromOrientation(
			rng:NextNumber(0, 2 * math.pi),
			rng:NextNumber(0, 2 * math.pi),
			rng:NextNumber(0, 2 * math.pi))
	end

	local function defensiveAngles(mode, record)
		if mode == "None" then return nil end
		local notBelow = shieldSide(record) ~= "Below"
		local pitch = ((mode == "Equipped") ~= notBelow) and 90 or -90
		return { kind = "Normalized", pitch = pitch, yaw = rng:NextNumber(0, 360) }
	end

	--- khook.lua:61356 -- `SendViewAngles(20, angles)`. The 20 is the driver's
	--- SLOT, not a priority, and the angles never move your camera: they only
	--- change what the server is told you are looking at, which is the whole
	--- point of the defensive posture.
	local function sendAngles(va)
		Rivals.ViewAngles.send(20, va)
		-- surface the lazy hook load into Diagnostics, once, on change
		local status = Rivals.ViewAngles.hookStatus
		if status ~= nil and Features.status.viewanglehooks ~= status then
			Features.status.viewanglehooks = status
		end
	end

	--- khook.lua:61348 `_ApplyPlan`.
	local function applyPlan(plan, record)
		local cf = plan.cframe
		if cf == nil or record == nil or plan.shouldSkipDefense then
			Rivals.Desync.set(cf)
			sendAngles(plan.viewAngles)
			return
		end
		local mode = ownShieldMode()
		Rivals.Desync.set(defensiveCFrame(cf, mode, record))
		if plan.isAimPose or plan.shouldDefendInPlace then
			lastDefensiveAngles = defensiveAngles(mode, record)
		end
		sendAngles(plan.viewAngles or lastDefensiveAngles)
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Target + weapon selection
	--=====================================================================--

	--- khook.lua:61312 -- the gun answers this itself; the fighter-wide probe
	--- reports any reload, which would stall the ragebot on the wrong weapon.
	local function itemIsReloading(item)
		if item == nil then return false end
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return item:IsReloading() end)
		if ok and value ~= nil then return value == true end
		return Features.isReloading()
	end

	--- khook.lua:88682 `f6253`. Not the aim profile's conditions: an enemy
	--- counts only while alive, not invincible and not deflecting.
	local function rageValid(record)
		if not record.IsEnemy or not record.Alive then return false end
		-- The registry's copy is refreshed every Heartbeat, but khook asks the
		-- live fighter here. Rechecking closes the one-frame window where spawn
		-- protection/ForceField turns on between registry and ragebot updates.
		local invincible = record.Invincible
		if record.Player ~= nil then
			local live = Rivals.isInvincible(Rivals.fighterFor(record.Player))
			if live ~= nil then invincible = live end
		end
		if invincible then return false end
		if isDeflecting(record) then return false end
		return true
	end

	--- khook.lua:88646 / 88706 `GetTarget`. No crosshair sorting: hackers first
	--- when the option is on, then the first valid enemy in iteration order.
	local function rageTarget()
		local list, n = Registry.list, Registry.count
		if cfg.PrioritizeHackers then
			for i = 1, n do
				local record = list[i]
				if record.Player and Features.hackerTags[record.Player]
					and rageValid(record) then return record end
			end
		end
		for i = 1, n do
			local record = list[i]
			local skip = cfg.PrioritizeHackers and record.Player ~= nil
				and Features.hackerTags[record.Player]
			if not skip and rageValid(record) then return record end
		end
		return nil
	end

	--- khook.lua:117152 `getAction`. Walks the item slots, keeps only the
	--- classes enabled in the config, ranks them by Weapons.Priority and
	--- returns Swap / Reload / Attack. An empty gun is a candidate only while
	--- it still has reserve; OnEmpty decides whether it is preferred (Reload)
	--- or held back as a fallback.
	local function weaponAction()
		local weapons = cfg.Weapons
		local onEmpty = weapons.OnEmpty
		local best, bestRank = nil, math.huge
		local emptyBest, emptyRank = nil, math.huge
		local anyEnabled = false

		local function consider(entry)
			local class = Rivals.itemClass(entry)
			if class == nil or weapons.Enabled[class] ~= true then return end
			anyEnabled = true

			local data = Rivals.field(entry, "Data")
			local ammo = Rivals.field(data, "Ammo")
			local reserve = Rivals.field(data, "AmmoReserve")
			local rank = table.find(weapons.Priority, class) or math.huge
			local isGun = Rivals.itemType(entry) == "Gun"
			local record = { item = entry, empty = false }

			if isGun and ammo == 0 then
				if (reserve == nil or reserve > 0) and rank < emptyRank then
					record.empty = true
					if onEmpty == "Reload" then
						bestRank, best = rank, record
					else
						emptyRank, emptyBest = rank, record
					end
				end
			elseif best == nil or rank < bestRank then
				bestRank, best = rank, record
			end
		end

		local items = Rivals.items()
		if items ~= nil then
			for _, entry in pairs(items) do consider(entry) end
		end

		if not anyEnabled then return nil end
		if best == nil then
			if onEmpty == "Swap" or emptyBest == nil then return nil end
			local equipped = Rivals.field(emptyBest.item, "IsEquipped") == true
			return { type = equipped and "Reload" or "Swap", item = emptyBest.item }
		end
		-- khook checks IsEquipped BEFORE the empty test, so an empty gun that is
		-- not in hand is swapped to rather than reloaded from a distance.
		if Rivals.field(best.item, "IsEquipped") ~= true then
			return { type = "Swap", item = best.item }
		end
		if best.empty then return { type = "Reload", item = best.item } end
		return { type = "Attack", item = best.item }
	end

	--- khook.lua:61389 `_ApplyForcedCrouch` into 12253 `SetForced`. The state
	--- hook writes to the server, and it only writes on a change; clearing puts
	--- the genuine replicated value back rather than a blind `false`.
	local forcedCrouch = nil
	local function forceCrouch(on)
		if forcedCrouch == on then return end
		forcedCrouch = on
		if on then
			Rivals.setForcedState("IsCrouching", true)
		else
			local actual = Rivals.stateValue("IsCrouching")
			Rivals.setForcedState("IsCrouching", actual == true)
		end
	end

	--=====================================================================--
	-- Update (khook.lua:61224)
	--=====================================================================--
	local function reset()
		attackStart, lastDefensiveAngles = nil, nil
		shootLocks.hitscan, shootLocks.melee = nil, nil
		Features.rageTargetWorld = nil
		hitboxWindowUntil, attackCooldown = -1, -1
		-- khook.lua:88196 / 39676 `ResetState`
		breaker.cooldown, breaker.surface = -1, nil
		-- khook.lua:61404 -- `SendViewAngles(20, nil)`
		Rivals.ViewAngles.send(20, nil)
		if gluedOurPart ~= nil then
			Rivals.Glue.free(gluedOurPart)
			gluedOurPart = nil
		end
		forceCrouch(false)
		Rivals.Desync.clear()
	end
	trove:Add(reset)

	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		local bind = Features.binds.Ragebot
		local effectiveEnabled = not UI.Visible and cfg.Enabled
			and (bind == nil or bind:IsActive())
		setEngineEnabled(effectiveEnabled)
		if not effectiveEnabled then reset() return end

		local me = Registry.localRecord
		local root = localRoot()
		-- khook.lua:61228-61236 -- no context, no environment, or dead: reset
		if root == nil or me == nil or not me.Alive or me.Environment == nil then
			Features.rageTargetWorld = nil
			reset()
			return
		end

		-- khook.lua:88087 `_BindEnvironment` -- the pool belongs to one arena
		if breaker.envId ~= me.Environment then
			breaker.envId = me.Environment
			table.clear(breaker.pool)
			table.clear(breaker.processed)
			breaker.surface = nil
		end

		-- everything below plans against where the CLIENT really is, never the
		-- position the server was last told about
		local clientCF = Rivals.Desync.clientCFrame(root)
		local mode = cfg.Evasion.Mode

		-- khook.lua:61239 -- Translocate short-circuits the whole pipeline: no
		-- target, no action, no plan, no crouch.
		if mode == "Translocate" then
			forceCrouch(false)
			Rivals.Desync.set(translocate(clientCF, rageTarget() ~= nil))
			Rivals.Desync.push(root)
			return
		end

		local record = rageTarget()
		-- khook.lua:61258 `_lastTargetWorld`
		Features.rageTargetWorld = record and record.Position or nil
		local action = weaponAction()
		local gate = record ~= nil and not gateTick(record)

		-- khook.lua:61270 `_Plan`
		local plan
		if action == nil then
			plan = evadePlan(clientCF, mode)
		elseif action.type == "Swap" then
			plan = evadePlan(clientCF, mode)
			plan.weaponAction = function() Rivals.equip(action.item) end
		elseif action.type == "Reload" then
			plan = evadePlan(clientCF, mode)
			plan.weaponAction = function() Rivals.reload() end
		elseif record == nil then
			plan = evadePlan(clientCF, mode)
		else
			local kind = Rivals.itemType(action.item)
			if kind == "Melee" then
				local cf, angles, act = meleePlan(dt, record, action.item, gate)
				plan = {
					cframe = cf, viewAngles = angles, weaponAction = act,
					shouldSkipDefense = true, shouldForceCrouch = true,
				}
			elseif kind ~= "Gun" then
				-- khook.lua:61306 -- a throwable has no strategy: empty plan
				plan = {}
			elseif itemIsReloading(action.item) then
				plan = evadePlan(clientCF, mode)
			else
				local cf, act = hitscanPlan(dt, record, action.item, gate)
				plan = {
					cframe = cf, weaponAction = act,
					shouldForceCrouch = true, isAimPose = act ~= nil,
				}
			end
		end

		applyPlan(plan, record)
		-- khook.lua:139020 `HeartbeatUpdate` -- the fake CFrame goes in here and
		-- comes back out at the top of the next frame.
		Rivals.Desync.push(root)
		forceCrouch(plan.shouldForceCrouch == true)
		-- khook.lua:61265 -- the weapon acts LAST, once the pose is in place
		if plan.weaponAction ~= nil then plan.weaponAction() end
	end))
	Features.status.ragebot = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Target Lock
--============================================================================--

function Features.TargetLock(trove)
	local cfg = Config.node({ "TargetLock" })
	local baseAimCfg = Config.node({ "Aimbot" })
	local locked = nil

	local function matches(input)
		if typeof(cfg.Bind) ~= "EnumItem" then return false end
		if cfg.Bind.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then return input.KeyCode == cfg.Bind end
		return input.UserInputType == cfg.Bind
	end

	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gpe)
		if gpe or not cfg.Enabled then return end
		if not matches(input) then return end
		if cfg.Mode == "Unlock" then
			locked = nil
			return
		end
		local aimCfg = Targeting.effectiveConfig("Aimbot") or baseAimCfg
		-- khook.lua:114280: Lock mode replaces the lock only when selection
		-- succeeds; an empty press leaves the existing lock intact.
		local candidate = Targeting.pick(aimCfg.Target, aimCfg.FovSettings, nil, true)
		if candidate ~= nil then locked = candidate end
	end))

	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not locked or not cfg.Enabled then return end
		if not locked.IsEnemy
			or (locked.Player ~= nil and Registry.byPlayer[locked.Player] ~= locked) then
			locked = nil
		end
	end))

	Features.getLockedTarget = function() return locked end
	Features.setLockedTarget = function(value) locked = value end
	Features.status.targetlock = "ok"
end

------------------------------------------------- local weapon-state probes --
-- Rivals exposes these on the local ClientFighter. The resolver binds it when
-- it can; the attribute reads are the fallback.

--- khook gates both the aimbot and the triggerbot on `HasEquippedGun()`
--- (khook.lua:116784, 134430) -- neither runs while you are holding a melee
--- weapon or a utility item. Resolved against ItemLibrary's Class/Type.
local gunCache, gunFrame = false, -1
function Features.hasEquippedGun()
	if gunFrame == Registry.frame then return gunCache end
	gunFrame = Registry.frame
	gunCache = false

	-- khook.lua:51470 / 134430 -- the fighter answers this itself.
	local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
	if fighter ~= nil then
		local behaviors = Rivals.field(fighter, "itemBehaviors")
		if behaviors ~= nil then
			local ok, value = pcall(function() return behaviors:HasEquippedGun() end)
			if ok and value ~= nil then
				gunCache = value == true
				return gunCache
			end
		end
	end

	-- khook.lua:32172 -- otherwise the equipped item's view type says it
	gunCache = Rivals.isGun()
	return gunCache
end

local scopedCache, scopedFrame = false, -1
function Features.isScoped()
	if scopedFrame == Registry.frame then return scopedCache end
	scopedFrame = Registry.frame
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item ~= nil then
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return item:IsAiming() end)
		if ok and value ~= nil then scopedCache = value == true return scopedCache end
		value = Rivals.field(item, "IsAiming")
		if value == nil then value = Rivals.field(Rivals.field(item, "Data"), "IsAiming") end
		if value ~= nil then scopedCache = value == true return scopedCache end
	end
	local fighter = Resolver.get("ClientFighter")
	if fighter then
		local ok, v = pcall(function() return fighter.IsAiming or fighter.Aiming end)
		if ok and v ~= nil then scopedCache = v == true return scopedCache end
	end
	local char = localCharacter()
	scopedCache = char ~= nil and (readAttr(char, "IsAiming") == true
		or readAttr(char, "IsScoped") == true)
	return scopedCache
end

local reloadCache, reloadFrame = false, -1
function Features.isReloading()
	if reloadFrame == Registry.frame then return reloadCache end
	reloadFrame = Registry.frame
	local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
	if item ~= nil then
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return item:IsReloading() end)
		if ok and value ~= nil then reloadCache = value == true return reloadCache end
		value = Rivals.field(item, "IsReloading")
		if value == nil then value = Rivals.field(Rivals.field(item, "Data"), "IsReloading") end
		if value ~= nil then reloadCache = value == true return reloadCache end
	end
	local fighter = Resolver.get("ClientFighter")
	if fighter then
		local ok, v = pcall(function() return fighter.IsReloading or fighter.Reloading end)
		if ok and v ~= nil then reloadCache = v == true return reloadCache end
	end
	local char = localCharacter()
	reloadCache = char ~= nil and (readAttr(char, "Reloading") == true
		or readAttr(char, "IsReloading") == true)
	return reloadCache
end
--============================================================================--
-- ESP
--============================================================================--
-- One drawing set per player, allocated on first sight and reused forever.
-- The render pass touches no allocations: the box comes from the registry's
-- cached rectangle, the label stack writes into a preallocated array, and
-- every Drawing property is only assigned when it actually changes.

local ESP = {}
ESP.objects = {}

--- khook.lua:101762-101791 -- the shared font Order: four Roblox built-ins and
--- three TTFs khook fetches from its asset repo. khook installs those three as
--- real Font objects; the download step is not here, so they fall back to the
--- nearest built-in face.
ESP.FONT_ORDER = { "Arial", "Roboto", "Ubuntu", "Inconsolata", "Minecraftia", "Proggy Clean", "Proggy Tiny" }

--- Pane labels are real TextLabels, so the Order maps onto Font objects.
ESP.FONT_FACE = {
	Arial = Enum.Font.Arial,
	Roboto = Enum.Font.Roboto,
	Ubuntu = Enum.Font.Ubuntu,
	Inconsolata = Enum.Font.Code,
	Minecraftia = Enum.Font.Arcade,
	["Proggy Clean"] = Enum.Font.Code,
	["Proggy Tiny"] = Enum.Font.Code,
}
function ESP.fontFace(name)
	local stored = ESP.FONT_FACE[name] or Enum.Font.Code
	if typeof(stored) == "Font" then return stored end
	local ok, face = pcall(Font.fromEnum, stored)
	return ok and face or Fonts.Mono
end

--- khook.lua:101762-101789 downloads these exact three faces and registers a
--- local FontFamily JSON through getcustomasset. Failure keeps the built-in
--- fallback, matching khook's non-fatal resolver behavior.
function ESP.installFonts()
	local assetLoader = getcustomasset or getsynasset
	if not (writefile and isfile and isfolder and makefolder and assetLoader) then return end
	local root = "Vexx V2/rivals_v3/fonts"
	if not isfolder("Vexx V2") then pcall(makefolder, "Vexx V2") end
	if not isfolder("Vexx V2/rivals_v3") then pcall(makefolder, "Vexx V2/rivals_v3") end
	if not isfolder(root) then pcall(makefolder, root) end
	local specs = {
		Minecraftia = { file = "Minecraftia-Regular.ttf", url = "https://github.com/kiciahook-org/v3-assets/raw/refs/heads/main/Minecraftia-Regular.ttf" },
		["Proggy Clean"] = { file = "Proggy-Clean.ttf", url = "https://github.com/kiciahook-org/v3-assets/raw/refs/heads/main/Proggy-Clean.ttf" },
		["Proggy Tiny"] = { file = "Proggy-Tiny.ttf", url = "https://github.com/kiciahook-org/v3-assets/raw/refs/heads/main/Proggy-Tiny.ttf" },
	}
	for name, spec in pairs(specs) do
		pcall(function()
			local fontPath = root .. "/" .. spec.file
			if not isfile(fontPath) then writefile(fontPath, game:HttpGet(spec.url)) end
			local asset = assetLoader(fontPath)
			local familyPath = root .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
			writefile(familyPath, HttpService:JSONEncode({
				name = name,
				faces = { { name = "Regular", weight = 400, style = "normal", assetId = asset } },
			}))
			ESP.FONT_FACE[name] = Font.new(assetLoader(familyPath),
				Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
		end)
	end
end

local SKELETON_R15 = {
	{ "Head", "UpperTorso" }, { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },
	{ "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" }, { "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" }, { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
	{ "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" }, { "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" }, { "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },
	{ "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" }, { "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" }, { "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },
	{ "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" }, { "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" }, { "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
}
local SKELETON_R6 = {
	{ "Head", "Torso" }, { "Torso", "Left Arm" }, { "Torso", "Right Arm" },
	{ "Torso", "Left Leg" }, { "Torso", "Right Leg" },
}
local MAX_BONES = math.max(#SKELETON_R15, 15)

local function newDrawing(class, props)
	if not Drawing then return nil end
	local ok, obj = pcall(Drawing.new, class)
	if not ok then return nil end
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do pcall(function() obj[k] = v end) end
	obj.Visible = false
	return obj
end

local function firstColor(seq)
	if typeof(seq) == "ColorSequence" then return seq.Keypoints[1].Value end
	if typeof(seq) == "Color3" then return seq end
	return Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
end

function ESP.sampleColorSequence(seq, alpha)
	if typeof(seq) ~= "ColorSequence" then return firstColor(seq) end
	local points = seq.Keypoints
	alpha = clamp(alpha, 0, 1)
	for i = 2, #points do
		local a, b = points[i - 1], points[i]
		if alpha <= b.Time then
			local span = math.max(b.Time - a.Time, 1e-6)
			return a.Value:Lerp(b.Value, (alpha - a.Time) / span)
		end
	end
	return points[#points].Value
end

--- Surviving khook refresh code routes every animated look through the shared
--- animation resolver. Shimmer and PingPong are colour oscillations; Perimeter
--- is handled by the box outline itself and keeps its base colour here.
function ESP.resolveColor(base, animation, clock)
	base = firstColor(base)
	if type(animation) ~= "table" or animation.Enabled ~= true then return base end
	if animation.Kind == "Shimmer" then
		local s = animation.Shimmer or {}
		local a = (math.sin(clock * math.pi * 2 * (s.Speed or 0.65)) + 1) * 0.5
		return base:Lerp(s.Color or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40), a)
	elseif animation.Kind == "PingPong" then
		local p = animation.PingPong or {}
		local a = (math.sin(clock * math.pi * 2 * (p.Speed or 1)) + 1) * 0.5
		return (p.BackgroundColor or base):Lerp(p.MainColor or base, a)
	end
	return base
end

local function textCase(text, mode)
	if mode == "UPPERCASE" then return string.upper(text) end
	if mode == "lowercase" then return string.lower(text) end
	return text
end

local function surround(text, mode)
	if mode == nil or mode == "None" then return text end
	-- khook.lua:1203-1206 stores the two delimiter characters directly.
	return string.sub(mode, 1, 1) .. text .. string.sub(mode, 2, 2)
end

--- Assign only on change: Drawing property writes cross the Lua/C boundary,
--- and an idle ESP frame otherwise burns thousands of them.
local function setv(obj, key, value)
	if obj[key] ~= value then obj[key] = value end
end

--============================================================================--
-- ESP panes (khook.lua:82771 / 96084 / 106166 / 120305)
--============================================================================--
-- khook does not draw ESP with the Drawing API at all -- the string "Drawing"
-- appears nowhere in khook.lua. Text and bars live in Roblox GUI panes
-- anchored to the four sides of the bounding box, each pane a UIListLayout
-- that stacks its elements outward. This is that system, verbatim where the
-- decompile still has it.
--
-- What is NOT recoverable: `esp.pane.Update` (106232), `esp.core.Refresh`
-- (120359), `UpdatePaneObjects` (120374) and `UpdateIndependentObjects`
-- (120380) all have destroyed bodies, so the per-frame update and culling
-- below is khookclone's own.

--- khook.lua:82771-82825, verbatim.
ESP.PRESETS = {
	CENTER = {
		side = "CENTER", anchorPoint = Vector2.zero, positionScale = Vector2.zero,
		spacingDirection = Vector2.zero, size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		fillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
		horizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
		verticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
	},
	LEFT = {
		side = "LEFT", anchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), positionScale = Vector2.zero,
		spacingDirection = Vector2.new(-1, 0), size = UDim2.fromScale(100, 1),
		fillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
		horizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
		verticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
	},
	RIGHT = {
		side = "RIGHT", anchorPoint = Vector2.zero, positionScale = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		spacingDirection = Vector2.new(1, 0), size = UDim2.fromScale(100, 1),
		fillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
		horizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
		verticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
	},
	TOP = {
		side = "TOP", anchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), positionScale = Vector2.zero,
		spacingDirection = Vector2.new(0, -1), size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 100),
		fillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
		horizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
		verticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom,
	},
	BOTTOM = {
		side = "BOTTOM", anchorPoint = Vector2.zero, positionScale = Vector2.new(0, 1),
		spacingDirection = Vector2.new(0, 1), size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 100),
		fillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,

		verticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
	},

--- khook.lua:96084 `f6632`, verbatim.
function ESP.panePosition(preset, spacing)
	return UDim2.new(
		preset.positionScale.X, preset.spacingDirection.X * spacing,
		preset.positionScale.Y, preset.spacingDirection.Y * spacing)
end

--- khook.lua:106182 `esp.pane:_Initialize`. The CENTER pane owns an inner host
--- frame; every other side uses its own container. List padding is 0 on CENTER
--- and 2 elsewhere, and the layout wraps.
function ESP.newPane(parent, preset)
	local container = make("Frame", {
		Visible = preset.side ~= "CENTER",
		AnchorPoint = preset.anchorPoint,
		Position = ESP.panePosition(preset, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	})
	pcall(function() container.Interactable = false end)

	local host
	if preset.side == "CENTER" then
		host = make("Frame", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = container,
		})
		pcall(function() host.Interactable = false end)
	else
		host = container
	end
	host.Size = preset.size

	local layout = make("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = (preset.side == "CENTER") and UDim.new() or UDim.new(0, 2),
		FillDirection = preset.fillDirection,
		HorizontalAlignment = preset.horizontalAlignment,
		VerticalAlignment = preset.verticalAlignment,
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Parent = host,
	})
	pcall(function() layout.Wraps = true end)

	container.Parent = parent

	local pane = { container = container, host = host, preset = preset, visible = false }
	--- khook.lua:106235
	function pane.SetSpacing(_, value)
		container.Position = ESP.panePosition(preset, value)
	end
	--- khook.lua:106239 -- deduped, as khook does
	function pane.SetVisible(_, on)
		if pane.visible == on then return end
		pane.visible = on
		container.Visible = on
	end
	return pane
end

--- khook.lua:120305 `esp.core.new` -- CENTER is the root and the other four
--- hang off its container.
function ESP.newCore(parent)
	local centre = ESP.newPane(parent, ESP.PRESETS.CENTER)
	local core = {
		centre = centre,
		left = ESP.newPane(centre.container, ESP.PRESETS.LEFT),
		right = ESP.newPane(centre.container, ESP.PRESETS.RIGHT),
		top = ESP.newPane(centre.container, ESP.PRESETS.TOP),
		bottom = ESP.newPane(centre.container, ESP.PRESETS.BOTTOM),
	}
	--- khook.lua:120383 `UpdateRect` -- the CENTER pane carries the box.
	function core.UpdateRect(_, x, y, w, h)
		centre.container.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
		centre.container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
	end
	--- khook.lua:120387 `SetSpacing`
	function core.SetSpacing(_, spacing)
		core.left:SetSpacing(spacing.Left)
		core.right:SetSpacing(spacing.Right)
		core.top:SetSpacing(spacing.Top)
		core.bottom:SetSpacing(spacing.Bottom)
	end
	--- khook.lua:120395 `SetPanesVisible`
	function core.SetVisible(_, on)
		centre:SetVisible(on)
	end
	function core.Destroy()
		centre.container:Destroy()
	end
	return core
end

--- A pane text element. khook builds these through per-element factories that
--- receive `{ layoutOrder, paneSide }`; the shape is the same.
function ESP.newPaneLabel(pane, order)
	local label = make("TextLabel", {
		LayoutOrder = order,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
		Text = "",
		TextSize = 14,
		TextXAlignment = pane.preset.side == "RIGHT" and Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			or (pane.preset.side == "LEFT" and Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
				or Enum.TextXAlignment.Center),
		Visible = false,
		Parent = pane.host,
	})
	pcall(function() label.Interactable = false end)
	local textStroke = make("UIStroke", {
		Name = "TextStroke", LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter,
		Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Parent = label,
	})
	make("UIGradient", {
		Name = "StrokeGradient", Enabled = false,
		Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(0, 0, 0)), Parent = textStroke,
	})
	make("UIGradient", {
		Name = "TextGradient", Enabled = false, Rotation = 45, Parent = label,
	})
	return label
end

--- khook.lua:30510-30625 `esp.label:_ApplyAnimationVisual`. Labels retain a
--- black mitered stroke, use a 45-degree UIGradient for Shimmer, and animate
--- PingPong through the same gradient rather than replacing TextColor3.
function ESP.applyLabelLook(label, color, transparency, animation, clock)
	local gradient = label:FindFirstChild("TextGradient")
	local stroke = label:FindFirstChild("TextStroke")
	local strokeGradient = stroke and stroke:FindFirstChild("StrokeGradient")
	local kind = type(animation) == "table" and animation.Enabled and animation.Kind or "None"
	label.TextTransparency = transparency or 0
	if stroke then
		stroke.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		stroke.Transparency = 0
	end
	if kind == "Shimmer" then
		local shimmer = animation.Shimmer or {}
		local shimmerColor = shimmer.Color or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
		gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, shimmerColor),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, color),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, shimmerColor),
		})
		gradient.Rotation = 45
		gradient.Offset = Vector2.new((clock * (shimmer.Speed or 0.65)) % 2 - 1, 0)
		gradient.Enabled = true
		label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		if strokeGradient then strokeGradient.Enabled = false end
	elseif kind == "PingPong" then
		local ping = animation.PingPong or {}
		local phase = (math.sin(clock * math.pi * 2 * (ping.Speed or 1)) + 1) * 0.5
		local background = ping.BackgroundColor or color
		local mainColor = ping.MainColor or color
		gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(background:Lerp(mainColor, phase),
			mainColor:Lerp(background, phase))
		gradient.Rotation = ping.Rotation or 0
		gradient.Offset = Vector2.zero
		gradient.Enabled = true
		label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		if strokeGradient then
			strokeGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(0, 0, 0))
			strokeGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
			strokeGradient.Enabled = true
		end
	else
		gradient.Enabled = false
		label.TextColor3 = color
		if strokeGradient then strokeGradient.Enabled = false end
	end
	label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(label.TextBounds.X + 3, label.TextBounds.Y)
end

--- khook.lua:138514 -- the health NUMBER is a label on the bar, not a separate
--- element: the bar answers SetValue / SetLabelVisible / SetLabelColor.
function ESP.newPaneBar(pane, order, vertical)
	local holder = make("Frame", {
		LayoutOrder = order,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = vertical and UDim2.fromOffset(4, 40) or UDim2.fromOffset(40, 4),
		Visible = false,
		Parent = pane.host,
	})
	pcall(function() holder.Interactable = false end)
	local outer = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Parent = holder,
	})
	local inner = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2), Parent = outer,
	})
	local gradient = make("UIGradient", { Enabled = false, Rotation = vertical and -90 or 0, Parent = inner })
	local consume = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = vertical and Vector2.zero or Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = vertical and UDim2.new() or UDim2.fromScale(1, 0),
		Size = vertical and UDim2.fromScale(1, 0) or UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Parent = inner,
	})
	local label = make("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 16),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = vertical and UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -1),
		Text = "",
		TextSize = 12,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = consume,
	})
	make("UIStroke", { LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter, Parent = label })
	return { holder = holder, outer = outer, fill = inner, consume = consume,
		label = label, gradient = gradient, vertical = vertical }
end

--============================================================================--
-- ESP preview (khook.lua:102989-103047)
--============================================================================--
-- khook drops a preview ESP renderer onto the viewport and switches its faction
-- with the Team/Enemy tab (`v17.new(page, viewport)` then `SetFaction`,
-- khook.lua:103013-103045). It reads the same config the live ESP reads, so the
-- preview is what you are about to see in game rather than a mock-up.
--
-- The overlay is GUI, parented into the ViewportFrame, so it clips to the pane
-- and needs no Drawing objects. Tracers and chams are deliberately absent:
-- neither has a meaning without a camera and a world.

ESP.preview = ESP.preview or nil

--- A ViewportFrame's camera has no `WorldToViewportPoint`, so the projection is
--- done by hand: into camera space, then the standard perspective divide using
--- the frame's own size and field of view.
function ESP.previewProject(camera, size, point)
	local local_ = camera.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(point)
	local depth = -local_.Z
	if depth <= 0.05 then return nil end
	local halfHeight = math.tan(math.rad(camera.FieldOfView) * 0.5)
	local halfWidth = halfHeight * (size.X / math.max(size.Y, 1))
	return Vector2.new(
		(local_.X / (depth * halfWidth) * 0.5 + 0.5) * size.X,
		(-local_.Y / (depth * halfHeight) * 0.5 + 0.5) * size.Y), depth
end

--- The dummy's screen rectangle inside the viewport, from the eight corners of
--- its bounding box -- the same all-corners rule `Registry.box` uses.
function ESP.previewRect()
	local preview = ESP.preview
	local vp = preview.viewport
	local model = vp._model
	local frame = vp._rt
	if model == nil or frame == nil then return nil end
	local cf, size = model:GetBoundingBox()
	local half = size * 0.5
	local area = frame.AbsoluteSize
	local minX, minY = math.huge, math.huge
	local maxX, maxY = -math.huge, -math.huge
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local o = BOX_OFFSETS[i]
		local point = (cf * CFrame.new(o.X * half.X, o.Y * half.Y, o.Z * half.Z)).Position
		local projected = ESP.previewProject(vp._camera, area, point)
		if projected == nil then return nil end
		if projected.X < minX then minX = projected.X end
		if projected.X > maxX then maxX = projected.X end
		if projected.Y < minY then minY = projected.Y end
		if projected.Y > maxY then maxY = projected.Y end
	end
	return minX, minY, maxX - minX, maxY - minY
end

--- khook.lua:103013 -- built once the viewport is realized and the dummy is in.
function ESP.startPreview()
	local preview = ESP.preview
	if preview == nil or preview.core ~= nil then return end
	local frame = preview.viewport:GetViewportFrame()
	if frame == nil then return end

	preview.core = ESP.newCore(frame)
	local core = preview.core
	preview.box = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = core.centre.container,
		ZIndex = 0,
	})
	preview.stroke = make("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1, Parent = preview.box })
	preview.name = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.top, 1)
	preview.health = ESP.newPaneBar(core.left, 1, true)
	preview.distance = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.bottom, 3)
	preview.weapon = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.bottom, 2)
	preview.rank = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.right, 2)
	preview.winstreak = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.right, 3)

	preview.conn = UI.Trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(ESP.updatePreview))
end

--- khook.lua:103034 -- the Team/Enemy tab drives which block the preview reads.
function ESP.setPreviewFaction(faction)
	if ESP.preview ~= nil then ESP.preview.faction = faction end
end

function ESP.updatePreview()
	local preview = ESP.preview
	if preview == nil or preview.core == nil then return end
	local frame = preview.viewport:GetViewportFrame()
	if frame == nil or not frame.Visible then return end

	local cfg = Config.node({ "ESP", preview.faction })
	local settings = Config.node({ "ESP", "Settings" })
	local x, y, w, h = ESP.previewRect()
	if x == nil or not Config.get({ "ESP", "Main", "Enabled" }) or not cfg.Enabled then
		preview.core:SetVisible(false)
		return
	end
	preview.core:SetVisible(true)
	preview.core:UpdateRect(x, y, w, h)
	preview.core:SetSpacing(settings.Spacing)

	preview.box.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	preview.box.Visible = cfg.Box.Enabled == true
	preview.stroke.Color = firstColor(cfg.Box.Color)

	local font = ESP.fontFace(settings.Font)
	local flagFont = ESP.fontFace(settings.FlagFont)

	local function label(obj, on, text, colour, isFlag)
		if not on or text == nil or text == "" then obj.Visible = false return end
		obj.Text = surround(textCase(tostring(text),
			isFlag and settings.FlagTextCase or settings.TextCase),
			isFlag and settings.FlagTextSurround or settings.TextSurround)
		obj.TextSize = isFlag and settings.FlagFontSize or settings.FontSize
		obj.FontFace = isFlag and flagFont or font
		obj.TextColor3 = colour
		obj.Visible = true
	end

	-- The dummy stands in for a player, so the sample values are what khook's
	-- own preview shows: a full-health target at no distance.
	label(preview.name, cfg.Name.Enabled, "Preview", firstColor(cfg.Name.Color), false)
	label(preview.distance, cfg.Distance.Enabled, "0m", firstColor(cfg.Distance.Color), true)
	label(preview.weapon, cfg.HeldWeapon.Enabled, "Assault Rifle", firstColor(cfg.HeldWeapon.Color), true)
	label(preview.rank, cfg.Rank.Enabled, 100, firstColor(cfg.Rank.Color), true)
	label(preview.winstreak, cfg.Winstreak.Enabled, "WS 10", firstColor(cfg.Winstreak.Color), true)

	local bar = preview.health
	if cfg.HealthBar.Enabled then
		bar.holder.Visible = true
		bar.holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, math.max(8, h))
		bar.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		bar.fill.BackgroundColor3 = cfg.HealthBar.ColorMode == "Reactive"
			and Color3.new(0, 1, 0) or firstColor(cfg.HealthBar.Color)
		if bar.label ~= nil then
			bar.label.Visible = cfg.HealthNumber.Enabled == true
			bar.label.Text = "100"
			bar.label.TextColor3 = firstColor(cfg.HealthNumber.Color)
			bar.label.FontFace = font
			bar.label.TextSize = settings.FontSize
		end
	else
		bar.holder.Visible = false
		if bar.label ~= nil then bar.label.Visible = false end
	end
end

--- GUI line used by khook's independent overlay objects. Keeping these in the
--- same ScreenGui as the panes also removes executor Drawing dependencies.
function ESP.newLine(parent, z)
	local frame = make("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = z or 4, Parent = parent,
	})
	make("UIGradient", { Name = "Gradient", Enabled = false, Parent = frame })
	pcall(function() frame.Interactable = false end)
	return frame
end

function ESP.setLine(frame, a, b, thickness)
	local d = b - a
	frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((a.X + b.X) * 0.5, (a.Y + b.Y) * 0.5)
	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude, thickness)
	frame.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
end

function ESP.newBoundingBox(parent)
	local root = make("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = parent,
	})
	pcall(function() root.Interactable = false end)
	local fillGradient = make("UIGradient", {
		Enabled = true, Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.8), Parent = root,
	})
	local outline = make("UIStroke", {
		ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Thickness = 1,
		Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Enabled = false, Parent = root,
	})
	local outlineGradient = make("UIGradient", { Enabled = false, Parent = outline })
	local image = make("ImageLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, Visible = false, ZIndex = 0, Parent = root,
	})
	local corners = {}
	for i = 1, 8 do
		corners[i] = { backer = ESP.newLine(root, 2), color = ESP.newLine(root, 3) }
		corners[i].backer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
	return { root = root, fillGradient = fillGradient, outline = outline,
		outlineGradient = outlineGradient, image = image, corners = corners }
end

function ESP.newHeadMarker(parent)
	local function stroke(frame)
		return make("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1.5, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = frame })
	end
	local cross = make("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 8, Parent = parent })
	local crossTargets = {}
	for i, size in ipairs({ UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.18), UDim2.fromScale(0.18, 1) }) do
		local f = make("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
			Size = size, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 8, Parent = cross })
		crossTargets[i] = { frame = f, stroke = stroke(f) }
	end
	local circle = make("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Visible = false, ZIndex = 8, Parent = parent })
	corner(circle, 999)
	local circleTargets = { { frame = circle, stroke = stroke(circle) } }
	local diamond = make("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Rotation = 45, Visible = false, ZIndex = 8, Parent = parent })
	local diamondTargets = { { frame = diamond, stroke = stroke(diamond) } }
	return {
		containers = { Cross = cross, Circle = circle, Diamond = diamond },
		targets = { Cross = crossTargets, Circle = circleTargets, Diamond = diamondTargets },
	}
end

function ESP.paintHeadMarker(marker, cfg, position, size)
	local shape = marker.containers[cfg.Shape] and cfg.Shape or "Cross"
	for name, container in pairs(marker.containers) do container.Visible = name == shape end
	local container = marker.containers[shape]
	container.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(position.X, position.Y)
	container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
	for _, target in ipairs(marker.targets[shape]) do
		target.frame.BackgroundColor3 = cfg.Color
		target.frame.BackgroundTransparency = cfg.Filled and cfg.Transparency or 1
		target.stroke.Color = cfg.Outline and cfg.OutlineColor or cfg.Color
		target.stroke.Transparency = cfg.Outline and cfg.OutlineTransparency or cfg.Transparency
	end
end

local function makeSet()
	local set = {
		bounding   = ESP.newBoundingBox(ESP.layer),
		tracer     = ESP.newLine(ESP.layer, 5),
		tracerOut  = ESP.newLine(ESP.layer, 4),
		headMarker = ESP.newHeadMarker(ESP.layer),
		skeleton   = {},
		highlight  = nil,
		glowHighlight = nil,
		shown      = false,
	}
	for i = 1, MAX_BONES do
		set.skeleton[i] = ESP.newLine(ESP.layer, 6)
	end

	-- khook.lua:138282 -- element to pane, verbatim:
	--   TOP    1 name
	--   LEFT   1 health bar (carrying the health number as its own label)
	--   BOTTOM 1 ammo bar   2 held weapon   3 distance
	--   RIGHT  1 deflecting 2 rank          3 winstreak
	local core = ESP.newCore(ESP.layer)
	set.core       = core
	set.name       = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.top, 1)
	set.healthBar  = ESP.newPaneBar(core.left, 1, true)
	set.ammoBar    = ESP.newPaneBar(core.bottom, 1, false)
	set.weapon     = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.bottom, 2)
	set.distance   = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.bottom, 3)
	set.deflect    = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.right, 1)
	set.rank       = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.right, 2)
	set.winstreak  = ESP.newPaneLabel(core.right, 3)
	return set
end

--- Every pane element off in one call.
--- A field on ESP rather than a top-level local: the main chunk is at Lua's
--- 200-local ceiling, and one more silently fails to compile.
function ESP.hidePanes(set)
	if set.core == nil then return end
	set.core:SetVisible(false)
	for _, key in ipairs({ "name", "weapon", "distance", "deflect", "rank", "winstreak" }) do
		local label = set[key]
		if label then label.Visible = false end
	end
	for _, key in ipairs({ "healthBar", "ammoBar" }) do
		local bar = set[key]
		if bar then bar.holder.Visible = false bar.label.Visible = false end
	end
end

local function hideSet(set)
	if not set.shown then return end
	set.shown = false
	ESP.hidePanes(set)
	for key, obj in pairs(set) do
		if key == "core" or key == "name" or key == "weapon" or key == "distance"
			or key == "deflect" or key == "rank" or key == "winstreak"
			or key == "healthBar" or key == "ammoBar" then
			-- handled by hidePanes
		else
		if key == "skeleton" then
			for _, l in ipairs(obj) do if l then l.Visible = false end end
		elseif key == "bounding" then
			obj.root.Visible = false
		elseif key == "headMarker" then
			for _, container in pairs(obj.containers) do container.Visible = false end
		elseif key == "highlight" or key == "glowHighlight" then
			if obj then obj.Enabled = false end
		elseif typeof(obj) == "Instance" then
			obj.Visible = false
		end
		end
	end
end

local function removeSet(set)
	if set.core then pcall(set.core.Destroy) set.core = nil end
	for key, obj in pairs(set) do
		if key == "name" or key == "weapon" or key == "distance" or key == "deflect"
			or key == "rank" or key == "winstreak" or key == "healthBar"
			or key == "ammoBar" then
			-- destroyed with the pane tree above
		elseif key == "skeleton" then
			for _, l in ipairs(obj) do pcall(function() l:Destroy() end) end
		elseif key == "bounding" then
			pcall(function() obj.root:Destroy() end)
		elseif key == "headMarker" then
			for _, container in pairs(obj.containers) do pcall(function() container:Destroy() end) end
		elseif key == "highlight" or key == "glowHighlight" then
			if obj then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
		elseif key ~= "shown" and obj then
			pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
		end
	end
end

local function applyChams(set, record, cfg)
	if not cfg.Enabled then
		if set.highlight then set.highlight.Enabled = false end
		if set.glowHighlight then set.glowHighlight.Enabled = false end
		return
	end
	local h = set.highlight
	if not h or h.Parent == nil then
		h = Instance.new("Highlight")
		h.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
		h.Parent = ESP.container
		set.highlight = h
	end
	if h.Adornee ~= record.Character then h.Adornee = record.Character end
	setv(h, "FillColor", cfg.InnerColor)
	setv(h, "FillTransparency", cfg.InnerTransparency)
	setv(h, "OutlineColor", cfg.OutlineColor)
	setv(h, "OutlineTransparency", cfg.OutlineTransparency)
	setv(h, "Enabled", true)
	if cfg.Glow then
		local glow = set.glowHighlight
		if not glow or glow.Parent == nil then
			glow = Instance.new("Highlight")
			glow.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			glow.FillTransparency = 1
			glow.Parent = ESP.container
			set.glowHighlight = glow
		end
		glow.Adornee = record.Character
		glow.OutlineColor = cfg.GlowColor
		glow.OutlineTransparency = 0.35
		glow.Enabled = true
	elseif set.glowHighlight then
		set.glowHighlight.Enabled = false
	end
end

--- Corner-style box: eight short segments instead of a full rectangle.
local function drawCorners(set, box, colour, thickness, len, animation, clock)
	local c = set.bounding.corners
	-- Corner lines are children of the bounding-box frame, so coordinates are
	-- local to that frame (the old Drawing implementation used screen space).
	local x, y, w, h = 0, 0, box.W, box.H
	local seg = math.min(len, w * 0.3, h * 0.3)
	local pts = {
		{ Vector2.new(x, y), Vector2.new(x + seg, y) },
		{ Vector2.new(x, y), Vector2.new(x, y + seg) },
		{ Vector2.new(x + w, y), Vector2.new(x + w - seg, y) },
		{ Vector2.new(x + w, y), Vector2.new(x + w, y + seg) },
		{ Vector2.new(x, y + h), Vector2.new(x + seg, y + h) },
		{ Vector2.new(x, y + h), Vector2.new(x, y + h - seg) },
		{ Vector2.new(x + w, y + h), Vector2.new(x + w - seg, y + h) },
		{ Vector2.new(x + w, y + h), Vector2.new(x + w, y + h - seg) },
	}
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local pair = c[i]
		if pair then
			ESP.setLine(pair.backer, pts[i][1], pts[i][2], thickness + 2)
			ESP.setLine(pair.color, pts[i][1], pts[i][2], thickness)
			if type(animation) == "table" and animation.Enabled
				and animation.Kind == "Perimeter" then
				local perimeter = animation.Perimeter or {}
				pair.color.BackgroundColor3 = ESP.sampleColorSequence(perimeter.Color,
					(clock * (perimeter.Speed or 1) + (i - 1) / 8) % 1)
			else
				pair.color.BackgroundColor3 = colour
			end
			pair.backer.Visible = true
			pair.color.Visible = true
		end
	end
end

--- Full style is also four backer/color pairs in khook (the Full module uses
--- scale 1 while Corner uses scale .25); it is not a lone UIStroke.
function ESP.drawFull(set, box, colour, thickness, animation, clock)
	local c, w, h = set.bounding.corners, box.W, box.H
	local pts = {
		{ Vector2.new(0, 0), Vector2.new(w, 0) },
		{ Vector2.new(w, 0), Vector2.new(w, h) },
		{ Vector2.new(w, h), Vector2.new(0, h) },
		{ Vector2.new(0, h), Vector2.new(0, 0) },
	}
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local pair = c[i]
		if i <= 4 then
			ESP.setLine(pair.backer, pts[i][1], pts[i][2], thickness + 2)
			ESP.setLine(pair.color, pts[i][1], pts[i][2], thickness)
			if type(animation) == "table" and animation.Enabled
				and animation.Kind == "Perimeter" then
				local perimeter = animation.Perimeter or {}
				pair.color.BackgroundColor3 = ESP.sampleColorSequence(perimeter.Color,
					(clock * (perimeter.Speed or 1) + (i - 1) / 4) % 1)
			else
				pair.color.BackgroundColor3 = colour
