			end
			pair.backer.Visible, pair.color.Visible = true, true
		else
			pair.backer.Visible, pair.color.Visible = false, false
		end
	end
end

local function hideCorners(set)
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local pair = set.bounding.corners[i]
		if pair then
			pair.backer.Visible = false
			pair.color.Visible = false
		end
	end
end

function ESP.init(trove)
	local root = Config.node({ "ESP" })
	local main, settings = root.Main, root.Settings
	task.spawn(ESP.installFonts)

	ESP.container = Instance.new("Folder")
	ESP.container.Name = "\0khookclone_esp\0"
	-- Highlight is a world adornment, so its owning folder must live in the
	-- DataModel's 3D tree; the GUI overlay itself remains in gethui below.
	ESP.container.Parent = Workspace
	trove:Add(ESP.container)

	-- khook keeps panes and independent objects in one inset-free ScreenGui.
	ESP.layer = make("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "\0khookclone_esp_panes\0",
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		DisplayOrder = 2 ^ 30 - 3,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
	})
	pcall(protectgui, ESP.layer)
	ESP.layer.Parent = gethui()
	trove:Add(ESP.layer)

	local function clear(record)
		local set = ESP.objects[record]
		if set then removeSet(set) ESP.objects[record] = nil end
	end
	trove:Add(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		for record in pairs(ESP.objects) do
			if record.Player == player then clear(record) end
		end
	end))
	trove:Add(function()
		for record in pairs(ESP.objects) do clear(record) end
	end)

	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local bind = Features.binds.ESP
		if not main.Enabled or (bind and not bind:IsActive()) then
			for _, set in pairs(ESP.objects) do hideSet(set) end
			return
		end

		local list, n = Registry.list, Registry.count
		local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
		local spacing = settings.Spacing
		local fontSize, flagSize = settings.FontSize, settings.FlagFontSize
		local mainFont, flagFont = ESP.fontFace(settings.Font), ESP.fontFace(settings.FlagFont)
		local seen = ESP._seen or {}
		ESP._seen = seen
		table.clear(seen)

		for i = 1, n do
			local record = list[i]
			local player = record.Player
			-- keyed by the RECORD, not the player: range dummies have no Player
			-- and a nil table index throws on every frame
			seen[record] = true

			local set = ESP.objects[record]
			if not set then
				set = makeSet()
				ESP.objects[record] = set
			end

			local cfg = record.IsEnemy and root.Enemy or root.Team
			local factionBind = record.IsEnemy and Features.binds.ESPEnemy or Features.binds.ESPTeam
			if not cfg.Enabled or (factionBind and not factionBind:IsActive()) then
				hideSet(set)
				continue
			end

			local box = main.Mode == "Dynamic"
				and Registry.dynamicBox(record) or Registry.box(record)
			if not box then hideSet(set) continue end
			set.shown = true

			local bx, by, bw, bh = box.X, box.Y, box.W, box.H
			if settings.OverrideRectSize.Enabled then
				bw = bw * settings.OverrideRectSize.Width
				bh = bh * settings.OverrideRectSize.Height
				bx = box.CenterX - bw * 0.5
				by = box.CenterY - bh * 0.5
			end

			local scale = 1
			if settings.DistanceScaling then
				-- khook.lua:63315-63327: distance scaling only shrinks (to 50%),
				-- never enlarges text above its configured size.
				scale = clamp(settings.DistanceScalingRef / math.max(1, record.Distance), 0.5, 1)
			end

			------------------------------------------------------------ box --
			local boxCfg = cfg.Box
			local bounding = set.bounding
			bounding.root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(bx, by)
			bounding.root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bw, bh)
			bounding.root.Visible = boxCfg.Enabled
				or (cfg.BoxImage.Enabled and cfg.BoxImage.Path ~= "")
			if boxCfg.Enabled then
				local colour = ESP.resolveColor(boxCfg.Color, boxCfg.Animation, os.clock())
				-- Both outline styles are explicit backer/color segments; their
				-- Perimeter look is applied segment-by-segment below.
				bounding.outlineGradient.Enabled = false
				if boxCfg.Style == "Corner" then
					bounding.outline.Enabled = false
					box.X, box.Y, box.W, box.H = bx, by, bw, bh
					drawCorners(set, box, colour, 1, bh * 0.25, boxCfg.Animation, os.clock())
				else
					bounding.outline.Enabled = false
					box.X, box.Y, box.W, box.H = bx, by, bw, bh
					ESP.drawFull(set, box, colour, 1, boxCfg.Animation, os.clock())
				end
			else
				bounding.outline.Enabled = false
				bounding.outlineGradient.Enabled = false
				hideCorners(set)
			end

			if cfg.FilledBox.Enabled then
				local fill = cfg.FilledBox
				local color = fill.Gradient.Enabled and fill.Gradient.Color or fill.Color
				bounding.fillGradient.Color = typeof(color) == "ColorSequence"
					and color or ColorSequence.new(color)
				bounding.fillGradient.Transparency = typeof(fill.Transparency) == "NumberSequence"
					and fill.Transparency or NumberSequence.new(fill.Transparency or 0.8)
				-- BoundingBox.Update keeps a 70-degree animation constant and the
				-- refresh path names this mode "Swirl" (khook.lua:122414/138399).
				bounding.fillGradient.Rotation = fill.Gradient.Animate
					and ((90 + os.clock() * 70) % 360) or 90
				bounding.fillGradient.Offset = Vector2.zero
				bounding.root.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			else
				bounding.root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			end

			----------------------------------------------------- box image --
			-- khook.lua:122295/138378 -- cropped ImageLabel inside the box.
			local img = cfg.BoxImage
			if img.Enabled and img.Path ~= "" then
				bounding.image.Image = img.Path
				bounding.image.ImageTransparency = img.Transparency or 0
				bounding.image.Visible = true
			else
				bounding.image.Visible = false
			end

			-------------------------------------------------- panes + bars --
			-- khook.lua:120383 -- the CENTER pane IS the bounding box; the four
			-- side panes hang off it and lay themselves out. Nothing here
			-- positions a label by hand.
			local core = set.core
			core:UpdateRect(bx, by, bw, bh)
			core:SetSpacing(spacing)
			core:SetVisible(true)

			local ratio = clamp(record.Health / record.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
			local hb = set.healthBar
			if cfg.HealthBar.Enabled then
				hb.holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, math.max(8, bh))
				hb.consume.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1 - ratio)
				local hbMode = cfg.HealthBar.ColorMode
				hb.gradient.Enabled = hbMode == "Gradient"
				hb.gradient.Color = cfg.HealthBar.Color
				if hbMode == "Reactive" then
					setv(hb.fill, "BackgroundColor3", ESP.sampleColorSequence(cfg.HealthBar.Color, ratio))
				else
					setv(hb.fill, "BackgroundColor3", firstColor(cfg.HealthBar.Color))
				end
				hb.gradient.Offset = cfg.HealthBar.Animate
					and Vector2.new(0, (os.clock() * 0.35) % 2 - 1) or Vector2.zero
				setv(hb.holder, "Visible", true)
				-- khook.lua:138519/138527 -- the health number is the BAR's label
				if cfg.HealthNumber.Enabled then
					setv(hb.label, "Text", tostring(math.floor(record.Health + 0.5)))
					setv(hb.label, "TextSize", math.max(8, math.floor(fontSize * scale + 0.5)))
					setv(hb.label, "TextColor3", cfg.HealthNumber.Color)
					setv(hb.label, "FontFace", mainFont)
					setv(hb.label, "Visible", true)
				else
					setv(hb.label, "Visible", false)
				end
			else
				setv(hb.holder, "Visible", false)
				setv(hb.label, "Visible", false)
			end

			local ab = set.ammoBar
			if cfg.AmmoBar.Enabled and record.Ammo and record.MaxAmmo and record.MaxAmmo > 0 then
				local ammoRatio = clamp(record.Ammo / record.MaxAmmo, 0, 1)
				ab.holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(8, bw), 4)
				ab.consume.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 - ammoRatio, 1)
				local abMode = cfg.AmmoBar.ColorMode
				ab.gradient.Enabled = abMode == "Gradient"
				ab.gradient.Color = cfg.AmmoBar.Color
				if abMode == "Reactive" then
					setv(ab.fill, "BackgroundColor3", ESP.sampleColorSequence(cfg.AmmoBar.Color, ammoRatio))
				else
					setv(ab.fill, "BackgroundColor3", firstColor(cfg.AmmoBar.Color))
				end
				ab.gradient.Offset = cfg.AmmoBar.Animate
					and Vector2.new((os.clock() * 0.35) % 2 - 1, 0) or Vector2.zero
				setv(ab.holder, "Visible", true)
			else
				setv(ab.holder, "Visible", false)
				setv(ab.label, "Visible", false)
			end

			--------------------------------------------------------- labels --
			local function label(obj, on, text, colour, isFlag, transparency, animation)
				if not on or text == nil or text == "" then
					setv(obj, "Visible", false)
					return
				end
				setv(obj, "Text", surround(
					textCase(tostring(text), isFlag and settings.FlagTextCase or settings.TextCase),
					isFlag and settings.FlagTextSurround or settings.TextSurround))
				setv(obj, "TextSize", math.max(8,
					math.floor((isFlag and flagSize or fontSize) * scale + 0.5)))
				setv(obj, "FontFace", isFlag and flagFont or mainFont)
				ESP.applyLabelLook(obj, firstColor(colour), transparency, animation, os.clock())
				setv(obj, "Visible", true)
			end

			label(set.name, cfg.Name.Enabled,
				player ~= nil
					and (settings.UseDisplayName and player.DisplayName or player.Name)
					or (record.IsDummy and "Dummy" or nil),
				cfg.Name.Color, false, cfg.Name.Transparency, cfg.Name.Animation)
			label(set.weapon, cfg.HeldWeapon.Enabled, record.Weapon, cfg.HeldWeapon.Color, false,
				cfg.HeldWeapon.Transparency, cfg.HeldWeapon.Animation)
			label(set.distance, cfg.Distance.Enabled,
				math.floor(record.Distance) .. "m", cfg.Distance.Color, false, nil, cfg.Distance.Animation)
			label(set.deflect, cfg.Deflecting.Enabled and record.Deflecting,
				"DEFLECTING", cfg.Deflecting.Color, true, cfg.Deflecting.Transparency, cfg.Deflecting.Animation)
			label(set.rank, cfg.Rank.Enabled, record.Rank, cfg.Rank.Color, true, nil, cfg.Rank.Animation)
			label(set.winstreak, cfg.Winstreak.Enabled,
				record.Winstreak and (record.Winstreak .. " WS") or nil,
				cfg.Winstreak.Color, true, nil, cfg.Winstreak.Animation)

			--------------------------------------------------------- tracer --
			local tracer = cfg.Tracer
			if tracer.Enabled then
				local from
				local origin = tracer.Origin
				if origin == "Top" then from = Vector2.new(vp.X * 0.5, 0)
				elseif origin == "Center" then from = Vector2.new(vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5)
				elseif origin == "Mouse" then from = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				else from = Vector2.new(vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y) end

				local to
				if tracer.Target == "Head" then to = Vector2.new(box.CenterX, by)
				elseif tracer.Target == "Center" then to = Vector2.new(box.CenterX, by + bh * 0.5)
				else to = Vector2.new(box.CenterX, by + bh) end

				ESP.setLine(set.tracer, from, to, tracer.Thickness)
				local traceGradient = set.tracer:FindFirstChild("Gradient")
				traceGradient.Enabled = tracer.Gradient
				if tracer.Gradient then
					traceGradient.Color = tracer.GradientColor
					set.tracer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				else
					set.tracer.BackgroundColor3 = tracer.Color
				end
				set.tracer.BackgroundTransparency = tracer.Transparency
				set.tracer.Visible = true
				if tracer.Outline then
					ESP.setLine(set.tracerOut, from, to, tracer.Thickness + tracer.OutlineThickness * 2)
					set.tracerOut.BackgroundColor3 = tracer.OutlineColor
					set.tracerOut.BackgroundTransparency = tracer.OutlineTransparency
					set.tracerOut.Visible = true
				else
					setv(set.tracerOut, "Visible", false)
				end
			else
				setv(set.tracer, "Visible", false)
				setv(set.tracerOut, "Visible", false)
			end

			---------------------------------------------------- head marker --
			if cfg.HeadMarker.Enabled and record.Head then
				local p = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(record.Head.Position)
				if p.Z > 0 then
					ESP.paintHeadMarker(set.headMarker, cfg.HeadMarker, Vector2.new(p.X, p.Y),
						clamp(bw * 0.24, 4, 128))
				else
					for _, container in pairs(set.headMarker.containers) do container.Visible = false end
				end
			else
				for _, container in pairs(set.headMarker.containers) do container.Visible = false end
			end

			------------------------------------------------------- skeleton --
			if cfg.Skeleton.Enabled then
				local parts = record.Parts
				local bones = record.SkeletonLinks
				local motorLinks = type(bones) == "table" and #bones > 0
				if not motorLinks then bones = parts.UpperTorso and SKELETON_R15 or SKELETON_R6 end
				local colour = cfg.Skeleton.Gradient
					and firstColor(cfg.Skeleton.GradientColor) or cfg.Skeleton.Color
				for b = 1, MAX_BONES do
					local line = set.skeleton[b]
					local link = bones[b]
					if line and link then
						local a, c
						if motorLinks then
							a, c = link[1], link[2]
						else
							a, c = parts[link[1]], parts[link[2]]
						end
						if a and c then
							local pa = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(a.Position)
							local pc = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(c.Position)
							if pa.Z > 0 and pc.Z > 0 then
								ESP.setLine(line, Vector2.new(pa.X, pa.Y), Vector2.new(pc.X, pc.Y),
									cfg.Skeleton.Thickness)
								local boneGradient = line:FindFirstChild("Gradient")
								boneGradient.Enabled = cfg.Skeleton.Gradient
								if cfg.Skeleton.Gradient then
									boneGradient.Color = cfg.Skeleton.GradientColor
									line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
								else
									line.BackgroundColor3 = colour
								end
								line.BackgroundTransparency = cfg.Skeleton.Transparency
								line.Visible = true
							else
								setv(line, "Visible", false)
							end
						else
							setv(line, "Visible", false)
						end
					elseif line then
						setv(line, "Visible", false)
					end
				end
			else
				for b = 1, MAX_BONES do
					local l = set.skeleton[b]
					if l and l.Visible then l.Visible = false end
				end
			end

			applyChams(set, record, cfg.Chams)
		end

		for record, set in pairs(ESP.objects) do
			if not seen[record] then hideSet(set) end
		end
	end))
	Features.status.esp = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Movement
--============================================================================--

function Features.Movement(trove)
	local cfg = Config.node({ "Movement" })
	local binds = Features.binds

	local function active(node, bind)
		return node.Enabled and (not bind or bind:IsActive())
	end

	------------------------------------------------ walk / sliding speed ---
	-- Exact khook path: replace the table upvalue containing BASE_WALKSPEED
	-- with a read proxy. `_GetWalkSpeed` is also called by MechanicsController
	-- `Slide`; khook distinguishes that call by debug.info(3, "n") and applies
	-- the independent Sliding multiplier only in that stack.
	task.spawn(function()
		local controller = Resolver.await("MechanicsController", 12)
		if controller == nil then
			markUnavailable("walkspeed", "MechanicsController._GetWalkSpeed not found")
			return
		end
		local method = Resolver.prototypeMethod(controller, "_GetWalkSpeed")
		if type(method) ~= "function" then
			markUnavailable("walkspeed", "MechanicsController._GetWalkSpeed not found")
			return
		end
		local index, original = Resolver.findUpvalue(method, function(value)
			return type(value) == "table" and rawget(value, "BASE_WALKSPEED") ~= nil
		end)
		if index == nil or original == nil or dbg.setupvalue == nil then
			markUnavailable("walkspeed", "BASE_WALKSPEED upvalue not found")
			return
		end
		local proxy = setmetatable({}, {
			__index = function(_, key)
				local value = rawget(original, key)
				if key ~= "BASE_WALKSPEED" then return value end
				local slide = cfg.Sliding
				local caller = dbg.info and dbg.info(3, "n") or nil
				if active(slide, binds.Sliding) and caller == "Slide" then
					return value * slide.Speed
				end
				local walk = cfg.WalkSpeed
				if active(walk, binds.WalkSpeed) then
					return value * walk.Speed
				end
				return value
			end,
		})
		local ok = pcall(dbg.setupvalue, method, index, proxy)
		if not ok then
			markUnavailable("walkspeed", "failed to install BASE_WALKSPEED proxy")
			return
		end
		trove:Add(function()
			pcall(dbg.setupvalue, method, index, original)
		end)
		Features.status.walkspeed = "ok"
	end)

	------------------------------------------------------------ jump ------
	-- khook.lua:87513-87635. Only `_HookFighter`, the live EntityAdded
	-- `CustomGravity` callback and that callback's protos own jump constants.
	-- The proxy exposes the same private unwrap key used by khook so repeated
	-- executions never stack multipliers.
	task.spawn(function()
		local controller = Resolver.await("MechanicsController", 12)
		local hookFighter = controller and Resolver.prototypeMethod(controller, "_HookFighter")
		if type(hookFighter) ~= "function" or dbg.getupvalues == nil or dbg.setupvalue == nil then
			markUnavailable("jumppower", "MechanicsController._HookFighter not found")
			return
		end
		local destroyed = false
		local unwrapKey = "meow\0d67"
		local patched = 0
		local function patchConstants(fn)
			if type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
			local ok, upvalues = pcall(dbg.getupvalues, fn)
			if not ok or type(upvalues) ~= "table" then return end
			for index, candidate in pairs(upvalues) do
				if type(candidate) == "table" then
					local original = nil
					if rawget(candidate, "BASE_JUMPPOWER") ~= nil then
						original = candidate
					else
						local mt = getmetatable(candidate)
						if type(mt) == "table" and type(rawget(mt, "__index")) == "function" then
							local got, value = pcall(function() return candidate[unwrapKey] end)
							if got and type(value) == "table" then original = value end
						end
					end
					if original ~= nil then
						local proxy
						proxy = setmetatable({}, {
							__index = function(_, key)
								if key == unwrapKey then return original end
								if destroyed then
									pcall(dbg.setupvalue, fn, index, original)
									return rawget(original, key)
								end
								local value = rawget(original, key)
								if key == "BASE_JUMPPOWER" and active(cfg.JumpPower, binds.JumpPower) then
									return value * cfg.JumpPower.Speed
								end
								return value
							end,
						})
						if pcall(dbg.setupvalue, fn, index, proxy) then patched += 1 end
						return
					end
				end
			end
		end

		patchConstants(hookFighter)
		local fighter = rawget(controller, "LocalFighter")
		local entityAdded = type(fighter) == "table" and rawget(fighter, "EntityAdded") or nil
		local node = type(entityAdded) == "table" and rawget(entityAdded, "_handlerListHead") or nil
		local callback = nil
		while node and callback == nil do
			local fn = rawget(node, "_fn")
			if type(fn) == "function" and dbg.getconstants then
				local got, constants = pcall(dbg.getconstants, fn)
				if got and type(constants) == "table" then
					for _, value in pairs(constants) do
						if value == "CustomGravity" then callback = fn break end
					end
				end
			end
			node = rawget(node, "_next")
		end
		if callback then
			patchConstants(callback)
			if dbg.getprotos then
				local got, protos = pcall(dbg.getprotos, callback)
				if got and type(protos) == "table" then
					for index in pairs(protos) do
						local okProto, values = pcall(dbg.getproto, callback, index, true)
						if okProto and type(values) == "table" then patchConstants(values[1]) end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		trove:Add(function() destroyed = true end)
		Features.status.jumppower = patched > 0 and "ok" or "BASE_JUMPPOWER upvalue not found"
	end)

	---------------------------------------------------------- noclip -------
	-- khook.lua:134132-134249. The set contains only parts that were
	-- collidable when observed; disabling therefore restores every entry to
	-- true, and live descendants are added/removed through character signals.
	local noclipParts, noclipCharTrove, noclipChar, noclipWasOn = {}, nil, nil, false
	local function clearNoclipCharacter()
		if noclipWasOn then
			for part in pairs(noclipParts) do if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = true end end
		end
		noclipWasOn = false
		if noclipCharTrove then noclipCharTrove:Destroy() noclipCharTrove = nil end
		table.clear(noclipParts)
		noclipChar = nil
	end
	local function bindNoclipCharacter(char)
		clearNoclipCharacter()
		if char == nil then return end
		noclipChar = char
		noclipCharTrove = Trove.new("movement.Noclip")
		for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then noclipParts[part] = true end
		end
		noclipCharTrove:Connect(char.DescendantAdded, function(part)
			if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then noclipParts[part] = true end
		end)
		noclipCharTrove:Connect(char.DescendantRemoving, function(part)
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then noclipParts[part] = nil end
		end)
	end
	trove:Add(clearNoclipCharacter)

	------------------------------------------------ shared movement input --
	-- khook sharedHelper204/sharedHelper219. Keyboard paths are camera-relative;
	-- touch/gamepad paths read Roblox PlayerModule's control vector.
	local playerModule = nil
	pcall(function() playerModule = require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts:WaitForChild("PlayerModule")) end)
	local function planar(vector)
		local flat = Vector3.new(vector.X, 0, vector.Z)
		return flat.Magnitude > 0 and flat.Unit or Vector3.zero
	end
	local function controlVector(includeVertical)
		if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return Vector3.zero end
		local cf, result = Camera.CFrame, Vector3.zero
		if UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled then
			local look = planar(cf.LookVector)
			local right = includeVertical and cf.RightVector or planar(cf.RightVector)
			if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then result += look end
			if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then result -= look end
			if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then result += right end
			if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then result -= right end
			if includeVertical then
				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then result += Vector3.yAxis end
				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
					or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
					result -= Vector3.yAxis
				end
			end
		elseif playerModule then
			local ok, move = pcall(function() return playerModule:GetControls():GetMoveVector() end)
			if ok and move.Magnitude > 0 then
				result += (includeVertical and cf.LookVector or planar(cf.LookVector)) * -move.Z
				result += (includeVertical and cf.RightVector or planar(cf.RightVector)) * move.X
			end
		end
		if not includeVertical and result.Magnitude > 0 then result = result.Unit end
		return result
	end

	---------------------------------------------------------- flight -------
	local flightAttachment, flightVelocity = nil, nil
	local function clearFlight()
		if flightVelocity then flightVelocity:Destroy() flightVelocity = nil end
		if flightAttachment then flightAttachment:Destroy() flightAttachment = nil end
	end
	local function applyFlight(root, velocity)
		if flightVelocity then flightVelocity.VectorVelocity = velocity return end
		flightAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
		flightAttachment.Parent = root
		flightVelocity = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
		flightVelocity.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
		flightVelocity.MaxForce = math.huge
		flightVelocity.VectorVelocity = velocity
		flightVelocity.Attachment0 = flightAttachment
		flightVelocity.Parent = root
	end
	trove:Add(clearFlight)

	------------------------------------------------ physics update ---------
	local function physicsUpdate(dt)
		local char = localCharacter()
		if char ~= noclipChar then bindNoclipCharacter(char) end
		local noclipOn = active(cfg.Noclip, binds.Noclip)
		if noclipOn then
			noclipWasOn = true
			for part in pairs(noclipParts) do if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = false end end
		elseif noclipWasOn then
			for part in pairs(noclipParts) do if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = true end end
			noclipWasOn = false
		end

		local root = localRoot()
		if root and active(cfg.Flight, binds.Flight) then
			local direction = controlVector(true)
			if direction.Magnitude > 0 then direction = direction.Unit end
			applyFlight(root, direction * cfg.Flight.Speed)
		else
			clearFlight()
		end

		if root and active(cfg.AutoStrafe, binds.AutoStrafe)
			and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
			root.CFrame = root.CFrame + controlVector(false) * (cfg.AutoStrafe.Speed / 100) * dt * 60
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity *= Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
		end
	end
	local physicsSignal = RunService.PreSimulation or RunService.Stepped
	trove:Add(physicsSignal:Connect(function(a, b)
		physicsUpdate(tonumber(b) or tonumber(a) or 0)
	end))
	local flightBlankName = "\0kc_flight_blank\0"
	RunService:BindToRenderStep(flightBlankName, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
		local root = localRoot()
		if flightVelocity and root then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
	end)
	trove:Add(function() pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(flightBlankName) end) end)

	------------------------------------------------------- long jump ------
	local function launchLongJump()
		local lj = cfg.LongJump
		if not lj.Enabled then return end
		local bind = binds.LongJump
		if not bind or not bind:IsActive() then return end
		local root = localRoot()
		if not root then return end

		local look = Camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
		if flat.Magnitude > 0 then
			flat = flat.Unit
		else
			local rootLook = root.CFrame.LookVector
			flat = Vector3.new(rootLook.X, 0, rootLook.Z).Unit
		end
		if lj.Mode == "Under Feet" then
			flat = (flat * lj.Behind + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)).Unit
		end
		local velocity = flat * lj.Force + Vector3.new(0, lj.UpwardVelocity, 0)
		local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
		local entity
		if fighter then
			local ok, value = pcall(function() return fighter:GetEntity() end)
			if ok then entity = value end
		end
		if entity == nil then return end
		pcall(function() entity:AirborneCancel() end)
		pcall(function() entity:AirborneTrigger(velocity, 4) end)
	end
	if binds.LongJump then
		trove:Connect(binds.LongJump.ValueChanged, launchLongJump)
	end

	Features.status.movement = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Camera / screen
--============================================================================--

function Features.CameraFeatures(trove)
	local fovCfg = Config.node({ "CameraFov" })
	local tpCfg = Config.node({ "ThirdPerson" })
	local srCfg = Config.node({ "StretchedResolution" })
	local fovCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	local fovBase = fovCamera and fovCamera.FieldOfView or 70
	local fovWasEnabled, fovWriting = false, false
	Resolver.sweep()
	local cameraController = Resolver.get("CameraController")

	local function bindFovCamera(camera)
		fovCamera = camera
		if camera == nil then return end
		fovBase = camera.FieldOfView
		trove:Add(camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FieldOfView"):Connect(function()
			if fovWriting then return end
			if fovCfg.Enabled then
				-- PropertyGuard semantics: remember the game's new underlying value,
				-- then keep the override applied.
				fovBase = camera.FieldOfView
				fovWriting = true
				camera.FieldOfView = fovCfg.Value
				fovWriting = false
			else
				fovBase = camera.FieldOfView
			end
		end))
	end
	bindFovCamera(fovCamera)
	trove:Add(Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
		bindFovCamera(Workspace.CurrentCamera)
	end))
	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local camera = fovCamera
		if camera == nil then return end
		if fovCfg.Enabled then
			fovWasEnabled = true
			if math.abs(camera.FieldOfView - fovCfg.Value) > 0.05 then
				fovWriting = true camera.FieldOfView = fovCfg.Value fovWriting = false
			end
		elseif fovWasEnabled then
			fovWasEnabled = false
			fovWriting = true camera.FieldOfView = fovBase fovWriting = false
		end
	end))

	------------------------------------------------ camera post-processing --
	-- khook applies both third-person offset and the stretched-resolution
	-- matrix immediately after Roblox's camera step (khook.lua:7220-7234).
	local tpParams = RaycastParams.new()
	tpParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
	local tpMode, tpReplica = "Default", nil
	local tpTransparency = {}
	local function clearReplica()
		if tpReplica then tpReplica:Destroy() tpReplica = nil end
	end
	local function restoreBody()
		clearReplica()
		for part, value in pairs(tpTransparency) do
			if part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = value end
		end
		table.clear(tpTransparency)
		tpMode = "Default"
	end
	local function rememberBody(char)
		for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") and tpTransparency[part] == nil then
				tpTransparency[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier
			end
		end
	end
	local function buildReplica(char)
		clearReplica()
		local oldArchivable = char.Archivable
		char.Archivable = true
		local ok, clone = pcall(function() return char:Clone() end)
		char.Archivable = oldArchivable
		if not ok or clone == nil then return end
		clone.Name = "\0khook ThirdPerson Replica\0"
		for _, object in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then
				object:Destroy()
			elseif object:IsA("BasePart") then
				object.CanCollide = false
				object.CanTouch = false
				object.CanQuery = false
				object.Massless = true
				object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
			end
		end
		clone.Parent = Workspace
		tpReplica = clone
	end
	local function setBodyMode(mode, char)
		if char == nil then restoreBody() return end
		rememberBody(char)
		if mode ~= tpMode then
			tpMode = mode
			if mode == "Replica" then buildReplica(char) else clearReplica() end
		end
		for part, original in pairs(tpTransparency) do
			if part.Parent then
				part.LocalTransparencyModifier = mode == "Native" and 0
					or mode == "Replica" and 1 or original
			end
		end
		if mode == "Replica" then
			if tpReplica == nil or tpReplica.Parent == nil then buildReplica(char) end
			if tpReplica then pcall(function() tpReplica:PivotTo(char:GetPivot()) end) end
		end
	end
	local bindName = "\0kc_player_camera\0"
	RunService:BindToRenderStep(bindName, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
		local bind = Features.binds.ThirdPerson
		local want = tpCfg.Enabled and (not bind or bind:IsActive())
		local pov = cameraController and rawget(cameraController, "CameraState")
		want = want and type(pov) == "table" and rawget(pov, "_pov_state") == "FirstPerson"
		local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
		local environmentID = nil
		if fighter then pcall(function() environmentID = fighter:Get("EnvironmentID") end) end
		want = want and environmentID ~= nil
		local char = localCharacter()
		setBodyMode(want and (tpCfg.ShowReplica and "Replica" or "Native") or "Default", char)
		local cf = Camera.CFrame
		local changed = false
		if want then
			local offset = Vector3.new(tpCfg.X, tpCfg.Y, tpCfg.Z)
			if tpCfg.RayCheck then
				local whitelist = nil
				pcall(function() whitelist = fighter:GetRaycastWhitelist(true) end)
				tpParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = whitelist or {}
				local hit = Workspace:Raycast(cf.Position, cf:VectorToWorldSpace(offset), tpParams)
				if hit then
					offset = cf:VectorToObjectSpace(hit.Position - cf.Position)
					if offset.Magnitude > 0.2 then offset = offset.Unit * (offset.Magnitude - 0.2) end
				end
			end
			cf = cf * CFrame.new(offset)
			Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Track
			changed = true
		end
		if srCfg.Enabled then
			cf = cf * CFrame.new(
				0, 0, 0,
				srCfg.X, 0, 0,
				0, srCfg.Y, 0,
				0, 0, 1)
			changed = true
		end
		if changed then Camera.CFrame = cf end
	end)
	trove:Add(function()
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(bindName) end)
		restoreBody()
	end)

	Features.status.camera = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Custom crosshair
--============================================================================--

function Features.Crosshair(trove)
	if not Drawing then markUnavailable("crosshair", "no Drawing API") return end
	local cfg = Config.node({ "CustomCrosshair" })

	local NAMES = { "Top", "Bottom", "Left", "Right" }
	local DIRS = {
		Top = Vector2.new(0, -1), Bottom = Vector2.new(0, 1),
		Left = Vector2.new(-1, 0), Right = Vector2.new(1, 0),
	}
	local lines, outlines = {}, {}
	for _, name in ipairs(NAMES) do
		lines[name] = newDrawing("Line", {})
		outlines[name] = newDrawing("Line", { Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) })
	end
	local dot = newDrawing("Circle", { Filled = true, NumSides = 20 })
	local text = newDrawing("Text", { Center = true, Outline = true })
	local imageDrawing = newDrawing("Image", {})

	trove:Add(function()
		for _, l in pairs(lines) do pcall(function() l:Remove() end) end
		for _, l in pairs(outlines) do pcall(function() l:Remove() end) end
		pcall(function() dot:Remove() end)
		pcall(function() text:Remove() end)
		pcall(function() imageDrawing:Remove() end)
	end)

	local rotation, spreadPhase, wasEnabled = 0, 0, false
	local loadedImagePath = nil
	local function animated(base, animation, sideIndex)
		local now = os.clock() * (animation.Speed or 1)
		if animation.Enabled and animation.Kind == "Perimeter" then
			return ESP.sampleColorSequence(animation.Perimeter.Color,
				(now * 0.2 + (sideIndex or 0) * 0.25) % 1)
		end
		return ESP.resolveColor(base, animation, now)
	end

	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		if not cfg.Enabled then
			if wasEnabled then
				wasEnabled = false
				for _, n in ipairs(NAMES) do
					lines[n].Visible = false
					outlines[n].Visible = false
				end
				dot.Visible = false
				text.Visible = false
				imageDrawing.Visible = false
			end
			return
		end
		wasEnabled = true

		local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
		local centre = (cfg.FollowTarget.Enabled and Features.lastAimScreen)
			or Vector2.new(vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5)

		local gap = cfg.Gap
		if cfg.Spread.Enabled then
			spreadPhase += dt * cfg.Spread.Speed
			gap = lerp(cfg.Spread.Range.Min, cfg.Spread.Range.Max, (math.sin(spreadPhase) + 1) * 0.5)
		end
		if cfg.Rotation.Enabled then
			rotation = (rotation + cfg.Rotation.Speed * dt) % 360
		else
			rotation = cfg.Rotation.Angle
		end

		local rad = math.rad(rotation)
		local cosR, sinR = math.cos(rad), math.sin(rad)
		local showLines = cfg.Style == "Lines"
		local len, thickness = cfg.Length, cfg.Thickness

		for index, name in ipairs(NAMES) do
			local seg = cfg[name]
			local line, outline = lines[name], outlines[name]
			if showLines and seg.Enabled then
				local d = DIRS[name]
				local dx = d.X * cosR - d.Y * sinR
				local dy = d.X * sinR + d.Y * cosR
				local from = Vector2.new(centre.X + dx * gap, centre.Y + dy * gap)
				local to = Vector2.new(centre.X + dx * (gap + len), centre.Y + dy * (gap + len))
				line.From, line.To = from, to
				setv(line, "Color", animated(seg.Color, cfg.Animation, index - 1))
				setv(line, "Thickness", thickness)
				setv(line, "Visible", true)
				if cfg.Outline.Enabled then
					outline.From, outline.To = from, to
					setv(outline, "Color", cfg.Outline.Color)
					setv(outline, "Thickness", thickness + cfg.Outline.Thickness * 2)
					setv(outline, "Visible", true)
				else
					setv(outline, "Visible", false)
				end
			else
				setv(line, "Visible", false)
				setv(outline, "Visible", false)
			end
		end

		if cfg.Style == "Dot" then
			dot.Position = centre
			setv(dot, "Radius", math.max(1, thickness))
			setv(dot, "Color", firstColor(cfg.Top.Color))
			setv(dot, "Visible", true)
		else
			setv(dot, "Visible", false)
		end

		if cfg.Style == "Image" and cfg.Image.Path ~= "" then
			if loadedImagePath ~= cfg.Image.Path then
				loadedImagePath = cfg.Image.Path
				local ok, data = pcall(function()
					if readfile and (not isfile or isfile(loadedImagePath)) then
						return readfile(loadedImagePath)
					end
					return nil
				end)
				if ok and data then pcall(function() imageDrawing.Data = data end) end
			end
			local size = cfg.Image.Size
			setv(imageDrawing, "Size", Vector2.new(size, size))
			setv(imageDrawing, "Position", centre - Vector2.new(size, size) * 0.5)
			setv(imageDrawing, "Transparency", 1 - cfg.Image.Transparency)
			setv(imageDrawing, "Visible", true)
		else
			setv(imageDrawing, "Visible", false)
		end

		if cfg.Text.Enabled and cfg.Text.Content ~= "" then
			setv(text, "Text", cfg.Text.Content)
			setv(text, "Size", cfg.Text.Size)
			setv(text, "Color", animated(cfg.Text.Color, cfg.Text.Animation, 0))
			setv(text, "Outline", cfg.Text.Outline.Enabled)
			setv(text, "OutlineColor", cfg.Text.Outline.Color)
			setv(text, "OutlineThickness", cfg.Text.Outline.Thickness)
			setv(text, "Font", ({ System = 0, Plex = 2, Monospace = 3, Inconsolata = 3 })[cfg.Text.Font] or 3)
			text.Position = Vector2.new(centre.X, centre.Y + cfg.Text.Offset)
			setv(text, "Visible", true)
		else
			setv(text, "Visible", false)
		end
	end))
	Features.status.crosshair = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Local chams / viewmodel highlight / wireframe / viewmodel offset
--============================================================================--

function Features.LocalVisuals(trove)
	local chamsCfg = Config.node({ "Chams" })
	local hlCfg = Config.node({ "ViewModelHighlight" })
	local wfCfg = Config.node({ "Wireframe" })
	local offCfg = Config.node({ "ViewModelOffset" })

	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "\0kc_visuals\0"
	folder.Parent = gethui()
	trove:Add(folder)

	local highlights = {}
	local function highlightFor(key, depthMode)
		local h = highlights[key]
		if not h or h.Parent == nil then
			h = Instance.new("Highlight")
			h.DepthMode = depthMode or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
			h.Parent = folder
			highlights[key] = h
		end
		return h
	end
	local bodyOriginal = {}
	local materialMap = {
		Ghost = Enum.Material.ForceField,
		Flat = Enum.Material.Neon,
		Foil = Enum.Material.Foil,
		Custom = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
		Reflective = Enum.Material.Glass,
	}
	local function restoreBody()
		for object, original in pairs(bodyOriginal) do
			if object.Parent then
				pcall(function()
					if original.Color then
						object.Color, object.Transparency, object.Material = original.Color, original.Transparency, original.Material
						if original.TextureID ~= nil and object:IsA("MeshPart") then object.TextureID = original.TextureID end
					elseif original.TextureID ~= nil then
						object.TextureID = original.TextureID
					else
						object.Transparency = original.Transparency
					end
				end)
			end
		end
		table.clear(bodyOriginal)
	end
	trove:Add(restoreBody)

	-- viewmodel lookup is cached; it only changes on weapon swap
	local vmCache, vmFrame = nil, -1
	local function viewmodel()
		if vmFrame == Registry.frame then return vmCache end
		vmFrame = Registry.frame
		if vmCache and vmCache.Parent then return vmCache end
		vmCache = nil
		for _, c in ipairs(Camera:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("Model") then vmCache = c break end
		end
		return vmCache
	end

	local wireframes = {}
	local function applyWireframe(key, model, node)
		local existing = wireframes[key]
		if not node.Enabled or not model then
			if existing then
				for _, h in ipairs(existing) do h:Destroy() end
				wireframes[key] = nil
			end
			return
		end
		if existing and existing.model == model then
			for _, h in ipairs(existing) do
				h.LineThickness = node.Width * 1000
				h.Color3 = node.Color
			end
			return
		end
		if existing then for _, h in ipairs(existing) do h:Destroy() end end
		local made = { model = model }
		for _, part in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then
				local box = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
				box.Adornee = part
				box.LineThickness = node.Width * 1000
				box.Color3 = node.Color
				box.SurfaceTransparency = 1
				box.Parent = folder
				table.insert(made, box)
			end
		end
		wireframes[key] = made
	end
	trove:Add(function()
		for _, set in pairs(wireframes) do
			for _, h in ipairs(set) do
				if typeof(h) == "Instance" then h:Destroy() end
			end
		end
	end)

	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local char = localCharacter()
		if chamsCfg.Character.Enabled and char then
			for _, object in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
				if object:IsA("BasePart") then
					if bodyOriginal[object] == nil then
						bodyOriginal[object] = { Color = object.Color, Transparency = object.Transparency, Material = object.Material }
					end
					object.Color = chamsCfg.Character.Color
					object.Transparency = chamsCfg.Character.Transparency
					local material = materialMap[chamsCfg.Character.Material]
					if material then object.Material = material else object.Material = bodyOriginal[object].Material end
					if object:IsA("MeshPart") and chamsCfg.Character.StripTextures then
						if bodyOriginal[object].TextureID == nil then bodyOriginal[object].TextureID = object.TextureID end
						object.TextureID = ""
					elseif object:IsA("MeshPart") and bodyOriginal[object].TextureID ~= nil then
						object.TextureID = bodyOriginal[object].TextureID
					end
				elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
					if bodyOriginal[object] == nil then bodyOriginal[object] = { Transparency = object.Transparency } end
					object.Transparency = chamsCfg.Character.StripTextures and 1 or bodyOriginal[object].Transparency
				end
			end
		elseif next(bodyOriginal) ~= nil then
			restoreBody()
		end

		local vm = viewmodel()

		local ha = highlightFor("Arms", Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop)
		if hlCfg.Arms.Enabled and vm then
			if ha.Adornee ~= vm then ha.Adornee = vm end
			setv(ha, "FillColor", hlCfg.Arms.FillColor)
			setv(ha, "FillTransparency", hlCfg.Arms.FillTransparency)
			setv(ha, "OutlineColor", hlCfg.Arms.OutlineColor)
			setv(ha, "OutlineTransparency", hlCfg.Arms.OutlineTransparency)
			setv(ha, "Enabled", true)
		else
			setv(ha, "Enabled", false)
		end

		applyWireframe("Arms", wfCfg.Arms.Enabled and vm or nil, wfCfg.Arms)
		applyWireframe("Item", wfCfg.Item.Enabled and vm or nil, wfCfg.Item)

		if offCfg.Enabled and vm then
			local cf = Camera.CFrame
				* CFrame.new(offCfg.X, offCfg.Y, offCfg.Z)
				* CFrame.Angles(math.rad(offCfg.Pitch), math.rad(offCfg.Yaw), math.rad(offCfg.Roll))
			pcall(function() vm:PivotTo(cf) end)
		end
	end))
	Features.status.visuals = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Local animation player
--============================================================================--

function Features.AnimationPlayer(trove)
	local cfg = Config.node({ "AnimationPlayer" })
	local track, animatorWait = nil, nil
	local applying = false
	Features.AnimationIds = Features.AnimationIds or {}

	local function stop()
		if animatorWait then animatorWait:Disconnect() animatorWait = nil end
		if track then pcall(function() track:Stop() end) track = nil end
	end
	local function resolveId()
		local custom = type(cfg.CustomId) == "string" and cfg.CustomId:match("%d+") or nil
		return custom or Features.AnimationIds[cfg.Animation]
	end
	local function apply()
		if applying then return end
		applying = true
		stop()
		if cfg.Enabled then
			local hum = localHumanoid()
			if hum then
				local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
				if not animator then
					animatorWait = hum.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
						if child:IsA("Animator") then task.defer(apply) end
					end)
				else
					local id = resolveId()
					if id then
						local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
						animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
						local ok, loaded = pcall(animator.LoadAnimation, animator, animation)
						animation:Destroy()
						if ok then
							track = loaded
							track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
							track.Looped = true
							track:Play()
							track:AdjustSpeed(cfg.Speed)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		applying = false
	end
	trove:Add(stop)
	trove:Add(Config.watchAll({
		{ "AnimationPlayer", "Enabled" },
		{ "AnimationPlayer", "Animation" },
		{ "AnimationPlayer", "CustomId" },
	}, apply))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "AnimationPlayer", "Speed" }, function()
		if track then pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(cfg.Speed) end) end
	end, true))
	if LocalPlayer then trove:Add(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.defer(apply) end)) end
	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not track then return end
		if not track.IsPlaying then task.defer(apply) return end
		local length = track.Length
		if length <= 0 then return end
		local first = clamp(cfg.Start, 0, 100) / 100 * length
		local last = clamp(cfg.End, 0, 100) / 100 * length
		if last > first and (track.TimePosition < first or track.TimePosition >= last) then
			track.TimePosition = first
		end
	end))
	Features.status.animationplayer = "ok"
end
--============================================================================--
-- World: lighting, post-processing, sky, weather, ambience
--============================================================================--

function Features.World(trove)
	local saved = {}
	local function remember(prop)
		if saved[prop] == nil then saved[prop] = Lighting[prop] end
	end
	trove:Add(function()
		for prop, value in pairs(saved) do
			pcall(function() Lighting[prop] = value end)
		end
	end)

	--- Apply one Lighting property from an {Enabled=,Value=} node.
	local function bindLighting(node, prop, key)
		Config.watchAll({
			{ "Lighting", node, "Enabled" },
			{ "Lighting", node, key or "Value" },
			{ "Lighting", "Enabled" },
		}, function()
			local master = Config.get({ "Lighting", "Enabled" })
			local cfg = Config.get({ "Lighting", node })
			remember(prop)
			if master and cfg and cfg.Enabled then
				pcall(function() Lighting[prop] = cfg[key or "Value"] end)
			else
				pcall(function() Lighting[prop] = saved[prop] end)
			end
		end)
	end

	bindLighting("ClockTime", "ClockTime")
	bindLighting("Brightness", "Brightness")
	bindLighting("EnvironmentDiffuseScale", "EnvironmentDiffuseScale")
	bindLighting("ExposureCompensation", "ExposureCompensation")
	bindLighting("GeographicLatitude", "GeographicLatitude")
	bindLighting("ShadowSoftness", "ShadowSoftness")
	bindLighting("ShadowColor", "ShadowColor")

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Lighting", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "Ambient", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "Ambient", "Indoor" },
		{ "Lighting", "Ambient", "Outdoor" },
	}, function()
		local master = Config.get({ "Lighting", "Enabled" })
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Lighting", "Ambient" })
		remember("Ambient")
		remember("OutdoorAmbient")
		if master and cfg.Enabled then
			Lighting.Ambient = cfg.Indoor
			Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = cfg.Outdoor
		else
			Lighting.Ambient = saved.Ambient
			Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = saved.OutdoorAmbient
		end
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Lighting", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "ColorShift", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "ColorShift", "Top" },
		{ "Lighting", "ColorShift", "Bottom" },
	}, function()
		local master = Config.get({ "Lighting", "Enabled" })
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Lighting", "ColorShift" })
		remember("ColorShift_Top")
		remember("ColorShift_Bottom")
		if master and cfg.Enabled then
			Lighting.ColorShift_Top = cfg.Top
			Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = cfg.Bottom
		else
			Lighting.ColorShift_Top = saved.ColorShift_Top
			Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = saved.ColorShift_Bottom
		end
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Lighting", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "Fog", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "Fog", "Color" },
		{ "Lighting", "Fog", "Start" },
		{ "Lighting", "Fog", "End" },
	}, function()
		local master = Config.get({ "Lighting", "Enabled" })
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Lighting", "Fog" })
		remember("FogColor") remember("FogStart") remember("FogEnd")
		if master and cfg.Enabled then
			Lighting.FogColor = cfg.Color
			Lighting.FogStart = cfg.Start
			Lighting.FogEnd = cfg.End
		else
			Lighting.FogColor = saved.FogColor
			Lighting.FogStart = saved.FogStart
			Lighting.FogEnd = saved.FogEnd
		end
	end)

	Config.watch({ "Lighting", "DisableGlobalShadows" }, function(v)
		remember("GlobalShadows")
		Lighting.GlobalShadows = not v
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Lighting", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "LightingStyle", "Enabled" },
		{ "Lighting", "LightingStyle", "Value" },
	}, function()
		local master = Config.get({ "Lighting", "Enabled" })
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Lighting", "LightingStyle" })
		remember("Technology")
		if master and cfg.Enabled then
			pcall(function()
				Lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology[cfg.Value] or Lighting.Technology
			end)
		end
	end)

	-------------------------------------------------------- post-processing --
	local effects = {}

	local function effect(class, name)
		local e = effects[name]
		if e and e.Parent then return e end
		e = Instance.new(class)
		e.Name = "\0kc_" .. name .. "\0"
		e.Enabled = false
		e.Parent = Lighting
		effects[name] = e
		trove:Add(e)
		return e
	end

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Bloom", "Enabled" }, { "Bloom", "Size" }, { "Bloom", "Threshold" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Bloom" })
		local e = effect("BloomEffect", "Bloom")
		e.Enabled = cfg.Enabled
		e.Size = cfg.Size or 24
		e.Threshold = cfg.Threshold or 0.95
		e.Intensity = 1
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "ColorCorrection", "Enabled" }, { "ColorCorrection", "TintColor" },
		{ "ColorCorrection", "Saturation" }, { "ColorCorrection", "Brightness" },
		{ "ColorCorrection", "Contrast" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "ColorCorrection" })
		local e = effect("ColorCorrectionEffect", "ColorCorrection")
		e.Enabled = cfg.Enabled
		e.TintColor = cfg.TintColor
		e.Saturation = cfg.Saturation or 0
		e.Brightness = cfg.Brightness or 0
		e.Contrast = cfg.Contrast or 0
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "DepthOfField", "Enabled" }, { "DepthOfField", "FarIntensity" },
		{ "DepthOfField", "FocusDistance" }, { "DepthOfField", "InFocusRadius" },
		{ "DepthOfField", "NearIntensity" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "DepthOfField" })
		local e = effect("DepthOfFieldEffect", "DepthOfField")
		e.Enabled = cfg.Enabled
		e.FarIntensity = cfg.FarIntensity or 0.75
		e.FocusDistance = cfg.FocusDistance or 0.05
		e.InFocusRadius = cfg.InFocusRadius or 10
		e.NearIntensity = cfg.NearIntensity or 0.75
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "SunRays", "Enabled" }, { "SunRays", "Intensity" }, { "SunRays", "Spread" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "SunRays" })
		local e = effect("SunRaysEffect", "SunRays")
		e.Enabled = cfg.Enabled
		e.Intensity = cfg.Intensity or 0.25
		e.Spread = cfg.Spread or 1
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Atmosphere", "Enabled" }, { "Atmosphere", "Color" }, { "Atmosphere", "Decay" },
		{ "Atmosphere", "Density" }, { "Atmosphere", "Offset" },
		{ "Atmosphere", "Glare" }, { "Atmosphere", "Haze" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Atmosphere" })
		local existing = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
		if not cfg.Enabled then
			if effects.Atmosphere then effects.Atmosphere:Destroy() effects.Atmosphere = nil end
			return
		end
		local a = effects.Atmosphere
		if not a or a.Parent == nil then
			a = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
			a.Name = "\0kc_Atmosphere\0"
			a.Parent = Lighting
			effects.Atmosphere = a
			trove:Add(a)
			if existing then existing.Density = 0 end
		end
		a.Color = cfg.Color
		a.Decay = cfg.Decay
		a.Density = cfg.Density or 0.395
		a.Offset = cfg.Offset or 0
		a.Glare = cfg.Glare or 0
		a.Haze = cfg.Haze or 0
	end)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "MotionBlur", "Enabled" }, { "MotionBlur", "Intensity" }, { "MotionBlur", "Sensitivity" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "MotionBlur" })
		local e = effect("BlurEffect", "MotionBlur")
		e.Enabled = cfg.Enabled
		e.Size = cfg.Enabled and (cfg.Intensity or 10) or 0
	end)

	-------------------------------------------------------------- skybox ---
	local SKYBOXES = {
		["Clear Blue"]  = "12064107",
		["Sunset"]      = "159454299",
		["Midnight"]    = "12064109",
		["Nebula"]      = "159454286",
		["Storm"]       = "12064115",
		["Void"]        = "12064111",
	}
	Features.SkyboxPresets = { "Clear Blue", "Sunset", "Midnight", "Nebula", "Storm", "Void" }

	Config.watchAll({ { "Skybox", "Enabled" }, { "Skybox", "Preset" } }, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Skybox" })
		if effects.Sky then effects.Sky:Destroy() effects.Sky = nil end
		if not cfg.Enabled or cfg.Preset == "" then return end
		local id = SKYBOXES[cfg.Preset]
		if not id then return end
		local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
		sky.Name = "\0kc_Sky\0"
		for _, face in ipairs({ "Bk", "Dn", "Ft", "Lf", "Rt", "Up" }) do
			sky["Skybox" .. face] = "rbxassetid://" .. id
		end
		sky.Parent = Lighting
		effects.Sky = sky
		trove:Add(sky)
	end)

	------------------------------------------------------------- weather ---
	local weatherEmitter, weatherPart

	local WEATHER_TEXTURES = {
		Snow = "rbxassetid://241594314",
		Rain = "rbxassetid://241594314",
		Ash  = "rbxassetid://241594314",
		Sand = "rbxassetid://241594314",
	}
	Features.WeatherPresets = { "Snow", "Rain", "Ash", "Sand" }

	local function teardownWeather()
		if weatherPart then weatherPart:Destroy() weatherPart = nil end
		weatherEmitter = nil
	end
	trove:Add(teardownWeather)

	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Weather", "Enabled" }, { "Weather", "Preset" }, { "Weather", "Intensity" },
		{ "Weather", "Rate" }, { "Weather", "Height" }, { "Weather", "Color" },
		{ "Weather", "Speed" }, { "Weather", "Size" }, { "Weather", "Spread" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Weather" })
		if not cfg.Enabled then teardownWeather() return end
		if not weatherPart or weatherPart.Parent == nil then
			weatherPart = Instance.new("Part")
			weatherPart.Name = "\0kc_weather\0"
			weatherPart.Anchored = true
			weatherPart.CanCollide = false
			weatherPart.CanQuery = false
			weatherPart.CanTouch = false
			weatherPart.Transparency = 1
			weatherPart.Size = Vector3.one
			weatherPart.Parent = Workspace
			weatherEmitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
			weatherEmitter.Parent = weatherPart
			trove:Add(weatherPart)
		end
		local e = weatherEmitter
		e.Texture = WEATHER_TEXTURES[cfg.Preset] or WEATHER_TEXTURES.Snow
		e.Rate = 60 * (cfg.Rate or 1) * (cfg.Intensity or 1)
		e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(3, 6)
		e.Speed = NumberRange.new(6 * (cfg.Speed or 1), 12 * (cfg.Speed or 1))
		e.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.25 * (cfg.Size or 1))
		e.Color = ColorSequence.new(cfg.Color)
		e.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(cfg.Spread or 25, cfg.Spread or 25)
		e.LightEmission = cfg.Glow or 0.6
		e.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
		e.Acceleration = Vector3.new(
			math.cos(math.rad(cfg.Wind and cfg.Wind.Angle or 0)) * (cfg.Wind and cfg.Wind.Strength or 0),
			-8,
			math.sin(math.rad(cfg.Wind and cfg.Wind.Angle or 0)) * (cfg.Wind and cfg.Wind.Strength or 0))
		e.ZOffset = 0
	end)

	-- follow the camera so the emitter always covers the player
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if weatherPart and weatherPart.Parent then
			local h = Config.get({ "Weather", "Height" }) or 45
			weatherPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(Camera.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, h, 0))
			weatherPart.Size = Vector3.new(120, 1, 120)
		end
	end))

	------------------------------------------------------------ ambience ---
	local ambienceSound
	Config.watchAll({
		{ "Ambience", "Enabled" }, { "Ambience", "CustomSound" }, { "Ambience", "Volume" },
	}, function()
		local cfg = Config.get({ "Ambience" })
		if not cfg.Enabled or cfg.CustomSound == "" then
			if ambienceSound then ambienceSound:Destroy() ambienceSound = nil end
			return
		end
		if not ambienceSound or ambienceSound.Parent == nil then
			ambienceSound = Instance.new("Sound")
			ambienceSound.Name = "\0kc_ambience\0"
			ambienceSound.Looped = true
			ambienceSound.Parent = SoundService
			trove:Add(ambienceSound)
		end
		local id = cfg.CustomSound
		if not string.match(id, "^rbxassetid://") then id = "rbxassetid://" .. id end
		if ambienceSound.SoundId ~= id then ambienceSound.SoundId = id end
		ambienceSound.Volume = cfg.Volume or 1
		if not ambienceSound.IsPlaying then ambienceSound:Play() end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--
-- Sound visualizer
--============================================================================--

function Features.SoundVisualizer(trove)
	local cfg = Config.node({ "SoundVisualizer" })
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "RivalsSoundVisualizer"
	folder.Parent = Workspace
	trove:Add(folder)

	local hooks, lastPlayedAt = {}, {}
	local active, circlePool, footprintPool = {}, {}, {}
	local descendantAdded

	local function groundCFrame(part)
		local _, yaw = part.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
		return CFrame.new(part.Position - Vector3.new(0, part.Size.Y * 0.5, 0))
			* CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
	end

	local function soundPart(sound)
		local parent = sound.Parent
		if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then return parent end
		if parent and parent:IsA("Attachment") and parent.Parent
			and parent.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then return parent.Parent end
		return nil
	end

	local function footParts(model)
		if model == nil then return {} end
		local out = {}
		for _, name in ipairs({ "LeftFoot", "RightFoot" }) do
			local part = model:FindFirstChild(name)
			if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(out, part) end
		end
		if #out == 0 then
			for _, name in ipairs({ "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }) do
				local part = model:FindFirstChild(name)
				if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(out, part) end
			end
		end
		return out
	end

	local function passesSource(source, model)
		if source == "All" or model == nil then return true end
		local isSelf = model == LocalPlayer.Character
		if source == "Self" then return isSelf end
		if isSelf then return false end
		local record = Registry.byModel[model]
		if record then
			if source == "Enemies" and not record.IsEnemy then return false end
			if source == "Team" and record.IsEnemy then return false end
		end
		return true
	end

	local function loudnessColor(value)
		if value <= 0.5 then return Color3.new(value * 2, 1, 0) end
		return Color3.new(1, (1 - value) * 2, 0)
	end

	local function acquireCircle()
		local adorn = table.remove(circlePool)
		if adorn == nil then
			adorn = Instance.new("CylinderHandleAdornment")
			adorn.Adornee = Workspace.Terrain
			adorn.AlwaysOnTop = true
			adorn.ZIndex = 5
			adorn.Height = 0.05
			adorn.Angle = 360
			adorn.Parent = folder
		end
		adorn.Visible = true
		return adorn
	end

	local function acquireFootprint()
		local adorn = table.remove(footprintPool)
		if adorn == nil then
			adorn = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
			adorn.Adornee = Workspace.Terrain
			adorn.AlwaysOnTop = true
			adorn.ZIndex = 5
			adorn.Parent = folder
		end
		adorn.Visible = true
		return adorn
	end

	local function recycle(effect)
		effect.adornment.Visible = false
		local pool = effect.isCircle and circlePool or footprintPool
		if #pool < 32 then table.insert(pool, effect.adornment) else effect.adornment:Destroy() end
	end

	local function push(effect)
		if #active >= 96 then recycle(table.remove(active, 1)) end
		table.insert(active, effect)
	end

	local function spawnCircle(cf, color, duration, radius)
		local adorn = acquireCircle()
		adorn.Color3, adorn.Transparency = color, 0.2
		adorn.Radius, adorn.InnerRadius = 0.01, 0
		adorn.CFrame = cf * CFrame.Angles(math.pi / 2, 0, 0)
		push({ adornment = adorn, isCircle = true, elapsed = 0,
			duration = duration, maxRadius = radius })
	end

	local function spawnFootprint(cf, size, color, duration)
		local adorn = acquireFootprint()
		adorn.Size, adorn.Color3, adorn.Transparency = size, color, 0.2
		adorn.CFrame = cf
		push({ adornment = adorn, isCircle = false, elapsed = 0,
			duration = duration, maxRadius = 0 })
	end

	local function spawnVisuals(mode, origin, feet, color, duration)
		if mode == "Circle" then
			if #feet > 0 then
				for _, part in ipairs(feet) do
					spawnCircle(groundCFrame(part), color, duration,
						math.max(part.Size.X, part.Size.Z) * 0.5 + 0.3)
				end
			else
				spawnCircle(groundCFrame(origin), color, duration,
					math.max(origin.Size.X, origin.Size.Z) * 0.5 + 0.3)
			end
		elseif #feet > 0 then
			for _, part in ipairs(feet) do
				spawnFootprint(groundCFrame(part),
					Vector3.new(part.Size.X + 0.1, 0.15, part.Size.Z + 0.1), color, duration)
			end
		else
			local size = origin.Size.Magnitude > 0 and origin.Size or Vector3.new(2, 0.2, 2)
			spawnFootprint(groundCFrame(origin), size, color, duration)
		end
	end

	local function onPlayed(sound)
		if sound.Volume <= 0 then return end
		local now = os.clock()
		if lastPlayedAt[sound] and now - lastPlayedAt[sound] < 0.15 then return end
		lastPlayedAt[sound] = now
		local origin = soundPart(sound)
		if origin == nil or Camera == nil then return end
		local distance = (Camera.CFrame.Position - origin.Position).Magnitude
		local maxDistance = sound.RollOffMaxDistance
		if maxDistance > 0 and maxDistance < distance then return end
		if cfg.MinVolume > 0 and sound.Volume < cfg.MinVolume then return end
		local model = origin:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		if not passesSource(cfg.Source, model) then return end
		local falloff = maxDistance > 0 and 1 - clamp(distance / maxDistance, 0, 1) or 1
		local loudness = clamp(sound.Volume * falloff, 0, 1)
		local feet = footParts(model)
		local kind = #feet > 0 and "Footsteps" or "Other"
		if not cfg.Types[kind] then return end
		local look = cfg[kind]
		local color = look.UseLoudnessColor and loudnessColor(loudness) or look.Color
		spawnVisuals(look.Mode, origin, feet, color, 0.6 + (2.5 - 0.6) * loudness)
	end

	local function forget(sound)
		local hook = hooks[sound]
		if hook == nil then return end
		hooks[sound], lastPlayedAt[sound] = nil, nil
		hook.played:Disconnect()
		hook.destroying:Disconnect()
	end

	local function track(sound)
		if descendantAdded == nil or hooks[sound] ~= nil or not sound:IsA("Sound") then return end
		hooks[sound] = {
			played = sound.Played:Connect(function() onPlayed(sound) end),
			destroying = sound.Destroying:Connect(function() forget(sound) end),
		}
	end

	local function stopTracking()
		if descendantAdded == nil then return end
		descendantAdded:Disconnect()
		descendantAdded = nil
		for sound in pairs(hooks) do forget(sound) end
		table.clear(lastPlayedAt)
	end

	local function setEnabled(enabled)
		if enabled == (descendantAdded ~= nil) then return end
		if enabled then
			descendantAdded = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
				if inst:IsA("Sound") then track(inst) end
			end)
			for _, inst in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if inst:IsA("Sound") then track(inst) end
			end
		else
			stopTracking()
		end
	end

	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "SoundVisualizer", "Enabled" }, setEnabled))
	trove:Add(RunService.PreRender:Connect(function(dt)
		for i = #active, 1, -1 do
			local effect = active[i]
			effect.elapsed += dt
			local alpha = clamp(effect.elapsed / effect.duration, 0, 1)
			if effect.isCircle then
				local radius = effect.maxRadius * (1 - (1 - alpha) ^ 5)
				effect.adornment.Radius = radius
				effect.adornment.InnerRadius = math.max(0, radius - 0.15)
				effect.adornment.Transparency = 0.2 + 0.8 * alpha ^ 4
			else
				effect.adornment.Transparency = 0.2 + 0.8 * alpha ^ 2
			end
			if alpha >= 1 then
				recycle(effect)
				table.remove(active, i)
			end
		end
	end))
	trove:Add(function()
		stopTracking()
		for _, effect in ipairs(active) do pcall(recycle, effect) end
		table.clear(active)
	end)
	Features.status.soundviz = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- Bullet tracers
--============================================================================--

function Features.BulletTracers(trove)
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "\0kc_tracers\0"
	folder.Parent = Workspace
	trove:Add(folder)

	--- Draw one tracer from origin to hit point.
	function Features.drawTracer(from, to)
		local cfg = Config.get({ "BulletTracers" })
		if not cfg or not cfg.Enabled then return end

		local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
		local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
		local p0 = Instance.new("Part")
		p0.Anchored, p0.CanCollide, p0.CanQuery, p0.CanTouch = true, false, false, false
		p0.Transparency = 1
		p0.Size = Vector3.one
		p0.CFrame = CFrame.new(from)
		p0.Parent = folder
		a0.Parent = p0

		local p1 = p0:Clone()
		p1.CFrame = CFrame.new(to)
		p1.Parent = folder
		a1.Parent = p1

		local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
		beam.Attachment0 = a0
		beam.Attachment1 = a1
		beam.Width0 = cfg.Width or 0.06
		beam.Width1 = cfg.Width or 0.06
		beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(cfg.Color)
		beam.LightEmission = cfg.Emission or 1
		beam.FaceCamera = true
		beam.TextureLength = cfg.TextureLength or 4
		beam.TextureSpeed = cfg.TextureSpeed or 1
		beam.Parent = p0

		task.spawn(function()
			local life = cfg.Lifetime or 0.6
			local fade = cfg.FadeTime or 0.35
			task.wait(math.max(0, life - fade))
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while os.clock() - t0 < fade do
				local a = (os.clock() - t0) / fade
				beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(a)
				if cfg.Expand then
					local w = (cfg.Width or 0.06) * (1 + a * (cfg.ExpandSpeed or 18) * 0.05 * (cfg.ExpandDamper or 0.7))
					beam.Width0, beam.Width1 = w, w
				end
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
			p0:Destroy()
			p1:Destroy()
		end)
	end
end

--============================================================================--
-- Hit / elimination feedback
--============================================================================--

function Features.Feedback(trove)
	local flashGui = make("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "\0kc_flash\0", IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		DisplayOrder = 2 ^ 30 - 2, ResetOnSpawn = false,
	})
	pcall(protectgui, flashGui)
	flashGui.Parent = gethui()
	trove:Add(flashGui)

	local flash = make("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = flashGui,
	})

	local function doFlash(color, duration)
		flash.BackgroundColor3 = color
		flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.75
		tween(flash, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 },
			TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out))
	end

	local function playSound(id, volume, pitch)
		if not id or id == "" then return end
		local s = Instance.new("Sound")
		s.SoundId = string.match(id, "^rbxassetid://") and id or ("rbxassetid://" .. id)
		s.Volume = volume or 1
		s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch or 1
		s.Parent = SoundService
		s:Play()
		s.Ended:Once(function() s:Destroy() end)
		task.delay(6, function() if s.Parent then s:Destroy() end end)
	end

	--- Public entry points; hooked by whatever detects a hit. Without game
	--- internals we drive them from humanoid health drops on other players.
	function Features.onPlayerHit(entity, damage, partName)
		local cfg = Config.get({ "PlayerHit" })
		if not cfg or not cfg.Enabled then return end
		if cfg.HitFlash.Enabled then doFlash(cfg.HitFlash.Color, cfg.HitFlash.Duration) end
		if cfg.Sound.Enabled then
			local isHead = partName == "Head"
			playSound(isHead and cfg.Sound.HeadName or cfg.Sound.BodyName,
				isHead and cfg.Sound.HeadVolume or cfg.Sound.BodyVolume,
				isHead and cfg.Sound.HeadPitch or cfg.Sound.BodyPitch)
		end
		if cfg.Notification.Enabled then
			local text = cfg.Notification.Text or ""
			text = string.gsub(text, "%%DNAME%%", (entity.Player and entity.Player.DisplayName or entity.Character and entity.Character.Name or "Target"))
			text = string.gsub(text, "%%NAME%%",
				(entity.Player and entity.Player.Name) or entity.Character and entity.Character.Name or "Target")
			text = string.gsub(text, "%%DMG%%", tostring(math.floor(damage or 0)))
			text = string.gsub(text, "%%PART%%", partName or "Body")
			Notify(text, 3)
		end
		if cfg.Chams.Enabled then
			local h = Instance.new("Highlight")
			h.Adornee = entity.Character
			h.FillColor = cfg.Chams.Color
			h.FillTransparency = cfg.Chams.Transparency or 0
			h.OutlineTransparency = 1
			h.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			h.Parent = gethui()
			task.delay(cfg.Chams.Duration or 0.8, function() h:Destroy() end)
		end
	end

	function Features.onPlayerEliminated(entity)
		local cfg = Config.get({ "PlayerElimination" })
		if not cfg or not cfg.Enabled then return end
		if cfg.KillFlash.Enabled then doFlash(cfg.KillFlash.Color, cfg.KillFlash.Duration) end
		if cfg.Sound.Enabled then playSound(cfg.Sound.Name, cfg.Sound.Volume, cfg.Sound.Pitch) end
		if cfg.Notification.Enabled then
			local text = cfg.Notification.Text or ""
			text = string.gsub(text, "%%DNAME%%", (entity.Player and entity.Player.DisplayName or entity.Character and entity.Character.Name or "Target"))
			text = string.gsub(text, "%%NAME%%", (entity.Player and entity.Player.Name or entity.Character and entity.Character.Name or "Target"))
			Notify(text, 3)
		end
	end

	-- health watcher: the closest we can get to a hit event without the game's
	-- own damage signal.
	-- Keyed by the record, not the player: range dummies are records with no
	-- Player at all, and a nil table index throws every frame.
	local lastHealth = {}
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		for _, entity in ipairs(Game.entities()) do
			local prev = lastHealth[entity]
			if prev and entity.Health < prev - 0.5 then
				Features.onPlayerHit(entity, prev - entity.Health, "Body")
			end
			lastHealth[entity] = entity.Health
		end
		for record, prev in pairs(lastHealth) do
			local char = record.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if prev > 0 and (not hum or hum.Health <= 0) then
				lastHealth[record] = 0
				Features.onPlayerEliminated(record)
			end
		end
	end))
end

--============================================================================--
-- Misc -- detectors, device spoof, local player spoofer
--============================================================================--
-- Auto-respawn, pickups, queue/vote/ban/loadout, tripmines, removables and the
-- weapon stat modifiers all live in the cosmetics/automation module now; this
-- keeps only what is purely client-side observation.

function Features.Misc(trove)
	local misc = Config.node({ "Misc" })
	local flagged = {}
	Features.flagged = flagged

	--------------------------------------------------------- hacker detector --
	-- Polled at 1 Hz, not per frame: nothing here changes between ticks.
	trove:Add(task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(1) do
			if misc.HackerDetector.Enabled then
				local list, n = Registry.list, Registry.count
				for i = 1, n do
					local record = list[i]
					local hum = record.Humanoid
					local suspicious = hum ~= nil and (hum.WalkSpeed > 40
						or hum.JumpPower > 90 or hum.JumpHeight > 15)
					-- khook.lua:60521 -- the detector is what adds the "Hacker"
					-- player tag the ragebot's PrioritizeHackers reads.
					if suspicious and record.Player and not flagged[record.Player] then
						flagged[record.Player] = true
						Features.hackerTags[record.Player] = true
						if misc.HackerDetector.Notify then
							Notify("Suspicious: " .. record.Player.Name, 4, misc.HackerDetector.Color)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	------------------------------------------------------------ mod detector --
	local function checkMod(player)
		if not misc.ModDetector.Enabled then return end
		local lower = string.lower(player.Name)
		for name in pairs(misc.ModDetector.Moderators) do
			if lower == string.lower(name) then
				Notify("Moderator joined: " .. player.Name, 6, misc.ModDetector.Color)
				return
			end
		end
	end
	trove:Add(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(checkMod))
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do checkMod(player) end

	------------------------------------------------------------ device spoof --
	local spoofApplied = false
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Misc", "DeviceSpoof", "Enabled" }, function(on)
		if not on or spoofApplied or not hookmetamethod then return end
		spoofApplied = true
		local ok = pcall(function()
			local VRService = cloneref(game:GetService("VRService"))
			local inputValues = {
				VR = { KeyboardEnabled = false, MouseEnabled = false, TouchEnabled = false, GamepadEnabled = false },
				Console = { KeyboardEnabled = false, MouseEnabled = false, TouchEnabled = false, GamepadEnabled = true },
				Mobile = { KeyboardEnabled = false, MouseEnabled = false, TouchEnabled = true, GamepadEnabled = false },
				Computer = { KeyboardEnabled = true, MouseEnabled = true, TouchEnabled = false, GamepadEnabled = false },
			}
			local old
			old = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", function(self, key)
				if misc.DeviceSpoof.Enabled then
					local kind = misc.DeviceSpoof.SpoofType
					if self == VRService and key == "VREnabled" then return kind == "VR" end
					if self == UserInputService then
						local values = inputValues[kind]
						if values and values[key] ~= nil then return values[key] end
					end
				end
				return old(self, key)
			end)
			trove:Add(function()
				if restorefunction then pcall(restorefunction, old) end
			end)
		end)
		Features.status.devicespoof = ok and "ok" or "unavailable: hook failed"
	end))

	------------------------------------------------------- player spoofer ----
	-- khook uses three independent sinks: Player property reads, replicated
	-- attributes, and profile data. All values remain client-side.
	local function spoofFor(player)
		return player == LocalPlayer and misc.PlayerSpoofer.LocalPlayer
			or misc.PlayerSpoofer.OtherPlayers
	end

	if hookmetamethod then
		local oldIndex
		oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", function(self, key)
			if typeof(self) == "Instance" and self:IsA("Player") then
				local scope = spoofFor(self)
				if key == "Name" and scope.Name.Enabled then return tostring(scope.Name.Value) end
				if key == "DisplayName" and scope.DisplayName.Enabled then
					return tostring(scope.DisplayName.Value)
				end
				if key == "UserId" and scope.Avatar.Enabled then
					local id = tonumber(scope.Avatar.Value)
					if id ~= nil then return id end
				end
			end
			return oldIndex(self, key)
		end)
		trove:Add(function()
			if restorefunction then pcall(restorefunction, oldIndex) end
		end)
	end

	-- Exact attribute names used by khook's AttributeSink.
	local ATTRIBUTE_FIELDS = {
		Winstreak = "StatisticDuelsWinStreak", Level = "Level",
		RankedElo = "DisplayELO", NametagStatus = "PlayerStatus",
	}
	local CONST_ATTRIBUTES = {
		Influencer = { "IsInfluencer", true },
		RobloxEmployee = { "IsRobloxEmployee", true },
		NosniyTeam = { "GroupRank", 255 },
	}
	local attributeOriginals = {}
	local function rememberAttribute(player, attribute)
		local byPlayer = attributeOriginals[player]
		if byPlayer == nil then byPlayer = {} attributeOriginals[player] = byPlayer end
		if byPlayer[attribute] == nil then
			byPlayer[attribute] = { value = player:GetAttribute(attribute) }
		end
	end
	local function restoreAttribute(player, attribute)
		local byPlayer = attributeOriginals[player]
		local saved = byPlayer and byPlayer[attribute]
		if saved == nil then return end
		pcall(player.SetAttribute, player, attribute, saved.value)
		byPlayer[attribute] = nil
	end
	local function refreshSpoofAttributes()
		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local scope = spoofFor(player)
			for key, attribute in pairs(ATTRIBUTE_FIELDS) do
				local node = scope[key]
				if node.Enabled then
					rememberAttribute(player, attribute)
					pcall(player.SetAttribute, player, attribute, node.Value)
				else
					restoreAttribute(player, attribute)
				end
			end
			for key, spec in pairs(CONST_ATTRIBUTES) do
				local attribute, value = spec[1], spec[2]
				if scope[key].Enabled then
					rememberAttribute(player, attribute)
					pcall(player.SetAttribute, player, attribute, value)
				else
					restoreAttribute(player, attribute)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local spoofClock = 0
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		spoofClock += dt
		if spoofClock >= 0.25 then spoofClock = 0 refreshSpoofAttributes() end
	end))
	trove:Add(function()
		for player, attributes in pairs(attributeOriginals) do
			if player.Parent then
				for attribute, saved in pairs(attributes) do
					pcall(player.SetAttribute, player, attribute, saved.value)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	-- Local profile values use the same CurrentData proxy as the cosmetic
	-- system. Percent controls are converted from menu percent to 0..1.
	local PROFILE_FIELDS = {
		Level = { "Level", 1 }, CasualWins = { "CasualWins", 1 },
		RankedWins = { "RankedWins", 1 }, CasualWinPercent = { "CasualWinPercent", 0.01 },
		RankedWinPercent = { "RankedWinPercent", 0.01 }, RankedElo = { "RankedCurrentELO", 1 },
		LeaderboardRank = { "LeaderboardRank", 1 }, FavoriteMap = { "FavoriteMap", 1 },
	}
	for key, spec in pairs(PROFILE_FIELDS) do
		local field, scale = spec[1], spec[2]
		Rivals.DataHook.set(field, function(original)
			local node = misc.PlayerSpoofer.LocalPlayer[key]
			if not node.Enabled then return original end
			return scale == 1 and node.Value or (tonumber(node.Value) or 0) * scale
		end)
		trove:Add(function() Rivals.DataHook.unset(field) end)
	end

	Features.status.misc = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--

function Features.Recorder(trove)
	-- khook.lua:5830-6289 / 133141-133662. Record actions and sparse position
	-- samples, then replay through PlayerModule's movement controller. The old
	-- clone stored a root CFrame every Heartbeat and teleported through them;
	-- that was neither a movement recording nor compatible with Rivals physics.
	local recordingData, recording, replaying = nil, false, false
	local recordStarted, lastPositionSample = 0, 0
	local lastMove, lastLook = Vector3.zero, nil
	local lastEquipped, lastCrouching, lastSliding = nil, false, false
	local replayState, replayMove = nil, Vector3.zero
	local methodRestores = {}
	local currentMap = tostring(game.PlaceId)
	local selectedMap = currentMap

	local function getMechanics()
		return Resolver.get("MechanicsController")
	end

	local function getControls()
		local ok, playerModule = pcall(function()
			return require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts:WaitForChild("PlayerModule"))
		end)
		if not ok or type(playerModule) ~= "table" then return nil, nil end
		local ok2, controls = pcall(playerModule.GetControls, playerModule)
		if not ok2 or type(controls) ~= "table" then return nil, nil end
		return controls, rawget(controls, "activeController")
	end

	local function readMoveVector()
		local controls = getControls()
		if controls then
			local ok, value = pcall(controls.GetMoveVector, controls)
			if ok and typeof(value) == "Vector3" then return value end
		end
		return Vector3.zero
	end

	local function equippedIndex()
		local item = Rivals.equippedItem()
		if item == nil then return nil end
		local index = Rivals.field(item, "index") or Rivals.field(item, "Index")
		if type(index) == "number" then return index end
		for key, candidate in pairs(Rivals.items() or {}) do
			if candidate == item and type(key) == "number" then return key end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function cameraRotation()
		local controller = Rivals.cameraController()
		local rotation = controller and rawget(controller, "Rotation")
		if typeof(rotation) == "Vector2" then return rotation end
		local x, y = Camera.CFrame:ToOrientation()
		return Vector2.new(y, x)
	end

	local function setCameraRotation(rotation)
		local controller = Rivals.cameraController()
		if controller and typeof(rotation) == "Vector2" then
			pcall(function() controller.Rotation = rotation end)
		end
	end

	local function mapAnchor()
		-- CurrentMap:GetAnchor is an identity CFrame in the lobby and a stable
