		-- map pivot in a round. Rivals' live controller does not expose that
		-- wrapper publicly, so retain the same relative model with the current
		-- environment model's pivot when it can be resolved.
		local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
		local environmentID
		if fighter then pcall(function() environmentID = fighter:Get("EnvironmentID") end) end
		if environmentID ~= nil then
			for _, child in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Model") and (child:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID") == environmentID
					or child.Name == tostring(environmentID)) then
					local ok, pivot = pcall(child.GetPivot, child)
					if ok then return pivot end
				end
		end
		end
		return CFrame.identity
	end

	local function inActiveRound()
		local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
		if fighter == nil or not localRoot() then return false end
		local alive = Rivals.isAlive(fighter)
		if alive == false then return false end
		local environmentID
		pcall(function() environmentID = fighter:Get("EnvironmentID") end)
		return environmentID ~= nil
	end

	local function offset()
		return math.max(0, os.clock() - recordStarted)
	end

	local function recordAction(action)
		if recording and recordingData then
			table.insert(recordingData.keypoints, { offset = offset(), action = action })
		end
	end

	local function hookRecordMethod(object, name, actionBuilder)
		if object == nil or type(hookfunction) ~= "function" then return end
		local target = Rivals.field(object, name)
		if type(target) ~= "function" then return end
		local old
		local ok = pcall(function()
			old = hookfunction(target, function(...)
				local args = { ... }
				if recording then
					local built = actionBuilder(args)
					if built then recordAction(built) end
				end
				return old(...)
			end)
		end)
		if ok then
			table.insert(methodRestores, function()
				if type(restorefunction) == "function" then pcall(restorefunction, target) end
			end)
		end
	end

	local function installActionHooks()
		local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
		hookRecordMethod(fighter, "Input", function(args)
			return { kind = "ItemInput", inputType = args[2] }
		end)
		hookRecordMethod(fighter, "QuickAttack", function(args)
			return { kind = "QuickAttack", attackType = args[2] }
		end)
	end

	local function clearActionHooks()
		for i = #methodRestores, 1, -1 do pcall(methodRestores[i]) end
		table.clear(methodRestores)
	end

	local function installMoveHook()
		local _, controller = getControls()
		if controller == nil or type(hookfunction) ~= "function" then return nil end
		local target = controller.GetMoveVector
		if type(target) ~= "function" then return nil end
		local old
		local ok = pcall(function()
			old = hookfunction(target, function(self, ...)
				if replaying then return replayMove end
				return old(self, ...)
			end)
		end)
		if not ok then return nil end
		return function()
			replayMove = Vector3.zero
			if type(restorefunction) == "function" then pcall(restorefunction, target) end
		end
	end

	local function packValue(value)
		local kind = typeof(value)
		if kind == "Vector2" then return { __t = "V2", value.X, value.Y } end
		if kind == "Vector3" then return { __t = "V3", value.X, value.Y, value.Z } end
		if kind == "CFrame" then return { __t = "CF", value:GetComponents() } end
		if kind == "table" then
			local out = {}
			for k, v in pairs(value) do out[k] = packValue(v) end
			return out
		end
		return value
	end

	local function unpackValue(value)
		if type(value) ~= "table" then return value end
		if value.__t == "V2" then return Vector2.new(value[1], value[2]) end
		if value.__t == "V3" then return Vector3.new(value[1], value[2], value[3]) end
		if value.__t == "CF" then return CFrame.new(table.unpack(value, 1, 12)) end
		local out = {}
		for k, v in pairs(value) do out[k] = unpackValue(v) end
		return out
	end
	local function recordingsFolder(map)
		return ROOT_FOLDER .. "/recordings/" .. tostring(map or currentMap)
	end
	local function ensureRecordingFolder(map)
		if not (isfolder and makefolder) then return false end
		if not isfolder(ROOT_FOLDER) then makefolder(ROOT_FOLDER) end
		local root = ROOT_FOLDER .. "/recordings"
		if not isfolder(root) then makefolder(root) end
		local folder = recordingsFolder(map)
		if not isfolder(folder) then makefolder(folder) end
		return true
	end
	local function safeName(name)
		name = tostring(name or ""):gsub("[^%w%-%_ ]", ""):sub(1, 64)
		return name ~= "" and name or nil
	end

	Features.RecorderSave = function(name)
		name = safeName(name)
		if not name then Notify("Enter a recording name", 2) return false end
		if not (writefile and ensureRecordingFolder(currentMap)) then Notify("Recording filesystem unavailable", 3) return false end
		if recordingData == nil then Notify("No recording to save", 2) return false end
		local packed = { Version = 2, Map = currentMap, Recording = packValue(recordingData) }
		local ok, encoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, packed)
		if not ok then Notify("Failed to encode recording", 3) return false end
		local wrote = pcall(writefile, recordingsFolder(currentMap) .. "/" .. name .. ".json", encoded)
		if wrote then Notify("Saved recording " .. name, 2) end
		return wrote
	end
	Features.RecorderMaps = function()
		local out = {}
		if not (listfiles and isfolder and isfolder(ROOT_FOLDER .. "/recordings")) then return out end
		for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(ROOT_FOLDER .. "/recordings")) do
			if isfolder(path) then
				local name = path:match("([^/\\]+)$")
				if name then out[#out + 1] = name end
			end
		end
		table.sort(out)
		return out
	end
	Features.RecorderNames = function(map)
		local out, folder = {}, recordingsFolder(map)
		if not (listfiles and isfolder and isfolder(folder)) then return out end
		for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(folder)) do
			local name = path:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
			if name then out[#out + 1] = name end
		end
		table.sort(out)
		return out
	end
	Features.RecorderLoad = function(map, name)
		name = safeName(name)
		if not (name and readfile and isfile) then return false end
		local path = recordingsFolder(map) .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
		if not isfile(path) then return false end
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path)) end)
		if not ok or type(decoded) ~= "table" then return false end
		if type(decoded.Recording) == "table" then
			recordingData = unpackValue(decoded.Recording)
		elseif type(decoded.Frames) == "table" and #decoded.Frames > 0 then
			-- One-time compatibility with the clone's obsolete teleport format.
			local first = decoded.Frames[1]
			if type(first.c) ~= "table" or #first.c ~= 12 then return false end
			local startCF = CFrame.new(table.unpack(first.c))
			recordingData = {
				mapName = tostring(map), displayPosition = startCF.Position,
				startCFrame = startCF, loadoutRequirements = {}, startEquippedIndex = nil,
				keypoints = { { offset = 0, action = { kind = "Look", rotation = Vector2.zero } } },
				positions = {},
			}
			for _, frame in ipairs(decoded.Frames) do
				if type(frame.c) == "table" and #frame.c == 12 then
					local cf = CFrame.new(table.unpack(frame.c))
					table.insert(recordingData.positions, { offset = tonumber(frame.t) or 0, position = cf.Position })
				end
			end
		else return false end
		selectedMap = tostring(map or currentMap)
		return type(recordingData.keypoints) == "table" and #recordingData.keypoints > 0
	end
	Features.RecorderDelete = function(name)
		name = safeName(name)
		if not (name and delfile and isfile) then return false end
		local path = recordingsFolder(selectedMap) .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
		if isfile(path) then delfile(path) return true end
		return false
	end
	Features.RecorderDebug = function()
		return {
			recording = recording, replaying = replaying,
			activeRound = inActiveRound(), hasRoot = localRoot() ~= nil,
			hasRecording = recordingData ~= nil,
		}
	end
	trove:Add(function()
		Features.RecorderSave, Features.RecorderMaps, Features.RecorderNames = nil, nil, nil
		Features.RecorderLoad, Features.RecorderDelete, Features.RecorderDebug = nil, nil, nil
	end)

	local replayRestore
	local function finishReplay(reason)
		if not replaying then return end
		replaying = false
		if replayRestore then pcall(replayRestore) replayRestore = nil end
		local mechanics = getMechanics()
		if mechanics then pcall(mechanics.SetCrouching, mechanics, false) end
		local bind = Features.binds and Features.binds.Replay
		if bind then bind.State = false KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire() end
		local cfg = Config.get({ "MovementRecorder" })
		if cfg and not cfg.HideNotifications then
			Notify(reason == "Diverged" and "Replay stopped (off course)" or "Finished replaying", 2)
		end
		replayState = nil
	end

	local function cameraRelative(worldDirection)
		local look = Vector3.new(Camera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, Camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
		if look.Magnitude < 0.0001 then return Vector3.zero end
		local right = Vector3.new(Camera.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, Camera.CFrame.RightVector.Z).Unit
		return Vector3.new(worldDirection:Dot(right), 0, -worldDirection:Dot(look.Unit))
	end

	local function approachRotation(current, target, dt, cfg)
		local delta = Vector2.new(target.X - current.X,
			(target.Y - current.Y + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi)
		if delta.Magnitude <= math.rad(0.1) then return target, true end
		local smooth = clamp((cfg.LookSmoothing or 35) / 100 * 0.3, 0, 1)
		local eased = 1 - math.pow(1 - smooth, dt * 60)
		local step = math.min(delta.Magnitude * eased, math.rad(cfg.LookAlignSpeed or 180) * dt)
		return current + delta.Unit * step, false
	end

	local function replayAction(action)
		local fighter, mechanics = Rivals.localFighter(), getMechanics()
		if action.kind == "Move" and typeof(action.direction) == "Vector3" then
			replayMove = action.direction
		elseif action.kind == "Look" and typeof(action.rotation) == "Vector2" then
			replayState.look = action.rotation
		elseif action.kind == "Equip" and fighter then
			task.spawn(function() pcall(fighter.EquipItem, fighter, action.index) end)
		elseif action.kind == "ItemInput" and fighter then
			task.spawn(function() pcall(fighter.Input, fighter, action.inputType) end)
		elseif action.kind == "QuickAttack" and fighter then
			task.spawn(function() pcall(fighter.QuickAttack, fighter, action.attackType) end)
		elseif action.kind == "Jump" and mechanics then
			local sliding = false pcall(function() sliding = mechanics.IsSliding == true end)
			if sliding then pcall(mechanics.HighJump, mechanics) else
				pcall(mechanics.DoubleJumpRequest, mechanics)
				pcall(mechanics.JumpRequest, mechanics)
			end
		elseif action.kind == "Crouch" and mechanics then
			pcall(mechanics.SetCrouching, mechanics, action.crouching)
		elseif action.kind == "Slide" and mechanics then
			task.spawn(function() pcall(mechanics.Slide, mechanics) end)
		end
	end

	local function startRecording(root, cfg)
		if not inActiveRound() then
			if not cfg.HideNotifications then Notify("You need to be alive and in a match", 2) end
			return false
		end
		local anchor = mapAnchor()
		recording, replaying = true, false
		recordStarted, lastPositionSample = os.clock(), 0
		lastMove, lastLook = Vector3.zero, cameraRotation()
		lastEquipped, lastCrouching, lastSliding = equippedIndex(), false, false
		recordingData = {
			mapName = currentMap, displayPosition = anchor:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position),
			startCFrame = anchor:ToObjectSpace(root.CFrame),
			keypoints = { { offset = 0, action = { kind = "Look", rotation = lastLook } } },
			loadoutRequirements = {}, startEquippedIndex = lastEquipped,
			startEquipCooldown = nil, positions = {},
		}
		clearActionHooks() installActionHooks()
		if not cfg.HideNotifications then Notify("Now recording", 2) end
		return true
	end

	local function stopRecording(cfg)
		if not recording then return end
		recording = false
		clearActionHooks()
		if not cfg.HideNotifications then Notify("Recording stopped", 2) end
	end

	local function sampleRecording(root)
		local move = readMoveVector()
		if move ~= lastMove then lastMove = move recordAction({ kind = "Move", direction = move }) end
		local look = cameraRotation()
		if lastLook == nil or (look - lastLook).Magnitude > 0.0001 then
			lastLook = look recordAction({ kind = "Look", rotation = look })
		end
		local equipped = equippedIndex()
		if equipped ~= lastEquipped then
			lastEquipped = equipped
			if equipped ~= nil then recordAction({ kind = "Equip", index = equipped }) end
		end
		local mechanics = getMechanics()
		local crouching, sliding = false, false
		if mechanics then
			pcall(function() crouching = mechanics.IsCrouching == true end)
			pcall(function() sliding = mechanics.IsSliding == true end)
		end
		if crouching ~= lastCrouching then
			lastCrouching = crouching recordAction({ kind = "Crouch", crouching = crouching })
		end
		if sliding ~= lastSliding then
			lastSliding = sliding
			if sliding then recordAction({ kind = "Slide" }) end
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - lastPositionSample >= 0.1 then
			lastPositionSample = now
			local anchor = mapAnchor()
			table.insert(recordingData.positions, {
				offset = offset(), position = anchor:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position),
			})
		end
	end

	local function startReplay(root, cfg)
		if not inActiveRound() then
			if not cfg.HideNotifications then Notify("You need to be alive and in a match", 2) end
			return false
		end
		if not recordingData or type(recordingData.keypoints) ~= "table" or #recordingData.keypoints == 0 then
			if not cfg.HideNotifications then Notify("No recording selected", 2) end
			return false
		end
		stopRecording(cfg)
		replaying = true
		replayRestore = installMoveHook()
		local firstLook
		for _, point in ipairs(recordingData.keypoints) do
			if point.action and point.action.kind == "Look" then firstLook = point.action.rotation break end
		end
		replayState = {
			kind = "Aligning", elapsed = 0, stuck = 0, best = math.huge,
			index = 1, offset = 0, positionIndex = 1, look = firstLook,
		}
		if recordingData.startEquippedIndex ~= nil then
			local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
			if fighter then task.spawn(function() pcall(fighter.EquipItem, fighter, recordingData.startEquippedIndex) end) end
		end
		if not cfg.HideNotifications then Notify("Now replaying", 2) end
		return true
	end

	local function updateReplay(root, dt, cfg)
		local state, anchor = replayState, mapAnchor()
		if not state then return end
		if state.kind == "Aligning" then
			state.elapsed += dt
			local target = (anchor * recordingData.startCFrame).Position
			local delta3 = target - root.Position
			local planar = Vector3.new(delta3.X, 0, delta3.Z)
			local distance = planar.Magnitude
			if distance <= (cfg.AlignSnapDistance or 0.2) then replayMove = Vector3.zero
			else replayMove = cameraRelative(planar.Unit) end
			if distance < state.best - 0.1 then state.best, state.stuck = distance, 0
			else state.stuck += dt end
			local lookDone = true
			if typeof(state.look) == "Vector2" then
				local nextLook
				nextLook, lookDone = approachRotation(cameraRotation(), state.look, dt, cfg)
				setCameraRotation(nextLook)
			end
			if (distance <= (cfg.AlignSnapDistance or 0.2) and lookDone) or state.stuck >= 1 then
				replayMove = Vector3.zero state.kind = "Playing" state.offset = 0
			end
			return
		end
		state.offset += dt
		local positions = recordingData.positions or {}
		while state.positionIndex < #positions and positions[state.positionIndex + 1].offset <= state.offset do
			state.positionIndex += 1
		end
		local a, b = positions[state.positionIndex], positions[state.positionIndex + 1]
		if a then
			local expected = a.position
			if b then
				local span = b.offset - a.offset
				local alpha = span > 0 and clamp((state.offset - a.offset) / span, 0, 1) or 0
				expected = a.position:Lerp(b.position, alpha)
			end
			if (anchor:PointToWorldSpace(expected) - root.Position).Magnitude > 15 then finishReplay("Diverged") return end
		end
		local keypoints = recordingData.keypoints
		while state.index <= #keypoints and state.offset >= (keypoints[state.index].offset or 0) do
			replayAction(keypoints[state.index].action or {}) state.index += 1
		end
		if typeof(state.look) == "Vector2" then
			local nextLook = approachRotation(cameraRotation(), state.look, dt, cfg)
			setCameraRotation(nextLook)
		end
		if state.index > #keypoints then finishReplay("Completed") end
	end

	trove:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
		if gpe or not recording then return end
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
			recordAction({ kind = "Jump" })
		end
	end))

	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		local cfg = Config.get({ "MovementRecorder" })
		if not cfg or not cfg.Enabled then
			if recording then stopRecording(cfg or {}) end
			if replaying then finishReplay("Stopped") end
			return
		end

		local recBind = Features.binds and Features.binds.Record
		local repBind = Features.binds and Features.binds.Replay
		local root = localRoot()
		if not root then return end

		local wantRecord = recBind and recBind:IsActive()
		if wantRecord and not recording and not replaying then
			if not startRecording(root, cfg) then recBind.State = false KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire() end
		elseif not wantRecord and recording then stopRecording(cfg) end
		if recording then sampleRecording(root) end

		local wantReplay = repBind and repBind:IsActive()
		if wantReplay and not replaying then
			if not startReplay(root, cfg) then repBind.State = false KeybindRegistry.changed:Fire() end
		elseif not wantReplay and replaying then finishReplay("Stopped") end
		if replaying then updateReplay(root, dt, cfg) end
	end))
	trove:Add(function()
		recording = false clearActionHooks()
		if replayRestore then pcall(replayRestore) replayRestore = nil end
	end)
	Features.status.recorder = "ok (input/keypoint replay)"
end
--============================================================================--
-- COSMETICS -- catalog, layered selections, skin changer, unlocker
--============================================================================--
-- khook models this as an ordered stack of selection layers over the game's
-- own item table (khook.lua:46172-46211): the Unlocker layer makes everything
-- appear owned, the SkinChanger layer overrides what is equipped, and
-- GetLayerSelections resolves the pair for a given weapon. That structure is
-- reproduced here; what it writes into is discovered at runtime.

local Cosmetics = {}
Cosmetics.Trove = Trove.new("cosmetics")
Cosmetics.changed = Signal.new()
Cosmetics.catalogChanged = Signal.new()


Cosmetics.FIELD_INVENTORY = "CosmeticInventory"
Cosmetics.FIELD_WEAPONS = "WeaponInventory"

Cosmetics.catalog = {
	Weapons   = {},          -- ordered weapon names
	Skins     = {},          -- weapon -> { skinName, ... }
	Wraps     = {},          -- { wrapName, ... }
	Charms    = {},
	Finishers = {},
	Rarities  = {},
	Types     = {},
	resolved  = false,
	source    = "none",
}

local UNSET = "Unselected"

------------------------------------------------------------------ catalog --

--- Pull the item catalogue out of the running game. Three strategies, most
--- specific first; whichever lands sets `source` so the UI can say where the
--- data came from instead of silently showing an empty grid.
--- Rivals keeps every cosmetic in ReplicatedStorage.Modules.CosmeticLibrary
--- (1,249 entries, each { Type, Rarity, Image, Description, ItemName? }) and
--- the weapon list in ItemLibrary.Items. Both are plain ModuleScripts, so the
--- catalogue is a require, not a search.
local function buildCatalogFromLibraries()
	local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	if not modules then return false end
	local cosModule = modules:FindFirstChild("CosmeticLibrary")
	local itemModule = modules:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary")
	if not (cosModule and itemModule) then return false end

	local okC, Cos = pcall(require, cosModule)
	local okI, Items = pcall(require, itemModule)
	if not (okC and okI and typeof(Cos) == "table" and typeof(Items) == "table") then
		return false
	end
	if typeof(Cos.Cosmetics) ~= "table" or typeof(Items.Items) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end

	local cat = Cosmetics.catalog
	table.clear(cat.Weapons)
	table.clear(cat.Skins)

	-- weapons, ordered the way the game orders them
	for name, data in pairs(Items.Items) do
		if typeof(name) == "string" and data.Class ~= "Utility" then
			table.insert(cat.Weapons, name)
		end
	end
	table.sort(cat.Weapons)
	table.insert(cat.Weapons, 1, "All")

	-- cosmetics bucketed by Type; ItemName ties a skin to its weapon
	local wraps, charms, finishers, emotes = {}, {}, {}, {}
	for name, data in pairs(Cos.Cosmetics) do
		if typeof(name) == "string" and typeof(data) == "table" then
			local entry = {
				name = name,
				image = data.Image or (typeof(Cos.Images) == "table" and Cos.Images[name] or nil),
				rarity = data.Rarity,
			}
			local kind = data.Type
			if kind == "Skin" then
				local weapon = data.ItemName
				if weapon then
					local list = cat.Skins[weapon]
					if not list then list = {} cat.Skins[weapon] = list end
					table.insert(list, entry)
				end
			elseif kind == "Wrap" then table.insert(wraps, entry)
			elseif kind == "Charm" then table.insert(charms, entry)
			elseif kind == "Finisher" then table.insert(finishers, entry)
			elseif kind == "Emote" then table.insert(emotes, entry)
			end
		end
	end
	local function byName(a, b) return a.name < b.name end
	for _, list in pairs(cat.Skins) do table.sort(list, byName) end
	table.sort(wraps, byName); table.sort(charms, byName)
	table.sort(finishers, byName); table.sort(emotes, byName)

	cat.Wraps, cat.Charms, cat.Finishers, cat.Emotes = wraps, charms, finishers, emotes
	cat.Types = {}
	for k, info in pairs(typeof(Cos.Types) == "table" and Cos.Types or {}) do
		if typeof(info) ~= "table" or info.NotCosmetic ~= true then table.insert(cat.Types, k) end
	end
	table.sort(cat.Types)
	cat.Rarities = {}
	for k in pairs(typeof(Cos.Rarities) == "table" and Cos.Rarities or {}) do table.insert(cat.Rarities, k) end
	table.sort(cat.Rarities)

	-- khook.lua:92909 `f6522` -- the rank table is built from the CURRENT
	-- season's profile, walking `RanksOrder` and reading each rank out of
	-- `Ranks`. `requiresLeaderboard` is simply whether the rank carries a
	-- `RequiredELOLeaderboardRanking` (khook.lua:92925), which is what makes
	-- Nemesis/Archnemesis need a placement rather than an ELO.
	pcall(function()
		local season = require(modules.SeasonLibrary)
		local profile = season.CurrentSeason.RankProfile
		local byName, order = {}, {}
		for _, rankName in ipairs(profile.RanksOrder) do
			local rank = profile.Ranks[rankName]
			byName[rankName] = {
				name = rankName,
				requiredELO = rank.RequiredELO,
				requiresLeaderboard = rank.RequiredELOLeaderboardRanking ~= nil,
			}
			table.insert(order, rankName)
		end
		Cosmetics.Rank.RANKS = byName
		Cosmetics.Rank.RANK_NAMES = order

		-- khook.lua:92934 `f6524` -- one season charm per season version.
		local charms, names = {}, {}
		local byVersion = season.SeasonsByVersion or {}
		local versions = {}
		for version in pairs(byVersion) do table.insert(versions, version) end
		table.sort(versions)
		for _, version in ipairs(versions) do
			local charmName = "Season " .. tostring(version)
			table.insert(charms, { charmName = charmName,
				seasonName = byVersion[version].Name, version = version })
			table.insert(names, charmName)
		end
		Cosmetics.Rank.SEASON_CHARMS = charms
		Cosmetics.Rank.SEASON_CHARM_NAMES = names
	end)

	Cosmetics.library = Cos
	-- khook.lua:142817 `_BuildAllItemNamesSet` needs the item list: a wrap,
	-- charm or finisher is marked owned FOR EVERY ITEM, not merely owned.
	Cosmetics.itemLibrary = Items
	cat.resolved = true
	cat.source = "CosmeticLibrary"
	Cosmetics.catalogChanged:Fire()
	return true
end

local function buildCatalog()
	if buildCatalogFromLibraries() then return true end

	local cat = Cosmetics.catalog
	local resolved = Resolver.get("ItemCatalog")

	local function ingest(tbl, source)
		if typeof(tbl) ~= "table" then return false end
		local skins = rawget(tbl, "Skins")
		if typeof(skins) ~= "table" then return false end

		table.clear(cat.Weapons)
		table.clear(cat.Skins)
		for weapon, variants in pairs(skins) do
			if typeof(weapon) == "string" then
				table.insert(cat.Weapons, weapon)
				local list = {}
				if typeof(variants) == "table" then
					for k, v in pairs(variants) do
						table.insert(list, typeof(k) == "string" and k or tostring(v))
					end
					table.sort(list)
				end
				cat.Skins[weapon] = list
			end
		end
		table.sort(cat.Weapons)
		table.insert(cat.Weapons, 1, "All")

		local function flatten(name)
			local out = {}
			local src = rawget(tbl, name)
			if typeof(src) == "table" then
				for k, v in pairs(src) do
					table.insert(out, typeof(k) == "string" and k or tostring(v))
				end
				table.sort(out)
			end
			return out
		end
		cat.Wraps = flatten("Wraps")
		cat.Charms = flatten("Charms")
		cat.Finishers = flatten("Finishers")
		cat.Rarities = flatten("Rarities")
		cat.Types = { "Skin", "Wrap", "Charm", "Finisher" }
		cat.resolved = true
		cat.source = source
		return true
	end

	if resolved and ingest(resolved, "gc-resolver") then
		Cosmetics.catalogChanged:Fire()
		return true
	end

	-- Fallback: look for a module in ReplicatedStorage whose name suggests an
	-- item/cosmetic table and require it read-only.
	for _, inst in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
		if inst:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			local lower = string.lower(inst.Name)
			if string.find(lower, "cosmetic") or string.find(lower, "skin")
				or string.find(lower, "item") then
				local ok, module = pcall(require, inst)
				if ok and ingest(module, "module:" .. inst.Name) then
					Cosmetics.catalogChanged:Fire()
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end

	cat.resolved = false
	cat.source = "unresolved"
	Cosmetics.catalogChanged:Fire()
	return false
end

function Cosmetics.RefreshCatalog()
	local ok, result = pcall(buildCatalog)
	return ok and result or false
end

function Cosmetics.GetWeapons()
	return Cosmetics.catalog.Weapons
end

function Cosmetics.GetSkins(weapon)
	return Cosmetics.catalog.Skins[weapon] or {}
end

--============================================================================--
-- COSMETICS STACK (khook.lua:35082 / 45871 / 77946 / 90727 / 106657 / 112271
--                  / 127982 / 142815)
--============================================================================--
-- khook does not have "a skin changer"; it has a six-layer model, and every
-- feature in the Cosmetics tab is a thin call into it. Ported here in khook's
-- own order, bottom up:
--
--   Cosmetics.Selection  cosmetic-type <-> slot-key helpers   (khook.lua:90727)
--   Cosmetics.Layers     priority-keyed layer helpers         (khook.lua:112271)
--   Cosmetics.Catalog    the filtered item catalogue          (khook.lua:142815)
--   Cosmetics.Intent     layered selection store              (khook.lua:77946)
--   Cosmetics.Resolver   intent -> concrete cosmetic set      (khook.lua:106657)
--   Cosmetics.Items      the orchestrator over all four       (khook.lua:35100)
--   the controller       the public surface                   (khook.lua:45925)
--
-- The DataMasker layer (khook.lua:43770) maps onto `Rivals.DataHook`: khook's
-- `_itemDataHook:Load()` installs exactly the spoof-and-merge closure at
-- khook.lua:17650 / 19584, which is what that hook now does.
--
-- NOT ported, and stubbed with the interfaces the ported code calls: the
-- favorited intent/resolver, `onlyUseFavoritesState`, the rank charm, the emote
-- intent/resolver, the user-server bridge, and snapshot export/import. Each has
-- its own sub-stack in khook and several destroyed bodies; the stubs answer
-- "nothing favorited, favorites-only off", which is khook's behaviour with an
-- empty favorites set, so every ported layer runs exactly as written.

--- khook.lua:127982
Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC = "NONE_COSMETIC"
Cosmetics.RANDOM_COSMETIC = "RANDOM_COSMETIC"

--- khook.lua:106055 -- the layer keys ARE the priorities: `resolveHighestPriority`
--- compares them with `<` directly (khook.lua:112299).
Cosmetics.PRIORITY = { Unlocker = 0, SkinChanger = 100 }

--- khook.lua:90727 `cosmeticTypeToSlotKey` and friends. `t34` is this map.
Cosmetics.Selection = { SLOT_KEY = { Skin = "skin", Wrap = "wrap", Charm = "charm", Finisher = "finisher" } }

--- khook.lua:143005 `v36.item` -- the weapon-cosmetic types, in khook's order.
Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES = { "Skin", "Wrap", "Charm", "Finisher" }

function Cosmetics.Selection.cloneWrapSelection(wrap)
	if wrap == nil then return nil end
	return { name = wrap.name, inverted = wrap.inverted == true }
end

--- khook.lua:90731
function Cosmetics.Selection.cloneCosmeticsSelection(sel)
	return {
		skin = sel.skin,
		wrap = Cosmetics.Selection.cloneWrapSelection(sel.wrap),
		charm = sel.charm,
		finisher = sel.finisher,
	}
end

--- khook.lua:90741
function Cosmetics.Selection.getChoiceType(choice) return choice.kind end

--- khook.lua:90745
function Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectionValue(layer, cosmeticType)
	return layer[Cosmetics.Selection.SLOT_KEY[cosmeticType]]
end

--- khook.lua:90749 -- a Wrap value is a table, so it is cloned rather than shared.
function Cosmetics.Selection.setSelectionValue(layer, cosmeticType, value)
	if cosmeticType == "Wrap" then
		layer.wrap = Cosmetics.Selection.cloneWrapSelection(value)
		return
	end
	layer[Cosmetics.Selection.SLOT_KEY[cosmeticType]] = value
end

--- khook.lua:90757
function Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectedCosmeticName(selection, cosmeticType)
	if cosmeticType ~= "Wrap" then
		return Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectionValue(selection, cosmeticType)
	end
	local wrap = selection.wrap
	return wrap ~= nil and wrap.name or nil
end

--- khook.lua:90770. The resolved Wrap keeps the intent's `inverted` flag while
--- taking the resolved name, which is what lets RANDOM_COSMETIC reroll a wrap
--- without losing the inversion the user picked.
function Cosmetics.Selection.applyResolvedSelection(out, effective, cosmeticType, name)
	if cosmeticType == "Wrap" then
		if name == nil then
			out.wrap = nil
		else
			local source = effective.wrap
			out.wrap = { name = name, inverted = source ~= nil and source.inverted == true }
		end
		return
	end
	Cosmetics.Selection.setSelectionValue(out, cosmeticType, name)
end

--- khook.lua:90778
function Cosmetics.Selection.applyChoice(layer, choice)
	local kind = Cosmetics.Selection.getChoiceType(choice)
	if choice.kind == "Wrap" then
		layer.wrap = Cosmetics.Selection.cloneWrapSelection(choice.wrap)
	else
		Cosmetics.Selection.setSelectionValue(layer, kind, choice.name)
	end
	return kind
end

--- khook.lua:90788
function Cosmetics.Selection.buildChoice(cosmeticType, name, inverted)
	if cosmeticType ~= "Wrap" then return { kind = cosmeticType, name = name } end
	return {
		kind = "Wrap",
		wrap = name ~= nil and { name = name, inverted = inverted == true } or nil,
	}
end

function Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty(layer)
	return layer.skin == nil and layer.wrap == nil
		and layer.charm == nil and layer.finisher == nil
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:112271 `getOrCreateLayer` / `removeLayerIfEmpty` / `resolveHighestPriority`
Cosmetics.Layers = {}

function Cosmetics.Layers.getOrCreate(layers, key, factory)
	local layer = layers[key]
	if layer == nil then
		layer = factory()
		layers[key] = layer
	end
	return layer
end

function Cosmetics.Layers.removeIfEmpty(layers, key, isEmpty)
	local layer = layers[key]
	if layer == nil or not isEmpty(layer) then return false end
	layers[key] = nil
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:112289. The layer key is the priority and the highest key that
--- both passes `isActive` and holds a value wins.
function Cosmetics.Layers.resolveHighestPriority(layers, getValue, isActive)
	local bestKey, bestValue = nil, nil
	for key, layer in pairs(layers) do
		if isActive == nil or isActive(key) then
			local value = getValue(layer)
			if value ~= nil and (bestKey == nil or bestKey < key) then
				bestValue, bestKey = value, key
			end
		end
	end
	return bestKey, bestValue
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:142815 `catalog`. Note what the inventory VALUES are: a skin is
--- `true`, while a wrap/charm/finisher is the set of every item name in the
--- game (khook.lua:142817 `_BuildAllItemNamesSet`). That is not decoration --
--- it is the `inventory[name][weaponName]` form the game's own
--- `OwnsCosmeticForWeapon` reads, so a universal cosmetic reads as owned for
--- every weapon rather than only "owned".
Cosmetics.Catalog = {}
Cosmetics.Catalog.__index = Cosmetics.Catalog

function Cosmetics.Catalog.buildAllItemNamesSet()
	local out = {}
	local lib = Cosmetics.library and Cosmetics.itemLibrary or nil
	if lib == nil or typeof(lib.Items) ~= "table" then return out end
	for name in pairs(lib.Items) do out[name] = true end
	return out
end

function Cosmetics.Catalog.new()
	local self = setmetatable({
		_typeSet = {},
		_raritySet = {},
		_inventory = {},
		_typeByName = {},
		_byType = { Skin = {}, Wrap = {}, Charm = {}, Finisher = {}, Emote = {} },
		_lookupByType = { Skin = {}, Wrap = {}, Charm = {}, Finisher = {}, Emote = {} },
		built = Signal.new(),
	}, Cosmetics.Catalog)
	return self
end

--- khook.lua:142862
function Cosmetics.Catalog:Build()
	self._inventory = {}
	self._typeByName = {}
	self._byType = { Skin = {}, Wrap = {}, Charm = {}, Finisher = {}, Emote = {} }
	self._lookupByType = { Skin = {}, Wrap = {}, Charm = {}, Finisher = {}, Emote = {} }

	local lib = Cosmetics.library
	if lib == nil or typeof(lib.Cosmetics) ~= "table" then
		self.built:Fire()
		return
	end
	local allItems = Cosmetics.Catalog.buildAllItemNamesSet()

	for name, data in pairs(lib.Cosmetics) do
		local kind = data.Type
		if self._typeSet[kind] and self._raritySet[data.Rarity] then
			if kind == "Skin" then
				local item = data.ItemName
				if self._byType.Skin[item] == nil then self._byType.Skin[item] = {} end
				if self._lookupByType.Skin[item] == nil then self._lookupByType.Skin[item] = {} end
				table.insert(self._byType.Skin[item], name)
				self._lookupByType.Skin[item][name] = true
				self._typeByName[name] = kind
				self._inventory[name] = true
			elseif kind == "Wrap" or kind == "Charm" or kind == "Finisher" then
				table.insert(self._byType[kind], name)
				self._lookupByType[kind][name] = true
				self._typeByName[name] = kind
				self._inventory[name] = allItems
			elseif kind == "Emote" then
				self._typeByName[name] = kind
				self._inventory[name] = true
			end
		end
	end
	self.built:Fire()
end

function Cosmetics.Catalog:SetIncludedTypes(set) self._typeSet = set end
function Cosmetics.Catalog:GetIncludedTypes() return self._typeSet end
function Cosmetics.Catalog:SetIncludedRarities(set) self._raritySet = set end
function Cosmetics.Catalog:GetIncludedRarities() return self._raritySet end

--- khook.lua:142943. The draw includes one extra slot so "no cosmetic" is a
--- possible roll -- khook offers NONE_COSMETIC exactly as often as any entry.
function Cosmetics.Catalog:GetRandomCosmetic(query)
	local pool
	if query.cosmeticType == "Skin" then
		pool = self._byType.Skin[query.itemName]
	else
		pool = self._byType[query.cosmeticType]
	end
	if pool == nil or #pool == 0 then return nil end
	local noneSlot = #pool + 1
	local roll = math.random(noneSlot)
	if roll == noneSlot then return Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC end
	return pool[roll]
end

--- khook.lua:142961
function Cosmetics.Catalog:IsEmpty(itemName, cosmeticType)
	local pool
	if cosmeticType == "Skin" then
		pool = self._byType.Skin[itemName]
	else
		pool = self._byType[cosmeticType]
	end
	return pool == nil or #pool == 0
end

--- khook.lua:142976
function Cosmetics.Catalog:IsInCatalog(query)
	local name = query.cosmeticName
	if name == nil then return false end
	if string.match(name, "^" .. Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC)
		or name == Cosmetics.RANDOM_COSMETIC then
		return true
	end
	if query.cosmeticType ~= "Skin" then
		return self._lookupByType[query.cosmeticType][name] == true
	end
	local pool = self._lookupByType.Skin[query.itemName]
	return pool ~= nil and pool[name] == true
end

--- khook.lua:142995
function Cosmetics.Catalog:IsCosmeticInCatalog(itemName, cosmeticName)
	if string.match(cosmeticName, "^" .. Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC) then return true end
	for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
		if self:IsInCatalog({ cosmeticType = cosmeticType, itemName = itemName,
			cosmeticName = cosmeticName }) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function Cosmetics.Catalog:IsCosmeticType(name, cosmeticType)
	return self._typeByName[name] == cosmeticType
end

function Cosmetics.Catalog:GetInventory() return self._inventory end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:77946 -- the layered selection store. `_states[itemName].layers`
--- is keyed by priority; a layer is a `{ skin, wrap, charm, finisher }` record.
Cosmetics.Intent = {}
Cosmetics.Intent.__index = Cosmetics.Intent

function Cosmetics.Intent.new()
	return setmetatable({ _activeLayers = {}, _states = {} }, Cosmetics.Intent)
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:_EnsureState(itemName)
	local state = self._states[itemName]
	if state == nil then
		state = { layers = {} }
		self._states[itemName] = state
	end
	return state
end

local function intentStateIsEmpty(state)
	return next(state.layers) == nil
end

--- khook.lua:77976 -- resolved one cosmetic type at a time, so a skin from one
--- layer and a charm from another coexist.
function Cosmetics.Intent:_ResolveEffective(state, exemptLayers)
	local selection, winners = {}, {}
	for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
		local function getValue(layer)
			return Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectionValue(layer, cosmeticType)
		end
		local function isActive(key)
			if exemptLayers ~= nil and exemptLayers[key] then return false end
			return self._activeLayers[key] == true
		end
		local winnerKey, value =
			Cosmetics.Layers.resolveHighestPriority(state.layers, getValue, isActive)
		winners[Cosmetics.Selection.SLOT_KEY[cosmeticType]] = winnerKey
		Cosmetics.Selection.setSelectionValue(selection, cosmeticType, value)
	end
	return { selection = selection, winnerPriorities = winners }
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:IsLayerActive(key)
	return self._activeLayers[key] == true
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:GetLayerSelections(itemName, key)
	local state = self._states[itemName]
	if state == nil then return nil end
	return state.layers[key]
end

--- khook.lua:78025 -- returns the set of items the change touched.
function Cosmetics.Intent:SetLayerActive(key, on)
	self._activeLayers[key] = on and true or nil
	local touched = {}
	for itemName, state in pairs(self._states) do
		if state.layers[key] ~= nil then touched[itemName] = true end
	end
	return touched
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:SetChoice(itemName, choice, key)
	local state = self:_EnsureState(itemName)
	local layer = Cosmetics.Layers.getOrCreate(state.layers, key, function() return {} end)
	Cosmetics.Selection.applyChoice(layer, choice)
	Cosmetics.Layers.removeIfEmpty(state.layers, key, Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty)
	if not intentStateIsEmpty(state) then
		return self:_ResolveEffective(state).selection
	end
	self._states[itemName] = nil
	return {}
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:ClearSelection(itemName, cosmeticType)
	local state = self._states[itemName]
	if state == nil then return nil end
	local changed = false
	for key, layer in pairs(state.layers) do
		if Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectionValue(layer, cosmeticType) ~= nil then
			Cosmetics.Selection.setSelectionValue(layer, cosmeticType, nil)
			changed = true
		end
		Cosmetics.Layers.removeIfEmpty(state.layers, key, Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty)
	end
	if not changed or not intentStateIsEmpty(state) then
		return self:_ResolveEffective(state).selection
	end
	self._states[itemName] = nil
	return nil
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:ClearSelectionAtPriority(itemName, cosmeticType, key)
	local state = self._states[itemName]
	if state == nil then return nil end
	local layer = state.layers[key]
	if layer == nil
		or Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectionValue(layer, cosmeticType) == nil then
		return self:_ResolveEffective(state).selection
	end
	Cosmetics.Selection.setSelectionValue(layer, cosmeticType, nil)
	Cosmetics.Layers.removeIfEmpty(state.layers, key, Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty)
	if not intentStateIsEmpty(state) then
		return self:_ResolveEffective(state).selection
	end
	self._states[itemName] = nil
	return nil
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:GetWinnerPriority(itemName, cosmeticType)
	local state = self._states[itemName]
	if state == nil then return nil end
	return self:_ResolveEffective(state)
		.winnerPriorities[Cosmetics.Selection.SLOT_KEY[cosmeticType]]
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:GetItemNames()
	local names = {}
	for itemName in pairs(self._states) do names[itemName] = true end
	return names
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:GetItemEffective(itemName)
	local state = self._states[itemName]
	if state == nil then return nil end
	local selection = self:_ResolveEffective(state).selection
	if Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty(selection) then return nil end
	return selection
end

function Cosmetics.Intent:GetEffectiveByItem()
	local out = {}
	for itemName in pairs(self._states) do
		out[itemName] = self:GetItemEffective(itemName)
	end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:35229 -- the data masker deliberately skips some layers, which is
--- how the SkinChanger stays a local-visual layer while the Unlocker reaches
--- the data (khook.lua:45884 `catalogExemptLayers`).
function Cosmetics.Intent:GetEffectiveByItemExcludingLayers(exempt)
	local out = {}
	for itemName, state in pairs(self._states) do
		local selection = self:_ResolveEffective(state, exempt).selection
		if not Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty(selection) then out[itemName] = selection end
	end
	return out
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:106657 -- turns the intent into a concrete cosmetic set, caching
--- per item and holding a random draw steady until it is invalidated.
Cosmetics.Resolver = {}
Cosmetics.Resolver.__index = Cosmetics.Resolver

function Cosmetics.Resolver.new(intent, catalog)
	return setmetatable({
		_intent = intent, _catalog = catalog,
		_resolved = {}, _random = {}, _dirty = {},
	}, Cosmetics.Resolver)
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:InvalidateItem(itemName) self._dirty[itemName] = true end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:InvalidateAll()
	for itemName in pairs(self._intent:GetItemNames()) do self._dirty[itemName] = true end
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:RerollAll()
	self._random = {}
	self:InvalidateAll()
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:RerollItem(itemName)
	self._random[itemName] = nil
	self._dirty[itemName] = true
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:_EnsureRandomSelections(itemName)
	local store = self._random[itemName]
	if store == nil then
		store = {}
		self._random[itemName] = store
	end
	return store
end

--- khook.lua:106704 -- a held draw stays valid only while it is in the
--- catalogue, and additionally favorited when favorites-only is on.
function Cosmetics.Resolver:_IsRandomSelectionValid(itemName, cosmeticType, name, favoritesOnly)
	if name == nil then return false end
	if not self._catalog:IsInCatalog({
		cosmeticType = cosmeticType, itemName = itemName, cosmeticName = name }) then
		return false
	end
	if favoritesOnly then
		return Cosmetics.favoritedResolver:Get(itemName, name)
	end
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:106719 -- with favorites-only on the draw comes from the item's
--- favorites instead of the catalogue. A favorited "none" is stored as
--- `NONE_COSMETIC_<Type>` and only counts for its own type (khook.lua:106740).
function Cosmetics.Resolver:_GetRandomForPool(itemName, cosmeticType, favoritesOnly)
	if not favoritesOnly then
		return self._catalog:GetRandomCosmetic({
			cosmeticType = cosmeticType, itemName = itemName })
	end
	local favorites = Cosmetics.favoritedResolver:GetItem(itemName)
	if favorites == nil then return nil end
	local pool, count = {}, 0
	for favorite in pairs(favorites) do
		local candidate
		if string.match(favorite, "^" .. Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC) then
			if string.split(favorite, "_")[3] == cosmeticType then
				candidate = Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC
			end
		else
			candidate = favorite
		end
		if candidate ~= nil and self._catalog:IsInCatalog({
			cosmeticType = cosmeticType, itemName = itemName, cosmeticName = candidate }) then
			count += 1
			pool[count] = candidate
		end
	end
	if count == 0 then return nil end
	return pool[math.random(count)]
end

--- khook.lua:106766 -- anything but RANDOM_COSMETIC passes through and drops
--- the stored draw, so switching away and back rerolls.
function Cosmetics.Resolver:_ResolveSelectedCosmeticName(itemName, cosmeticType, store, name, favoritesOnly)
	if name ~= Cosmetics.RANDOM_COSMETIC then
		store[cosmeticType] = nil
		return name
	end
	local drawn = store[cosmeticType]
	if not self:_IsRandomSelectionValid(itemName, cosmeticType, drawn, favoritesOnly) then
		drawn = self:_GetRandomForPool(itemName, cosmeticType, favoritesOnly)
		store[cosmeticType] = drawn
	end
	return drawn
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:_BuildResolved(itemName)
	local effective = self._intent:GetItemEffective(itemName) or {}
	local store = self:_EnsureRandomSelections(itemName)
	local out = {}
	for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
		local name = self:_ResolveSelectedCosmeticName(itemName, cosmeticType, store,
			Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectedCosmeticName(effective, cosmeticType),
			Cosmetics.onlyUseFavorites:Get(itemName, cosmeticType))
		Cosmetics.Selection.applyResolvedSelection(out, effective, cosmeticType, name)
	end
	return out
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:_PruneRemovedItemState()
	local live = self._intent:GetItemNames()
	for _, tbl in ipairs({ self._dirty, self._resolved, self._random }) do
		for itemName in pairs(tbl) do
			if not live[itemName] then tbl[itemName] = nil end
		end
	end
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:ResolveAll()
	self:_PruneRemovedItemState()
	for itemName in pairs(self._intent:GetItemNames()) do
		if self._intent:GetItemEffective(itemName) == nil then
			self._dirty[itemName] = nil
			self._resolved[itemName] = nil
			self._random[itemName] = nil
		elseif self._dirty[itemName] or self._resolved[itemName] == nil then
			self._dirty[itemName] = nil
			self._resolved[itemName] = self:_BuildResolved(itemName)
		end
	end
	return self._resolved
end

function Cosmetics.Resolver:ResolveItem(itemName)
	if self._intent:GetItemEffective(itemName) == nil then
		self._dirty[itemName] = nil
		self._resolved[itemName] = nil
		self._random[itemName] = nil
		return nil
	end
	if not (self._dirty[itemName] or self._resolved[itemName] == nil) then
		return self._resolved[itemName]
	end
	local built = self:_BuildResolved(itemName)
	self._dirty[itemName] = nil
	self._resolved[itemName] = built
	return built
end
