--- khook.lua:42260 `favoritedIntent` -- `_proposed[itemName][cosmeticName]`.
--- A favorite is stored as `true` or removed; there is no `false`.
Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent = {}
Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent.__index = Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent

function Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent.new()
	return setmetatable({ _proposed = {}, changed = Signal.new() }, Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent)
end

function Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent:_GetOrCreate(itemName)
	local entry = self._proposed[itemName]
	if entry == nil then
		entry = {}
		self._proposed[itemName] = entry
	end
	return entry
end

--- khook.lua:42282
function Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent:Get(itemName, cosmeticName)
	local entry = self._proposed[itemName]
	return entry ~= nil and entry[cosmeticName] == true
end

--- khook.lua:42292
function Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent:Set(itemName, cosmeticName, on)
	self:_GetOrCreate(itemName)[cosmeticName] = on and true or nil
	self.changed:Fire()
end

function Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent:GetProposedByItem() return self._proposed end

--- khook.lua:42305 -- empty item entries are dropped from the export.
function Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent:ExportState()
	local out = {}
	for itemName, entry in pairs(self._proposed) do
		if next(entry) ~= nil then out[itemName] = table.clone(entry) end
	end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:42322
function Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent:ImportState(state)
	local out = {}
	if typeof(state) == "table" then
		for itemName, entry in pairs(state) do
			if typeof(entry) == "table" and next(entry) ~= nil then
				local kept = {}
				for cosmeticName, value in pairs(entry) do
					if value == true then kept[cosmeticName] = true end
				end
				if next(kept) ~= nil then out[itemName] = kept end
			end
		end
	end
	self._proposed = out
	self.changed:Fire()
end

--============================================================================--
--- The favorited resolver. khook builds it from the intent and the catalog
--- (khook.lua:45876 `v213.new(favoritedIntent, catalog)`); its own body is
--- destroyed, but every call site pins the contract exactly:
---   `Get(item, cosmeticName)` -> boolean          (khook.lua:106556)
---   `GetItem(item)`           -> iterable of names (khook.lua:106571)
---   `GetByItem()`             -> the whole FavoritedCosmetics field (19037)
--- and the random-from-favorites walk at khook.lua:106585 shows a favorited
--- "none" is stored as `NONE_COSMETIC_<Type>`, filtered by splitting on "_".
--- A favorite only counts while it is in the catalogue, which is why the
--- resolver holds the catalog at all.
Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver = {}
Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver.__index = Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver

function Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver.new(intent, catalog)
	local self = setmetatable({
		_intent = intent, _catalog = catalog, changed = Signal.new(),
	}, Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver)
	intent.changed:Connect(function() self.changed:Fire() end)
	catalog.built:Connect(function() self.changed:Fire() end)
	return self
end

function Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver:_isLive(itemName, cosmeticName)
	if string.match(cosmeticName, "^" .. Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC) then return true end
	return self._catalog:IsCosmeticInCatalog(itemName, cosmeticName)
end

function Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver:Get(itemName, cosmeticName)
	if not self._intent:Get(itemName, cosmeticName) then return false end
	return self:_isLive(itemName, cosmeticName)
end

function Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver:GetItem(itemName)
	local entry = self._intent:GetProposedByItem()[itemName]
	if entry == nil then return nil end
	local out = {}
	for cosmeticName in pairs(entry) do
		if self:_isLive(itemName, cosmeticName) then out[cosmeticName] = true end
	end
	if next(out) == nil then return nil end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:19036 -- this REPLACES the data field rather than merging into it,
--- so the game sees exactly the favorites khook resolves.
function Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver:GetByItem()
	local out = {}
	for itemName in pairs(self._intent:GetProposedByItem()) do
		local entry = self:GetItem(itemName)
		if entry ~= nil then out[itemName] = entry end
	end
	return out
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:65543 `onlyUseFavoritesState` -- a per-item, per-cosmetic-type
--- tri-state: nil means "inherit the game's own setting", true/false override.
Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites = {}
Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites.__index = Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites

function Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites.new()
	return setmetatable({ _proposed = {}, changed = Signal.new() }, Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites)
end

--- khook.lua:65551
function Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites:GetOrNil(itemName, cosmeticType)
	local entry = self._proposed[itemName]
	if entry == nil then return nil end
	local value = entry[cosmeticType]
	if value == nil then return nil end
	return value == true
end

function Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites:Get(itemName, cosmeticType)
	return self:GetOrNil(itemName, cosmeticType) == true
end

--- khook.lua:65571 -- returns whether anything actually changed, and empties
--- the item entry when its last override is cleared.
function Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites:Set(itemName, cosmeticType, value)
	local entry = self._proposed[itemName]
	value = value and true or nil
	if entry == nil then
		if value == nil then return false end
		entry = {}
		self._proposed[itemName] = entry
	end
	if entry[cosmeticType] == value then return false end
	entry[cosmeticType] = value
	if next(entry) == nil then self._proposed[itemName] = nil end
	self.changed:Fire()
	return true
end

function Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites:ExportState()
	local out = {}
	for itemName, entry in pairs(self._proposed) do
		local kept = {}
		for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
			if entry[cosmeticType] ~= nil then kept[cosmeticType] = entry[cosmeticType] end
		end
		if next(kept) ~= nil then out[itemName] = kept end
	end
	return out
end

function Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites:ImportState(state)
	local out = {}
	if typeof(state) == "table" then
		for itemName, entry in pairs(state) do
			if typeof(entry) == "table" then
				local kept = {}
				for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
					if entry[cosmeticType] ~= nil then kept[cosmeticType] = entry[cosmeticType] end
				end
				if next(kept) ~= nil then out[itemName] = kept end
			end
		end
	end
	self._proposed = out
	self.changed:Fire()
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:81586 -- the generic priority-layered `key -> value` store. khook
--- reuses it wherever a layer stack is needed over plain values; the emote
--- intent is the instance that survives here (`SetEmote` -> `Set(slot, name,
--- priority)`, khook.lua:54470).
Cosmetics.LayeredStore = {}
Cosmetics.LayeredStore.__index = Cosmetics.LayeredStore

function Cosmetics.LayeredStore.new()
	return setmetatable({ _layers = {}, changed = Signal.new() }, Cosmetics.LayeredStore)
end

local function layeredStoreIsEmpty(layer) return next(layer) == nil end

--- khook.lua:81641
function Cosmetics.LayeredStore:Set(key, value, priority)
	priority = priority or 0
	local layer = Cosmetics.Layers.getOrCreate(self._layers, priority, function() return {} end)
	if layer[key] == value then return false end
	layer[key] = value
	self.changed:Fire()
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:81659 -- with no priority every layer is cleared.
function Cosmetics.LayeredStore:Clear(key, priority)
	if priority ~= nil then
		local layer = self._layers[priority]
		if layer == nil or layer[key] == nil then return false end
		layer[key] = nil
		Cosmetics.Layers.removeIfEmpty(self._layers, priority, layeredStoreIsEmpty)
		self.changed:Fire()
		return true
	end
	local changed = false
	for key2, layer in pairs(self._layers) do
		if layer[key] ~= nil then
			layer[key] = nil
			changed = true
		end
		Cosmetics.Layers.removeIfEmpty(self._layers, key2, layeredStoreIsEmpty)
	end
	if not changed then return false end
	self.changed:Fire()
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:81591 `f5760` -- the union of every key any layer sets, each
--- resolved by the highest priority that holds it.
function Cosmetics.LayeredStore:GetAll()
	local keys, out = {}, {}
	for _, layer in pairs(self._layers) do
		for key in pairs(layer) do keys[key] = true end
	end
	for key in pairs(keys) do
		local _, value = Cosmetics.Layers.resolveHighestPriority(self._layers,
			function(layer) return layer[key] end, nil)
		if value ~= nil then out[key] = value end
	end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:81707 -- exported as a list of `{ priority, entries }` records.
function Cosmetics.LayeredStore:ExportState()
	local layers = {}
	for priority, layer in pairs(self._layers) do
		if not layeredStoreIsEmpty(layer) then
			table.insert(layers, { priority = priority, entries = table.clone(layer) })
		end
	end
	return { layers = layers }
end

--- khook.lua:81696 -- the priority is read back with `tonumber`, so a JSON
--- round trip that stringified the key still lands on the right layer.
function Cosmetics.LayeredStore:ImportState(state)
	local out = {}
	if typeof(state) == "table" and typeof(state.layers) == "table" then
		for _, record in pairs(state.layers) do
			if typeof(record) == "table" and typeof(record.entries) == "table" then
				local priority = tonumber(record.priority)
				if priority ~= nil and not layeredStoreIsEmpty(record.entries) then
					out[priority] = table.clone(record.entries)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	self._layers = out
	self.changed:Fire()
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:92971 -- a season rank charm is named "Season <n>", and its
--- metadata is the ELO/leaderboard pair the charm renders from.
Cosmetics.Rank = { RANKS = {} }

function Cosmetics.Rank.isSeasonRankCharm(name)
	if typeof(name) ~= "string" or #name < 8 or string.sub(name, 1, 7) ~= "Season " then
		return false
	end
	return tonumber(string.sub(name, 8)) ~= nil
end

function Cosmetics.Rank.getRank(rankName) return Cosmetics.Rank.RANKS[rankName] end

--- khook.lua:92984 -- Unranked carries no ELO, and a rank that requires a
--- leaderboard placement defaults to 1 when none was given.
function Cosmetics.Rank.buildMetadata(rankName, leaderboardRank)
	local rank = Cosmetics.Rank.RANKS[rankName]
	if rank == nil then return nil end
	if rank.name == "Unranked" then
		return { SeasonELO = nil, SeasonLeaderboardRank = leaderboardRank }
	end
	if rank.requiresLeaderboard and leaderboardRank == nil then leaderboardRank = 1 end
	return { SeasonELO = rank.requiredELO, SeasonLeaderboardRank = leaderboardRank }
end

--- khook.lua:52417 `rankCharm`
Cosmetics.RankCharm = {}
Cosmetics.RankCharm.__index = Cosmetics.RankCharm

function Cosmetics.RankCharm.new()
	return setmetatable({ _overrides = {}, changed = Signal.new() }, Cosmetics.RankCharm)
end

--- khook.lua:52425 -- an unknown rank name is treated as "clear".
function Cosmetics.RankCharm:SetForSeason(charmName, rankName, leaderboardRank)
	if rankName ~= nil and Cosmetics.Rank.getRank(rankName) == nil then rankName = nil end
	local current = self._overrides[charmName]
	if rankName == nil then
		if current == nil then return end
		self._overrides[charmName] = nil
		self.changed:Fire()
		return
	end
	if current ~= nil and current.rankName == rankName
		and current.leaderboardRank == leaderboardRank then
		return
	end
	self._overrides[charmName] = { rankName = rankName, leaderboardRank = leaderboardRank }
	self.changed:Fire()
end

function Cosmetics.RankCharm:GetForSeason(charmName)
	local entry = self._overrides[charmName]
	if entry == nil then return nil, nil end
	return entry.rankName, entry.leaderboardRank
end

function Cosmetics.RankCharm:GetAllOverrides() return self._overrides end

function Cosmetics.RankCharm:SetAllOverrides(overrides)
	self._overrides = overrides or {}
	self.changed:Fire()
end

--- khook.lua:52464
function Cosmetics.RankCharm:ResolveMetadata(charmName)
	if not Cosmetics.Rank.isSeasonRankCharm(charmName) then return nil end
	local entry = self._overrides[charmName]
	if entry == nil then return nil end
	return Cosmetics.Rank.buildMetadata(entry.rankName, entry.leaderboardRank)
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:54437 `emotes` -- an intent (the layered store), a resolver that
--- filters it against the catalogue, and the `EquippedEmotes` data hook
--- (khook.lua:113888). The `_use` and `_emoteCancellation` halves -- playing an
--- emote and cancelling it -- are not ported: they drive the game's emote
--- remotes and their bodies are destroyed.
Cosmetics.Emotes = { intent = nil, resolver = nil, enabled = false }

--- khook.lua:113905 -- clone the real value, lay the resolved slots over it.
function Cosmetics.Emotes.setEnabled(on)
	on = on and true or false
	if Cosmetics.Emotes.enabled == on then return false end
	Cosmetics.Emotes.enabled = on
	if on then
		Rivals.DataHook.set("EquippedEmotes", function(original)
			local out = typeof(original) == "table" and table.clone(original) or {}
			for slot, name in pairs(Cosmetics.Emotes.resolver:GetAll()) do
				out[slot] = name
			end
			return out
		end)
	else
		Rivals.DataHook.unset("EquippedEmotes")
	end
	Rivals.DataHook.trigger("EquippedEmotes")
	return true
end

--- The emote resolver: the intent's merged layers, minus anything the
--- catalogue does not carry (khook.lua:54443 `v186.new(catalog, intent)`).
---
--- `GetAll` returns the DATA shape, not bare names, because khook's equipped
--- hook merges it straight into the field (khook.lua:113911). An `EquippedEmotes`
--- entry is `{ Name = ... }` like every other equipped cosmetic -- the game reads
--- `v.Name` off it (Lobby.Buttons:357) and throws `random (interval is empty)`
--- when it is a bare string, because the name list it builds comes out empty.
function Cosmetics.Emotes.newResolver(intent, catalog)
	local self = { changed = Signal.new() }
	function self:GetAll()
		local out = {}
		for slot, name in pairs(intent:GetAll()) do
			if name ~= nil and catalog:IsCosmeticType(name, "Emote") then
				out[slot] = { Name = name }
			end
		end
		return out
	end
	intent.changed:Connect(function()
		self.changed:Fire()
		if Cosmetics.Emotes.enabled then Rivals.DataHook.trigger("EquippedEmotes") end
	end)
	catalog.built:Connect(function() self.changed:Fire() end)
	return self
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:35100 -- the item store: intent + resolver + data masker + the
--- rendering layer, kept in step. `_catalogExemptLayers` is `{[SkinChanger]=true}`
--- (khook.lua:45883): a skin-changer pick is never reconciled against the
--- catalogue, so it survives a narrower Unlock All filter.
Cosmetics.Items = {}
Cosmetics.Items.__index = Cosmetics.Items

function Cosmetics.Items.new(catalog)
	local self = setmetatable({
		_catalog = catalog,
		_intent = Cosmetics.Intent.new(),
		_isEnabled = false,
		_favoritedRefreshSuspendDepth = 0,
		_catalogExemptLayers = { [Cosmetics.PRIORITY.SkinChanger] = true },
		_dataMaskingExemptLayers = {},
	}, Cosmetics.Items)
	self._resolver = Cosmetics.Resolver.new(self._intent, catalog)
	return self
end

--- khook.lua:35154 -- the suppression depth stops a catalogue rebuild from
--- re-entering SyncAll once per favourites change.
function Cosmetics.Items:WithFavoritesSuppressed(fn)
	self._favoritedRefreshSuspendDepth += 1
	local ok, err = pcall(fn)
	self._favoritedRefreshSuspendDepth -= 1
	if not ok then error(err, 0) end
end

--- khook.lua:35164 -- the masker is dropped while imported state is staged, so
--- half-built selections never reach the game's data.
function Cosmetics.Items:_WithDataMaskingDisabled(fn)
	local wasEnabled = self._isEnabled
	if wasEnabled then Cosmetics.Masker.setEnabled(false) end
	local ok, err = pcall(fn)
	if wasEnabled then Cosmetics.Masker.setEnabled(true) end
	if not ok then error(err, 0) end
end

--- khook.lua:35179. A selection that has fallen out of the catalogue is dropped
--- -- but only at the layer that set it, and never for an exempt layer.
function Cosmetics.Items:_ClearInvalidSelectedCosmetic(itemName, cosmeticType)
	local effective = self._intent:GetItemEffective(itemName)
	if effective == nil then return end
	local name = Cosmetics.Selection.getSelectedCosmeticName(effective, cosmeticType)
	if name == Cosmetics.RANDOM_COSMETIC
		and not self._catalog:IsEmpty(itemName, cosmeticType) then
		return
	end
	if name == nil then return end
	if self._catalog:IsInCatalog({
		cosmeticType = cosmeticType, itemName = itemName, cosmeticName = name }) then
		return
	end
	local winner = self._intent:GetWinnerPriority(itemName, cosmeticType)
	if winner == nil then
		self._intent:ClearSelection(itemName, cosmeticType)
		return
	end
	if self._catalogExemptLayers[winner] then return end
	self._intent:ClearSelectionAtPriority(itemName, cosmeticType, winner)
end

function Cosmetics.Items:_ReconcileCatalogSelections()
	for itemName in pairs(self._intent:GetItemNames()) do
		for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
			self:_ClearInvalidSelectedCosmetic(itemName, cosmeticType)
		end
	end
end

--- khook.lua:35222
function Cosmetics.Items:SyncAll(reconcile)
	if reconcile then self:_ReconcileCatalogSelections() end
	self._resolver:InvalidateAll()
	Cosmetics.Masker.replaceAll(
		self._intent:GetEffectiveByItemExcludingLayers(self._dataMaskingExemptLayers))
	self:RenderIfEnabled()
end

function Cosmetics.Items:RenderIfEnabled()
	if self._isEnabled then Cosmetics.Render.all(self._resolver:ResolveAll(), LocalPlayer) end
end

function Cosmetics.Items:IsLayerActive(key) return self._intent:IsLayerActive(key) end

function Cosmetics.Items:GetLayerSelections(itemName, key)
	return self._intent:GetLayerSelections(itemName, key)
end

function Cosmetics.Items:SetLayerActive(key, on)
	self._intent:SetLayerActive(key, on)
	self:SyncAll(false)
end

function Cosmetics.Items:SetChoice(itemName, choice, key)
	self._intent:SetChoice(itemName, choice, key)
	self:SyncAll(false)
end

function Cosmetics.Items:ClearItemSelection(itemName, cosmeticType, key)
	if key == nil then
		self._intent:ClearSelection(itemName, cosmeticType)
	else
		self._intent:ClearSelectionAtPriority(itemName, cosmeticType, key)
	end
	self:SyncAll(false)
end

--- khook.lua:35277
function Cosmetics.Items:SetEnabled(on)
	if self._isEnabled == on then return end
	self._isEnabled = on
	Cosmetics.Masker.setEnabled(on)
	-- khook.lua:35284 -- the rank patch is armed with the renderer, not the store.
	Cosmetics.Render.setRankOverridesActive(on)
	if on then
		Cosmetics.Render.all(self._resolver:ResolveAll(), LocalPlayer)
	else
		Cosmetics.Render.clearAll(LocalPlayer)
	end
end

function Cosmetics.Items:RefreshDataIfEnabled() Cosmetics.Masker.refreshIfEnabled() end

--- khook.lua:35240 -- staged state is reconciled and rerolled with the masker
--- down, so a half-imported selection never reaches the game's data.
function Cosmetics.Items:RefreshImportedState(reconcile)
	self:_WithDataMaskingDisabled(function()
		self:SyncAll(reconcile)
		self._resolver:RerollAll()
	end)
	self:RenderIfEnabled()
end

function Cosmetics.Items:RerollAll()
	self._resolver:RerollAll()
	self:RenderIfEnabled()
end

function Cosmetics.Items:GetResolvedAll() return self._resolver:ResolveAll() end

--============================================================================--
-- ItemDataHook (khook.lua:130294-130540)
--============================================================================--
-- This is how khook actually patches weapon data, and it is NOT a spoof of the
-- `WeaponInventory` field. khook swaps a CONSTANT inside the game's own
-- `PlayerDataController.GetWeaponData` so that it calls a method name the game
-- can never produce -- `"GetWeaponData\0ohaio"` (khook.lua:130311) -- and
-- installs its own function on `PlayerDataUtility` under that name.
--
-- Doing it this way rather than rewriting the inventory array buys three things
-- khookclone did not have:
--   * it patches ANY player's weapon data, not just the local one, which is
--     what makes remote overrides render at all (khook.lua:130489);
--   * `Level` / `Prestige` / `XP` are copied back from the original explicitly
--     (130506-130508), so a cosmetic override can never disturb progression;
--   * when nothing changed it returns the ORIGINAL table, identity included,
--     which is what the game's own selection diffing compares on.
--
-- What it does NOT do -- and khook has no other code that does -- is unlock
-- weapons. `_FindOriginalItemData` reads the REAL inventory and returns nil for
-- anything the player does not own, so a weapon you have not unlocked has no
-- weapon data and therefore no cosmetics. Searching khook for a weapon
-- unlocker (`OwnsAllWeapons`, `FreeWeaponUnlockCheck`, `WeaponKeys`, ...)
-- returns nothing: Unlock All is cosmetics only.

Cosmetics.ItemHook = {
	ALIAS = "GetWeaponData\0ohaio",
	restore = nil,
}

--- khook.lua:130313 `f8957`
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.target()
	local controller = Rivals.DataHook.controller()
	if controller == nil then return nil end
	local mt = getmetatable(controller)
	local index = typeof(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index") or nil
	if typeof(index) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local fn = rawget(index, "GetWeaponData")
	return typeof(fn) == "function" and fn or nil
end

function Cosmetics.ItemHook.utility()
	return Rivals.module(function()
		return require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PlayerDataUtility)
	end, "_playerDataUtility")
end

--- khook.lua:130386 -- reads the REAL inventory, so an override can only ever
--- decorate a weapon the player actually has.
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.findOriginal(weaponName)
	local inventory = Rivals.DataHook.getOriginal("WeaponInventory")
	if typeof(inventory) ~= "table" then return nil end
	for _, entry in pairs(inventory) do
		if typeof(entry) == "table" and entry.Name == weaponName then return entry end
	end
	return nil
end

--- khook.lua:130345 `f8958` -- NONE_COSMETIC clears a weapon-cosmetic slot
--- rather than writing a record named "none"; anything else is cloned so the
--- selection store and the weapon data never share a table.
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.fieldValue(cosmeticType, value)
	if typeof(value) ~= "table" then return value end
	if value.Name ~= Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC then return table.clone(value) end
	for _, kind in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
		if kind == cosmeticType then return nil end
	end
	return table.clone(value)
end

--- khook.lua:130351 `f8959` -- lay a resolved selection onto a weapon-data
--- record. The field names ARE the cosmetic types: Skin, Wrap, Charm, Finisher.
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.applyCosmetics(_weaponName, data, selection)
	if selection == nil then return data, false end
	if data == nil then data = {} end
	for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
		local slot = Cosmetics.Selection.SLOT_KEY[cosmeticType]
		local value = selection[slot]
		if value ~= nil then
			local payload
			if cosmeticType == "Wrap" then
				payload = { Name = value.name, Inverted = value.inverted == true }
			else
				payload = { Name = value }
			end
			data[cosmeticType] = Cosmetics.ItemHook.fieldValue(cosmeticType, payload)
		end
	end
	return data, true
end

--- khook.lua:130405 -- a favorites-only override rides along on the cosmetic
--- record itself, as `OnlyUseFavorites`, and only where a real cosmetic is set.
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.applyOnlyUseFavoritesMask(weaponName, data)
	local changed = false
	for _, cosmeticType in ipairs(Cosmetics.ITEM_TYPES) do
		local flag = Cosmetics.onlyUseFavorites:GetOrNil(weaponName, cosmeticType)
		if flag ~= nil then
			if data == nil then data = {} end
			local current = data[cosmeticType]
			local name = typeof(current) == "table" and current.Name or nil
			if name ~= nil and name ~= Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC then
				changed = true
				local copy = typeof(current) == "table" and table.clone(current) or {}
				copy.OnlyUseFavorites = flag and true or nil
				data[cosmeticType] = copy
			end
		end
	end
	return data, changed
end

--- khook.lua:130440 -- a season rank charm carries `Metadata` describing the
--- rank it should render at.
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.applyRankCharmMask(data)
	if data == nil then return data, false end
	local charm = data.Charm
	if typeof(charm) ~= "table" then return data, false end
	local metadata = Cosmetics.rankCharm:ResolveMetadata(charm.Name)
	if metadata == nil then return data, false end
	local copy = table.clone(charm)
	copy.Metadata = metadata
	data.Charm = copy
	return data, true
end

--- The replacement `GetWeaponData`. khook.lua:130483.
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.getWeaponData(_self, controller, weaponName)
	local original = Cosmetics.ItemHook.findOriginal(weaponName)
	if original == nil then return nil end

	local player = rawget(controller, "Player") or rawget(controller, "_player_object")
	if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") then
		player = LocalPlayer
	end

	local selection
	if player == LocalPlayer then
		local rendered = Cosmetics.Render.store[LocalPlayer]
		selection = rendered ~= nil and rendered[weaponName] or nil
		if selection == nil then selection = Cosmetics.Masker.store[weaponName] end
	else
		local overrides = Cosmetics.Remote.overrides[player]
		selection = overrides ~= nil and overrides[weaponName] or nil
	end

	local patched, changed = Cosmetics.ItemHook.applyCosmetics(
		weaponName, table.clone(original), selection)
	local masked, maskChanged = Cosmetics.ItemHook.applyOnlyUseFavoritesMask(weaponName, patched)
	local rankChanged = false
	if player == LocalPlayer then
		masked, rankChanged = Cosmetics.ItemHook.applyRankCharmMask(masked)
	end

	-- khook.lua:130503 -- untouched means the ORIGINAL table, identity and all.
	if not (changed or maskChanged or rankChanged) then return original end

	masked.Level = original.Level
	masked.Prestige = original.Prestige
	masked.XP = original.XP
	return masked
end

--- khook.lua:130459 `Load`
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.load()
	if Cosmetics.ItemHook.restore ~= nil then return true end
	if debug.getconstants == nil or debug.setconstant == nil then
		return false, "executor has no constant access"
	end
	local fn = Cosmetics.ItemHook.target()
	if fn == nil then return false, "GetWeaponData function unavailable" end
	local utility = Cosmetics.ItemHook.utility()
	if utility == nil then return false, "PlayerDataUtility unavailable" end

	local ok, constants = pcall(debug.getconstants, fn)
	if not ok or typeof(constants) ~= "table" then return false, "constants unreadable" end
	for index, value in pairs(constants) do
		if value == "GetWeaponData" then
			Cosmetics.ItemHook.restore = { fn = fn, index = index, original = value }
			pcall(debug.setconstant, fn, index, Cosmetics.ItemHook.ALIAS)
			break
		end
	end
	if Cosmetics.ItemHook.restore == nil then
		return false, "GetWeaponData constant not found in PlayerDataController"
	end

	rawset(utility, Cosmetics.ItemHook.ALIAS, Cosmetics.ItemHook.getWeaponData)
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:130515 `Revert`
function Cosmetics.ItemHook.revert()
	local restore = Cosmetics.ItemHook.restore
	if restore == nil then return end
	Cosmetics.ItemHook.restore = nil
	pcall(debug.setconstant, restore.fn, restore.index, restore.original)
	local utility = Cosmetics.ItemHook.utility()
	if utility ~= nil then rawset(utility, Cosmetics.ItemHook.ALIAS, nil) end
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:43770 `dataMasker` -- the only layer that reaches the game's data.
--- Its `_itemDataHook:Load()` installs the spoof-and-merge closure at
--- khook.lua:17650 / 19584 over the PlayerData hook, and `_Refresh` re-fires
--- both change signals (khook.lua:43851).
Cosmetics.Masker = { enabled = false, store = {}, cache = {}, frame = 0 }

--- The frame counter the memo keys on. Bumped once per heartbeat, and bumped
--- eagerly by `refresh` so a selection change is never served a stale value.
function Cosmetics.Masker.invalidate()
	Cosmetics.Masker.frame += 1
end

function Cosmetics.Masker.replaceAll(byItem)
	Cosmetics.Masker.store = byItem or {}
	if Cosmetics.Masker.enabled then Cosmetics.Masker.refresh() end
end

function Cosmetics.Masker.setEnabled(on)
	on = on and true or false
	if Cosmetics.Masker.enabled == on then return end
	Cosmetics.Masker.enabled = on
	if on then Cosmetics.Masker.load() else Cosmetics.Masker.revert() end
end

function Cosmetics.Masker.refreshIfEnabled()
	if Cosmetics.Masker.enabled then Cosmetics.Masker.refresh() end
end

--- khook.lua:43851 `_Refresh` -- the change signals AND the equipment scene.
--- The signal alone is not enough: the lobby grid and the weapon buttons cache
--- what they have already built, so khook re-runs their own update methods.
function Cosmetics.Masker.refresh()
	Cosmetics.Masker.invalidate()
	Rivals.DataHook.trigger(Cosmetics.FIELD_INVENTORY)
	Rivals.DataHook.trigger(Cosmetics.FIELD_WEAPONS)
	Cosmetics.Scene.equipment()
end

--- The weapon-data patch reads straight out of the render and remote stores, so
--- there is nothing to rebuild when a selection changes -- only to announce.
function Cosmetics.Masker.dress() end

--- khook.lua:17650 `f1286` and 19584 `f1460` -- clone the real value, lay the
--- resolver's output over it, hand that back.
--- Both spoofs are memoised for one frame. The game reads these fields several
--- times a frame, and rebuilding a 535-entry inventory (or the whole weapon
--- array) on every read costs more than the whole rest of the feature: measured
--- in the lobby it halved the frame rate. One rebuild per frame bounds the cost
--- and the value can be at most one frame stale, which nothing here can see.
function Cosmetics.Masker.cached(field, source, build)
	local entry = Cosmetics.Masker.cache[field]
	local frame = Cosmetics.Masker.frame
	if entry ~= nil and entry.frame == frame and entry.source == source then
		return entry.value
	end
	local value = build(source)
	Cosmetics.Masker.cache[field] = { frame = frame, source = source, value = value }
	return value
end

function Cosmetics.Masker.load()
	local hook = Rivals.DataHook

	-- khook.lua:128157 -- the catalogue REPLACES the field. khookclone used to
	-- clone the player's real inventory and merge into it, which is wrong in
	-- one visible way: an entry the player genuinely owns but the current Type
	-- or Rarity filter excludes stayed owned, so narrowing the filter never
	-- took anything away.
	hook.set(Cosmetics.FIELD_INVENTORY, function(_original)
		return Cosmetics.Masker.cached(Cosmetics.FIELD_INVENTORY, Cosmetics.Masker.frame,
			function()
				return Cosmetics.controller.catalog:GetInventory()
			end)
	end)

	-- khook.lua:130459 -- weapon data is patched at the source, not spoofed as
	-- a field. `WeaponInventory` is left untouched.
	local ok, err = Cosmetics.ItemHook.load()
	Features.status.cosmeticitemhook = ok and "ok (GetWeaponData patched)"
		or ("unavailable: " .. tostring(err))

	Cosmetics.Masker.refresh()
end

function Cosmetics.Masker.revert()
	Cosmetics.ItemHook.revert()
	Rivals.DataHook.destroy()
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:35113 `_rendering` -- khook re-renders the local view models and
--- the character appearances from the resolved set. That layer is not ported:
--- its bodies are destroyed and it reaches into the game's view-model reloader
--- (`ReloadAllWithCosmetics`, khook.lua:36795). Spoofing the data is what the
--- view model reads from anyway, so the visible result follows the next time
--- the game rebuilds it.
--============================================================================--
-- Rendering + remote overrides + the user-server bridge
-- (khook.lua:35314 / 43805 / 67448 / 102140)
--============================================================================--

Cosmetics.Scene = { _equipmentQueued = false, _selectionQueued = false }

--============================================================================--
-- Scene refresh + view-model reload (khook.lua:129917 / 36807 / 19559)
--============================================================================--
-- Spoofing the data is only half of it. The game caches what it has already
-- built, so khook pokes its own refresh methods afterwards -- that is
-- `equipmentScene()` at khook.lua:43854, and the view-model rebuild in a match.
--
-- Every one of these calls runs at **thread identity 2** (khook.lua:129897).
-- Game code checks the caller's identity; calling straight from an executor
-- thread is rejected.

--- khook.lua:129922 `f8932` -- resolve a method off the object's metatable and
--- return a thunk that calls it. khook never calls `obj:Method()` directly,
--- because these objects proxy `__index`.
function Cosmetics.Scene.thunk(object, methodName)
	if object == nil then return nil end
	local mt = getmetatable(object)
	local index = typeof(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index") or nil
	local method = typeof(index) == "table" and rawget(index, methodName) or nil
	if method == nil then method = rawget(object, methodName) end
	if typeof(method) ~= "function" then return nil end
	return function() coroutine.wrap(method)(object) end
end

--- khook.lua:129895 `f8921` -- run a list of thunks at identity 2, restoring
--- the identity afterwards.
---
--- The thread this runs on must be one WE own, never `task.spawn`. `task.spawn`
--- hands back a **pooled** thread: raise it to identity 2, have a thunk yield,
--- and the restore below lands while that thread is still suspended -- it then
--- goes back to the scheduler pool still at identity 2 and poisons whatever
--- picks it up next. That is not theoretical; it took out the executor's own
--- script runner, and every later load died at Bootstrap with
--- "The current thread cannot access 'Instance' (lacking capability Plugin)"
--- while the game kept running, so the menu simply never appeared.
---
--- A `coroutine.create` thread is never pooled, so the worst case is one
--- orphaned coroutine instead of a corrupted scheduler.
function Cosmetics.Scene.run(thunks)
	local co = coroutine.create(function()
		if getthreadidentity == nil or setthreadidentity == nil then
			for _, thunk in ipairs(thunks) do
				if thunk ~= nil then pcall(thunk) end
			end
			return
		end
		local identity = getthreadidentity()
		local raised = pcall(setthreadidentity, 2)
		for _, thunk in ipairs(thunks) do
			if thunk ~= nil then pcall(thunk) end
		end
		if raised then pcall(setthreadidentity, identity) end
	end)
	local ok, err = coroutine.resume(co)
	if not ok then warn("[khookclone] scene refresh: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

--- The UI objects khook drives (khook.lua:129934-129945). khook's `v32` is
--- `Equipment[<3-element path>]` -- a walk through the LIVE object graph, not a
--- module require. That distinction matters: requiring
--- `UserInterface.Equipment.Interface.Customize.Cosmetics` hands back the CLASS,
--- whose `_weapon_slots` and friends are nil, so every refresh call silently did
--- nothing. The Equipment and Lobby modules are singletons, so they are the
--- entry points and everything else hangs off them as fields.
---
--- Not cached: the lobby rebuilds these on some transitions, and a stale
--- reference is the same silent no-op.
function Cosmetics.Scene.objects()
	local out = {}
	pcall(function()
		local ui = LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.UserInterface
		local equipment = require(ui.Equipment)
		local interface = rawget(equipment, "Interface")
		if typeof(interface) == "table" then
			local customize = rawget(interface, "Customize")
			out.cosmetics = typeof(customize) == "table" and rawget(customize, "Cosmetics") or nil
			-- khook.lua:129945 -- the weapon list is not in khook's own scene
			-- refresh; it rides the `WeaponInventory` data-changed signal, which
			-- the game wires to `_GenerateDeferred` (Equipment.Interface.Left:679).
			out.left = rawget(interface, "Left")
		end
		out.floatingModel = rawget(equipment, "FloatingModel")
		local lobby = require(ui.Lobby)
		out.buttons = rawget(lobby, "Buttons")
	end)
	if next(out) == nil then return nil end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:129940 `equipmentScene` -- the equipped-cosmetic grid and the
--- lobby weapon buttons both re-read the player data.
---
--- Coalesced to one run per frame: a single selection change walks
--- SyncAll -> Masker.replaceAll -> refresh and then RenderIfEnabled ->
--- Render.all -> refreshIfEnabled, so the naive version rebuilt the lobby
--- grid several times for one click.
function Cosmetics.Scene.equipment()
	if Cosmetics.Scene._equipmentQueued then return end
	local objects = Cosmetics.Scene.objects()
	if objects == nil then return end
	Cosmetics.Scene._equipmentQueued = true
	task.defer(function()
		Cosmetics.Scene._equipmentQueued = false
		Cosmetics.Scene.run({
			Cosmetics.Scene.thunk(objects.cosmetics, "_BulkUpdateEquipped"),
			Cosmetics.Scene.thunk(objects.buttons, "_UpdateButtonInformation"),
			-- The weapon list rebuilds its slots from
			-- `GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData(GetWeaponData(name))`
			-- (Equipment.Interface.Left:548), so the picked skin only reaches
			-- those icons on a regenerate. `_GenerateDeferred` self-coalesces.
			Cosmetics.Scene.thunk(objects.left, "_GenerateDeferred"),
		})
	end)
end

--- khook.lua:129938 `cosmeticsSelectionScene`, plus the floating model rebuild
--- khook wraps at 129945. This is what puts the skin on the model you are
--- looking at in the Equipment menu.
function Cosmetics.Scene.selection()
	if Cosmetics.Scene._selectionQueued then return end
	local objects = Cosmetics.Scene.objects()
	if objects == nil then return end
	Cosmetics.Scene._selectionQueued = true
	task.defer(function()
		Cosmetics.Scene._selectionQueued = false
		Cosmetics.Scene.run({
			Cosmetics.Scene.thunk(objects.cosmetics, "OnStateChanged"),
			Cosmetics.Scene.thunk(objects.floatingModel, "_GenerateViewModel"),
		})
	end)
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:36861 `v284` -- not a deep copy, a KEY ENCODER. The source handed
--- to `_CreateViewModel` is read with enum-encoded keys: it looks for
--- `source[ToEnum("Data")]`, which is `source["\u{00b8}"]`, and a plain `.Data`
--- reads as nil. khook applies the encoder twice, once to the record and once
--- to the wrapper, which is exactly this.
function Cosmetics.Scene.encodeKeys(tbl)
	local enums = Rivals.enumLibrary()
	if enums == nil then return tbl end
	local out = {}
	for key, value in pairs(tbl) do
		local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return enums:ToEnum(key) end)
		out[(ok and encoded) or key] = value
	end
	return out
end

--- The view model's own `Data` record, confirmed against a live one:
--- `{ Name = <view model name>, ObjectID = ... }`, with `Wrap` and `Charm`
--- added when they are set. `Name` is what picks the model --
--- `ClientViewModel.new` does `Info = ItemLibrary.ViewModels[Name]` and
--- `ItemModel = Utility:LookThrough(ViewModels, Name):Clone()` (ClientViewModel
--- lines 101-105) -- and `Charm` is read as a table with `.Name`
--- (ClientViewModel:1708), `Wrap` through `GetWrap` (line 416).
---
--- Note the skin CANNOT come from the spoofed `WeaponInventory`: the item's
--- data is created server-side, so re-equipping rebuilds the plain view model.
--- Patching this record is the only way, which is why khook does it.
function Cosmetics.Scene.buildViewModelData(itemName, selection)
	local name = itemName
	local skin = selection ~= nil and selection.skin or nil
	if skin ~= nil and skin ~= Cosmetics.RANDOM_COSMETIC
		and not string.match(skin, "^" .. Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC) then
		name = skin
	end

	local record = { Name = name }
	local wrap = selection ~= nil and selection.wrap or nil
	if wrap ~= nil and wrap.name ~= nil
		and not string.match(wrap.name, "^" .. Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC) then
		record.Wrap = { Name = wrap.name, Inverted = wrap.inverted == true }
	end
	local charm = selection ~= nil and selection.charm or nil
	if charm ~= nil and not string.match(charm, "^" .. Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC) then
		record.Charm = { Name = charm }
	end
	return Cosmetics.Scene.encodeKeys({ Data = Cosmetics.Scene.encodeKeys(record) })
end

--- khook.lua:36808-36895, step for step.
function Cosmetics.Scene.reloadViewModel(item, itemName, selection)
	if typeof(item) ~= "table" then return false end
	local viewModel = rawget(item, "ViewModel")
	if viewModel == nil then return false end
	local data = rawget(viewModel, "Data")
	if data == nil then return false end

	local source = Cosmetics.Scene.buildViewModelData(itemName, selection)

	local clientItem, clientViewModel
	local ok = pcall(function()
		local classes = LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses
		clientItem = require(classes.ClientFighter.ClientItem)
		clientViewModel = require(classes.ClientFighter.ClientItem.ClientViewModel)
	end)
	if not ok or clientItem == nil or clientViewModel == nil then return false end

	local created
	Cosmetics.Scene.run({ function()
		-- khook.lua:36821 -- destroy the old one first
		pcall(function() coroutine.wrap(clientViewModel.Destroy)(viewModel) end)
		pcall(function()
			created = coroutine.wrap(clientItem._CreateViewModel)(item, source)
		end)
		-- If the rebuild failed the item is left without a view model, so put
		-- the plain one back rather than leaving the weapon invisible.
		if created == nil then
			pcall(function()
				created = coroutine.wrap(clientItem._CreateViewModel)(item,
					Cosmetics.Scene.buildViewModelData(itemName, nil))
			end)
			if created ~= nil then rawset(item, "ViewModel", created) end
			return
		end
		rawset(item, "ViewModel", created)
		-- khook.lua:36879 -- the arms data comes off the fighter and has to be
		-- handed to the new view model before it is equipped.
		local fighter = rawget(item, "ClientFighter") or Rivals.localFighter()
		if fighter ~= nil then
			local mt = getmetatable(fighter)
			local index = typeof(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index") or nil
			local getArms = typeof(index) == "table" and rawget(index, "GetArmsData") or nil
			if getArms ~= nil then
				local arms = table.pack(coroutine.wrap(getArms)(fighter))
				pcall(function()
					coroutine.wrap(clientViewModel.SetArmsData)(created, table.unpack(arms, 1, arms.n))
				end)
			end
		end
		pcall(function() coroutine.wrap(clientViewModel.Equip)(created, true) end)
		-- khook.lua:36890 -- the equip spring is zeroed so the weapon does not
		-- fly in from the last frame's position.
		local spring = rawget(created, "_equip_spring")
		if spring ~= nil then
			rawset(spring, "_position0", 0)
			rawset(spring, "_velocity0", 0)
		end
	end })
	return true
end

--- khook.lua:36795 `ReloadAllWithCosmetics` -- every equipped item whose name
--- matches gets rebuilt.
function Cosmetics.Scene.reloadAll(itemName, selection)
	local fighter = Rivals.localFighter()
	if fighter == nil then return false end
	local items = rawget(fighter, "Items")
	if typeof(items) ~= "table" then return false end
	local any = false
	for _, item in pairs(items) do
		if typeof(item) == "table" and rawget(item, "Name") == itemName then
			if Cosmetics.Scene.reloadViewModel(item, itemName, selection) then any = true end
		end
	end
	return any
end

--- Rebuild every item the render store knows about. Used when the master
--- switch flips, where there is no single item to key off.
function Cosmetics.Scene.reloadEverything()
	local rendered = Cosmetics.Render.store[LocalPlayer]
	if rendered == nil then return end
	for itemName, selection in pairs(rendered) do
		Cosmetics.Scene.reloadAll(itemName, selection)
	end
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:102203 `_store` -- the rendering layer keeps the last selection it
--- rendered PER PLAYER, and `RenderAll` diffs against it so only the items that
--- actually changed are reloaded. That bookkeeping is the whole reason khook
--- does not rebuild every view model on every sync.
Cosmetics.Render = { store = {}, rankOverridesActive = false }

function Cosmetics.Render.getAll(player)
	local entry = Cosmetics.Render.store[player]
	if entry == nil then
		entry = {}
		Cosmetics.Render.store[player] = entry
	end
	return entry
end

local function selectionsEqual(a, b)
	if a == nil or b == nil then return a == b end
	if a.skin ~= b.skin or a.charm ~= b.charm or a.finisher ~= b.finisher then return false end
	local wa, wb = a.wrap, b.wrap
	if wa == nil or wb == nil then return wa == wb end
	return wa.name == wb.name and (wa.inverted == true) == (wb.inverted == true)
end

--- khook.lua:36795 `ReloadAllWithCosmetics`. Only the local player's view
--- models can be rebuilt this way -- a remote player's cosmetics are rendered
--- on their character model, which khook does through a separate hook that did
--- not survive the decompile.
function Cosmetics.Render.reload(player, itemName, selection)
	if player ~= LocalPlayer then return end
	Cosmetics.Scene.reloadAll(itemName, selection)
end

--- khook.lua:102201 `RenderAll` -- diff, reload what changed, then reload what
--- disappeared.
function Cosmetics.Render.all(byItem, player)
	player = player or LocalPlayer
	byItem = byItem or {}
	local previous = Cosmetics.Render.getAll(player)
	local next_ = {}
	for itemName, selection in pairs(byItem) do
		next_[itemName] = selection
		if not selectionsEqual(previous[itemName], selection) then
			Cosmetics.Render.reload(player, itemName, selection)
		end
	end
	for itemName in pairs(previous) do
		if next_[itemName] == nil then
			Cosmetics.Render.reload(player, itemName, nil)
		end
	end
	Cosmetics.Render.store[player] = next_
	Cosmetics.Masker.refreshIfEnabled()
end

--- khook.lua:102231 `Render` -- the single-item path, used when a view model is
--- created while the store is already enabled (khook.lua:35130).
function Cosmetics.Render.one(itemName, selection, player)
	player = player or LocalPlayer
	local entry = Cosmetics.Render.getAll(player)
	if selectionsEqual(entry[itemName], selection) then return end
	entry[itemName] = selection
	Cosmetics.Render.reload(player, itemName, selection)
	Cosmetics.Masker.refreshIfEnabled()
end

--- khook.lua:102247 `ClearAll`
function Cosmetics.Render.clearAll(player)
	player = player or LocalPlayer
	local entry = Cosmetics.Render.getAll(player)
	for itemName in pairs(entry) do
		Cosmetics.Render.reload(player, itemName, nil)
	end
	Cosmetics.Render.store[player] = {}
end

--- khook.lua:102190 -- the season-charm patch is armed with the renderer and
--- only does work while an override exists.
function Cosmetics.Render.setRankOverridesActive(on)
	on = on and true or false
	if Cosmetics.Render.rankOverridesActive == on then return end
	Cosmetics.Render.rankOverridesActive = on
	if on and next(Cosmetics.rankCharm:GetAllOverrides()) ~= nil then
		Cosmetics.Render.reloadSeasonCharms()
	end
end

--- khook.lua:102172 `_ReloadEquippedSeasonCharms` -- only items whose charm is
--- an actual season charm are touched.
function Cosmetics.Render.reloadSeasonCharms(player)
	player = player or LocalPlayer
	for itemName, selection in pairs(Cosmetics.Render.getAll(player)) do
		local charm = selection ~= nil and selection.charm or nil
		if charm ~= nil and charm ~= Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC
			and Cosmetics.Rank.isSeasonRankCharm(charm) then
			Cosmetics.Render.reload(player, itemName, selection)
		end
	end
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:43815 / 35314 -- remote players. khook keeps a second set of
--- overrides per player so another khook user's shared selections can be
--- rendered on their character without touching your own.
Cosmetics.Remote = { overrides = {} }

function Cosmetics.Remote.setOverrides(player, byItem, merge)
	if not merge then Cosmetics.Remote.overrides[player] = nil end
	local entry = Cosmetics.Remote.overrides[player]
	if entry == nil then
		entry = {}
		Cosmetics.Remote.overrides[player] = entry
	end
	for itemName, selection in pairs(byItem or {}) do
		if selection == nil or next(selection) == nil then
			entry[itemName] = nil
		else
			entry[itemName] = selection
		end
	end
	Cosmetics.Render.all(entry, player)
end

function Cosmetics.Remote.clearOverrides(player)
	Cosmetics.Remote.overrides[player] = nil
	Cosmetics.Render.clearAll(player)
	Cosmetics.Render.store[player] = nil
end

function Cosmetics.Items:SetRemotePlayerOverrides(player, byItem, merge)
	Cosmetics.Remote.setOverrides(player, byItem, merge)
end

function Cosmetics.Items:ClearRemotePlayerOverrides(player)
	Cosmetics.Remote.clearOverrides(player)
end

--============================================================================--
--- khook.lua:67448 `userServerBridge`. khook users in one server swap their
--- cosmetic selections so they see each other's spoofed skins: a peer payload
--- arrives and becomes remote overrides, a peer leaves and they are cleared,
--- and `NotifyStoresUpdated` queues a send of your own payload, deduped against
--- the last one sent.
---
--- The TRANSPORT is not ported. khook's "user server" is an external service
--- with its own protocol, and there is nothing in the decompile that pins the
--- wire format -- inventing one would be fiction. `Cosmetics.Bridge.send` is
--- the seam: everything above it is khook's, and it is gated on the
--- `UserServer.ShareSkins` toggle exactly as khook gates it (khook.lua:67486).
Cosmetics.Bridge = {
	shareActive = false,
	sendQueued = false,
	lastSentPayload = nil,
}

--- khook.lua:67452 -- the payload is the local resolved selections plus the
--- resolved emote slots.
function Cosmetics.Bridge.buildPayload()
	local items = {}
	for itemName, selection in pairs(Cosmetics.controller.items:GetResolvedAll()) do
		items[itemName] = Cosmetics.Selection.cloneCosmeticsSelection(selection)
	end
	return { items = items, emotes = Cosmetics.Emotes.resolver:GetAll() }
end

local function payloadsEqual(a, b)
	if a == nil or b == nil then return a == b end
	for itemName, selection in pairs(a.items) do
		if not selectionsEqual(selection, b.items[itemName]) then return false end
	end
	for itemName in pairs(b.items) do
		if a.items[itemName] == nil then return false end
	end
	for slot, name in pairs(a.emotes) do
		if b.emotes[slot] ~= name then return false end
	end
	for slot in pairs(b.emotes) do
		if a.emotes[slot] == nil then return false end
	end
	return true
end

--- The seam: khook posts the payload to its user server here.
function Cosmetics.Bridge.send(_payload) end

--- khook.lua:67443 -- coalesced to one send per frame, and skipped entirely
--- when the payload has not moved.
function Cosmetics.Bridge.notifyStoresUpdated()
	if not Cosmetics.Bridge.shareActive then return end
	if Cosmetics.Bridge.sendQueued then return end
	Cosmetics.Bridge.sendQueued = true
	task.defer(function()
		Cosmetics.Bridge.sendQueued = false
		if not Cosmetics.Bridge.shareActive then return end
		local payload = Cosmetics.Bridge.buildPayload()
		if payloadsEqual(Cosmetics.Bridge.lastSentPayload, payload) then return end
		Cosmetics.Bridge.lastSentPayload = payload
		Cosmetics.Bridge.send(payload)
	end)
end

--- khook.lua:67481 -- driven by the ShareSkins toggle; turning it on forces a
--- resend by dropping the dedupe.
function Cosmetics.Bridge.setShareActive(on)
	on = on and true or false
	if Cosmetics.Bridge.shareActive == on then return end
	Cosmetics.Bridge.shareActive = on
	Cosmetics.Bridge.lastSentPayload = nil
	if on then Cosmetics.Bridge.notifyStoresUpdated() end
end

--- khook.lua:67462 `_HandlePeerPayload`
function Cosmetics.Bridge.handlePeerPayload(player, payload)
	if typeof(payload) ~= "table" then return end
	Cosmetics.controller.items:SetRemotePlayerOverrides(player, payload.items or {}, false)
end

--- khook.lua:67468 `peerRemoved`
function Cosmetics.Bridge.handlePeerRemoved(player)
	Cosmetics.controller.items:ClearRemotePlayerOverrides(player)
end

Cosmetics.favoritedIntent = Cosmetics.FavoritedIntent.new()
Cosmetics.onlyUseFavorites = Cosmetics.OnlyUseFavorites.new()
Cosmetics.rankCharm = Cosmetics.RankCharm.new()
Cosmetics.isImportingState = false

--- khook.lua:45925 `t195` -- the controller. Every call the Cosmetics tab makes
--- lands here, and it does nothing itself: it forwards into the catalog or the
--- item store and then fires `storesUpdated`.
Cosmetics.controller = {
	catalog = Cosmetics.Catalog.new(),
	items = nil,
	runtimeEnabled = false,
	storesUpdated = Cosmetics.changed,
}
Cosmetics.controller.items = Cosmetics.Items.new(Cosmetics.controller.catalog)
Cosmetics.favoritedResolver =
	Cosmetics.FavoritedResolver.new(Cosmetics.favoritedIntent, Cosmetics.controller.catalog)
Cosmetics.Emotes.intent = Cosmetics.LayeredStore.new()
Cosmetics.Emotes.resolver =
	Cosmetics.Emotes.newResolver(Cosmetics.Emotes.intent, Cosmetics.controller.catalog)

--- khook.lua:35143 -- a favourites change re-syncs the store, unless a
--- catalogue rebuild or a state import is holding the suspension depth up.
Cosmetics.favoritedResolver.changed:Connect(function()
	local items = Cosmetics.controller.items
	if items._favoritedRefreshSuspendDepth > 0 then return end
	items:SyncAll(false)
	if Cosmetics.favoritedHookApplied then
		Rivals.DataHook.trigger("FavoritedCosmetics")
	end
end)

--- khook.lua:46150 -- these three feed `storesUpdated` but never during an import.
for _, signal in ipairs({ Cosmetics.favoritedResolver.changed,
	Cosmetics.Emotes.resolver.changed, Cosmetics.rankCharm.changed }) do
	signal:Connect(function()
		if Cosmetics.isImportingState then return end
		Cosmetics.controller.fireStoresUpdated()
	end)
end

--- khook.lua:45928 / 46154 -- the bridge is the one listener khook wires onto
--- storesUpdated itself.
function Cosmetics.controller.fireStoresUpdated()
	Cosmetics.controller.storesUpdated:Fire()
	Cosmetics.Bridge.notifyStoresUpdated()
end

end

--- khook.lua:45989 -- a catalogue rebuild runs with favourites suppressed and
--- is followed by a reconciling sync, which is what drops selections that the
--- new filter has removed.
function Cosmetics.controller.rebuildCatalog()
	local ctrl = Cosmetics.controller
	ctrl.items:WithFavoritesSuppressed(function() ctrl.catalog:Build() end)
	ctrl.items:SyncAll(true)
end

--- khook.lua:46010 -- a favorites-only change re-syncs and re-announces the
--- data, because it changes what a RANDOM draw is allowed to pick.
function Cosmetics.controller.setOnlyUseFavorites(itemName, cosmeticType, on)
	local changed = Cosmetics.onlyUseFavorites:Set(itemName, cosmeticType, on)
	if changed then
		Cosmetics.controller.items:SyncAll(false)
		Cosmetics.controller.items:RefreshDataIfEnabled()
		Cosmetics.controller.fireStoresUpdated()
	end
	return changed
end

function Cosmetics.controller.toggleFavorited(itemName, cosmeticName)

		not Cosmetics.favoritedIntent:Get(itemName, cosmeticName))
end

end

--- khook.lua:46162 `SetRuntimeEnabled` -- the master switch, and the only thing
--- that arms the data masker.
function Cosmetics.controller.setRuntimeEnabled(on)
	on = on and true or false
	local ctrl = Cosmetics.controller
	if ctrl.runtimeEnabled == on then return end
	ctrl.runtimeEnabled = on
	-- khook.lua:46167 -- unlocker controller, emotes, then the item store.

	Cosmetics.Emotes.setEnabled(on)

end

--- khook.lua:19029 `Apply` / 19044 `Revert` -- the FavoritedCosmetics field is
--- REPLACED, not merged: khook hands the game exactly the favorites it resolves.
Cosmetics.favoritedHookApplied = false

function Cosmetics.setFavoritedHookApplied(on)
	on = on and true or false
	if Cosmetics.favoritedHookApplied == on then return end
	Cosmetics.favoritedHookApplied = on
	if on then
		Rivals.DataHook.set("FavoritedCosmetics", function()
			return Cosmetics.favoritedResolver:GetByItem()
		end)
	else
		Rivals.DataHook.unset("FavoritedCosmetics")
	end
	Rivals.DataHook.trigger("FavoritedCosmetics")
end

--- khook.lua:46172 -- the default layer is the Unlocker, not the skin changer.
function Cosmetics.SetItemSelection(weapon, choice, key)

--============================================================================--
-- Snapshot export / import (khook.lua:45943 `_BuildSnapshotContext`,
-- 46025 `_ApplyImportedState`, 46050 `ExportState`, 46054 `ImportState`)
--============================================================================--
-- The two functions pin the schema between them: what the context carries is
-- what comes out, and what `_ApplyImportedState` reads is what goes in.

--- khook.lua:78198 -- the intent exports as a list of `{ item, priority,
--- selection }` records, so the layer keys survive a JSON round trip.
function Cosmetics.Intent:ExportState()
	local out = {}
	for itemName, state in pairs(self._states) do
		for priority, layer in pairs(state.layers) do
			if not Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty(layer) then
				table.insert(out, {
					item = itemName,
					priority = priority,
					selection = Cosmetics.Selection.cloneCosmeticsSelection(layer),
				})
			end
		end
	end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:78237 -- the active-layer set is imported alongside, because a
--- layer with selections but no activation is invisible.
function Cosmetics.Intent:ImportState(records, activeLayers)
	self._states = {}
	self._activeLayers = {}
	if typeof(activeLayers) == "table" then
		for key, on in pairs(activeLayers) do
			local priority = tonumber(key) or key
			self._activeLayers[priority] = on and true or nil
		end
	end
	if typeof(records) ~= "table" then return end
	for _, record in pairs(records) do
		if typeof(record) == "table" and typeof(record.selection) == "table"
			and record.item ~= nil then
			local priority = tonumber(record.priority)
			if priority ~= nil then
				local state = self:_EnsureState(record.item)
				state.layers[priority] =
					Cosmetics.Selection.cloneCosmeticsSelection(record.selection)
				Cosmetics.Layers.removeIfEmpty(state.layers, priority, Cosmetics.Selection.isEmpty)
			end
		end
	end
end

Cosmetics.Snapshot = { VERSION = 1 }

--- khook.lua:45943 -- everything the snapshot needs, gathered in one place.
function Cosmetics.Snapshot.buildContext()
	local ctrl = Cosmetics.controller
	return {
		catalog = ctrl.catalog,
		items = ctrl.items,
		favoritedIntent = Cosmetics.favoritedIntent,
		onlyUseFavoritesState = Cosmetics.onlyUseFavorites,
		emotesIntent = Cosmetics.Emotes.intent,
		rankCharm = Cosmetics.rankCharm,
		runtimeEnabled = ctrl.runtimeEnabled,
		unlockerSelectionPriority = Cosmetics.PRIORITY.Unlocker,
		skinChangerSelectionPriority = Cosmetics.PRIORITY.SkinChanger,
	}
end

--- khook.lua:46050 `ExportState`
function Cosmetics.Snapshot.export()
	local ctx = Cosmetics.Snapshot.buildContext()
	return {
		version = Cosmetics.Snapshot.VERSION,
		runtimeEnabled = ctx.runtimeEnabled,
		types = table.clone(ctx.catalog:GetIncludedTypes()),
		rarities = table.clone(ctx.catalog:GetIncludedRarities()),
		itemSelections = ctx.items._intent:ExportState(),
		unlockerEnabled = ctx.items:IsLayerActive(ctx.unlockerSelectionPriority),
		skinChangerEnabled = ctx.items:IsLayerActive(ctx.skinChangerSelectionPriority),
		onlyUseFavoritesSelections = ctx.onlyUseFavoritesState:ExportState(),
		favoritedCosmetics = ctx.favoritedIntent:ExportState(),
		emotes = ctx.emotesIntent:ExportState(),
		rankCharmOverride = table.clone(ctx.rankCharm:GetAllOverrides()),
	}
end

--- khook.lua:46055 `normalizeImport` -- its body is destroyed; the shape it has
--- to produce is exactly what `_ApplyImportedState` reads, so this validates
--- against that and reports the same `{ success, message }` pair.
function Cosmetics.Snapshot.normalizeImport(raw)
	if typeof(raw) ~= "table" then
		return { success = false, message = "snapshot is not a table" }
	end
	local function tbl(value) return typeof(value) == "table" and value or {} end
	return {
		success = true,
		message = "",
		state = {
			runtimeEnabled = raw.runtimeEnabled == true,
			types = tbl(raw.types),
			rarities = tbl(raw.rarities),
			itemSelections = tbl(raw.itemSelections),
			unlockerEnabled = raw.unlockerEnabled == true,
			skinChangerEnabled = raw.skinChangerEnabled == true,
			onlyUseFavoritesSelections = tbl(raw.onlyUseFavoritesSelections),
			favoritedCosmetics = tbl(raw.favoritedCosmetics),
			emotes = tbl(raw.emotes),
			rankCharmOverride = tbl(raw.rankCharmOverride),
		},
	}
end

--- khook.lua:45932 `_BuildImportedActiveLayers`
function Cosmetics.Snapshot.buildImportedActiveLayers(state)
	local out = {}
	if state.unlockerEnabled then out[Cosmetics.PRIORITY.Unlocker] = true end
	if state.skinChangerEnabled then out[Cosmetics.PRIORITY.SkinChanger] = true end
	return out
end

--- khook.lua:46025 `_ApplyImportedState`. The order matters: runtime off first
--- so nothing half-built reaches the game, the whole staging done with
--- favourites suppressed, then runtime restored last.
function Cosmetics.Snapshot.apply(state)
	local ctrl = Cosmetics.controller
	Cosmetics.isImportingState = true
	ctrl.setRuntimeEnabled(false)

	ctrl.items:WithFavoritesSuppressed(function()
		ctrl.catalog:SetIncludedRarities(state.rarities)
		ctrl.catalog:SetIncludedTypes(state.types)
		ctrl.catalog:Build()
		ctrl.items._intent:ImportState(state.itemSelections,
			Cosmetics.Snapshot.buildImportedActiveLayers(state))
		ctrl.items._resolver:InvalidateAll()
		Cosmetics.onlyUseFavorites:ImportState(state.onlyUseFavoritesSelections)
		Cosmetics.favoritedIntent:ImportState(state.favoritedCosmetics)
		Cosmetics.Emotes.intent:ImportState(state.emotes)
		Cosmetics.rankCharm:SetAllOverrides(state.rankCharmOverride)
		ctrl.items:RefreshImportedState(true)
	end)

	Cosmetics.isImportingState = false
	ctrl.setRuntimeEnabled(state.runtimeEnabled)
end

function Cosmetics.ExportState() return Cosmetics.Snapshot.export() end

--- khook.lua:46054
function Cosmetics.ImportState(raw)
	local normalized = Cosmetics.Snapshot.normalizeImport(raw)
	if not normalized.success then
		return { success = false, message = normalized.message }
	end
	Cosmetics.Snapshot.apply(normalized.state)
	Cosmetics.controller.fireStoresUpdated()
	return { success = true, message = "" }
end

-- khook keeps Cosmetics presets separate from the script-wide config.  The
-- snapshot schema above is the file payload, so loading a preset restores the
-- unlock filters, both active layers, selections, favorites and rank charms.
Cosmetics.Presets = {}
Cosmetics.Presets.Folder = ROOT_FOLDER .. "/cosmetics"

function Cosmetics.Presets.EnsureFolder()
	if not (isfolder and makefolder) then return false end
	if not isfolder(ROOT_FOLDER) then makefolder(ROOT_FOLDER) end
	if not isfolder(Cosmetics.Presets.Folder) then makefolder(Cosmetics.Presets.Folder) end
	return true
end

function Cosmetics.Presets.CleanName(name)
	name = tostring(name or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
	name = name:gsub('[<>:"/\\|%?%*]', "")
	return name
end

function Cosmetics.Presets.List()
	local out = {}
	if not (listfiles and Cosmetics.Presets.EnsureFolder()) then return out end
	for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(Cosmetics.Presets.Folder)) do
		local name = string.match(path, "([^/\\]+)%.json$")
		if name then table.insert(out, name) end
	end
	table.sort(out)
	return out
end

function Cosmetics.Presets.Save(name)
	name = Cosmetics.Presets.CleanName(name)
	if name == "" then return false, "invalid name" end
	if not (writefile and Cosmetics.Presets.EnsureFolder()) then return false, "no filesystem" end
	local ok, payload = pcall(function()
		return HttpService:JSONEncode(encodeValue(Cosmetics.ExportState()))
	end)
	if not ok then return false, tostring(payload) end
	local wrote, err = pcall(writefile, Cosmetics.Presets.Folder .. "/" .. name .. ".json", payload)
	if not wrote then return false, tostring(err) end
	Config.set({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" }, name)
	return true
end

function Cosmetics.Presets.Load(name)
	name = Cosmetics.Presets.CleanName(name)
	local path = Cosmetics.Presets.Folder .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
	if name == "" or not (readfile and isfile and isfile(path)) then return false, "not found" end
	local ok, state = pcall(function()
		return decodeValue(HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path)))
	end)
	if not ok then return false, tostring(state) end
	local result = Cosmetics.ImportState(state)
	if not result.success then return false, result.message end

	-- Keep the config-bound controls in lockstep with the imported snapshot.
	Config.set({ "Cosmetics", "Enabled" }, state.runtimeEnabled == true)
	Config.set({ "Cosmetics", "SkinChanger" }, state.skinChangerEnabled == true)
	Config.set({ "UnlockAll", "Enabled" }, state.unlockerEnabled == true)
	Config.set({ "UnlockAll", "Cosmetics" }, state.types or {})
	Config.set({ "UnlockAll", "Rarities" }, state.rarities or {})
	Config.set({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" }, name)
	return true
end

function Cosmetics.Presets.Delete(name)
	name = Cosmetics.Presets.CleanName(name)
	local path = Cosmetics.Presets.Folder .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
	if name == "" or not (delfile and isfile and isfile(path)) then return false end
	local ok = pcall(delfile, path)
	if ok and Config.get({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" }) == name then
		Config.set({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" }, "")
	end
	return ok
end

--- The data hook holds the only external state, so unloading is one call.
function Cosmetics.RestoreAll()
	Cosmetics.controller.items:SetEnabled(false)
	Cosmetics.setFavoritedHookApplied(false)
	Cosmetics.Emotes.setEnabled(false)
	Rivals.DataHook.destroy()
end
Cosmetics.Trove:Add(Cosmetics.RestoreAll)

--- Pushes the two Unlock All dropdowns into the catalogue and rebuilds. khook
--- does this from `SetUnlockedTypes` / `SetUnlockedRarities` (khook.lua:42634 /
--- 42650); an empty selection means "everything", which is how khook's
--- multi-dropdowns read when nothing is ticked.
function Cosmetics.ApplyFilters()
	local cfg = Config.node({ "UnlockAll" })
	local lib = Cosmetics.library
	local types, rarities = {}, {}
	local anyType = next(cfg.Cosmetics) == nil
	local anyRarity = next(cfg.Rarities) == nil

	if lib ~= nil and typeof(lib.Types) == "table" then
		for kind, info in pairs(lib.Types) do
			if typeof(info) ~= "table" or info.NotCosmetic ~= true then
				types[kind] = anyType or cfg.Cosmetics[kind] == true
			end
		end
	end
	if lib ~= nil and typeof(lib.Rarities) == "table" then
		for rarity in pairs(lib.Rarities) do
			rarities[rarity] = anyRarity or cfg.Rarities[rarity] == true
		end
	end

	Cosmetics.controller.catalog:SetIncludedTypes(types)
	Cosmetics.controller.catalog:SetIncludedRarities(rarities)
	Cosmetics.controller.rebuildCatalog()
	Cosmetics.controller.fireStoresUpdated()
end

Cosmetics.Refresh = Cosmetics.ApplyFilters
Cosmetics.Apply = Cosmetics.ApplyFilters
Cosmetics.ApplyAll = Cosmetics.ApplyFilters
Cosmetics.ApplyUnlocker = Cosmetics.ApplyFilters

function Cosmetics.init(trove)
	trove:Add(Cosmetics.Trove)
	local presetsReady = false
	local autoSaveQueued = false

	-- khook.lua:42660 -- "Cosmetics Enabled" is the master switch (SetRuntimeEnabled);
	-- the unlocker and skin changer are layers under it (khook.lua:46188/46197),
	-- and the two dropdowns filter the catalogue (khook.lua:45998/46004).
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Cosmetics", "Enabled" }, function(on)
		Cosmetics.SetRuntimeEnabled(on == true)
	end))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Cosmetics", "SkinChanger" }, function(on)
		Cosmetics.SetSkinChangerEnabled(on == true)
	end))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "UnlockAll", "Enabled" }, function(on)
		Cosmetics.SetUnlockerEnabled(on == true)
	end))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "UnlockAll", "Cosmetics" }, Cosmetics.ApplyFilters))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "UnlockAll", "Rarities" }, Cosmetics.ApplyFilters))
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(Cosmetics.Masker.invalidate))
	-- khook.lua:67486 -- sharing is gated on this toggle and nothing else.
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "UserServer", "ShareSkins" }, function(on)
		Cosmetics.Bridge.setShareActive(on == true)
	end))
	-- khook.lua:67472 -- a peer leaving drops their overrides.
	trove:Add(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(Cosmetics.Bridge.handlePeerRemoved))
	trove:Add(Cosmetics.changed:Connect(function()
		local pref = Config.node({ "Cosmetics", "Presets" })
		if not presetsReady or not pref.AutoSave or pref.AutoSaveName == "" or autoSaveQueued then return end
		autoSaveQueued = true
		task.defer(function()
			autoSaveQueued = false
			if presetsReady and pref.AutoSave and pref.AutoSaveName ~= "" then
				Cosmetics.Presets.Save(pref.AutoSaveName)
			end
		end)
	end))

	-- khook.lua:43854 -- a respawn rebuilds the view models, so the data has to
	-- be re-announced once they exist again.
	if LocalPlayer then
		trove:Add(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			task.delay(1.5, Cosmetics.Masker.refreshIfEnabled)
		end))
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		Resolver.await("ItemCatalog", 10)
		Cosmetics.RefreshCatalog()
		Cosmetics.ApplyFilters()
		local pref = Config.node({ "Cosmetics", "Presets" })
		if pref.AutoLoad and pref.AutoLoadName ~= "" then
			Cosmetics.Presets.Load(pref.AutoLoadName)
		end
		presetsReady = true
	end)

	Features.status.cosmetics = "ok"
end

--============================================================================--
-- ITEM MODIFIERS -- weapon stat patching
--============================================================================--
-- khook routes every stat change through an "info modifier" that holds a set
-- of named changes and rewrites the game's weapon stat block (khook.lua:134939
-- onward: SetChange("MeleeCooldown", { percentage = ... })). Same shape here,
-- over the stat table the resolver finds.

local InfoModifier = {}
InfoModifier.changes = {}     -- key -> { percentage = n } | { enabled = bool } | { count = n }
InfoModifier.originals = {}   -- weaponTable -> { field -> value }
InfoModifier.Trove = Trove.new("item_modifiers")

--- Which stat field each change maps onto. Several spellings are tried
--- because the field name is the one thing the decompile cannot tell us.
--- Real field names, read straight out of ReplicatedStorage.Modules
--- .ItemLibrary.Items in a live Rivals server. Guns and melee use different
--- schemas, so several changes map onto more than one field.
local FIELD_ALIASES = {
	Recoil        = { "ShootRecoil" },
	AimSpeed      = { "AimSpeed" },
	FireCooldown  = { "ShootCooldown", "BurstCooldown" },
	AimCooldown   = { "AimScopePercent", "AimFOVOffset" },
	MeleeCooldown = { "AttackCooldown", "AttackDelay", "ThrowCooldown" },
	DashCooldown  = { "EquipCooldown" },
	MeleeRange    = { "AttackReach", "AttackConeAngle" },
	Spread        = { "ShootSpread", "AimSpreadMultiplier", "ShootSpreadPerVelocityUnit" },
	Reload        = { "ReloadLength" },
	WalkSpeed     = { "WalkSpeedMultiplier" },
	AutomaticWeapon = { "InputSpammingEnabled" },
	MaxDoubleJumps  = { "MaxDoubleJumps" },
}

--- khook modifies the `Info` table on each live ClientItem, not the immutable
--- ItemLibrary catalogue (khook.lua:150243-150375). Re-resolving this small
--- map also naturally picks up weapons created after a respawn/loadout swap.
local function statTable()
	local items = Rivals.items()
	if type(items) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local infos = {}
	for slot, item in pairs(items) do
		local info = itemField(item, "Info")
		if type(info) == "table" then infos[slot] = info end
	end
	return infos
end

local function rememberField(tbl, field)
	local entry = InfoModifier.originals[tbl]
	if not entry then entry = {} InfoModifier.originals[tbl] = entry end
	if entry[field] == nil then entry[field] = tbl[field] end
end

local function restoreAll()
	for tbl, fields in pairs(InfoModifier.originals) do
		for field, value in pairs(fields) do
			pcall(function() tbl[field] = value end)
		end
	end
	table.clear(InfoModifier.originals)
end
InfoModifier.Trove:Add(restoreAll)

--- Walk every weapon's stat block and apply the current change set.
local function reapply()
	local root = statTable()
	if typeof(root) ~= "table" then return false end

	for _, block in pairs(root) do
		if typeof(block) == "table" then
			for key, change in pairs(InfoModifier.changes) do
				local aliases = FIELD_ALIASES[key]
				if aliases then
					for _, field in ipairs(aliases) do
						local current = rawget(block, field)
						if current ~= nil then
							rememberField(block, field)
							local base = InfoModifier.originals[block][field]
							if change == nil then
								block[field] = base
							elseif change.percentage and typeof(base) == "number" then
								block[field] = base * change.percentage
							elseif key == "AutomaticWeapon" and change.enabled then
								if type(base) == "table" then
									local spam = table.clone(base)
									spam.StartShooting = 0
									spam.StartReloading = 0
									block[field] = spam
								end
							elseif change.enabled ~= nil then
								block[field] = change.enabled
							elseif change.count then
								block[field] = change.count
							elseif change.value then
								block[field] = change.value
							end
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return true
end

function InfoModifier.SetChange(key, change)
	if change == nil then
		-- restore just this key's fields before dropping it
		InfoModifier.changes[key] = nil
		local aliases = FIELD_ALIASES[key]
		if aliases then
			for tbl, fields in pairs(InfoModifier.originals) do
				for _, field in ipairs(aliases) do
					if fields[field] ~= nil then
						pcall(function() tbl[field] = fields[field] end)
						fields[field] = nil
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return
	end
	InfoModifier.changes[key] = change
	reapply()
end

function Features.ItemModifiers(trove)
	trove:Add(InfoModifier.Trove)
	local cfg = Config.node({ "ItemModifiers" })
	local removables = Config.node({ "Removables" })

	------------------------------------------------------ no gun tracers ----
	-- khook.lua:83558-83638. Do not delete/hide anything named "tracer".
	-- Gun:_LocalTracers calls the `_Tracers` method through a string constant;
	-- khook redirects only that constant to an empty alias on the same class.
	-- This preserves every value `_LocalTracers` indexes and avoids the game's
	-- `attempt to index nil with ''` failure seen when tracer data is damaged.
	task.spawn(function()
		local gun
		local deadline = os.clock() + 12
		repeat
			local scripts = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
			local modules = scripts and scripts:FindFirstChild("Modules")
			local itemTypes = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("ItemTypes")
			local module = itemTypes and itemTypes:FindFirstChild("Gun")
			if module then
				local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
				if ok and type(value) == "table" then gun = value end
			end
			if gun == nil then task.wait(0.25) end
		until gun ~= nil or os.clock() >= deadline
		if gun == nil or dbg.getconstants == nil or dbg.setconstant == nil then
			markUnavailable("noguntracers", "Gun._LocalTracers constant access unavailable")
			return
		end

		local method = rawget(gun, "_LocalTracers")
		if type(method) ~= "function" then
			local mt = getmetatable(gun)
			local index = type(mt) == "table" and rawget(mt, "__index") or nil
			method = type(index) == "table" and rawget(index, "_LocalTracers") or nil
			if type(method) == "function" then gun = index end
		end
		if type(method) ~= "function" then
			markUnavailable("noguntracers", "Gun._LocalTracers not found")
			return
		end

		local alias = "_Tracers\0NoGunTracers"
		local restore
		local function disable()
			if restore == nil then return end
			pcall(dbg.setconstant, restore.method, restore.index, restore.value)
			rawset(gun, alias, nil)
			restore = nil
		end
		local function enable()
			if restore ~= nil then return end
			local ok, constants = pcall(dbg.getconstants, method)
			if not ok or type(constants) ~= "table" then return end
			for index, value in pairs(constants) do
				if value == "_Tracers" then
					rawset(gun, alias, function() end)
					local applied = pcall(dbg.setconstant, method, index, alias)
					if applied then
						restore = { method = method, index = index, value = value }
						Features.status.noguntracers = "ok"
					else
						rawset(gun, alias, nil)
					end
					break
				end
			end
		end
		local function sync(on)
			if on then enable() else disable() end
		end
		trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Removables", "NoGunTracers" }, sync))
		trove:Add(disable)
	end)

	--- percentage-style change bound to a { Enabled, Percentage } node
	local function bindPercent(key, node)
		local function push()
			InfoModifier.SetChange(key, node.Enabled and { percentage = node.Percentage / 100 } or nil)
		end
		trove:Add(Config.watch({ "ItemModifiers", key, "Enabled" }, push))
		trove:Add(Config.watch({ "ItemModifiers", key, "Percentage" }, push, true))
	end

	bindPercent("Recoil", cfg.Recoil)
	bindPercent("AimSpeed", cfg.AimSpeed)
	bindPercent("FireCooldown", cfg.FireCooldown)
	bindPercent("AimCooldown", cfg.AimCooldown)
	bindPercent("MeleeCooldown", cfg.MeleeCooldown)
	bindPercent("DashCooldown", cfg.DashCooldown)

	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "ItemModifiers", "AutomaticWeapon" }, function(on)
		InfoModifier.SetChange("AutomaticWeapon", on and { enabled = true } or nil)
	end))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "ItemModifiers", "InfiniteDoubleJumps" }, function(on)
		InfoModifier.SetChange("MaxDoubleJumps", on and { count = 676767 } or nil)
	end))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "ItemModifiers", "NoSpread" }, function(on)
		InfoModifier.SetChange("Spread", on and { value = 0 } or nil)
	end))
	local function pushRange()
		InfoModifier.SetChange("MeleeRange",
			cfg.ExtendMeleeRange.Enabled and { value = cfg.ExtendMeleeRange.Range } or nil)
	end
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "ItemModifiers", "ExtendMeleeRange", "Enabled" }, pushRange))
	trove:Add(Config.watch({ "ItemModifiers", "ExtendMeleeRange", "Range" }, pushRange, true))

	--------------------------------------------------- animation disablers --
	-- These are character-level, so they work without the stat table.
	local stoppedTracks = {}
	local ANIMATION_FLAGS = {
		NoShootAnimation  = { "shoot", "fire", "attack" },
		NoSprintAnimation = { "sprint", "run" },
		NoEquipAnimation  = { "equip", "draw" },
		NoReloadAnimation = { "reload" },
	}

	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local hum = localHumanoid()
		if not hum then return end
		local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
		if not animator then return end
		for flag, needles in pairs(ANIMATION_FLAGS) do
			if cfg[flag] then
				for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					local name = string.lower(track.Name)
					for _, needle in ipairs(needles) do
						if string.find(name, needle) then
							track:Stop(0)
							stoppedTracks[track] = true
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if cfg.NoMotion then
			local root = localRoot()
			if root then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
		end
	end))

	------------------------------------------------ camera shake and sway ---
	local savedShake
	trove:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if cfg.NoCameraShake or cfg.NoCameraSway then
			local fighter = Resolver.get("ClientFighter")
			if fighter then
				pcall(function()
					if cfg.NoCameraShake then fighter.ShakeIntensity = 0 end
					if cfg.NoCameraSway then fighter.SwayIntensity = 0 end
				end)
			end
		end
	end))
	local _ = savedShake

	-------------------------------------------------------- grenade fuse ----
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local fuse = cfg.GrenadeFuse
		if not fuse.Enabled then return end
		local char = localCharacter()
		if not char then return end
		for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") and child:GetAttribute("Fuse") ~= nil then
				if fuse.RemoveFuse then
					child:SetAttribute("Fuse", 0)
				elseif fuse.ExplodeOn == "Never" then
					child:SetAttribute("Fuse", math.huge)
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	------------------------------------------------------------ removables --
