	-- Scope and hitmarker are class hooks in khook, not name-based GUI scans
	-- (khook.lua:23180-23308, 38690-38820). Patch the same exact Rivals
	-- methods so newly-created weapon interfaces inherit the override too.
	do
		local scripts = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
		local modules = scripts and scripts:FindFirstChild("Modules")
		local scopeModule = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("ClientReplicatedClasses")
		if scopeModule then
			scopeModule = scopeModule:FindFirstChild("ClientFighter")
			scopeModule = scopeModule and scopeModule:FindFirstChild("ClientItem")
			scopeModule = scopeModule and scopeModule:FindFirstChild("ItemInterface")
			scopeModule = scopeModule and scopeModule:FindFirstChild("Mouse")
			scopeModule = scopeModule and scopeModule:FindFirstChild("Scope")
		end
		local crosshairModule = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Crosshair")
		local okScope, scopeClass = pcall(require, scopeModule)
		local okCrosshair, crosshairClass = pcall(require, crosshairModule)
		if okScope and type(scopeClass) == "table" and type(scopeClass.SetActive) == "function" then
			local originalSetActive = scopeClass.SetActive
			local function applyScope(instance)
				if rawget(instance, "_is_scope_active") ~= true then return end
				local overlayVisible = not removables.NoScopeOverlay
				local reticleVisible = not removables.NoScopeReticle
				local circle, blur, reticle = rawget(instance, "CircleFrame"),
					rawget(instance, "BlurFrame"), rawget(instance, "ReticleContainer")
				if circle then circle.Visible = overlayVisible end
				if blur then blur.Visible = overlayVisible end
				if reticle then reticle.Visible = reticleVisible end
			end
			local function eachScope(callback)
				if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return end
				for _, object in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
					if type(object) == "table" and getmetatable(object) == scopeClass then pcall(callback, object) end
				end
			end
			scopeClass.SetActive = function(instance, active)
				local result = table.pack(originalSetActive(instance, active))
				applyScope(instance)
				return table.unpack(result, 1, result.n)
			end
			local function refreshScopes()
				eachScope(function(instance)
					originalSetActive(instance, rawget(instance, "_is_scope_active") == true)
					applyScope(instance)
				end)
			end
			trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Removables", "NoScopeOverlay" }, refreshScopes))
			trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Removables", "NoScopeReticle" }, refreshScopes, true))
			trove:Add(function()
				scopeClass.SetActive = originalSetActive
				eachScope(function(instance)
					originalSetActive(instance, rawget(instance, "_is_scope_active") == true)
				end)
			end)
		end
		if okCrosshair and type(crosshairClass) == "table" and type(crosshairClass._Update) == "function" then
			local originalUpdate = crosshairClass._Update
			local function applyHitmarker(instance)
				local frame = rawget(instance, "Frame")
				local marker = frame and frame:FindFirstChild("Hitmarker")
				if marker and removables.NoHitmarker then marker.Visible = false end
			end
			local function eachCrosshair(callback)
				if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return end
				for _, object in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
					if type(object) == "table" and getmetatable(object) == crosshairClass then pcall(callback, object) end
				end
			end
			crosshairClass._Update = function(instance, ...)
				local result = table.pack(originalUpdate(instance, ...))
				applyHitmarker(instance)
				return table.unpack(result, 1, result.n)
			end
			local function refreshHitmarkers()
				eachCrosshair(function(instance)
					pcall(originalUpdate, instance)
					applyHitmarker(instance)
				end)
			end
			trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Removables", "NoHitmarker" }, refreshHitmarkers))
			trove:Add(function()
				crosshairClass._Update = originalUpdate
				eachCrosshair(function(instance) pcall(originalUpdate, instance) end)
			end)
		end
	end

	-- Flash/burn/muzzle layers still need a fallback for executor builds where
	-- the game's replicated-effect reservation surface is unavailable.
	local hidden = {}
	local PATTERNS = {
		NoFlashbang    = { "flash" },
		NoBurnEffect   = { "burn", "fire" },
		NoAdsVignette  = { "vignette", "ads" },
		NoMuzzleFlash  = { "muzzle" },
	}

	local function sweepRemovables()
		local pg = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		if not pg then return end
		for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
			if gui:IsA("GuiObject") then
				local lower = string.lower(gui.Name)
				for flag, needles in pairs(PATTERNS) do
					for _, needle in ipairs(needles) do
						if string.find(lower, needle) then
							if removables[flag] then
								if hidden[gui] == nil then hidden[gui] = gui.Visible end
								if gui.Visible then gui.Visible = false end
							elseif hidden[gui] ~= nil then
								gui.Visible = hidden[gui]
								hidden[gui] = nil
							end
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- once on change, plus a slow sweep for layers created later; never per frame
	for flag in pairs(PATTERNS) do
		trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Removables", flag }, sweepRemovables))
	end
	trove:Add(task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(2) do sweepRemovables() end
	end))
	trove:Add(function()
		for gui, visible in pairs(hidden) do
			if gui.Parent then gui.Visible = visible end
		end
	end)

	-- A new ClientItem gets a fresh Info table. khook observes the fighter's
	-- item-added signal; a throttled refresh is equivalent here and remains
	-- inert while no modifier is enabled.
	local refreshClock = 0
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		refreshClock += dt
		if refreshClock < 0.5 then return end
		refreshClock = 0
		if next(InfoModifier.changes) ~= nil then reapply() end
	end))
	if statTable() ~= nil then
		reapply()
		Features.status.itemmodifiers = "ok"
	else
		-- The fighter may not exist during bootstrap; the refresh above applies
		-- changes as soon as its live items are constructed.
		Features.status.itemmodifiers = "waiting"
	end
end

--============================================================================--
-- MATCH AUTOMATION
--============================================================================--
-- Queue, vote, ban, loadout, pickups and tripmines all go through remotes.
-- The names are discovered by scanning ReplicatedStorage once, then cached.

local Remotes = { cache = {}, scanned = false }

--- Exact paths under ReplicatedStorage.Remotes, enumerated from a live
--- server. No name guessing: if a path is missing the feature reports itself
--- unavailable instead of firing something unrelated.
local REMOTE_PATHS = {
	Respawn      = { "Duels", "RespawnNow" },
	Vote         = { "Duels", "Vote" },
	SwitchItems  = { "Duels", "SwitchItems" },
	PickWeapons  = { "Duels", "PickWeaponsAheadOfTime" },
	Rematch      = { "Duels", "Rematch" },
	LeaveDuel    = { "Duels", "LeaveDuel" },
	SwitchTeam   = { "Duels", "SwitchTeam" },
	JoinQueue    = { "Matchmaking", "JoinQueue" },
	LeaveQueue   = { "Matchmaking", "TryLeaveQueue" },
	PlayAgain    = { "Matchmaking", "PlayAgain" },
	BackToHub    = { "Matchmaking", "BackToHub" },
	QueueJoin    = { "Misc", "QueueJoin" },
	QueueLeave   = { "Misc", "QueueLeave" },
	QueueReady   = { "Misc", "QueueReady" },
	TrashItems   = { "Misc", "ShootingRangeTrashItems" },
	UseItem      = { "Replication", "Fighter", "UseItem" },
	PickFighter  = { "Replication", "Fighter", "PickWeapons" },
	ResetChar    = { "Replication", "Fighter", "ResetCharacter" },
	EquipCosmetic= { "Data", "EquipCosmetic" },
	UnlockWeapon = { "Data", "UnlockWeapon" },
	RequestData  = { "Data", "RequestPlayerData" },
}

function Remotes.scan()
	if Remotes.scanned then return end
	Remotes.scanned = true
	local root = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	if not root then return end
	for key, path in pairs(REMOTE_PATHS) do
		local node = root
		for _, step in ipairs(path) do
			node = node and node:FindFirstChild(step)
		end
		if node and (node:IsA("RemoteEvent") or node:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then
			Remotes.cache[key] = node
		end
	end
end

function Remotes.fire(key, ...)
	Remotes.scan()
	local remote = Remotes.cache[key]
	if not remote then return false, "remote not found: " .. key end
	local ok, err = pcall(function(...)
		if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			remote:FireServer(...)
		else
			return remote:InvokeServer(...)
		end
	end, ...)
	return ok, err
end

function Features.MatchAutomation(trove)
	local misc = Config.node({ "Misc" })
	Remotes.scan()

	------------------------------------------------------- auto respawn ----
	local respawning = false
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not misc.AutoRespawn.Enabled or respawning then return end
		local hum = localHumanoid()
		if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then
			respawning = true
			task.delay(1.5, function()
				Remotes.fire("Respawn")
				task.wait(2)
				respawning = false
			end)
		end
	end))

	--------------------------------------------------------- auto queue ----
	trove:Add(task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(3) do
			if misc.AutoQueue.Enabled then
				local inMatch = Registry.localRecord and Registry.localRecord.Alive
				if not inMatch then
					Remotes.fire("JoinQueue", misc.AutoQueue.Queue)
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	---------------------------------------------------------- auto vote ----
	-- Votes come up as a prompt; watch for the UI/attribute that signals one
	-- and answer with the highest-priority selected map.
	trove:Add(task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(1) do
			if misc.AutoVote.Enabled then
				local maps = misc.AutoVote.Maps
				if next(maps) ~= nil then
					for map, on in pairs(maps) do
						if on then
							Remotes.fire("Vote", map)
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	----------------------------------------------------------- auto ban ----
	local bannedThisRound = false
	trove:Add(task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(2) do
			if misc.AutoBan.Enabled and not bannedThisRound then
				for weapon, on in pairs(misc.AutoBan.FirstWeapons) do
					if on then
						local ok = Remotes.fire("PickWeapons", weapon)
						if ok then bannedThisRound = true end
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end))
	if LocalPlayer then
		trove:Add(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			bannedThisRound = false
		end))
	end

	------------------------------------------------------- auto loadout ----
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() end))
	if LocalPlayer then
		trove:Add(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			if not misc.AutoLoadout.Enabled then return end
			task.delay(1, function()
				local loadouts = misc.AutoLoadout.Loadouts
				for slot, weapon in pairs(loadouts) do
					Remotes.fire("SwitchItems", slot, weapon)
				end
			end)
		end))
	end

	-------------------------------------------------------- auto pickup ----
	-- Drops are parts in the workspace; instead of scanning every frame this
	-- listens for additions and keeps a small live set.
	local drops = {}
	local function considerDrop(inst)
		if inst:IsA("BasePart") and (inst.Name == "_drop" or inst:GetAttribute("DropType")) then
			drops[inst] = inst:GetAttribute("DropType") or "Ammo"
		end
	end
	for _, inst in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do considerDrop(inst) end
	trove:Add(Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(considerDrop))
	trove:Add(Workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(inst) drops[inst] = nil end))

	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not misc.AutoPickupDrops.Enabled then return end
		local root = localRoot()
		if not root then return end
		local wanted = misc.AutoPickupDrops.Types
		local anyType = next(wanted) == nil
		for part, kind in pairs(drops) do
			if not part.Parent then
				drops[part] = nil
			elseif anyType or wanted[kind] then
				if (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 60 then
					if firetouchinterest then
						pcall(firetouchinterest, root, part, 0)
						pcall(firetouchinterest, root, part, 1)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	------------------------------------------------------- tripmines -------
	trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local mines = misc.SubspaceTripmines
		if not mines.AutoTrigger and not mines.TeamTrigger then return end
		local root = localRoot()
		if not root then return end
		for _, inst in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if inst:IsA("Model") and string.find(string.lower(inst.Name), "tripmine") then
				local owner = inst:GetAttribute("Owner")
				local isTeam = owner and LocalPlayer and owner == LocalPlayer.Name
				if (mines.AutoTrigger and not isTeam) or (mines.TeamTrigger and isTeam) then
					local part = inst.PrimaryPart or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
					if part and firetouchinterest then
						pcall(firetouchinterest, root, part, 0)
						pcall(firetouchinterest, root, part, 1)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	local found = 0
	for _ in pairs(Remotes.cache) do found += 1 end
	Features.status.match = found > 0
		and ("ok (" .. found .. " remotes bound)")
		or "unavailable: no matching remotes in this place"
end
--============================================================================--
-- 9. MENU BUILD
--============================================================================--
-- Same tab tree, same section titles and the same option set khook exposes.

Features.binds = {}

--- All menu construction hangs off one table: thirteen top-level locals
--- would otherwise eat into Lua's 200-per-function limit for no benefit.
local Build = {}

function Build.bindKeys(path)
	return {
		Key        = { path[1], path[2], "Keybind", "Bind" },
		Mode       = { path[1], path[2], "Keybind", "Kind" },
		ShowInList = { path[1], path[2], "Keybind", "ShowInList" },
		Invisible  = { path[1], path[2], "Keybind", "Invisible" },
	}
end

function Build.rootBindKeys(name)
	return {
		Key        = { name, "Keybind", "Bind" },
		Mode       = { name, "Keybind", "Kind" },
		ShowInList = { name, "Keybind", "ShowInList" },
		Invisible  = { name, "Keybind", "Invisible" },
	}
end

--- The Main section shared by Aimbot / Silent Aim / Triggerbot: master
--- toggle + bind, then the timing options.
function Build.aimMainSection(grid, name, label, opts)
	local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Main", Side = "left" })
	local toggle, bind = sec:AddFeature({
		Label = label,
		Tooltip = opts.Tooltip,
		Config = { name, "Enabled" },
		Keybind = Build.rootBindKeys(name),
		ListLabel = label,
	})
	Features.binds[name] = bind
	return sec, toggle
end

--- Target-selection block. Identical shape for every aim feature.
function Build.targetSection(grid, name, side, existing)
	local sec = existing or grid:AddSection({ Title = "Target", Side = side or "right" })

	-- khook.lua:17834 `f1315` -- the four conditions are ONE multi-dropdown
	-- with display labels, not four toggles.
	sec:AddMultiDropdown({
		Label = "Target Conditions",
		Options = { "Visible", "Vulnerable", "NotDeflecting", "NotShielded" },
		Labels = { NotDeflecting = "Not Deflecting", NotShielded = "Not Shielded" },
		Config = { name, "Target", "Conditions" },
	})

	-- khook.lua:17843 `f1316` -- two options, and Reduction/Center belong to
	-- the Surface branch rather than sitting loose.
	local ttype = sec:AddDropdown({
		Label = "Target Type",
		Options = { "Center", "Surface" },
		Config = { name, "Target", "Type" },
	})
	local surfaceGroup = sec:AddGroup({ Source = ttype, Option = "Surface" })
	surfaceGroup:AddSlider({
		Label = "Reduction (%)", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 0.01,
		Config = { name, "Target", "Surface", "ReductionPercentage" },
	})
	surfaceGroup:AddToggle({
		Label = "Center", Config = { name, "Target", "Surface", "Center" },
	})

	-- khook.lua:17868 `f1317` -- Closest or Random, and Head (%) only matters
	-- to Random.
	local partSel = sec:AddDropdown({
		Label = "Part Selection",
		Options = { "Closest", "Random" },
		Config = { name, "Target", "PartSelection", "Type" },
	})
	sec:AddGroup({ Source = partSel, Option = "Random" }):AddSlider({
		Label = "Head (%)", Min = 0, Max = 100,
		Config = { name, "Target", "PartSelection", "HeadPercent" },
	})

	sec:AddDivider()

	-- khook.lua:17394 `f1267` -- two multi-dropdowns over the hitbox profile's
	-- AvailableOptions, not four toggles. The stored value is a set of part
	-- names, which is what the selector and the triggerbot both key on.
	for _, group in ipairs({ "Ground", "Air" }) do
		sec:AddMultiDropdown({
			Label = (group == "Ground") and "Target Parts On Ground" or "Target Parts In Air",
			Options = table.clone(HitboxProfiles.DEFAULT.AvailableOptions),
			Config = { name, "Target", "TargetParts", group },
		})
	end

	sec:AddDivider()

	return sec
end

--- khook.lua:67811 `f4855` -- the layout every aim page shares: Target and FOV
--- are two tabs of one boxed MultiSection on the right, and Per Weapon Settings
--- sits on the left. khookclone had them as two loose sections and no overlay
--- editor at all.
function Build.aimTargetLayout(grid, name)
	local targetSec, fovSec = grid:AddMultiSection({ Titles = { "Target", "FOV" }, Side = "right" })
	Build.targetSection(grid, name, nil, targetSec)
	Build.fovSection(grid, name, nil, fovSec)
	Build.perWeaponSection(grid, name, "left")
	return targetSec, fovSec
end

--- Every base weapon Rivals ships, in the game's own order. `ItemLibrary`
--- exposes this as `ItemOrder` (index -> name); skins live in `ViewModels` and
--- carry no back-reference, so the base names are what the overlay keys on.
function Build.allWeapons()
	if Build._weapons then return Build._weapons end
	local names = {}
	local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	local module = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary")
	if module then
		local ok, lib = pcall(require, module)
		if ok and typeof(lib) == "table" and typeof(lib.ItemOrder) == "table" then
			for i = 1, 128 do
				local n = lib.ItemOrder[i]
				if type(n) == "string" and n ~= "MISSING_WEAPON" then table.insert(names, n) end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(names)
	Build._weapons = names
	return names
end

--- khook.lua:125674 `f8500`. A weapon picker plus an Active toggle: choosing a
--- weapon points the Target section at that weapon's overlay, "General" points
--- it back at the shared block. Active is what decides whether the overlay is
--- consulted at all.
function Build.perWeaponSection(grid, name, side, basePath)
	local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Per Weapon Settings", Side = side or "left" })

	local options = { "General" }
	for _, w in ipairs(Build.allWeapons()) do table.insert(options, w) end

	local master = sec:AddToggle({
		Label = "Gun Specific", Config = { name, "GunSpecific", "Enabled" },
	})
	local picker = sec:AddDropdown({
		Row = master.Row, Options = options, Default = "General",
	})
	local active = sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Active" })

	local function refresh()
		local weapon = picker.Value
		if weapon == nil or weapon == "General" then
			active:Set(false, true)
			Config.setEditProfile(name, nil)
			return
		end
		local profile = Config.get({ "GunSpecific", weapon })
		local activeMap = type(profile) == "table" and profile.Active or nil
		active:Set(type(activeMap) == "table" and activeMap[name] == true, true)
		Config.setEditProfile(name, weapon)
	end

	picker:Connect(picker.ValueChanged, refresh)
	active:Connect(active.ValueChanged, function(on)
		local weapon = picker.Value
		if weapon == nil or weapon == "General" then
			if on then active:Set(false, true) end
			return
		end
		Config.set({ "GunSpecific", weapon, "Active", name }, on)
		-- Active changes whether the edit view resolves to the overlay or General.
		Config.editProfileChanged:Fire(name, weapon)
	end)
	master:Connect(master.ValueChanged, function()
		Config.editProfileChanged:Fire(name, Config._editProfiles[name])
	end)
	sec._trove:Add(function() Config.setEditProfile(name, nil) end)

	refresh()
	return sec
end

--- FOV circle block.
function Build.fovSection(grid, name, side, existing)
	local sec = existing or grid:AddSection({ Title = "FOV", Side = side or "left" })
	local enabled = sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Use FOV", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Enabled" } })
	local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = enabled })

	local kind = g:AddDropdown({
		Label = "Kind", Options = { "Circle", "Custom" },
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Kind" },
	})
	local circleGroup = g:AddGroup({ Source = kind, Option = "Circle" })
	local vis = circleGroup:AddToggle({
		Label = "Visible", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Visible" },
	})
	circleGroup:AddColor({
		Row = vis.Row, Gradient = true, Alpha = true,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Stroke", "Gradient" },
		AlphaConfig = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Stroke", "Transparency" },
	})
	circleGroup:AddSlider({
		Label = "Radius", Min = 1, Max = 400, Step = 1,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Radius" },
	})
	circleGroup:AddSlider({
		Label = "Thickness", Min = 0, Max = 25, Step = 1,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Stroke", "Thickness" },
	})
	circleGroup:AddToggle({ Label = "Follow Target", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "FollowTarget" } })
	circleGroup:AddToggle({ Label = "From Muzzle", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "FromMuzzle" } })
	local fill = circleGroup:AddToggle({
		Label = "Filled", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Fill", "Enabled" },
	})
	circleGroup:AddColor({
		Row = fill.Row, Gradient = true, Alpha = true,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Fill", "Gradient" },
		AlphaConfig = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Fill", "Transparency" },
	})
	local anim = circleGroup:AddToggle({
		Label = "Animated", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Animated", "Enabled" },
	})
	circleGroup:AddGroup({ Source = anim }):AddSlider({
		Label = "Speed (deg/s)", Min = 0, Max = 360, Step = 1,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Circle", "Animated", "Speed" },
	})

	local customGroup = g:AddGroup({ Source = kind, Option = "Custom" })
	customGroup:AddTextBox({ Label = "Asset ID", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Custom", "AssetId" } })
	customGroup:AddToggle({ Label = "Visible", Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Custom", "Visible" } })
	customGroup:AddSlider({
		Label = "Scale Factor", Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.1,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Custom", "ScaleFactor" },
	})
	customGroup:AddSlider({
		Label = "Rotation", Min = 0, Max = 360, Step = 1,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Custom", "Rotation" },
	})
	local color = customGroup:AddLabel({ Label = "Color" })
	customGroup:AddColor({
		Row = color.Row, Alpha = true,
		Config = { name, "FovSettings", "Custom", "Color" },
		AlphaConfig = { name, "FovSettings", "Custom", "Transparency" },
	})
	return sec
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.Combat(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "Combat",
		Icon = Icons.Combat,
		Description = "Aim assistance, target lock, flick, and ragebot.",
	})

	-- Body deferred: nothing in this tab is constructed until the tab
	-- is first opened, so start-up builds one page instead of eight.
	tab:OnFirstOpen(function()

		------------------------------------------------------------- Aimbot ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Aimbot", Icon = Icons.Aimbot,
				Description = "Camera-based aim assistance.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local main = Build.aimMainSection(grid, "Aimbot", "Aimbot", {
				Tooltip = "Moves your camera toward the selected target.",
			})
			main:AddToggle({ Label = "Disable On Reload",     Config = { "Aimbot", "DisableOnReload" } })
			main:AddToggle({ Label = "Enable Only On Scope",  Config = { "Aimbot", "EnableOnlyOnScope" } })
			main:AddSlider({ Label = "Switch Delay (s)",  Min = 0, Max = 2, Step = 0.01, Config = { "Aimbot", "SwitchDelay" } })
			main:AddSlider({ Label = "Reaction Time (s)", Min = 0, Max = 2, Step = 0.01, Config = { "Aimbot", "ReactionTime" } })

			-- khook.lua:149652-149692. Three options in this order, one Speed
			-- slider per mode group, and no Smoothing slider: that field is
			-- legacy, folded into Speed by khook's own config migration
			-- (khook.lua:27567) and then deleted. SigmaMouse exists in the
			-- dispatch but khook does not surface it here either.
			local kind = main:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Aimbot Type",
				Options = { "Linear", "AI", "Spring" },
				Config = { "Aimbot", "Mode", "Type" },
			})
			for _, mode in ipairs({ "Linear", "AI", "Spring" }) do
				main:AddGroup({ Source = kind, Option = mode }):AddSlider({
					Label = "Speed", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01,
					Config = { "Aimbot", "Mode", "Speed" },
				})
			end

			-- Target Lock lives on the Aimbot page in khook
			local lockSec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Target Lock", Side = "right" })
			local lockToggle = lockSec:AddToggle({ Label = "Target Lock", Config = { "TargetLock", "Enabled" } })
			lockSec:AddKeybind({
				Row = lockToggle.Row, ListLabel = "Target Lock", InKeybindList = true,
				Default = { Key = Enum.KeyCode.Y, Mode = "Tap" },
				ConfigMap = {
					Key = { "TargetLock", "Bind" },
					ShowInList = { "TargetLock", "BindShowInList" },
					Invisible = { "TargetLock", "BindInvisible" },
				},
			})
			local lockGroup = lockSec:AddGroup({ Source = lockToggle })
			lockGroup:AddToggle({ Label = "Lock Only", Config = { "TargetLock", "LockOnly" },
				Tooltip = "Do not acquire a fallback target while nothing is locked." })
			lockGroup:AddDropdown({ Label = "Mode", Options = { "Lock", "Unlock" }, Config = { "TargetLock", "Mode" } })

			Build.aimTargetLayout(grid, "Aimbot")
		end

		---------------------------------------------------------- Silent Aim ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Silent Aim", Icon = Icons.SilentAim,
				Description = "Redirect projectiles toward targets without moving the camera.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local main = Build.aimMainSection(grid, "SilentAim", "Silent Aim", {
				Tooltip = "Rewrites the shot's raycast instead of your camera.",
			})
			main:AddSlider({ Label = "Hit Chance (%)",    Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "SilentAim", "ActivationChance" } })
			main:AddSlider({ Label = "Reaction Time (s)", Min = 0, Max = 2, Step = 0.01, Config = { "SilentAim", "ReactionTime" } })
			main:AddSlider({ Label = "Switch Delay (s)",  Min = 0, Max = 2, Step = 0.01, Config = { "SilentAim", "SwitchDelay" } })

			Build.aimTargetLayout(grid, "SilentAim")
		end

		----------------------------------------------------------- Flickbot ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Flickbot", Icon = Icons.Flickbot,
				Description = "Snap to targets for quick burst plays.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Main", Side = "left" })
			local toggle, bind = sec:AddFeature({
				Label = "Flickbot",
				Config = { "Flickbot", "Enabled" },
				Keybind = Build.rootBindKeys("Flickbot"),
				KeybindMode = "Hold",
				ListLabel = "Flickbot",
			})
			Features.binds.Flickbot = bind

			local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = toggle })
			g:AddToggle({ Label = "Shoot", Config = { "Flickbot", "Shoot" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Shot Delay (ms)",   Min = 0, Max = 500, Step = 1, Config = { "Flickbot", "ShotDelay" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Cooldown (ms)",     Min = 0, Max = 2000, Step = 10, Config = { "Flickbot", "Cooldown" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Flick Duration (ms)", Min = 10, Max = 600, Step = 5, Config = { "Flickbot", "FlickDuration" } })

			local tuning = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Tuning", Side = "right" })
			tuning:AddSlider({ Label = "Curvature", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "Flickbot", "Curvature" },
				Tooltip = "How much the flick path arcs instead of going straight." })
			tuning:AddSlider({ Label = "Humanness", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "Flickbot", "Humanness" },
				Tooltip = "Jitter added to the path so the motion is not perfectly clean." })
		end

		--------------------------------------------------------- Triggerbot ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Triggerbot", Icon = Icons.Triggerbot,
				Description = "Auto-fire when hitboxes align with your crosshair.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Main", Side = "left" })
			local toggle, bind = sec:AddFeature({
				Label = "Triggerbot",
				Config = { "Triggerbot", "Enabled" },
				Keybind = Build.rootBindKeys("Triggerbot"),
				ListLabel = "Triggerbot",
			})
			Features.binds.Triggerbot = bind

			local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = toggle })
			g:AddToggle({ Label = "Disable On Reload",  Config = { "Triggerbot", "DisableOnReload" } })
			g:AddToggle({ Label = "Activate On Scope",  Config = { "Triggerbot", "ActivateOnScope" } })
			-- khook.lua:9327-9349 -- these read "Not Deflecting"/"Not Shielded",
			-- and both timers run to 2s, not 1.
			g:AddToggle({ Label = "Not Deflecting",     Config = { "Triggerbot", "NotDeflecting" } })
			g:AddToggle({ Label = "Not Shielded",       Config = { "Triggerbot", "NotShielded" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Reaction Time (s)",  Min = 0, Max = 2, Step = 0.01, Config = { "Triggerbot", "ReactionTime" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Release Time (s)",   Min = 0, Max = 2, Step = 0.01, Config = { "Triggerbot", "ReleaseTime" } })

			-- khook.lua:9352 `f667` -- Target on the right, Per Weapon Settings
			-- on the left, the same pair the aim pages get.
			local parts = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Target", Side = "right" })
			for _, group in ipairs({ "Ground", "Air" }) do
				parts:AddMultiDropdown({
					Label = (group == "Ground") and "Target Parts On Ground" or "Target Parts In Air",
					Options = table.clone(HitboxProfiles.DEFAULT.AvailableOptions),
					Config = { "Triggerbot", "TargetParts", group },
				})
			end
			Build.perWeaponSection(grid, "Triggerbot", "left", { "Triggerbot" })
		end

		------------------------------------------------------------ Ragebot ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Ragebot", Icon = Icons.Ragebot,
				Description = "Aggressive targeting, weapon priority, and evasive movement.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			-- khook.lua:43983 `f3183` -- the whole page is two sections,
			-- "General" on the left and "Strategy" on the right, not four.
			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "General", Side = "left" })
			local toggle, bind = sec:AddFeature({
				Label = "Ragebot",
				Config = { "Ragebot", "Enabled" },
				Keybind = Build.rootBindKeys("Ragebot"),
				ListLabel = "Ragebot",
			})
			Features.binds.Ragebot = bind

			-- khook.lua:43937-43950 -- Prioritize Hackers first, then Stability
			-- 0..1.5 in thousandths, then Shoot Frames 1..5.
			sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Prioritize Hackers", Config = { "Ragebot", "PrioritizeHackers" } })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "Stability",    Min = 0, Max = 1.5, Step = 0.001, Config = { "Ragebot", "Stability" } })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "Shoot Frames", Min = 1, Max = 5, Step = 1,       Config = { "Ragebot", "ShootFrames" } })

			-- khook.lua:43953 `f3181` -- a labelled row whose gear holds the
			-- per-class enable toggles, then the ranked list itself, then
			-- On Empty. The Priority array was already in the config schema but
			-- had no control at all.
			local prioLbl = sec:AddLabel({ Label = "Weapon Priority", NoSeparator = true })
			local prioGear = sec:AddGear({ Row = prioLbl.Row })
			for _, class in ipairs({ "Primary", "Secondary", "Melee" }) do
				prioGear:AddToggle({ Label = class, Config = { "Ragebot", "Weapons", "Enabled", class } })
			end
			sec:AddOrderedList({
				Items = { "Primary", "Secondary", "Melee" },
				Default = { "Primary", "Secondary", "Melee" },
				Config = { "Ragebot", "Weapons", "Priority" },
			})
			sec:AddDropdown({
				Label = "On Empty",
				Options = { "SwapOrReload", "Reload", "Swap" },
				Labels = { SwapOrReload = "Swap or Reload" },
				Config = { "Ragebot", "Weapons", "OnEmpty" },
			})

			local evasion = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Strategy", Side = "right" })
			local mode = evasion:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Evasion Mode",
				Options = { "Random", "ProjectileBreaker", "Translocate" },
				Config = { "Ragebot", "Evasion", "Mode" },
			})

			-- khook.lua:241794 -- Character Origin, Base Radius from
			-- 5 with effectively no ceiling, and Random Range 0..1 in tenths.
			local rnd = evasion:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "Random" })
			rnd:AddToggle({ Label = "Character Origin", Config = { "Ragebot", "Evasion", "Random", "AnchorFromCharacter" } })
			rnd:AddSlider({ Label = "Base Radius",  Min = 5, Max = 100000000,       Config = { "Ragebot", "Evasion", "Random", "BaseRadius" } })
			rnd:AddSlider({ Label = "Random Range", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.1,   Config = { "Ragebot", "Evasion", "Random", "RadiusRandomFactor" } })

			local tl = evasion:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "Translocate" })
			tl:AddSlider({ Label = "Offset", Min = -5, Max = 5, Step = 0.1, Config = { "Ragebot", "Evasion", "Translocate", "Offset" } })
		end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.Player(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "Player", Icon = Icons.Player,
		Description = "Movement, camera, screen, body, and spoofing.",
	})

	-- Body deferred: nothing in this tab is constructed until the tab
	-- is first opened, so start-up builds one page instead of eight.
	tab:OnFirstOpen(function()

		---------------------------------------------------------- Movement ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Movement", Icon = Icons.Movement,
				Description = "Walk speed, jump, flight, noclip, and air strafing.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Movement", Side = "left" })

			local function movementFeature(label, key, sliderLabel, min, max, step)
				local toggle, bind = sec:AddFeature({
					Label = label,
					Config = { "Movement", key, "Enabled" },
					Keybind = Build.bindKeys({ "Movement", key }),
					ListLabel = label,
				})
				Features.binds[key] = bind
				if sliderLabel then
					sec:AddGroup({ Source = toggle }):AddSlider({
						Label = sliderLabel, Min = min, Max = max, Step = step,
						Config = { "Movement", key, "Speed" },
					})
				end
				return toggle
			end

			movementFeature("WalkSpeed", "WalkSpeed", "Multiplier", 1, 10, 0.01)
			movementFeature("Sliding Speed", "Sliding", "Multiplier", 1, 10, 0.01)
			movementFeature("Jump Power", "JumpPower", "Multiplier", 0.1, 10, 0.01)
			movementFeature("Flight", "Flight", "Speed", 25, 500, 1)
			movementFeature("Noclip", "Noclip")
			movementFeature("Auto Strafe", "AutoStrafe", "Speed", 1, 200, 1)
			sec:AddToggle({
				Label = "Infinite Double Jumps",
				Config = { "ItemModifiers", "InfiniteDoubleJumps" },
			})

			local ljToggle, ljBind = sec:AddFeature({
				Label = "Long Jump",
				Config = { "Movement", "LongJump", "Enabled" },
				Keybind = Build.bindKeys({ "Movement", "LongJump" }),
				KeybindMode = "Tap",
				ListLabel = "Long Jump",
			})
			Features.binds.LongJump = ljBind
			local ljGroup = sec:AddGroup({ Source = ljToggle })
			ljGroup:AddDropdown({ Label = "Mode", Options = { "Under Feet", "Forward" }, Config = { "Movement", "LongJump", "Mode" } })
			ljGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Force",           Min = 0, Max = 300, Step = 1, Config = { "Movement", "LongJump", "Force" } })
			ljGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Upward Velocity", Min = 0, Max = 200, Step = 1, Config = { "Movement", "LongJump", "UpwardVelocity" } })
			ljGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Behind Offset",   Min = 0, Max = 8, Step = 0.1, Config = { "Movement", "LongJump", "Behind" } })

			local rec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Movement Recorder", Side = "right" })
			local recToggle = rec:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "MovementRecorder", "Enabled" } })
			rec:AddToggle({ Label = "Hide UI",            Config = { "MovementRecorder", "HideUI" } })
			rec:AddToggle({ Label = "Hide Notifications", Config = { "MovementRecorder", "HideNotifications" } })
			rec:AddDivider({ Label = "Markers" })
			rec:AddSlider({ Label = "Open Distance", Min = 1, Max = 25, Step = 0.5, Config = { "MovementRecorder", "OpenDistance" } })
			rec:AddSlider({ Label = "Render Distance", Min = 10, Max = 250, Step = 1, Config = { "MovementRecorder", "RenderDistance" } })
			rec:AddDivider({ Label = "Replay" })
			rec:AddSlider({
				Label = "Look Align Speed", Min = 45, Max = 720, Step = 5,
				Tooltip = "How fast the camera turns to the recording's starting look angle",
				Config = { "MovementRecorder", "LookAlignSpeed" },
			})
			rec:AddSlider({
				Label = "Look Smoothness", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1,
				Tooltip = "How smoothened camera look is",
				Config = { "MovementRecorder", "LookSmoothing" },
			})
			rec:AddSlider({
				Label = "Align Snap Distance", Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.1,
				Tooltip = "Once this close to the start, position is considered aligned",
				Config = { "MovementRecorder", "AlignSnapDistance" },
			})
			rec:AddDivider({ Label = "Controls" })
			Features.binds.Record = rec:AddKeybind({
				ListLabel = "Record", InKeybindList = true,
				Label = "Record", Default = { Key = "None", Mode = "Toggle" },
				ConfigMap = {
					Key = { "MovementRecorder", "Record", "Keybind", "Bind" },
					Mode = { "MovementRecorder", "Record", "Keybind", "Kind" },
					ShowInList = { "MovementRecorder", "Record", "Keybind", "ShowInList" },
					Invisible = { "MovementRecorder", "Record", "Keybind", "Invisible" },
				},
			})
			Features.binds.Replay = rec:AddKeybind({
				ListLabel = "Replay", InKeybindList = true,
				Label = "Replay", Default = { Key = "None", Mode = "Toggle" },
				ConfigMap = {
					Key = { "MovementRecorder", "Replay", "Keybind", "Bind" },
					Mode = { "MovementRecorder", "Replay", "Keybind", "Kind" },
					ShowInList = { "MovementRecorder", "Replay", "Keybind", "ShowInList" },
					Invisible = { "MovementRecorder", "Replay", "Keybind", "Invisible" },
				},
			})
			local function syncRecorderBinds(on)
				Features.binds.Record:SetFeatureEnabled(on)
				Features.binds.Replay:SetFeatureEnabled(on)
			end
			recToggle.ValueChanged:Connect(syncRecorderBinds)
			syncRecorderBinds(recToggle.Value)
			rec:AddDivider({ Label = "Save" })
			local recordingName = rec:AddTextBox({ Label = "Recording Name", Placeholder = "my recording" })
			rec:AddButton({ Label = "Save Recording", OnClick = function()
				if Features.RecorderSave then Features.RecorderSave(recordingName.Value) end
			end })

			local recordings = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Recordings", Side = "right" })
			local mapDrop = recordings:AddDropdown({ Label = "Map", Options = {} })
			local recordingDrop = recordings:AddDropdown({ Label = "Config", Options = {} })
			local function refreshRecordings()
				local maps = Features.RecorderMaps and Features.RecorderMaps() or {}
				mapDrop:SetOptions(maps)
				local names = Features.RecorderNames and Features.RecorderNames(mapDrop.Value) or {}
				recordingDrop:SetOptions(names)
			end
			mapDrop.ValueChanged:Connect(refreshRecordings)
			recordingDrop.ValueChanged:Connect(function(name)
				if Features.RecorderLoad and type(name) == "string" then
					Features.RecorderLoad(mapDrop.Value, name)
				end
			end)
			recordings:AddButton({ Label = "Delete Recording", Confirm = true, OnClick = function()
				if Features.RecorderDelete then Features.RecorderDelete(recordingDrop.Value) end
				refreshRecordings()
			end })
			refreshRecordings()
		end

		------------------------------------------------------------ Camera ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Camera", Icon = Icons.Camera,
				Description = "Third person and field of view.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Third Person", Side = "left" })
			local toggle, bind = sec:AddFeature({
				Label = "Third Person",
				Config = { "ThirdPerson", "Enabled" },
				Keybind = Build.rootBindKeys("ThirdPerson"),
				ListLabel = "Third Person",
			})
			Features.binds.ThirdPerson = bind
			sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Show Replica", Config = { "ThirdPerson", "ShowReplica" } })
			sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Ray Check", Config = { "ThirdPerson", "RayCheck" },
				Tooltip = "Pull the camera in when a wall is between it and you." })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "X", Min = -15, Max = 15, Step = 0.1, Config = { "ThirdPerson", "X" } })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "Y", Min = -5, Max = 10, Step = 0.1, Config = { "ThirdPerson", "Y" } })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "Z", Min = 0, Max = 15, Step = 0.1, Config = { "ThirdPerson", "Z" } })

			local fov = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Camera FOV", Side = "right" })
			fov:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "CameraFov", "Enabled" } })
			fov:AddSlider({
				Label = "FOV", Min = 30, Max = 120, Step = 1, Config = { "CameraFov", "Value" },
			})
		end

		------------------------------------------------------------ Screen ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Screen", Icon = Icons.Screen,
				Description = "Overlay removal and stretched resolution.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local rem = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Overlays", Side = "left" })
			rem:AddToggle({ Label = "No Flashbang",     Config = { "Removables", "NoFlashbang" } })
			rem:AddToggle({ Label = "No Burn Effect",   Config = { "Removables", "NoBurnEffect" } })
			rem:AddToggle({ Label = "No ADS Vignette",  Config = { "Removables", "NoAdsVignette" } })
			rem:AddToggle({ Label = "No Scope Overlay", Config = { "Removables", "NoScopeOverlay" } })
			rem:AddToggle({ Label = "No Scope Reticle", Config = { "Removables", "NoScopeReticle" } })
			rem:AddToggle({ Label = "No Hitmarker",     Config = { "Removables", "NoHitmarker" } })

			local sr = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Stretched Resolution", Side = "right" })
			sr:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "StretchedResolution", "Enabled" } })
			sr:AddSlider({ Label = "X ratio", Min = 0.01, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "StretchedResolution", "X" } })
			sr:AddSlider({ Label = "Y ratio", Min = 0.01, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "StretchedResolution", "Y" } })
		end

		--------------------------------------------------------- Crosshair ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Crosshair", Icon = Icons.Crosshair,
				Description = "Custom crosshair and text.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Crosshair", Side = "left" })
			local toggle = sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Enabled" } })
			local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = toggle })
			local style = g:AddDropdown({ Label = "Style", Options = { "Lines", "Image" }, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Style" } })
			g:AddSlider({
				Label = "Rotation", Min = 0, Max = 360, Step = 1,
				Tooltip = "Static tilt of the whole crosshair",
				Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Rotation", "Angle" },
			})
			local linesGroup = g:AddGroup({ Source = style, Option = "Lines" })
			linesGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Length", Min = 1, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Length" } })
			linesGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Thickness", Min = 1, Max = 20, Step = 1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Thickness" } })
			linesGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Gap", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Gap" } })
			local outline = linesGroup:AddToggle({ Label = "Outline", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Outline", "Enabled" } })
			linesGroup:AddColor({ Row = outline.Row, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Outline", "Color" } })
			linesGroup:AddGroup({ Source = outline }):AddSlider({
				Label = "Outline Thickness", Min = 0, Max = 10, Step = 1,
				Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Outline", "Thickness" },
			})
			linesGroup:AddDivider({ Label = "Lines" })
			for _, side in ipairs({ "Top", "Bottom", "Left", "Right" }) do
				local t = linesGroup:AddToggle({ Label = side, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", side, "Enabled" } })
				linesGroup:AddColor({ Row = t.Row, Gradient = true, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", side, "Color" } })
			end
			local imageGroup = g:AddGroup({ Source = style, Option = "Image" })
			imageGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Image Size", Min = 8, Max = 256, Step = 1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Image", "Size" } })
			imageGroup:AddSlider({ Label = "Transparency", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Image", "Transparency" } })
			imageGroup:AddDivider({ Label = "Image Selection" })
			imageGroup:AddTextBox({ Label = "Path", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Image", "Path" } })

			local motion = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Effects", Side = "right" })
			local rot = motion:AddToggle({ Label = "Rotating", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Rotation", "Enabled" } })
			local rg = motion:AddGroup({ Source = rot })
			rg:AddSlider({ Label = "Rotation Speed", Min = 0, Max = 720, Step = 1, Tooltip = "Degrees per second", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Rotation", "Speed" } })

			local motionLines = motion:AddGroup({ Source = style, Option = "Lines" })
			local spread = motionLines:AddToggle({ Label = "Spread", Tooltip = "Pulses the gap outward by the range below", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Spread", "Enabled" } })
			local sg = motionLines:AddGroup({ Source = spread })
			sg:AddRangeSlider({ Label = "Range", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Spread", "Range" } })
			sg:AddSlider({ Label = "Spread Speed", Min = 0, Max = 20, Step = 0.1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Spread", "Speed" } })
			local function addAnimation(host, base)
				local function animPath(...)
					local path = { "CustomCrosshair" }
					for _, key in ipairs(base) do path[#path + 1] = key end
					for i = 1, select("#", ...) do path[#path + 1] = select(i, ...) end
					return path
				end
				local enabled = host:AddToggle({ Label = "Animation", Config = animPath("Enabled") })
				local group = host:AddGroup({ Source = enabled })
				local mode = group:AddDropdown({
					Label = "Mode", Options = { "Shimmer", "Perimeter", "PingPong" },
					Config = animPath("Kind"),
				})
				group:AddSlider({ Label = "Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.1, Config = animPath("Speed") })
				local shimmer = group:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "Shimmer" })
				shimmer:AddColor({ Label = "Fade Color", Config = animPath("Shimmer", "Color") })
				local perimeter = group:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "Perimeter" })
				perimeter:AddColor({ Label = "Gradient", Gradient = true, Config = animPath("Perimeter", "Color") })
				local ping = group:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "PingPong" })
				ping:AddSlider({ Label = "Rotation", Min = 0, Max = 360, Step = 1, Config = animPath("PingPong", "Rotation") })
				ping:AddColor({ Label = "Background Color", Config = animPath("PingPong", "BackgroundColor") })
				ping:AddColor({ Label = "Main Color", Config = animPath("PingPong", "MainColor") })
			end
			addAnimation(motionLines, { "Animation" })

			local text = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Text", Side = "right" })
			local tToggle = text:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Tooltip = "A custom label beneath the crosshair", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Enabled" } })
			local tg = text:AddGroup({ Source = tToggle })
			tg:AddTextBox({ Label = "Content", FocusLostOnly = true, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Content" } })
			tg:AddDropdown({ Label = "Font", Options = { "Inconsolata", "Plex", "Monospace", "System" }, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Font" } })
			tg:AddSlider({ Label = "Size", Min = 6, Max = 48, Step = 1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Size" } })
			tg:AddSlider({ Label = "Offset", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Tooltip = "Pixels below the crosshair", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Offset" } })
			tg:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Gradient = true, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Color" } })
			local textOutline = tg:AddToggle({ Label = "Outline", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Outline", "Enabled" } })
			tg:AddColor({ Row = textOutline.Row, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Outline", "Color" } })
			tg:AddGroup({ Source = textOutline }):AddSlider({ Label = "Outline Thickness", Min = 0, Max = 10, Step = 1, Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "Text", "Outline", "Thickness" } })
			addAnimation(tg, { "Text", "Animation" })

			local fToggle = motion:AddToggle({ Label = "Follow Target", Tooltip = "Drags the crosshair toward the aimbot / silent aim target", Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "FollowTarget", "Enabled" } })
			motion:AddGroup({ Source = fToggle }):AddDropdown({
				Label = "Mode", Options = { "Crosshair", "Target" },
				Config = { "CustomCrosshair", "FollowTarget", "Mode" },
			})
		end

		-------------------------------------------------------------- Body ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Body", Icon = Icons.Body,
				Description = "Body color and looping animations.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local anim = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Animation Player", Side = "left" })
			anim:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "AnimationPlayer", "Enabled" } })
			anim:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Animation", Options = { "Cat Girl Bounce", "Custom" },
				Config = { "AnimationPlayer", "Animation" },
			})
			anim:AddTextBox({ Label = "Custom Animation ID", Config = { "AnimationPlayer", "CustomId" } })
			anim:AddSlider({ Label = "Speed", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.01, Config = { "AnimationPlayer", "Speed" } })
			anim:AddSlider({ Label = "Start", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "AnimationPlayer", "Start" } })
			anim:AddSlider({ Label = "End", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1, Config = { "AnimationPlayer", "End" } })

			local color = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Color", Side = "right" })
			local character = color:AddToggle({ Label = "Character", Config = { "Chams", "Character", "Enabled" } })
			color:AddColor({
				Row = character.Row, Alpha = true,
				Config = { "Chams", "Character", "Color" },
				AlphaConfig = { "Chams", "Character", "Transparency" },
			})
			local cg = color:AddGroup({ Source = character })
			cg:AddDropdown({ Label = "Material", Options = { "Original", "Ghost", "Flat", "Foil", "Custom", "Reflective" }, Config = { "Chams", "Character", "Material" } })
			cg:AddToggle({ Label = "Strip Textures", Config = { "Chams", "Character", "StripTextures" } })
		end

		------------------------------------------------------------- Spoof ----
		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Spoof", Icon = Icons.Spoof,
				Description = "Device, your identity, and other players.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local dev = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Device Spoof", Side = "right" })
			local devToggle = dev:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Misc", "DeviceSpoof", "Enabled" } })
			dev:AddDropdown({
				Row = devToggle.Row, Options = { "Computer", "Mobile", "Console", "VR" },
				Config = { "Misc", "DeviceSpoof", "SpoofType" },
			})

			local NUMERIC = {
				{ "Winstreak", "Winstreak" }, { "Level", "Level" },
				{ "CasualWins", "Casual Wins" }, { "RankedWins", "Ranked Wins" },
				{ "RankedElo", "Ranked Elo" }, { "LeaderboardRank", "Leaderboard Rank" },
			}
			local PERCENT = {
				{ "CasualWinPercent", "Casual Win Percent" },
				{ "RankedWinPercent", "Ranked Win Percent" },
			}
			local TEXTUAL = {
				{ "Name", "Name" }, { "DisplayName", "Display Name" },
				{ "Avatar", "Avatar" }, { "FavoriteMap", "Favorite Map" },
				{ "NametagStatus", "Nametag Status" },
			}
			local FLAGS = {
				{ "Influencer", "Influencer" },
				{ "RobloxEmployee", "Roblox Employee" },
				{ "NosniyTeam", "Nosniy Team" },
			}

			local spoofer = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Player Spoofer", Side = "left" })
			local localSpoof, otherSpoof = spoofer:AddMultiSection({
				Titles = { "Local Player", "Other Players" },
			})
			local function fillSpoofer(sec, who)
				for _, field in ipairs(TEXTUAL) do
					local t = sec:AddToggle({ Label = field[2], Config = { "Misc", "PlayerSpoofer", who, field[1], "Enabled" } })
					sec:AddGroup({ Source = t }):AddTextBox({
						Label = "Value", Config = { "Misc", "PlayerSpoofer", who, field[1], "Value" },
					})
				end
				for _, entry in ipairs(NUMERIC) do
					local path = { "Misc", "PlayerSpoofer", who, entry[1], "Value" }
					local t = sec:AddToggle({ Label = entry[2], Config = { "Misc", "PlayerSpoofer", who, entry[1], "Enabled" } })
					sec:AddGroup({ Source = t }):AddTextBox({
						Label = "Value", Default = tostring(Config.get(path) or 0),
						OnChanged = function(value)
							local number = tonumber(value)
							if number ~= nil then Config.set(path, number) end
						end,
					})
				end
				for _, entry in ipairs(PERCENT) do
					local t = sec:AddToggle({ Label = entry[2], Config = { "Misc", "PlayerSpoofer", who, entry[1], "Enabled" } })
					sec:AddGroup({ Source = t }):AddSlider({ Label = "Value", Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 0.1, Config = { "Misc", "PlayerSpoofer", who, entry[1], "Value" } })
				end
				for _, flag in ipairs(FLAGS) do
					sec:AddToggle({ Label = flag[2], Config = { "Misc", "PlayerSpoofer", who, flag[1], "Enabled" } })
				end
				return sec
			end

			fillSpoofer(localSpoof, "LocalPlayer")
			fillSpoofer(otherSpoof, "OtherPlayers")
				:AddLabel({ Text = "Spoofing is client-side only; the server keeps the real values." })
		end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.Weapon(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "Weapon", Icon = Icons.Weapon,
		Description = "Weapon stats, viewmodel, effects, and hit feedback.",
	})

	-- Body deferred: nothing in this tab is constructed until the tab
	-- is first opened, so start-up builds one page instead of eight.
	tab:OnFirstOpen(function()

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Stats", Icon = Icons.Stats,
				Description = "Recoil, spread, cooldowns, and melee.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			--- khook's percentage row: a toggle with a "Percentage (%)" slider in a
			--- group beneath it (khook.lua:98060).
			local function percentRow(sec, label, key, min, max)
				local t = sec:AddToggle({ Label = label, Config = { "ItemModifiers", key, "Enabled" } })
				sec:AddGroup({ Source = t }):AddSlider({
					Label = "Percentage (%)", Min = min, Max = max, Step = 1,
					Config = { "ItemModifiers", key, "Percentage" },
				})
				return t
			end

			local firing = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Firing", Side = "left" })
			percentRow(firing, "Recoil", "Recoil", 0, 100)
			firing:AddToggle({ Label = "No Spread",        Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoSpread" } })
			firing:AddToggle({ Label = "Automatic Weapon", Config = { "ItemModifiers", "AutomaticWeapon" } })
			percentRow(firing, "Aim Speed", "AimSpeed", 50, 500)

			local melee = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Melee", Side = "left" })
			melee:AddToggle({ Label = "Always Backstab", Config = { "ItemModifiers", "AlwaysBackstab" } })
			local mt = melee:AddToggle({
				Label = "Extend Melee Range",
				Config = { "ItemModifiers", "ExtendMeleeRange", "Enabled" },
			})
			melee:AddGroup({ Source = mt }):AddSlider({
				Label = "Range", Min = 5, Max = 20, Step = 1,
				Config = { "ItemModifiers", "ExtendMeleeRange", "Range" },
			})

			local cooldowns = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Cooldowns", Side = "right" })
			percentRow(cooldowns, "Fire Cooldown",  "FireCooldown", 0, 100)
			percentRow(cooldowns, "Aim Cooldown",   "AimCooldown", 1, 100)
			percentRow(cooldowns, "Melee Cooldown", "MeleeCooldown", 0, 100)
			percentRow(cooldowns, "Dash Cooldown",  "DashCooldown", 0, 100)

			local nade = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Grenade Fuse", Side = "right" })
			local nt = nade:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "ItemModifiers", "GrenadeFuse", "Enabled" } })
			local ng = nade:AddGroup({ Source = nt })
			ng:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Explode On", Options = { "Impact", "Timer", "Never" },
				Config = { "ItemModifiers", "GrenadeFuse", "ExplodeOn" },
			})
			ng:AddToggle({ Label = "Remove Fuse", Config = { "ItemModifiers", "GrenadeFuse", "RemoveFuse" } })

			local motion = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Motion", Side = "left" })
			motion:AddToggle({ Label = "No Motion",           Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoMotion" } })
			motion:AddToggle({ Label = "No Camera Shake",     Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoCameraShake" } })
			motion:AddToggle({ Label = "No Camera Sway",      Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoCameraSway" } })
			motion:AddToggle({ Label = "Infinite Double Jumps", Config = { "ItemModifiers", "InfiniteDoubleJumps" } })

			local anims = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Animations", Side = "right" })
			anims:AddToggle({ Label = "No Shoot Animation",  Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoShootAnimation" } })
			anims:AddToggle({ Label = "No Sprint Animation", Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoSprintAnimation" } })
			anims:AddToggle({ Label = "No Equip Animation",  Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoEquipAnimation" } })
			anims:AddToggle({ Label = "No Reload Animation", Config = { "ItemModifiers", "NoReloadAnimation" } })

			local status = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Stat Table", Side = "left" })
			local statusLabel = status:AddLabel({ Text = "Locating the weapon stat table..." })
			task.spawn(function()
				local found = Resolver.await("WeaponInfo", 12)
				statusLabel:Set(found
					and "Bound to the running game's weapon stat table. Percentage changes are applied against each weapon's original value and restored on unload."
					or "Not found in this place. The stat toggles stay inert rather than writing to the wrong table; everything on the Motion and Animations panels still works.")
			end)
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Viewmodel", Icon = Icons.Viewmodel,
				Description = "Offset, highlight, color, animations, and motion.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Offset", Side = "left" })
			local t = sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "ViewModelOffset", "Enabled" } })
			local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = t })
			for _, axis in ipairs({ "X", "Y", "Z" }) do
				g:AddSlider({ Label = axis, Min = -5, Max = 5, Step = 0.01, Config = { "ViewModelOffset", axis } })
			end
			for _, rot in ipairs({ "Pitch", "Yaw", "Roll" }) do
				g:AddSlider({ Label = rot, Min = -180, Max = 180, Step = 1, Config = { "ViewModelOffset", rot } })
			end

			local hl = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Highlight", Side = "right" })
			for _, key in ipairs({ "Arms", "Item" }) do
				local ht = hl:AddToggle({ Label = key, Config = { "ViewModelHighlight", key, "Enabled" } })
				local hg = hl:AddGroup({ Source = ht })
				hg:AddColor({ Label = "Fill Color", Config = { "ViewModelHighlight", key, "FillColor" } })
				hg:AddSlider({ Label = "Fill Transparency", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "ViewModelHighlight", key, "FillTransparency" } })
				hg:AddColor({ Label = "Outline Color", Config = { "ViewModelHighlight", key, "OutlineColor" } })
				hg:AddSlider({ Label = "Outline Transparency", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "ViewModelHighlight", key, "OutlineTransparency" } })
			end

			local wf = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Wireframe", Side = "left" })
			for _, key in ipairs({ "Arms", "Item" }) do
				local wt = wf:AddToggle({ Label = key, Config = { "Wireframe", key, "Enabled" } })
				local wg = wf:AddGroup({ Source = wt })
				wg:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { "Wireframe", key, "Color" } })
				wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Width", Min = 0.0005, Max = 0.02, Step = 0.0005, Config = { "Wireframe", key, "Width" } })
			end
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Effects", Icon = Icons.Effects,
				Description = "Bullet tracers and muzzle effects.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Bullet Tracers", Side = "left" })
			local t = sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "BulletTracers", "Enabled" } })
			local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = t })
			g:AddDropdown({ Label = "Style", Options = { "Beam", "Line", "Trail" }, Config = { "BulletTracers", "Style" } })
			g:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { "BulletTracers", "Color" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Width",     Min = 0.01, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "BulletTracers", "Width" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Lifetime",  Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.05,  Config = { "BulletTracers", "Lifetime" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Fade Time", Min = 0, Max = 3, Step = 0.05,    Config = { "BulletTracers", "FadeTime" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Emission",  Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01,    Config = { "BulletTracers", "Emission" } })
			g:AddSlider({ Label = "Glow",      Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05,    Config = { "BulletTracers", "Glow" } })

			local expand = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Expand", Config = { "BulletTracers", "Expand" } })
			local eg = g:AddGroup({ Source = expand })
			eg:AddSlider({ Label = "Expand Speed",  Min = 0, Max = 60, Step = 1,    Config = { "BulletTracers", "ExpandSpeed" } })
			eg:AddSlider({ Label = "Expand Damper", Min = 0, Max = 2, Step = 0.05,  Config = { "BulletTracers", "ExpandDamper" } })

			local tex = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Texture", Side = "right" })
			tex:AddSlider({ Label = "Texture Length", Min = 0.1, Max = 20, Step = 0.1, Config = { "BulletTracers", "TextureLength" } })
			tex:AddSlider({ Label = "Texture Speed",  Min = 0, Max = 10, Step = 0.1,   Config = { "BulletTracers", "TextureSpeed" } })
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Feedback", Icon = Icons.Feedback,
				Description = "Hit and elimination feedback.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local function feedbackSection(title, side, node, flashKey, flashLabel)
				local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = title, Side = side })
				local t = sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { node, "Enabled" } })
				local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = t })

				local flash = g:AddToggle({ Label = flashLabel, Config = { node, flashKey, "Enabled" } })
				local fg = g:AddGroup({ Source = flash })
				fg:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { node, flashKey, "Color" } })
				fg:AddSlider({ Label = "Duration", Min = 0.05, Max = 3, Step = 0.05, Config = { node, flashKey, "Duration" } })

				local notif = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Notification", Config = { node, "Notification", "Enabled" } })
				g:AddGroup({ Source = notif }):AddTextBox({
					Label = "Text", Config = { node, "Notification", "Text" },
					Tooltip = "%NAME% %DNAME% %DMG% %PART% are replaced at send time.",
				})

				local chams = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Chams", Config = { node, "Chams", "Enabled" } })
				local cg = g:AddGroup({ Source = chams })
				cg:AddColor({ Label = "Chams Color", Config = { node, "Chams", "Color" } })
				cg:AddSlider({ Label = "Chams Transparency", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { node, "Chams", "Transparency" } })
				cg:AddSlider({ Label = "Chams Duration", Min = 0.05, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { node, "Chams", "Duration" } })

				local snd = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Sound", Config = { node, "Sound", "Enabled" } })
				return sec, g:AddGroup({ Source = snd })
			end

			local _, hitSound = feedbackSection("Player Hit", "left", "PlayerHit", "HitFlash", "Hit Flash")
			hitSound:AddTextBox({ Label = "Head Sound", Placeholder = "rbxassetid", Config = { "PlayerHit", "Sound", "HeadName" } })
			hitSound:AddSlider({ Label = "Head Volume", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "PlayerHit", "Sound", "HeadVolume" } })
			hitSound:AddSlider({ Label = "Head Pitch",  Min = 0.1, Max = 3, Step = 0.05, Config = { "PlayerHit", "Sound", "HeadPitch" } })
			hitSound:AddTextBox({ Label = "Body Sound", Placeholder = "rbxassetid", Config = { "PlayerHit", "Sound", "BodyName" } })
			hitSound:AddSlider({ Label = "Body Volume", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "PlayerHit", "Sound", "BodyVolume" } })
			hitSound:AddSlider({ Label = "Body Pitch",  Min = 0.1, Max = 3, Step = 0.05, Config = { "PlayerHit", "Sound", "BodyPitch" } })
			hitSound:AddToggle({ Label = "Disable Game Sound", Config = { "PlayerHit", "Sound", "DisableGameSound" } })

			local _, killSound = feedbackSection("Elimination", "right", "PlayerElimination", "KillFlash", "Kill Flash")
			killSound:AddTextBox({ Label = "Sound", Placeholder = "rbxassetid", Config = { "PlayerElimination", "Sound", "Name" } })
			killSound:AddSlider({ Label = "Volume", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "PlayerElimination", "Sound", "Volume" } })
			killSound:AddSlider({ Label = "Pitch",  Min = 0.1, Max = 3, Step = 0.05, Config = { "PlayerElimination", "Sound", "Pitch" } })
		end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.ESP(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "ESP", Icon = Icons.ESP,
		Description = "Player overlays, bounding boxes, labels, and chams.",
	})

	-- Body deferred: nothing in this tab is constructed until the tab
	-- is first opened, so start-up builds one page instead of eight.
	tab:OnFirstOpen(function()

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Players", Icon = Icons.Main,
				Description = "Toggle ESP and configure per-team overlays.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local main = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Main", Side = "left" })
			local toggle, bind = main:AddFeature({
				Label = "ESP",
				Config = { "ESP", "Main", "Enabled" },
				Keybind = { Key = { "ESP", "Main", "Keybind", "Bind" },
				            Mode = { "ESP", "Main", "Keybind", "Kind" },
				            ShowInList = { "ESP", "Main", "Keybind", "ShowInList" },
				            Invisible = { "ESP", "Main", "Keybind", "Invisible" } },
				ListLabel = "ESP",
			})
			Features.binds.ESP = bind
			main:AddGroup({ Source = toggle }):AddDropdown({
				Label = "Mode", Options = { "Static", "Dynamic" }, Config = { "ESP", "Main", "Mode" },
			})

			--- Both factions get an identical control set -- khook emits the
			--- block twice (khook.lua:76802 `f5427` and its clone at 77265).
			--- Every visual carries a gear button; the gear is where khook
			--- hides the per-element animation, so a flat toggle+swatch row is
			--- only about a third of the real control surface.
			--- khook.lua:102981 -- Team and Enemy are two TABS of one section on
			--- the left, not two sections side by side. That is what leaves the
			--- right-hand column free for the Preview, and what gives the
			--- preview something to follow.
			local teamSec, enemySec = grid:AddMultiSection({
				Titles = { "Team", "Enemy" }, Side = "left",
			})

			local function factionSection(sec, faction)
				local on, fbind = sec:AddFeature({
					Label = "Enabled",
					Config = { "ESP", faction, "Enabled" },
					Keybind = { Key = { "ESP", faction, "Keybind", "Bind" },
					            Mode = { "ESP", faction, "Keybind", "Kind" },
					            ShowInList = { "ESP", faction, "Keybind", "ShowInList" },
					            Invisible = { "ESP", faction, "Keybind", "Invisible" } },
					ListLabel = faction .. " ESP",
				})
				Features.binds["ESP" .. faction] = fbind
				local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = on })

				local function path(...) return { "ESP", faction, ... } end

				--- khook.lua:76862 `f5431`. The animation block every text and
				--- box element shares: an Animate toggle gating a kind
				--- dropdown, then one group per kind. Perimeter is only offered
				--- where a gradient can orbit an outline, which is why khook
				--- passes the flag rather than always listing it.
				local function animGear(gear, key, divider, perimeter)
					if divider then gear:AddDivider() end
					local an = gear:AddToggle({ Label = "Animate", Config = path(key, "Animation", "Enabled") })
					local ag = gear:AddGroup({ Source = an })
					local kinds = { "Shimmer", "PingPong" }
					if perimeter then table.insert(kinds, 2, "Perimeter") end
					local kindLbl = ag:AddLabel({ Label = "Animate Kind" })
					local kind = ag:AddDropdown({
						Row = kindLbl.Row, Options = kinds, Config = path(key, "Animation", "Kind"),
					})

					local sh = ag:AddGroup({ Source = kind, Option = "Shimmer" })
					sh:AddSlider({ Label = "Shimmer Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.1,
						NoSeparator = true, Config = path(key, "Animation", "Shimmer", "Speed") })
					local fade = sh:AddLabel({ Label = "Fade Color", NoSeparator = true })
					sh:AddColor({ Row = fade.Row, Config = path(key, "Animation", "Shimmer", "Color") })

					if perimeter then
						local pe = ag:AddGroup({ Source = kind, Option = "Perimeter" })
						pe:AddSlider({ Label = "Orbit Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.1,
							NoSeparator = true, Config = path(key, "Animation", "Perimeter", "Speed") })
						local pgl = pe:AddLabel({ Label = "Gradient" })
						pe:AddColor({ Row = pgl.Row, Gradient = true,
							Config = path(key, "Animation", "Perimeter", "Color") })
					end

					local pp = ag:AddGroup({ Source = kind, Option = "PingPong" })
					pp:AddSlider({ Label = "Bounce Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.1,
						NoSeparator = true, Config = path(key, "Animation", "PingPong", "Speed") })
					pp:AddSlider({ Label = "Rotation", Min = 0, Max = 360,
						NoSeparator = true, Config = path(key, "Animation", "PingPong", "Rotation") })
					local bgl = pp:AddLabel({ Label = "Background Color", NoSeparator = true })
					pp:AddColor({ Row = bgl.Row, Config = path(key, "Animation", "PingPong", "BackgroundColor") })
					local mnl = pp:AddLabel({ Label = "Main Color" })
					pp:AddColor({ Row = mnl.Row, Config = path(key, "Animation", "PingPong", "MainColor") })
				end

				--- A text row: toggle, inline swatch, gear holding the animation.
				local function textEl(label, key, withAlpha)
					local t = g:AddToggle({ Label = label, Config = path(key, "Enabled") })
					g:AddColor({
						Row = t.Row, Alpha = withAlpha or nil,
						Config = path(key, "Color"),
						AlphaConfig = withAlpha and path(key, "Transparency") or nil,
					})
					animGear(g:AddGear({ Row = t.Row }), key, false, false)
					return t
				end

				--- khook.lua:76805 `f5429`. Health and ammo bars share a gear
				--- whose colour controls swap with the mode: one swatch for
				--- Solid, a Full/Low pair for Reactive, an editable gradient
				--- for Gradient.
				local function barEl(label, key)
					local t = g:AddToggle({ Label = label, Config = path(key, "Enabled") })
					local gear = g:AddGear({ Row = t.Row })
					gear:AddToggle({ Label = "Animate", Config = path(key, "Animate") })
					local cml = gear:AddLabel({ Label = "Color Mode" })
					local mode = gear:AddDropdown({
						Row = cml.Row, Options = { "Solid", "Reactive", "Gradient" },
						Config = path(key, "ColorMode"),
					})
					local solid = gear:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "Solid" })
					local sl = solid:AddLabel({ Label = "Color" })
					solid:AddColor({ Row = sl.Row, Config = path(key, "Color") })

					local react = gear:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "Reactive" })
					local fl = react:AddLabel({ Label = "Full" })
					react:AddColor({ Row = fl.Row, Config = path(key, "Color"), SequenceEnd = "high" })
					local lo = react:AddLabel({ Label = "Low" })
					react:AddColor({ Row = lo.Row, Config = path(key, "Color"), SequenceEnd = "low" })

					local grad = gear:AddGroup({ Source = mode, Option = "Gradient" })
					local gl = grad:AddLabel({ Label = "Color" })
					grad:AddColor({ Row = gl.Row, Gradient = true, Config = path(key, "Color") })
					return t
				end

				textEl("Name", "Name", true)
				textEl("Distance", "Distance", false)
				textEl("Rank", "Rank", false)
				textEl("Winstreak", "Winstreak", false)

				-- Health Number is the one text element khook leaves unanimated.
				local hn = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Health Number", Config = path("HealthNumber", "Enabled") })
				g:AddColor({ Row = hn.Row, Config = path("HealthNumber", "Color") })

				textEl("Held Weapon", "HeldWeapon", true)
				textEl("Deflecting", "Deflecting", true)

				-- Box: its gear also owns the Filled Box controls (khook.lua:77047).
				local boxT = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Box", Config = path("Box", "Enabled") })
				g:AddColor({ Row = boxT.Row, Config = path("Box", "Color") })
				local boxGear = g:AddGear({ Row = boxT.Row })
				local styleLbl = boxGear:AddLabel({ Label = "Style" })
				boxGear:AddDropdown({
					Row = styleLbl.Row, Options = { "Full", "Corner" }, Config = path("Box", "Style"),
				})
				animGear(boxGear, "Box", true, true)
				boxGear:AddDivider()
				local fillT = boxGear:AddToggle({ Label = "Filled Box", Config = path("FilledBox", "Enabled") })
				boxGear:AddColor({
					Row = fillT.Row, Gradient = true,
					Config = path("FilledBox", "Color"),
					AlphaConfig = path("FilledBox", "Transparency"),
					GradientEnabledConfig = path("FilledBox", "Gradient", "Enabled"),
					GradientColorConfig = path("FilledBox", "Gradient", "Color"),
				})
				boxGear:AddToggle({ Label = "Animate Gradient", Config = path("FilledBox", "Gradient", "Animate") })

				-- khook.lua:54610 `f3907` -- Box Image: a toggle gating a
				-- Transparency slider and the image path. Present in the config
				-- schema all along but it had no control at all.
				local imgT = boxGear:AddToggle({ Label = "Box Image", Config = path("BoxImage", "Enabled") })
				local imgG = boxGear:AddGroup({ Source = imgT })
				imgG:AddSlider({
					Label = "Transparency", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01,
					Config = path("BoxImage", "Transparency"),
				})
				imgG:AddTextBox({ Label = "Image", Config = path("BoxImage", "Path") })

				g:AddDivider()
				barEl("Health Bar", "HealthBar")
				barEl("Ammo Bar", "AmmoBar")

				local chamsT = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Chams", Config = path("Chams", "Enabled") })
				g:AddColor({
					Row = chamsT.Row, Alpha = true,
					Config = path("Chams", "InnerColor"),
					AlphaConfig = path("Chams", "InnerTransparency"),
				})
				local cGear = g:AddGear({ Row = chamsT.Row })
				local outLbl = cGear:AddLabel({ Label = "Outline" })
				cGear:AddColor({
					Row = outLbl.Row, Alpha = true,
					Config = path("Chams", "OutlineColor"),
					AlphaConfig = path("Chams", "OutlineTransparency"),
				})
				local glow = cGear:AddToggle({ Label = "Glow", Config = path("Chams", "Glow") })
				cGear:AddColor({ Row = glow.Row, Config = path("Chams", "GlowColor") })

				local skelT = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Skeleton", Config = path("Skeleton", "Enabled") })
				g:AddColor({
					Row = skelT.Row, Alpha = true, Gradient = true,
					Config = path("Skeleton", "Color"),
					AlphaConfig = path("Skeleton", "Transparency"),
					GradientEnabledConfig = path("Skeleton", "Gradient"),
					GradientColorConfig = path("Skeleton", "GradientColor"),
				})
				g:AddGear({ Row = skelT.Row }):AddSlider({
					Label = "Skeleton Thickness", Min = 1, Max = 5, Config = path("Skeleton", "Thickness"),
				})

				local headT = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Head Marker", Config = path("HeadMarker", "Enabled") })
				g:AddColor({
					Row = headT.Row, Alpha = true,
					Config = path("HeadMarker", "Color"),
					AlphaConfig = path("HeadMarker", "Transparency"),
				})
				-- khook hangs these off a plain group, not a gear (77163).
				local hmg = g:AddGroup({ Source = headT })
				hmg:AddDropdown({
					Label = "Shape", Options = { "Cross", "Circle", "Diamond" },
					Config = path("HeadMarker", "Shape"),
				})
				hmg:AddToggle({ Label = "Filled", Config = path("HeadMarker", "Filled") })
				local hmOut = hmg:AddToggle({ Label = "Outline", Config = path("HeadMarker", "Outline") })
				hmg:AddColor({
					Row = hmOut.Row, Alpha = true,
					Config = path("HeadMarker", "OutlineColor"),
					AlphaConfig = path("HeadMarker", "OutlineTransparency"),
				})

				local traceT = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Tracer", Config = path("Tracer", "Enabled") })
				g:AddColor({
					Row = traceT.Row, Alpha = true, Gradient = true,
					Config = path("Tracer", "Color"),
					AlphaConfig = path("Tracer", "Transparency"),
					GradientEnabledConfig = path("Tracer", "Gradient"),
					GradientColorConfig = path("Tracer", "GradientColor"),
				})
				local tGear = g:AddGear({ Row = traceT.Row })
				tGear:AddSlider({ Label = "Thickness", Min = 1, Max = 5, Config = path("Tracer", "Thickness") })
				local orgLbl = tGear:AddLabel({ Label = "Origin" })
				tGear:AddDropdown({
					Row = orgLbl.Row, Options = { "Bottom", "Top", "Center", "Mouse" },
					Config = path("Tracer", "Origin"),
				})
				local tgtLbl = tGear:AddLabel({ Label = "Target" })
				tGear:AddDropdown({
					Row = tgtLbl.Row, Options = { "Feet", "Head", "Center" },
					Config = path("Tracer", "Target"),
				})
				local tOut = tGear:AddToggle({ Label = "Outline", Config = path("Tracer", "Outline") })
				local tog = tGear:AddGroup({ Source = tOut })
				local tocLbl = tog:AddLabel({ Label = "Color" })
				tog:AddColor({
					Row = tocLbl.Row, Alpha = true,
					Config = path("Tracer", "OutlineColor"),
					AlphaConfig = path("Tracer", "OutlineTransparency"),
				})
				tog:AddSlider({ Label = "Thickness", Min = 1, Max = 3, Config = path("Tracer", "OutlineThickness") })
				return sec
			end

			factionSection(teamSec, "Team")
			factionSection(enemySec, "Enemy")

			--- khook.lua:102989 -- a "Preview" section on the right holding a
			--- 400px viewport, and khook's exact camera options. The dummy is a
			--- real R15 avatar built from userId 1 (khook.lua:102947 `f7088`),
			--- pivoted to the origin with its Animate script removed so it
			--- stands still, and the camera starts at 225 degrees.
			local preview = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Preview", Side = "right" })
			local viewport = preview:AddViewport({
				Height = 400, AutoRotate = true, RotateSpeed = 0.45,
				FieldOfView = 18, DistanceMul = 4,
			})
			ESP.preview = { viewport = viewport, faction = "Enemy" }

			-- khook.lua:103034 `f7096` -- the preview follows whichever faction
			-- tab is open, and khook starts it on Enemy (103016).
			teamSec.Opened:Connect(function() ESP.setPreviewFaction("Team") end)
			enemySec.Opened:Connect(function() ESP.setPreviewFaction("Enemy") end)

			viewport:OnRealized(function()
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, description = pcall(function()
						return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserIdAsync(1)
					end)
					if not (ok and description ~= nil) then return end
					local built, model = pcall(function()
						return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescriptionAsync(
							description, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
					end)
					if not (built and model ~= nil) then return end
					model:PivotTo(CFrame.identity)
					local animate = model:FindFirstChild("Animate")
					if animate ~= nil then animate:Destroy() end
					viewport:SetModel(model)
					viewport:SetRotation(math.rad(225))
					model:Destroy()
					ESP.startPreview()
				end)
			end)
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Sound", Icon = Icons.Sound,
				Description = "Sound source visualization and audio indicators.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Sound Visualizer", Side = "left" })
			sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "SoundVisualizer", "Enabled" } })
			sec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Source", Options = { "All", "Self", "Enemies", "Team" },
				Config = { "SoundVisualizer", "Source" } })
			sec:AddMultiDropdown({ Label = "Sound Types", Options = { "Footsteps", "Other" },
				Config = { "SoundVisualizer", "Types" } })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "Min Volume", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01,
				Config = { "SoundVisualizer", "MinVolume" } })

			local footsteps, other = grid:AddMultiSection({
				Titles = { "Footsteps", "Other" }, Side = "right",
			})
			for key, styles in pairs({ Footsteps = footsteps, Other = other }) do
				styles:AddDropdown({ Label = "Mode", Options = { "Circle", "Origin" },
