					Config = { "SoundVisualizer", key, "Mode" } })
				styles:AddToggle({ Label = "Loudness Color",
					Config = { "SoundVisualizer", key, "UseLoudnessColor" } })
				styles:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { "SoundVisualizer", key, "Color" } })
			end
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Appearance", Icon = Icons.Appearance,
				Description = "Font, text format, flag style, and layout options.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Text", Side = "left" })
			sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Use Player Display Name", Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "UseDisplayName" } })
			-- khook.lua:56690/56710 -- Font and Flag Font over the shared font
			-- Order list: four Roblox built-ins plus three TTFs khook downloads
			-- from its asset repo (khook.lua:101762-101789).
			sec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Font", Options = table.clone(ESP.FONT_ORDER),
				Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "Font" } })
			sec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Flag Font", Options = table.clone(ESP.FONT_ORDER),
				Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "FlagFont" } })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "Font Size",      Min = 8, Max = 16, Step = 1, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "FontSize" } })
			sec:AddSlider({ Label = "Flag Font Size", Min = 8, Max = 16, Step = 1, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "FlagFontSize" } })
			sec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Text Case",      Options = { "Standard", "UPPERCASE", "lowercase" }, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "TextCase" } })
			sec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Flag Text Case", Options = { "Standard", "UPPERCASE", "lowercase" }, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "FlagTextCase" } })
			sec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Text Surround",      Options = { "None", "[]", "()" }, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "TextSurround" } })
			sec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Flag Text Surround", Options = { "None", "[]", "()" }, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "FlagTextSurround" } })

			local layout = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Layout", Side = "right" })
			local ors = layout:AddToggle({ Label = "Override ESP Size", Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "OverrideRectSize", "Enabled" } })
			local og = layout:AddGroup({ Source = ors })
			og:AddSlider({ Label = "Width",  Min = 0.5, Max = 1.5, Step = 0.1, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "OverrideRectSize", "Width" } })
			og:AddSlider({ Label = "Height", Min = 0.5, Max = 1.5, Step = 0.1, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "OverrideRectSize", "Height" } })
			for _, side in ipairs({ "Left", "Top", "Right", "Bottom" }) do
				layout:AddSlider({ Label = side .. " Spacing", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 1, Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "Spacing", side } })
			end
			local ds = layout:AddToggle({ Label = "Distance Scaling", Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "DistanceScaling" } })
			layout:AddGroup({ Source = ds }):AddSlider({
				Label = "Reference Distance (m)", Min = 10, Max = 200, Step = 1,
				Config = { "ESP", "Settings", "DistanceScalingRef" },
			})
		end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.World(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "World", Icon = Icons.World,
		Description = "Lighting, post-processing, sky, and ambient sound.",
	})

	-- Body deferred: nothing in this tab is constructed until the tab
	-- is first opened, so start-up builds one page instead of eight.
	tab:OnFirstOpen(function()

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Lighting", Icon = Icons.Lighting,
				Description = "Ambient lighting, brightness, and shadows.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Main", Side = "left" })
			local master = sec:AddToggle({ Label = "Custom Lighting", Config = { "Lighting", "Enabled" } })
			local g = sec:AddGroup({ Source = master })

			local amb = g:AddToggle({ Label = "Ambient", Config = { "Lighting", "Ambient", "Enabled" } })
			local ag = g:AddGroup({ Source = amb })
			ag:AddColor({ Label = "Indoor",  Config = { "Lighting", "Ambient", "Indoor" } })
			ag:AddColor({ Label = "Outdoor", Config = { "Lighting", "Ambient", "Outdoor" } })

			local function valued(label, node, min, max, step)
				local t = g:AddToggle({ Label = label, Config = { "Lighting", node, "Enabled" } })
				g:AddGroup({ Source = t }):AddSlider({
					Label = label, Min = min, Max = max, Step = step,
					Config = { "Lighting", node, "Value" },
				})
			end
			valued("Clock Time", "ClockTime", 0, 24, 0.1)
			valued("Brightness", "Brightness", 0, 10, 0.1)
			valued("Environment Diffuse", "EnvironmentDiffuseScale", 0, 1, 0.01)
			valued("Exposure", "ExposureCompensation", -5, 5, 0.05)
			valued("Geographic Latitude", "GeographicLatitude", -90, 90, 0.1)
			valued("Shadow Softness", "ShadowSoftness", 0, 1, 0.01)

			local shadows = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Shadows", Side = "right" })
			shadows:AddToggle({ Label = "Disable Global Shadows", Config = { "Lighting", "DisableGlobalShadows" } })
			local sc = shadows:AddToggle({ Label = "Shadow Color", Config = { "Lighting", "ShadowColor", "Enabled" } })
			shadows:AddGroup({ Source = sc }):AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { "Lighting", "ShadowColor", "Value" } })
			local ls = shadows:AddToggle({ Label = "Lighting Style", Config = { "Lighting", "LightingStyle", "Enabled" } })
			shadows:AddGroup({ Source = ls }):AddDropdown({
				Label = "Technology", Options = { "Legacy", "Voxel", "ShadowMap", "Future" },
				Config = { "Lighting", "LightingStyle", "Value" },
			})
			shadows:AddToggle({ Label = "Prioritize Lighting Quality", Config = { "Lighting", "PrioritizeLightingQuality" } })

			local fog = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Fog", Side = "left" })
			local ft = fog:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Lighting", "Fog", "Enabled" } })
			local fg = fog:AddGroup({ Source = ft })
			fg:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { "Lighting", "Fog", "Color" } })
			fg:AddSlider({ Label = "Start", Min = 0, Max = 5000, Step = 10,     Config = { "Lighting", "Fog", "Start" } })
			fg:AddSlider({ Label = "End",   Min = 0, Max = 100000, Step = 100,  Config = { "Lighting", "Fog", "End" } })

			local cs = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Color Shift", Side = "right" })
			local ct = cs:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Lighting", "ColorShift", "Enabled" } })
			local cg = cs:AddGroup({ Source = ct })
			cg:AddColor({ Label = "Top",    Config = { "Lighting", "ColorShift", "Top" } })
			cg:AddColor({ Label = "Bottom", Config = { "Lighting", "ColorShift", "Bottom" } })
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Effects", Icon = Icons.WorldEffects,
				Description = "Color correction, grading, bloom, sun rays, and depth of field.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local bloom = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Bloom", Side = "left" })
			local bt = bloom:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Bloom", "Enabled" } })
			local bg = bloom:AddGroup({ Source = bt })
			bg:AddSlider({ Label = "Size",      Min = 0, Max = 56, Step = 1,    Config = { "Bloom", "Size" } })
			bg:AddSlider({ Label = "Threshold", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.01,  Config = { "Bloom", "Threshold" } })

			local cc = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Color Correction", Side = "right" })
			local cct = cc:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "ColorCorrection", "Enabled" } })
			local ccg = cc:AddGroup({ Source = cct })
			ccg:AddColor({ Label = "Tint", Config = { "ColorCorrection", "TintColor" } })
			ccg:AddSlider({ Label = "Saturation", Min = -1, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "ColorCorrection", "Saturation" } })
			ccg:AddSlider({ Label = "Brightness", Min = -1, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "ColorCorrection", "Brightness" } })
			ccg:AddSlider({ Label = "Contrast",   Min = -1, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "ColorCorrection", "Contrast" } })

			local dof = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Depth Of Field", Side = "left" })
			local dt = dof:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "DepthOfField", "Enabled" } })
			local dg = dof:AddGroup({ Source = dt })
			dg:AddSlider({ Label = "Far Intensity",  Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01,  Config = { "DepthOfField", "FarIntensity" } })
			dg:AddSlider({ Label = "Focus Distance", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.001, Config = { "DepthOfField", "FocusDistance" } })
			dg:AddSlider({ Label = "In Focus Radius",Min = 0, Max = 100, Step = 1,   Config = { "DepthOfField", "InFocusRadius" } })
			dg:AddSlider({ Label = "Near Intensity", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01,  Config = { "DepthOfField", "NearIntensity" } })

			local sun = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Sun Rays", Side = "right" })
			local st = sun:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "SunRays", "Enabled" } })
			local sg = sun:AddGroup({ Source = st })
			sg:AddSlider({ Label = "Intensity", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "SunRays", "Intensity" } })
			sg:AddSlider({ Label = "Spread",    Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "SunRays", "Spread" } })

			local mb = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Motion Blur", Side = "left" })
			local mt = mb:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "MotionBlur", "Enabled" } })
			local mg = mb:AddGroup({ Source = mt })
			mg:AddSlider({ Label = "Intensity",   Min = 0, Max = 56, Step = 1,   Config = { "MotionBlur", "Intensity" } })
			mg:AddSlider({ Label = "Sensitivity", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "MotionBlur", "Sensitivity" } })

			local cg2 = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Color Grading", Side = "right" })
			local cgt = cg2:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "ColorGrading", "Enabled" } })
			cg2:AddGroup({ Source = cgt }):AddDropdown({
				Label = "Tonemapper", Options = { "Default", "Filmic", "Vibrant", "Muted" },
				Config = { "ColorGrading", "TonemapperPreset" },
			})
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Sky", Icon = Icons.Sky,
				Description = "Skybox, atmosphere, and ambient sound.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local sky = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Skybox", Side = "left" })
			local skyT = sky:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Skybox", "Enabled" } })
			sky:AddGroup({ Source = skyT }):AddDropdown({
				Label = "Preset", Options = Features.SkyboxPresets or { "Clear Blue" },
				Height = 180, Config = { "Skybox", "Preset" },
			})

			local atm = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Atmosphere", Side = "right" })
			local at = atm:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Atmosphere", "Enabled" } })
			local ag = atm:AddGroup({ Source = at })
			ag:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { "Atmosphere", "Color" } })
			ag:AddColor({ Label = "Decay", Config = { "Atmosphere", "Decay" } })
			ag:AddSlider({ Label = "Density", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.005, Config = { "Atmosphere", "Density" } })
			ag:AddSlider({ Label = "Offset",  Min = -1, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "Atmosphere", "Offset" } })
			ag:AddSlider({ Label = "Glare",   Min = 0, Max = 10, Step = 0.1,  Config = { "Atmosphere", "Glare" } })
			ag:AddSlider({ Label = "Haze",    Min = 0, Max = 10, Step = 0.1,  Config = { "Atmosphere", "Haze" } })

			local weather = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Weather", Side = "left" })
			local wt = weather:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Weather", "Enabled" } })
			local wg = weather:AddGroup({ Source = wt })
			wg:AddDropdown({ Label = "Preset", Options = Features.WeatherPresets or { "Snow" }, Config = { "Weather", "Preset" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Intensity", Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "Weather", "Intensity" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Rate",      Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "Weather", "Rate" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Height",    Min = 5, Max = 200, Step = 1,  Config = { "Weather", "Height" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Speed",     Min = 0, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "Weather", "Speed" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Size",      Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Step = 0.05, Config = { "Weather", "Size" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Spread",    Min = 0, Max = 180, Step = 1,  Config = { "Weather", "Spread" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Glow",      Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.01, Config = { "Weather", "Glow" } })
			wg:AddColor({ Label = "Color", Config = { "Weather", "Color" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Wind Strength", Min = 0, Max = 50, Step = 0.5, Config = { "Weather", "Wind", "Strength" } })
			wg:AddSlider({ Label = "Wind Angle",    Min = 0, Max = 360, Step = 1,  Config = { "Weather", "Wind", "Angle" } })

			local amb = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Ambience", Side = "right" })
			local at2 = amb:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Ambience", "Enabled" } })
			local ag2 = amb:AddGroup({ Source = at2 })
			ag2:AddTextBox({ Label = "Sound ID", Placeholder = "rbxassetid", Config = { "Ambience", "CustomSound" } })
			ag2:AddSlider({ Label = "Volume", Min = 0, Max = 3, Step = 0.05, Config = { "Ambience", "Volume" } })
		end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.Cosmetics(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "Cosmetics", Icon = Icons.Cosmetics,
		Description = "Skin changer, unlocker, presets, and cosmetic overrides.",
	})

	-- Shared across the five sub-tabs: the weapon dropdown drives every grid.
	local weaponPicker
	local grids = {}          -- kind -> skin changer widget
	local invertedToggle
	local SPECIAL_ITEMS = {
		{ name = "Unselected", special = true },
		{ name = "None",       special = true },
		{ name = "Random",     special = true },
	}

	local function choiceName(name)
		if name == nil or name == "Unselected" then return nil end
		if name == "None" then return Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC end
		if name == "Random" then return Cosmetics.RANDOM_COSMETIC end
		return name
	end

	local function displayName(name)
		if name == nil then return "Unselected" end
		if name == Cosmetics.NONE_COSMETIC then return "None" end
		if name == Cosmetics.RANDOM_COSMETIC then return "Random" end
		return name
	end

	local function gridItems(items)
		local out = table.clone(SPECIAL_ITEMS)
		for _, item in ipairs(items or {}) do table.insert(out, item) end
		return out
	end

	local function currentWeapon()
		local v = weaponPicker and weaponPicker.Value
		if not v or v == "" then v = Cosmetics.GetWeapons()[1] or "All" end
		return v
	end

	--- Push the stored selections for the current weapon into all four grids.
	local function syncSelections()
		local weapon = currentWeapon()
		local entry = weapon ~= "All" and Cosmetics.GetSkinChangerSelections(weapon) or nil
		if not entry then
			for _, g in pairs(grids) do g:Set("Unselected", true) end
			if invertedToggle then invertedToggle:Set(false, true) end
			return
		end
		if grids.Skin then grids.Skin:Set(displayName(entry.skin), true) end
		if grids.Charm then grids.Charm:Set(displayName(entry.charm), true) end
		if grids.Finisher then grids.Finisher:Set(displayName(entry.finisher), true) end
		if grids.Wrap then
			grids.Wrap:Set(displayName(entry.wrap and entry.wrap.name), true)
			if invertedToggle then
				invertedToggle:Set(entry.wrap and entry.wrap.inverted or false, true)
			end
		end
	end

	--- Applying a choice fans out over every weapon when "All" is selected,
	--- which is how khook's weapon picker behaves (khook.lua:78699).
	local function applyChoice(kind, name)
		local weapon = currentWeapon()
		-- khook only permits the three action cards while "All" is selected on
		-- the Skins page; a concrete skin belongs to one weapon only.
		if weapon == "All" and kind == "Skin"
			and name ~= "Unselected" and name ~= "None" and name ~= "Random" then
			return
		end
		-- khook.lua:90788 `buildChoice` -- a Wrap choice is
		-- `{ kind = "Wrap", wrap = { name, inverted } }`; everything else is
		-- `{ kind, name }`. Building it by hand got the Wrap shape wrong.
		local choice = Cosmetics.Selection.buildChoice(kind, choiceName(name),
			kind == "Wrap" and invertedToggle ~= nil and invertedToggle.Value or false)
		if weapon ~= "All" then
			Cosmetics.SetSkinChangerChoice(weapon, choice)
			return
		end
		for _, w in ipairs(Cosmetics.GetWeapons()) do
			if w ~= "All" then Cosmetics.SetSkinChangerChoice(w, choice) end
		end
	end

	----------------------------------------------------------- General ------
	do
		local page = tab:AddTab({
			Label = "General", Icon = Icons.General,
			Description = "Skin changer, unlocker, weapon selection, and presets.",
		})
		page:OnFirstOpen(function()
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			-- khook.lua:42655 -- "Main" comes first and holds the one switch that
			-- arms the whole tab; nothing below it does anything while it is off.
			local main = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Main", Side = "left" })
			main:AddToggle({ Label = "Cosmetics Enabled", Config = { "Cosmetics", "Enabled" } })

			local configs = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Configs", Side = "right" })
			local configName = configs:AddTextBox({ Label = "Config Name" })
			local configList
			local refreshPresetLists
			configs:AddButton({ Label = "Create", Callback = function()
				local name = Cosmetics.Presets.CleanName(configName.Value)
				if name == "" then Notify("Enter a cosmetics config name", 2) return end
				local ok, err = Cosmetics.Presets.Save(name)
				Notify(ok and ("Created cosmetics config '" .. name .. "'")
					or ("Create failed: " .. tostring(err)), 3)
				refreshPresetLists()
			end })
			configList = configs:AddList({
				Items = Cosmetics.Presets.List(), Height = 140,
				OnChanged = function(name)
					Config.set({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" }, name)
				end,
			})
			local autoLoadName, autoSaveName
			refreshPresetLists = function()
				local names = Cosmetics.Presets.List()
				configList:SetItems(names)
				autoLoadName:SetOptions(names)
				autoSaveName:SetOptions(names)
			end
			configs:AddButton({ Label = "Load", Callback = function()
				local name = configList.Selected or Config.get({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" })
				local ok, err = Cosmetics.Presets.Load(name)
				Notify(ok and ("Loaded cosmetics config '" .. tostring(name) .. "'")
					or ("Load failed: " .. tostring(err)), 3)
				syncSelections()
			end })
			configs:AddButton({ Label = "Save", Callback = function()
				local name = configList.Selected or Config.get({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" })
				local ok, err = Cosmetics.Presets.Save(name)
				Notify(ok and ("Saved cosmetics config '" .. tostring(name) .. "'")
					or ("Save failed: " .. tostring(err)), 3)
				refreshPresetLists()
			end })
			configs:AddButton({ Label = "Delete", Callback = function()
				local name = configList.Selected or Config.get({ "Cosmetics", "Presets", "Name" })
				if Cosmetics.Presets.Delete(name) then Notify("Deleted cosmetics config '" .. tostring(name) .. "'", 2) end
				refreshPresetLists()
			end })
			configs:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Load", Config = { "Cosmetics", "Presets", "AutoLoad" } })
			autoLoadName = configs:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Auto Load Config", Options = Cosmetics.Presets.List(),
				Config = { "Cosmetics", "Presets", "AutoLoadName" }, CloseOnSelect = true,
			})
			configs:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Save", Config = { "Cosmetics", "Presets", "AutoSave" } })
			autoSaveName = configs:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Auto Save Config", Options = Cosmetics.Presets.List(),
				Config = { "Cosmetics", "Presets", "AutoSaveName" }, CloseOnSelect = true,
			})

			local unlock = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Unlock All", Side = "left" })
			local ut = unlock:AddToggle({ Label = "Unlock All", Config = { "UnlockAll", "Enabled" } })
			local ug = unlock:AddGroup({ Source = ut })
			local typeDrop = ug:AddMultiDropdown({
				Label = "Cosmetics", Options = Cosmetics.catalog.Types,
				Config = { "UnlockAll", "Cosmetics" },
			})
			local rarityDrop = ug:AddMultiDropdown({
				Label = "Rarities", Options = Cosmetics.catalog.Rarities,
				Config = { "UnlockAll", "Rarities" },
			})

			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Skin Changer", Side = "left" })
			sec:AddToggle({
				Label = "Skin Changer Enabled",
				Config = { "Cosmetics", "SkinChanger" },
			})
			weaponPicker = sec:AddDropdown({
				Label = "Weapon",
				Options = Cosmetics.GetWeapons(),
				Default = Cosmetics.GetWeapons()[1],
				Search = true,
				Height = 220,
				OnChanged = function()
					local weapon = currentWeapon()
					if grids.Skin then
						grids.Skin:SetItems(gridItems(weapon ~= "All" and Cosmetics.GetSkins(weapon) or {}))
					end
					syncSelections()
				end,
			})

			local other = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Other", Side = "left" })
			other:AddToggle({ Label = "Share Skins", Config = { "UserServer", "ShareSkins" } })
			other:AddToggle({ Label = "Show Active", Config = { "UserServer", "ShowActive" } })
			other:AddButton({ Label = "Reroll Random Cosmetics", Callback = Cosmetics.RerollAll })

			-- khook.lua:245886-246044 -- season charm metadata override.
			local rank = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Rank Charm Override", Side = "right" })
			local seasonLabels, seasonNames = {}, {}
			for _, item in ipairs(Cosmetics.Rank.SEASON_CHARMS or {}) do
				local label = ("Season %s (%s)"):format(tostring(item.version), tostring(item.seasonName))
				table.insert(seasonLabels, label)
				seasonNames[label] = item.charmName
			end
			local ranks = { "Off" }
			for _, name in ipairs(Cosmetics.Rank.RANK_NAMES or {}) do table.insert(ranks, name) end
			local seasonDrop = rank:AddDropdown({ Label = "Season", Options = seasonLabels, CloseOnSelect = true })
			local rankDrop = rank:AddDropdown({ Label = "Rank", Options = ranks, Default = "Off", CloseOnSelect = true })
			local leaderboard = rank:AddTextBox({ Label = "Leaderboard Rank (optional, e.g. 1)" })
			local updatingRank = false
			local function selectedSeason() return seasonNames[seasonDrop.Value] end
			local function pushRank()
				if updatingRank then return end
				local season = selectedSeason()
				if not season then return end
				local name = rankDrop.Value
				local place = tonumber(string.match(leaderboard.Value or "", "^%s*(.-)%s*$"))
				if place then place = math.max(1, math.floor(place)) end
				local selectedRank = (name == nil or name == "Off") and nil or name
				Cosmetics.GetRankCharm():SetForSeason(season, selectedRank, place)
			end
			local function pullRank()
				updatingRank = true
				local name, place = Cosmetics.GetRankCharm():GetForSeason(selectedSeason())
				rankDrop:Set(name or "Off", true)
				leaderboard:Set(place and tostring(place) or "", true)
				updatingRank = false
			end
			seasonDrop:Connect(seasonDrop.ValueChanged, pullRank)
			rankDrop:Connect(rankDrop.ValueChanged, pushRank)
			leaderboard:Connect(leaderboard.ValueChanged, pushRank)

			local function refreshCatalogControls()
				local cat = Cosmetics.catalog
				weaponPicker:SetOptions(cat.Weapons)
				typeDrop:SetOptions(cat.Types)
				rarityDrop:SetOptions(cat.Rarities)

				table.clear(seasonLabels)
				table.clear(seasonNames)
				for _, item in ipairs(Cosmetics.Rank.SEASON_CHARMS or {}) do
					local label = ("Season %s (%s)"):format(tostring(item.version), tostring(item.seasonName))
					table.insert(seasonLabels, label)
					seasonNames[label] = item.charmName
				end
				seasonDrop:SetOptions(seasonLabels)
				local rankOptions = { "Off" }
				for _, name in ipairs(Cosmetics.Rank.RANK_NAMES or {}) do table.insert(rankOptions, name) end
				rankDrop:SetOptions(rankOptions)
				syncSelections()
			end
			page._trove:Connect(Cosmetics.catalogChanged, refreshCatalogControls)
			refreshCatalogControls()
		end)
	end

	------------------------------------------- Skins / Wraps / Charms -------
	-- Each is a one-column page: a Search box over a card grid, exactly as
	-- khook lays them out (Columns = 1, khook.lua:78727).
	local COSMETIC_PAGES = {
		{ label = "Skins",     kind = "Skin",     icon = Icons.Skins,
		  desc = "Browse and apply weapon skin variants." },
		{ label = "Wraps",     kind = "Wrap",     icon = Icons.Wraps,
		  desc = "Pick wraps and toggle inverted patterns." },
		{ label = "Charms",    kind = "Charm",    icon = Icons.Charms,
		  desc = "Browse and apply weapon charms." },
		{ label = "Finishers", kind = "Finisher", icon = Icons.Finishers,
		  desc = "Choose finisher cosmetics for the selected weapon." },
	}

	for _, entry in ipairs(COSMETIC_PAGES) do
		local page = tab:AddTab({ Label = entry.label, Icon = entry.icon, Description = entry.desc })
		page:OnFirstOpen(function()
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 1 })
			local sec = grid:AddSection({ Title = entry.label })

			if entry.kind == "Wrap" then
				invertedToggle = sec:AddToggle({
					Label = "Inverted",
					OnChanged = function()
						if grids.Wrap and grids.Wrap.Value ~= "Unselected" then
							applyChoice("Wrap", grids.Wrap.Value)
						end
					end,
				})
			end

			local search = sec:AddTextBox({ Label = "Search", Placeholder = "filter" })
			local changer = sec:AddSkinChanger({
				Height = 600,
				OnChanged = function(name) applyChoice(entry.kind, name) end,
			})
			grids[entry.kind] = changer
			search:Connect(search.ValueChanged, function(text) changer:SetFilter(text) end)

			-- populate on first open, as khook does
			local cat = Cosmetics.catalog
			if entry.kind == "Skin" then
				local weapon = currentWeapon()
				changer:SetItems(gridItems(weapon ~= "All" and Cosmetics.GetSkins(weapon) or {}))
			elseif entry.kind == "Wrap" then
				changer:SetItems(gridItems(cat.Wraps))
			elseif entry.kind == "Charm" then
				changer:SetItems(gridItems(cat.Charms))
			else
				changer:SetItems(gridItems(cat.Finishers))
			end
			syncSelections()

			page._trove:Connect(Cosmetics.catalogChanged, function()
				local c = Cosmetics.catalog
				if entry.kind == "Skin" then
					local weapon = currentWeapon()
					changer:SetItems(gridItems(weapon ~= "All" and Cosmetics.GetSkins(weapon) or {}))
				elseif entry.kind == "Wrap" then changer:SetItems(gridItems(c.Wraps))
				elseif entry.kind == "Charm" then changer:SetItems(gridItems(c.Charms))
				else changer:SetItems(gridItems(c.Finishers)) end
				syncSelections()
			end)
		end)
	end
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.Automation(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "Automation", Icon = Icons.Automation,
		Description = "Detection, match flow, and tripmines.",
	})

	-- Body deferred: nothing in this tab is constructed until the tab
	-- is first opened, so start-up builds one page instead of eight.
	tab:OnFirstOpen(function()

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Detection", Icon = Icons.Detection,
				Description = "Detect hackers and modders.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local hack = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Hacker Detector", Side = "left" })
			local ht = hack:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Misc", "HackerDetector", "Enabled" } })
			local hg = hack:AddGroup({ Source = ht })
			hg:AddToggle({ Label = "Notify", Config = { "Misc", "HackerDetector", "Notify" } })
			hg:AddColor({ Label = "Highlight Color", Config = { "Misc", "HackerDetector", "Color" } })
			hg:AddLabel({ Text = "Flags players whose humanoid values are outside the game's normal range." })

			local mod = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Mod Detector", Side = "right" })
			local mt = mod:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Misc", "ModDetector", "Enabled" } })
			local mg = mod:AddGroup({ Source = mt })
			mg:AddColor({ Label = "Highlight Color", Config = { "Misc", "ModDetector", "Color" } })
			local modList = mg:AddList({ Items = {}, Height = 120 })
			local nameBox = mg:AddTextBox({ Label = "Add Name", Placeholder = "username" })

			local function refreshMods()
				local names = {}
				for name in pairs(Config.get({ "Misc", "ModDetector", "Moderators" }) or {}) do
					table.insert(names, name)
				end
				table.sort(names)
				modList:SetItems(names)
			end
			mg:AddButton({
				Label = "Add",
				Callback = function()
					local name = nameBox.Value
					if name ~= "" then
						local mods = Config.get({ "Misc", "ModDetector", "Moderators" }) or {}
						mods[name] = true
						Config.set({ "Misc", "ModDetector", "Moderators" }, mods)
						refreshMods()
						nameBox:Set("")
					end
				end,
			})
			mg:AddButton({
				Label = "Remove Selected",
				Callback = function()
					if modList.Selected then
						local mods = Config.get({ "Misc", "ModDetector", "Moderators" }) or {}
						mods[modList.Selected] = nil
						Config.set({ "Misc", "ModDetector", "Moderators" }, mods)
						refreshMods()
					end
				end,
			})
			refreshMods()
		end

		do
			local page = tab:AddTab({
				Label = "Match", Icon = Icons.Match,
				Description = "Queue, vote, respawn, loadout, bans, and pickups.",
			})
			local grid = page:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

			local flow = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Match Flow", Side = "left" })
			flow:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Respawn", Config = { "Misc", "AutoRespawn", "Enabled" } })
			local q = flow:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Queue", Config = { "Misc", "AutoQueue", "Enabled" } })
			flow:AddGroup({ Source = q }):AddDropdown({
				Label = "Queue", Options = { "1v1", "2v2", "Ranked", "Casual" },
				Config = { "Misc", "AutoQueue", "Queue" },
			})
			local v = flow:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Vote", Config = { "Misc", "AutoVote", "Enabled" } })
			flow:AddGroup({ Source = v }):AddMultiDropdown({
				Label = "Maps", Options = {}, Config = { "Misc", "AutoVote", "Maps" },
			})

			local loadout = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Loadout", Side = "right" })
			local lt = loadout:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Loadout", Config = { "Misc", "AutoLoadout", "Enabled" } })
			local lg = loadout:AddGroup({ Source = lt })
			lg:AddToggle({ Label = "Per Map",            Config = { "Misc", "AutoLoadout", "PerMap" } })
			lg:AddToggle({ Label = "Multiple Loadouts",  Config = { "Misc", "AutoLoadout", "MultipleLoadouts" } })

			local ban = loadout:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Ban", Config = { "Misc", "AutoBan", "Enabled" } })
			local bg = loadout:AddGroup({ Source = ban })
			bg:AddMultiDropdown({ Label = "First Ban",  Options = {}, Config = { "Misc", "AutoBan", "FirstWeapons" } })
			bg:AddMultiDropdown({ Label = "Second Ban", Options = {}, Config = { "Misc", "AutoBan", "SecondWeapons" } })

			local pickups = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Pickups", Side = "left" })
			local pt = pickups:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Pickup Drops", Config = { "Misc", "AutoPickupDrops", "Enabled" } })
			pickups:AddGroup({ Source = pt }):AddMultiDropdown({
				Label = "Types", Options = { "Ammo", "Health", "Armor", "Weapon" },
				Config = { "Misc", "AutoPickupDrops", "Types" },
			})

			local mines = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Subspace Tripmines", Side = "right" })
			mines:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Trigger", Config = { "Misc", "SubspaceTripmines", "AutoTrigger" } })
			mines:AddToggle({ Label = "Team Trigger", Config = { "Misc", "SubspaceTripmines", "TeamTrigger" } })

			local exec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Auto Execute", Side = "left" })
			local et = exec:AddToggle({ Label = "Enabled", Config = { "Misc", "AutoExecuteScript", "Enabled" } })
			local eg = exec:AddGroup({ Source = et })
			local scriptList = eg:AddList({ Items = {}, Height = 110 })
			eg:AddButton({
				Label = "Refresh",
				Callback = function()
					local files = {}
					if listfiles and isfolder and isfolder(ROOT_FOLDER .. "/autoexec") then
						for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(ROOT_FOLDER .. "/autoexec")) do
							table.insert(files, (string.match(f, "([^/\\]+)$")))
						end
					end
					scriptList:SetItems(files)
				end,
			})
			eg:AddLabel({ Text = "Files in " .. ROOT_FOLDER .. "/autoexec run on load." })
		end
	end)
end

--============================================================================--

function Build.Settings(window)
	local tab = UI.AddTab(window, {
		Label = "Settings",
		Icon = "rbxassetid://106205298246017",
		Description = "Menu preferences, config management, and theme customization.",
	})

	-- khook.lua:8556 -- General carries everything except the palette.
	local general = tab:AddTab({
		Label = "General", Icon = Icons.Settings,
		Description = "Menu preferences, config management, and theme customization.",
	})
	-- khook.lua:8489 -- Theming has no icon of its own and falls back.
	local theming = tab:AddTab({
		Label = "Theming",
		Description = "Accent and panel colours.",
	})

	general:OnFirstOpen(function()
		local grid = general:Grid({ Columns = 2 })

		-- khook.lua:8578-8667 -------------------------------------------------
		local menuSec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Menu", Side = "left" })
		if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			local menuBind = menuSec:AddKeybind({
				Label = "Menu Keybind", ListLabel = "Menu", InKeybindList = true,
				Modes = { "Tap", "Off" },
				Default = { Key = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift, Mode = "Tap" },
				ConfigMap = { Key = { "Menu", "Keybind", "Bind" } },
			})
			menuBind:SetFeatureEnabled(true)
		end
		menuSec:AddDropdown({ Label = "Font", Options = { "Inconsolata" }, Default = "Inconsolata" })
		if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			menuSec:AddToggle({
				Label = "Show Keybinds", Default = true,
				OnChanged = function(v)
					KeybindList.Enabled = v
					if KeybindList.refresh then KeybindList.refresh() end
				end,
			})
		end
		menuSec:AddToggle({ Label = "Show Watermark", Config = { "Watermark", "Enabled" } })
		if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			menuSec:AddToggle({ Label = "Invisible Menu Button", Default = false })
			menuSec:AddButton({ Label = "Edit Keybinds", Callback = function() end })
		end
		menuSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Unload", Confirm = true,
			Callback = function()
				if getgenv().__khookclone then getgenv().__khookclone.Unload() end
			end,
		})

		-- khook.lua:8672-8920 -------------------------------------------------
		local cfgSec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Configuration", Side = "right" })
		local nameBox = cfgSec:AddTextBox({ Label = "Config Name" })
		local list
		local autoSaveName
		local autoLoadName

		local function refreshList()
			local names = Config.list()
			if list then list:SetItems(names) end
			if autoSaveName then autoSaveName:SetOptions(names) end
			if autoLoadName then autoLoadName:SetOptions(names) end
		end

		cfgSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Create",
			Callback = function()
				local name = string.gsub(nameBox.Value, "%s+", "")
				if name == "" then return end
				local ok, err = Config.save(name)
				if not ok then Notify("Create failed: " .. tostring(err), 3) end
				refreshList()
			end,
		})
		cfgSec:AddLabel({ Text = "Configs", NoSeparator = true })
		list = cfgSec:AddList({ Items = Config.list(), Height = 140 })
		cfgSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Load", Confirm = true,
			Callback = function()
				local name = list.Selected
				if not name then return end
				local ok, err = Config.load(name)
				if not ok then Notify("Load failed: " .. tostring(err), 3) end
			end,
		})
		cfgSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Save", Confirm = true,
			Callback = function()
				local name = list.Selected
				if name then Config.save(name) end
			end,
		})
		cfgSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Delete", Confirm = true,
			Callback = function()
				if list.Selected then Config.delete(list.Selected) refreshList() end
			end,
		})
		cfgSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Export to Clipboard",
			Callback = function()
				if not setclipboard then return end
				local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONEncode(encodeValue(Config.data))
				end)
				if ok then setclipboard(encoded) end
			end,
		})
		local importBox = cfgSec:AddTextBox({ Label = "Import (paste config)", FocusLostOnly = true })
		cfgSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Import", Confirm = true,
			Callback = function()
				if importBox.Value == "" then return end
				local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
					return decodeValue(HttpService:JSONDecode(importBox.Value))
				end)
				if not ok or typeof(decoded) ~= "table" then return end
				local fresh = deepCopy(Config.defaults)
				deepMergeKnown(fresh, decoded)
				mergeInto(Config.data, fresh)
				Config.refreshAll()
			end,
		})
		local autoSave = cfgSec:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Save", Default = false })
		autoSaveName = cfgSec:AddDropdown({
			Label = "Auto Save Config", Options = Config.list(), CloseOnSelect = true,
		})
		local autoLoad = cfgSec:AddToggle({ Label = "Auto Load", Default = false })
		autoLoadName = cfgSec:AddDropdown({
			Label = "Auto Load Config", Options = Config.list(), CloseOnSelect = true,
		})
		autoSave:Connect(Config.changed, function()
			if autoSave.Value and autoSaveName.Value then Config.save(autoSaveName.Value) end
		end)
		autoLoad:Connect(autoLoad.ValueChanged, function(on)
			if on and autoLoadName.Value then Config.load(autoLoadName.Value) end
		end)
	end)

	theming:OnFirstOpen(function()
		local grid = theming:Grid({ Columns = 1 })

		------------------------------------------------------------ theme -----
		local themeSec = grid:AddSection({ Title = "Theme Colors", Side = "full" })
		for _, key in ipairs({
			"Accent", "Outline", "Background", "ElementBackground",
			"TabButtonSelected", "Unselected", "TextColor",
			"ToggleCircleUnselected", "ToggleBackgroundUnselected",
		}) do
			themeSec:AddColor({
				Label = key,
				Config = { "Theme", key },
				Default = Theme.get(key),
				OnChanged = function(c) Theme.set(key, c) end,
			})
		end
		themeSec:AddButton({
			Label = "Reset Theme",
			Callback = function()
				for key, color in pairs(Config.defaults.Theme) do
					Config.set({ "Theme", key }, color)
					Theme.set(key, color)
				end
				Notify("Theme reset", 2)
			end,
		})
	end)
end
--============================================================================--
-- 10. BOOTSTRAP
--============================================================================--

local Bootstrap = {}

--- khook.lua:104536 `FadeCanvasGroup` -- the whole window is one CanvasGroup
--- so opening and closing is a single group fade, not a Visible flip.
local function toggleMenu(visible)
	if visible == nil then visible = not UI.Visible end
	UI.Visible = visible
	if not visible then
		Popups.close()
		Tooltip.hide()
		local t = tween(UI.Root, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
		t.Completed:Once(function()
			if not UI.Visible then UI.Root.Visible = false end
		end)
	else
		UI.Root.Visible = true
		tween(UI.Root, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, TWEEN_MAIN)
	end
end

--- Theme colours live in the config like everything else, so a loaded profile
--- repaints the menu.
local function bindThemeToConfig(trove)
	for key in pairs(Theme.Colors) do
		trove:Add(Config.watch({ "Theme", key }, function(color)
			if typeof(color) == "Color3" then Theme.set(key, color) end
		end))
	end
end

function Bootstrap.start()
	-- The registry has to be live before any feature or the menu reads it.
	Registry.start(Registry.Trove)
	FEATURE_TROVE:Add(Registry.Trove)
	FEATURE_TROVE:Add(Resolver.Trove)

	local window = UI.build()

	Build.Combat(window)
	Build.Player(window)
	Build.Weapon(window)
	Build.ESP(window)
	Build.World(window)
	Build.Cosmetics(window)
	Build.Automation(window)
	Build.Settings(window)

	if window.Tabs[1] then window.Tabs[1]:Open() end

	----------------------------------------------------------- features ----
	local ft = FEATURE_TROVE
	local started, failed = {}, {}

	local function start(name, fn, ...)
		local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
		if ok then
			table.insert(started, name)
			if Features.status[name] == nil then Features.status[name] = "ok" end
		else
			table.insert(failed, name)
			Features.status[name] = "error: " .. tostring(err)
			warn(("[khookclone] %s failed to start: %s"):format(name, tostring(err)))
		end
	end

	--- khook.lua:135877 -- one ticker walks every animated colour, whatever
	--- element registered it.
	--- khook.lua:138983 -- the desync restore is bound at the highest render
	--- priority so the real CFrame is back before anything else reads it.
	start("desync", function(trove)
		-- khook.lua:14670 -- the view-angle driver is flushed once a frame
		trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(Rivals.ViewAngles.flush))
		trove:Add(Rivals.ViewAngles.clearAll)
		-- khook.lua:25612 -- both hooks come back out on unload
		trove:Add(Rivals.ViewAngles.revertHooks)
		RunService:BindToRenderStep("\0kc_desync\0",
			Enum.RenderPriority.First.Value, Rivals.Desync.restore)
		trove:Add(function()
			pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("\0kc_desync\0") end)
			Rivals.Desync.restore()
		end)
	end, ft)
	start("coloranim", function(trove)
		ColorAnimator.restore()
		trove:Add(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(ColorAnimator.step))
	end, ft)
	start("viewangles",     ViewAngles.init, ft)
	start("keybinds",       startKeybindDispatch, ft)
	start("fov",            FovCircle.init, ft)
	start("aimbot",         Features.Aimbot, ft)
	start("silentaim",      Features.SilentAim, ft)
	start("triggerbot",     Features.Triggerbot, ft)
	start("flickbot",       Features.Flickbot, ft)
	start("ragebot",        Features.Ragebot, ft)
	start("targetlock",     Features.TargetLock, ft)
	start("esp",            ESP.init, ft)
	start("movement",       Features.Movement, ft)
	start("camera",         Features.CameraFeatures, ft)
	start("crosshair",      Features.Crosshair, ft)
	start("visuals",        Features.LocalVisuals, ft)
	start("animationplayer", Features.AnimationPlayer, ft)
	start("world",          Features.World, ft)
	start("soundviz",       Features.SoundVisualizer, ft)
	start("tracers",        Features.BulletTracers, ft)
	start("feedback",       Features.Feedback, ft)
	start("misc",           Features.Misc, ft)
	start("recorder",       Features.Recorder, ft)
	start("cosmetics",      Cosmetics.init, ft)
	start("itemmodifiers",  Features.ItemModifiers, ft)
	start("match",          Features.MatchAutomation, ft)

	----------------------------------------------------------- overlays ----
	start("watermark",   Watermark.init, Overlay.Trove)
	start("keybindlist", KeybindList.init, Overlay.Trove)
	bindThemeToConfig(Overlay.Trove)

	--------------------------------------------------- background resolve ---
	-- The GC sweep is expensive, so it runs a handful of times right after
	-- load (while the game finishes streaming its modules in) and then stops.
	-- Nothing polls it afterwards; the Settings page has a manual rescan.
	task.spawn(function()
		for attempt = 1, 6 do
			if Resolver.sweep() > 0 then Cosmetics.RefreshCatalog() end
			local pending = false
			for name in pairs(Resolver.signatures) do
				if Resolver.cache[name] == nil then pending = true break end
			end
			if not pending then break end
			task.wait(attempt <= 2 and 1 or 4)
		end
	end)

	------------------------------------------------------- menu keybind ----
	UI.Trove:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
		if gpe then return end
		local bind = Config.get({ "Menu", "Keybind", "Bind" }) or Enum.KeyCode.RightShift
		if input.KeyCode == bind then toggleMenu() end
	end))

	-- khook.lua:121268 -- the collapsed pill advertises the shortcut it binds
	UI.Trove:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
		if gpe or not UI.Visible then return end
		if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.F then return end
		if not (UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
			or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.RightControl)) then return end
		local tab = UI.Window and UI.Window.ActiveTab
		if tab and tab._search then tab._search.Expand() end
	end))

	UI.UIScale.Scale = Config.get({ "Menu", "Scale" }) or 1
	UI.Root.Visible = true
	tween(UI.Root, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, TWEEN_MAIN)

	------------------------------------------------------------- unload ----
	local function unload()
		pcall(Popups.close)
		pcall(function() Cosmetics.RestoreAll() end)
		pcall(function() FEATURE_TROVE:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() Overlay.Trove:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() UI.Trove:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() Game.Trove:Destroy() end)
		table.clear(ESP.objects)
		getgenv().__khookclone = nil
		if shared then shared.__khookclone = nil end
	end

	getgenv().__khookclone = {
		Version = CLONE_VERSION,
		UI = UI,
		Config = Config,
		Theme = Theme,
		Features = Features,
		Registry = Registry,
		Resolver = Resolver,
		Rivals = Rivals,
		Motion = Motion,
		ESP = ESP,
		Cosmetics = Cosmetics,
		Notify = Notify,
		Toggle = toggleMenu,
		Unload = unload,
	}
	if shared then shared.__khookclone = getgenv().__khookclone end

	--------------------------------------------------------- auto-execute ---
	task.spawn(function()
		if not (Config.get({ "Misc", "AutoExecuteScript", "Enabled" })
			and listfiles and isfolder and readfile) then return end
		local dir = ROOT_FOLDER .. "/autoexec"
		if not isfolder(dir) then return end
		for _, file in ipairs(listfiles(dir)) do
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				local chunk = loadstring(readfile(file))
				if chunk then chunk() end
			end)
			if not ok then warn("[khookclone] autoexec " .. file .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
		end
	end)

	Notify(("khook clone loaded -- %d modules"):format(#started), 4)
	if #failed > 0 then
		Notify("Failed: " .. table.concat(failed, ", ") .. " (see Settings > Diagnostics)", 6)
	end
	return getgenv().__khookclone
end

local ok, err = pcall(Bootstrap.start)
if not ok then
	warn("[khookclone] fatal: " .. tostring(err))
	pcall(function() FEATURE_TROVE:Destroy() end)
	pcall(function() Overlay.Trove:Destroy() end)
	pcall(function() UI.Trove:Destroy() end)
	error(err, 0)
end

return getgenv().__khookclone
